		paṭumaṇi iṉaniri uṇīiya kōvalar
		viṭunilam uṭitta kaluḻ2kaṭ kūval
		kaṉṟuṭi maṭappiṭi kaḷiṟoṭu taṭavarum
	10.	puṉṟali maṉṟat taṅkuṭi cīṟūrt
		tuṇiyoṭu tuccil irukkuṅ kollō
		kaṇiyōr añcāk kaṭuṅkaṭ kāḷiyoṭu
		elli muṉṉuṟac celluṅ kollō
		evviṉi ceyuṅkol nōkō yāṉē
	15.	aripeytu potinta tericilampu kaḻīi
		3yāyaṟi vuṟutal añci
		vēyuyar piṟaṅkal maliyiṟan tōḷē.		- kayamaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15-17. ari peytu potinta teri cilampu kaḻīi - paraliṉiyiṭṭu mūṭiya ārāyntu koṇṭa cilampiṉi nīkki,  yāy aṟivuṟutal añci - taṉ tāy aṟitali añci, vēy uyar piṟaṅkal mali iṟantōḷ - mūṅkil uyarnta pakka maliyi yuṭiya maliyit taṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ kaṭantuceṉṟa eṉ makaḷ,
1-6. pacitta yāṉip paḻaṅkaṇ aṉṉa - paciyuṟṟa yāṉiyiṉ polivaṟṟa kaṇpōṉṟa, vaṟu cuṉi mukanta kōṭit teḷviḷi- nīraṟṟa cuṉiyuṭ pukuntuvarum mēl kāṟṟiṉ teḷinta oli, vicittu vāṅku paṟiyiṉ viṭar akattu iyampa - tōliṉi iḻuttuk kaṭṭiya paṟiyolipōla malippiḷappil olikka, katirkāl ampiṇi uṇīiya - melintu nīṇṭa kāliṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya peṇmāṉ uṇṇutaṟku, pukal ēṟu - ataṉpāl viruppam mikka kalimāṉ, kutirkāl iruppi veṇ pū uṇṇātu - kutir pōṉṟa aṭimarattiṉiyuṭiya iruppi marattiṉ veḷḷiya pūviṉit tāṉ uṇṇātu, āṇ kural viḷikkum - taṉatu āṇkural tōṉṟa ataṉi aḻikkum, cēṇpāl - cēymik kaṇṇuḷḷa,
6-10. viyaṉ curi paṭumaṇi iṉam niri uṇīiya - periya maṭiyiṉiyum olikkum maṇiyiṉiyumuṭiya kūṭṭamāya āṉiri kaḷ paruka, kōvalar - āyar, viṭunilam uṭitta kaluḻkaṇ kūval - taricu nilattē kaṟkaḷi uṭittu iyaṟṟiya pilkum ūṟṟuk kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kiṇaṟṟiṉi, kaṉṟu uṭi maṭappiṭi kaḷiṟoṭu taṭavarum - kaṉṟiṉiyuṭiya iḷiya piṭi taṉ kaḷiṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭit taṭavip pārkkum, puṉtali maṉṟattu am kuṭi cīṟūr - polivaṟṟa iṭattiṉiyuṭiya maṉṟiṉiyum, aḻakiya kuṭiyiruppiṉiyum uṭiya cīṟūriṉ kaṇṇē,
11-14. elli tuṇiyoṭu tuccil irukkuṅkol -iravil taṉ tuṇivaṉuṭaṉ ōr otukkiṭattē taṅkiyiruppaḷō (aṉṟi avviravil), kaṇiyōr añcāk kaṭuṅkaṇ kāḷiyoṭu - ampiṉi eyyum āṟalip pōri añcāta taṟukaṇmiyi yuṭiya kāḷiyāya taṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ, muṉ uṟac cellumkol - avaṉ muṉṉē naṭantu celluvāḷō? e viṉi ceyum kol - eṉṉa kāriyattiṉic ceyvaḷō? yāṉ nōku - yāṉ nōvēṉā kiṉṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) yāy aṟivuṟutalañci cilampu kaḻīi maliyiṟan tōḷ, cēṇpāl cīṟūr elli tuṇiyoṭu tuccil irukkuṅkol, kāḷiyoṭu muṉṉuṟac celluṅkol, evviṉi ceyuṅkol? yāṉ nōku.
(vi-ri) paciyāl vāṭiya yāṉiyiṉ kaṇ kuḻintu paciyiṉṟi yiruttaliṉ ataṉi vaṟuñcuṉikku uvami kūṟiṉār. vicittu vāṅku paṟi - kaṭippiṉi iḻuttu olippikkum iḻuku paṟi eṉṟumām. iruppiyiṉ pū, piṇiyum tāṉum uṇpataṟkup pōtiya taṉṟākaliṉ, piṇiyi māttiram uṇpikka vēṇṭiya kalimāṉ, tāṉ ataṉi uṇṇāmalē piṇiyi viḷikkum eṉṟār. āṇkural viḷikkum eṉṟatu, piṇi ētamiṉṟeṉat tuṇintu varutaṟkut taṉatu āṇmit taṉmiyuṭiya kuralāl viḷikkum eṉṟapaṭi. cēṇpāluḷḷa cīṟūr eṉak kūṭṭuka. viyaṉ cuval eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ periya kaḻuttiṉ kaṇ (poruntiya maṇi) eṉṟurikka. viṭunilam - payir ceyyātu viṭappaṭṭa nilam. kaṉṟuṭi maṭappiṭi kaḷiṟoṭu taṭavarum eṉṟataṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēṉum uṇpataṟku nīrillāmi peṟṟām. tuccil - piṟar maṉiyil uḷḷa otukkiṭam. ittakiya kāṭṭiṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa cīṟūril irāppoḻutu tuṇiva ṉoṭu tuccil iruttal, aṉṟi avaṉuṭaṉ naṭantu cellutal āya iraṇṭum tuṉpam viḷippaṉavē eṉpāḷ evviṉi ceyuṅ kol nōkō yāṉē eṉṟāḷ.
322. kuṟiñci
(allakuṟippaṭṭup pōkiṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		vayaṅkuveyil ñemiyap pāay miṉ ṉuvacipu
		mayaṅkutuḷi poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		ārāk kāmam aṭūuniṉ ṟalippa
		iṟuvari vīḻnariṉ naṭuṅkit teṟuvarap
	5.	pāmpeṟi kōliṟ ṟamiyi vikit
		tēmputi kollō neñcē urumicik
		kaḷiṟukaṇ kūṭiya vāṇmayaṅku ñāṭpiṉ
		oḷiṟuvēṟ ṟāṉik kaṭuntērt titiyaṉ
		varupuṉal iḻitaru marampayil iṟumpiṟ
	10.	piṟiyuṟaḻ maruppiṟ kaṭuṅkaṭ 1 paṉṟik
		kuṟiyār koṭuvari kuḻumuñ cāral
		aṟiyuṟu tīntēṉ kuṟavar aṟuppa
		muyalunar muṟṟā ēṟṟaru neṭuñcimip
		pukalarum potiyil 2pōlap
	15.	peṟalaruṅ kuriyaṇam aṇaṅki yōḷē.		- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 6. neñcē!
15. nam aṇaṅkiyōḷ - nammi iṅṅaṉam varuttiya nam kātali,
6-15. urum ici kaḷiṟu kaṇkūṭiya vāḷ mayaṅku ñāṭpiṉ - iṭipōṉṟa muḻakkiṉiyuṭiya āṇ yāṉikaḷ tiraṇṭa vāṭkaḷ piṉṉiya pōrkkaṇṇē, oḷiṟu vēl tāṉi kaṭumtēr titiyaṉ - viḷaṅkum vēliṉiyuṭiya cēṉiyiṉiyum kaṭiya tēriṉiyum uṭiya titiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, varupuṉal iḻitaru maram payil iṟumpil - maliyiṉiṉṟu varum aruvi viḻum maraṅkaḷ neruṅkiya kāṭṭil, piṟi uṟaḻ maruppiṉ kaṭuṅkaṇ paṉṟi - piṟimatiyiṉi yotta kōṭṭiṉiyum añcāmiyiṉiyu muṭiya paṉṟiyiṉatu, kuṟi ār koṭuvari kuḻumum cāral - iṟiccit tuṇṭiṉit tiṉṟa pulikaḷ kūṭi muḻaṅkum pakka maliyiṉiyum, aṟi uṟu tīm tēṉ aṟuppa muyalunar kuṟavar - uccippāṟiyil uḷḷa iṉiya tēṉi aṟutteṭukka muyalum kuṟavarkaḷum, muṟṟā - eytiyaṟiyāta, ēṟṟu aru neṭu cimi - ēṟutal ariya nīṇṭa uccimaliyiṉiyu muṭiya, pukal arum potiyil pōl - pukutaṟ kariya potiyil maliyiṉip pōla, peṟal aruṅ kuriyaḷ - peṟutaṟku arumi yāyavaḷ aṉṟē! (ataṉi uṇarātu),
1-6. vayaṅku veyil ñemiya pāay - viḷaṅkum veyil maṟiyavē (mēkam) parantu, miṉṉu vacipu- miṉṉal piḷantiṭa, mayaṅku tuḷi poḻinta - mikka maḻiyic corinta, pāṉāṭ kaṅkul - naṭu iravil, ārāk kāmam aṭūu niṉṟu alippa - amiyāta kāmam varuttā niṉṟu alittiṭa, iṟu vari vīḻnariṉ - mali mukaṭṭiṉiṉṟu vīḻntiṟappār pōla, teṟu vara naṭuṅki - accam uṇṭāka naṭuṅki, pāmpu eṟi kōlil - kōlāl aṭikkappaṭṭa pāmpu pōl, tamiyi viki - vēṟu tuṇiyiṉṟi yiruntu, tēmputi kollō - vāṭuviyō.
(muṭipu) neñcē! em aṇaṅkiyōḷ, potiyil pōlap peṟalaruṅ kuriyaḷ; (ataṉi uṇarātu) pāṉāṭ kaṅkul ārāk kāmam aṭūu niṉ ṟalippa, naṭuṅki, kōleṟi pāmpiṉ tēmputi kollō.
(vi-ri) mēkam pāay eṉa varuvitturikka. vacipu - vaciya eṉat tirikka. kāmam aṭūu niṉṟu alippa eṉpataṉi iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalākak koṇṭu, alittalāl vīḻnar pōla eṉavum alittalāl naṭuṅki eṉavum koḷka. iṟu vari - maliyiṉ iṟuvāy. pāmpeṟi kōliṉ eṉpataṉik kōl eṟi pāmpiṉ eṉa māṟuka. aṭiyuṇṭa pāmpu ceyalaṟṟuk kiṭappatu pōla, nīyum ceyalaṟṟuk kiṭattiyō eṉpāṉ pāmpu eṟi kōlil tēmputi kollō eṉṟāṉ. kaḷiṟu kaṇ kūṭiya: kaṇ, aci. titiyaṉatu potiyil eṉavum, cāraliṉiyum cimiyiṉiyu muṭiya potiyil eṉavum kūṭṭuka. paṉṟikkuṟi - paṉṟiyiṉ taci.  ārtal - uṇṭal. paṉṟi kuṟiyāk koṭuvari eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ paṉṟiyik koṉṟu puli (muḻaṅkum) eṉka. tīntēṉ aṟuppa muyalunar kuṟavar muṟṟā eṉak koṇṭu kūṭṭuka. kuṟavarum muṟṟā eṉac ciṟappummi tokkatākak koḷka.
323. pāli
(piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		immeṉ pēralar ivvūr namvayiṟ 		
		ceyvōr ēccol vāṭak kātalar
		varuvar eṉpatu 1vāyva tākal
		aiya ceyya mataṉila ciṟiyaniṉ
	5.	aṭinilaṉ uṟutal añcip piyat
		taṭavaral otukkan takikoḷa iyalik
		kāṇiya vammō kaṟpumēm paṭuvi
		palavuppala taṭiiya vēypayi laṭukkattu
		yāṉic celliṉam kaṭuppa vāṉattu
	10.	vayaṅkukatir maḻuṅkap pāayp pāmpiṉ
		pipaṭa iṭikkuṅ kaṭuṅkural ēṟṟoṭu
		āli yaḻituḷi taliik
		kālvīḻt taṉṟuniṉ katuppuṟam puyalē.
- paṟanāṭṭup peruṅ koṟṟaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 7. kaṟpu mēmpaṭuvi - kaṟpiṉāl mēmpaṭṭa taliviyē, 
8-13. palavu pala taṭiiya - palā maraṅkaḷ pala tiraṇṭuḷḷa vēypayil aṭukkattu - mūṅkil neruṅkiya pakka maliyil, yāṉi celiṉam kaṭuppa - cellukiṉṟa yāṉik kūṭṭattiṉip pōla, vāṉattu - vāṉiṉkaṇ, vayaṅku katir maḻuṅka pāay - viḷaṅkum ñāyiṟu maḻuṅkumāṟu paravi, pāmpiṉ pi paṭa iṭikkum kaṭu kural ēṟṟoṭu - pāmpiṉatu paṭam aḻiya iṭikkum kaṭiya kuraliṉiyuṭiya iṭiyēṟṟuṭaṉ, āli aḻi tuḷi talii - nīrkkaṭṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya mikka tuḷikaḷip peytu, niṉ katuppu uṟaḻ puyal - niṉ kūntali yotta mēkam, kāl vīḻttaṉṟu - kāl iṟakkiyatu;
1-3. im eṉ pēr alar - immeṉṟu eḻum periya alariṉi, iūr - ivvūriṉkaṇ, namvayiṉ ceyvōr - nampāṟ ceyvōratu, ē col vāṭa - cerukkuṟṟa col aḻiya, kātalar varuvar eṉpatu - nam kātalar vantu viṭuvar eṉpatu, vāyvatu ākal- uṇmi yātali,
4-7. niṉ - niṉatu, aiya ceyya mataṉ ila ciṟiya aṭi - aḻakiya civanta valiyaṟṟa ciṟiya aṭikaḷ, nilaṉ uṟutal añci - nilattiṟ poruntutali añci, piya - melleṉa, taṭavaral otukkam taki koḷa iyali - uṭal vaḷintu naṭakkum naṭi aḻaku peṟa naṭantu, kāṇiya vammō - kāṇutaṟka varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) kaṟpu mēmpaṭuvi, puyal kāl vīḻttaṉṟu; pēr alar namvayiṟ ceyvōr, ēccol vāṭak kātalar varuvareṉpatu vāyvatākal, takikoḷa iyali, kāṇiya vammō.
(vi-ri) immeṉ pēralar - iṭiviṭāta periya alar eṉka. ēc col - ēkkaḻuttamuṭiya col, tarukkuṭiya col. vāṭutal - oḻital. vāyvatākal kāṇiya vammō eṉavum taki koḷa iyali vammō eṉavum kūṭṭuka. taṭavaral - vaḷivu. otukkam - naṭi. vāṉattu vayaṅku katir eṉavum, vāṉattup pāay eṉavum vāṉam eṉpatu īriṭattum ceṉṟu iyiyum. puyal kāl vīḻt taṉṟu ākalāl, iṉi, kārkālat toṭakkattē kātalar varutal uṇmi eṉa vēṉiliṉ iṟutikkaṇ tōḻi taliviyi vaṟpuṟut tiṉāḷ eṉka.
324. mulli
(viṉi muṟṟiya talimakaṉ karuttuṇarntu uḻiyar colliyatu.)
		viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlun tiruntiḻit
		taṭameṉ paṇittōḷ maṭamoḻi yarivi
		taḷiriyaṟ kiḷḷi iṉitiṉ eṭutta
		1vaḷarāp piḷḷit tūvi yaṉṉa
	5.	2vārpeyal vaḷartta pimpayirp puṟaviṟ
		paṟikkaṇ aṉṉa niṟiccuṉi tōṟun
		tuḷipaṭu mokkuḷ tuḷḷuvaṉa cālat
		toḷiporu pokuṭṭut tōṉṟuvaṉa māya
		vaḷiciṉi yutirttaliṉ veṟikoḷpu tāayc
	10.	ciraṟciṟa kēyppa aṟaṟkaṇ varitta
		vaṇṭuṇ naṟuvī tumitta nēmi
		taṇṇila maruṅkiṟ pōḻnta vaḻiyuḷ
		niricel pāmpiṉ viripunīr muṭukac
		cellum neṭuntaki tērē
	15.	mulli māli nakarpukal āyntē.
- okkūr mācāttiyār.
(co-ḷ) 3-15. taḷir iyal kiḷḷi - taḷiriṉ iyal vāynta kiḷi, iṉitiṉ eṭutta - iṉitu vaḷartta, vaḷarāp piḷḷi tūvi aṉṉa - iḷiya pārppiṉ ciṟakiṉi yotta, vār peyal vaḷartta pi payir puṟavil - peyyum maḻi vaḷartta paciya payiriṉiyuṭiya kāṭṭil, paṟi kaṇ aṉṉa niṟic cuṉi tōṟum, paṟiyiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa niṟitaliyuṭiya cuṉikaḷ tōṟum tuḷipaṭu mokkuḷ tuḷḷuvaṉa cāla - maḻi peytalāl uṇṭākiya kumiḻikaḷ kutippaṉavaṟṟi yoppa, toḷi poru pokuṭṭu tōṉṟuvaṉa māya - cēṟṟiṉ kaṇṇē porutalāl eḻum kumiḻikaḷ tōṉṟi maṟiya, vaḷi ciṉi utirttaliṉ veṟi koḷpu tāay - ciṉiyiṉiṉṟum kāṟṟu utirttalāl maṇam koṇṭu parantu, ciral ciṟaku ēyppa aṟal kaṇ varitta - ciccilip paṟaviyiṉ ciṟakiṉi yoppa aṟaliṉiṭattuk kōlam ceyta, vaṇṭu uṇ naṟuvī tumitta nēmi - vaṇṭukaḷ tēṉ uṇṇum naṟiya pūkkaḷi aṟuttu ēkiya tēruruḷ, taṇ nila maruṅkil pōḻnta vaḻiyuḷ - kuḷirnta nilattiṭattē piḷantu ceṉṟa cuvaṭṭil, niri cel pāmpiṉ viripu nīr muṭuka - oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka varici yuṟṟuc cellum pāmpu pōla virintu nīr muṭukic cella, mulli māli nakar pukal āyntu - mulli malarum mālik kālattē nakariṉkaṇ pukutali āyntuṇarntu, neṭuntaki tēr cellum - peruntakiyāya talivaṉ tēr cellāniṟkum;
1-2. tiruntu iḻi taṭa mel paṇi tōḷ maṭa moḻi arivi - tiruntiya aṇiyiṉiyum periya meṉmi vāynta mūṅkil pōlum tōḷiṉiyum maṭappam vāynta moḻiyiṉiyu muṭiya talivi, viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlum - iṉṟu viruntayartalum peṟuvāḷ pōlum.
(muṭipu) neṭuntaki tēr, puṟavil, toḷi poru pokuṭṭut tōṉṟuvaṉa māya, nēmi pōḻnta vaḻiyuḷ niri cel pāmpiṉ viripu nīr muṭuka, māli, nakar pukal āyntu cellum; arivi viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlum.
(vi-ri) arivi viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlum eṉka. viruntum, ummi ciṟappu. vaḷar iḷam piḷḷi eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku vaḷartaṟkuriya iḷam piḷḷi eṉka. cāla - oppa eṉṉum poruṭṭu. toḷi - cēṟu. pokuṭṭu - kalaṅkal nīril eḻum kumiḻi. talivaṉ tēr ēṟic celvaṉ eṉṟataṉi, talivaṉatu tēr cellum eṉṟār. cellum ākaliṉ viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlum eṉa iyitturikka.
(mē-ḷ) `āṟṟatu paṇpum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, talivaṉ mīḷuṅ kāl viruntu peṟukuvaḷ kolleṉa iḷiyōr talivi niliyurittalukku icceyyuḷik kāṭṭiṉar nac.
325. pāli
(2koṇṭu nīṅkak karuti yoḻinta talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ
 talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		amma vāḻi tōḻi kātalar
		veṇmaṇal nivanta polaṅkaṭi neṭunakar
		naḷiyiruṅ kaṅkuṟ puṇarkuṟi vāytta
		kaḷavuṅ kimmika 3vaḷarntaṉ ṟaṉṉiyum
	5.	uṭkoṇ ṭōvāḷ kākkum piṟperitu
		ivaṇuṟi vevaṉō aḷiyaḷeṉ ṟaruḷi
		āṭunaṭip polinta pukaṟciyiṉ 4nāṭukōḷ
		aḷḷaṉip paṇitta atiyaṉ piṉṟi
		vaḷḷuyir mākkiṇi 5kaṇṇavin tāṅku
	10.	malikaviṉ aḻinta kaṉikaṭaṟ ṟaruñcuram
		veyya maṉṟaniṉ viyeyi ṟuṇīiya
		taṇmaḻi yorunāḷ taliiyiya oṇṇutal
		olkiyal arivi niṉṉoṭu celkañ
		ciṉṉāḷ āṉṟaṉi yākeṉap paṉṉāḷ.
	15.	ulivil uḷḷamoṭu viṉivali 6yuṟīi
		ellām perumpiṟi tāka vaṭāatu
		7nalvēṟ pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷati
		vāṭkaṇ vāṉat teṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭi
		āṭkol yāṉi atarpārt talkum
	20.	cōli attam mālip pōki
		oḻiyac ceṉṟōr maṉṟa
		paḻiyevaṉ āṅkol nōytaru pālē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, amma - yāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēṭpāyāka, kātalar - nam kātalar, veṇmaṇal nivanta - veḷḷiya maṇal uyarnta, polaṉ kaṭi neṭu nakar - polivu peṟṟa vāyiliyuṭiya periya maṉiyiṉkaṇ, naḷi iru kaṅkul - iruḷ ceṟinta nīṇṭa iravil, puṇar kuṟi vāytta - puṇarum kuṟi aminta, kaḷavum - kaḷavoḻukkamum, kimika vaḷarntaṉṟu - varampi kantu nīṇṭatu, aṉṉiyum -, uṭ koṇṭu - ikkaḷaviṉi uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭu, ōvāḷ kākkum - nīṅkātavaḷākik kāval ceytirukkum, piṉ peritu ivaṇ uṟivu evaṉ - piṉṉar mikavum iṅkut taṅkiyiruttal eṅṅaṉam iyalvatākum, aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷ, eṉṟu aruḷi - eṉṟu irakkamuṟṟu,
7-14. āṭu naṭip polinta pukaṟciyiṉ - veṉṟi yoḻukkat tāl mēmpaṭṭa viruppattiṉāl, aḷḷaṉi nāṭukōḷ paṇitta- aḷḷaṉ eṉpāṉi nāṭṭiṉik koḷḷumāṟu paṇitta, atiyaṉ piṉṟi - atiyaṉ eṉpāṉ tuñciya piṉpu, vaḷ uyir mā kiṇi kaṇ avintāṅku - ciṟanta oliyiṉiyuṭiya periya kiṇiyāṉatu oli aṭaṅkiṉāṟpōla, mali kaviṉ aḻinta kaṉi kaṭaṟṟu aru curam - maliyiṉ aḻaku oḻinta ceṟinta kāṭākiya ariya pāli vaḻikaḷ, veyyamaṉṟa - oru taliyākak koṭiyaṉa, niṉ vi eyiṟu uṇīiya - niṉ kūriya paṟkaḷ nīr parukumāṟu, taṇ maḻi oru nāḷ taliiyiya - kuḷirnta maḻi orunāḷ peyvatāka, oḷ nutal olku iyal arivi - oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉiyum, taḷarum iyaliṉiyumuṭiya ariviyē, niṉṉoṭu celkam - piṉ niṉṉuṭaṉ celvēm, cilanāḷ āṉṟaṉi āka eṉa - cila nāḷ amintiruppāyāka eṉṟu,
14-16. pal nāḷ ulivu il uḷḷamoṭu - palakālam taḷar talillāta ūkkattuṭaṉ, viṉi vali yuṟīi - koṇṭutalik kaḻitalākiya viṉiyi vaṟpuṟut turittu, ellām perum piṟitāka - aviyellām aḻintupōka,
16-21. vaṭāatu - vaṭakkiṉkaṇ, nal vēl pāṇaṉ nalnāṭ ṭuḷḷati - nalla vēliyuṭiya pāṇaṉatu ciṟanta nāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷatāya, vāḷ kaṇ vāṉattu eṉṟūḻ nīḷ iṭi - oḷi poruntiya vāṉiṉ kaṇṇākiya ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmi mikka nīṇṭa curattilē, āḷ kol yāṉi - neṟic celvōrkaḷik koṉṟiṭum yāṉi, atar pārttu alkum - vaḻiyiṉip pārttut taṅkiyirukkum, cōli attam māli pōki - cōlikaḷi yuṭiya kāṭṭu neṟiyil mālikkālattēpōy, oḻiya ceṉṟōr - nām ivaṇ oḻintirukkac ceṉṟār ātaliṉ,
21-22. nōy taru pāl - nōyiṉic  ceyyum pakutiyāl, maṉṟa- orutaliyāka, paḻi evaṉ ām kol - yātu paḻi uṇṭākumō.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! kātalar, kaḷavuṅ kimmika vaḷarntaṉṟu; aṉṉiyum uṭkoṇṭu ōvāḷ kākkum; piṉperitu ivaṇ uṟivu evaṉō; aḷiyaḷ; eṉṟaruḷi, aruñcuram, veyya niṉeyiṟu uṇīiya maḻi orunāḷ tali iyiya; arivi ! niṉṉoṭu celkam; ciṉṉāḷ āṉṟaṉi yākeṉa viṉivali yuṟīi, ellām perumpiṟitāka, eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭi attam mālip pōki, oḻiyac ceṉṟōr; nōytarupāl maṉṟa paḻi evaṉ āṅkol.
(vi-ri) kaṅkuṟ puṇar kuṟi vāytta eṉṟatu, iravuk kuṟikkaṇ puṇartal aminta eṉṟapaṭi. `āṭunaṭi...... atiyaṉ' eṉpatiliruntu, aḷḷaṉ eṉpāṉ taṉakku veṟṟiyi aḷittāṉ eṉṟa makiḻcciyāl, atiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ avaṉukkup paricilāka nāṭu nalkiṉaṉ pōlum eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. atiyaṉ tuñciya piṉpu eṉpatu kiṇi kaṇṇavintāṅku eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām. kaṇ - kiṇiyiṉ aṭikkappaṭum iṭam. kaṇ avital - aṭipaṭā toḻital. kiṇikaṇṇavintāṅku malikaviṉaḻinta eṉka. eyiṟu- vāykku ākupeyar. perumpiṟitāka - aḻintoḻiya. vāṭkaṇ vāṉattu, oḷi poruntiya vāṉiṉkaṇ eṉka. vāṉkaṇ - ñāyiṟu; `vāṉkaṇ viḻiyā vikaṟi yāmattu'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. namakku nōyic ceyyum pakutiyāl avarukku yātu paḻiyuṇṭākumō eṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. namakku varum paḻi eṉṉi eṉṟāḷum ām.
326. marutam
(tōḻi talimakaṉi vāyil maṟuttatu.)
		2ūral avvāy urutta tittip
		pēramar maḻikkaṭ peruntōṭ ciṟunutal
		nallaḷ amma kuṟumakaḷ celvar
		kaṭuntēr kuḻitta ñeḷḷal āṅkaṇ
	5.	neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum aṭṭa vāyil
		iruṅkatirk kaḻaṉip peruṅkaviṉ aṉṉa
		nalampā rāṭṭi naṭiyeḻiṟ polintu
		viḻaviṟ celīiyar vēṇṭum veṉvēl
		iḻiyaṇi yāṉic cōḻaṉ maṟavaṉ
	10.	kaḻiyaḷan taṟiyāk kāvirip paṭappip
		3puṉaṉmali putaviṟ pōōr kiḻavōṉ
		paḻiyaṉ ōkkiya vēlpōṟ
		piḻiyala kaṇṇavaḷ nōkkiyōr tiṟattē.		   - paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. ūral a vāy urutta titti - ūralākiya aḻaku vāynta uruppeṟṟa tēmaliyum, pēr amar maḻi kaṇ - periya amar ceyyum kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum, peru tōḷ ciṟu nutal - periya tōḷiṉiyum ciṟiya neṟṟiyiṉiyum uṭiya, kuṟumakaḷ - niṉ iḷiya paratti, nallaḷ amma - aḻakiṟ ciṟantāḷāvaḷ (ākaliṉ, taliva!)
8-13. veṉ vēl iḻi aṇi yāṉi cōḻaṉ maṟavaṉ - veṟṟi poruntiya vēliyum aṇikalaṉ aṇinta yāṉiyiyu muṭiya cōḻaṉatu paṭittalivaṉum, kaḻi aḷantu aṟiyā kāviri paṭappi - ōṭak kōlāl āḻam aḷantaṟiyap paṭāta kāviriyiṉ kariyiṉic cārnta tōṭṭaṅkaḷiyum, puṉalmali putaviṉ - nīr niṟintu ōṭum matakukaḷiyum uṭiya, pōōr kiḻavōṉ - pōr eṉṉum ūrukkut talivaṉumākiya, paḻiyaṉ - paḻiyaṉ eṉpāṉ, ōkkiya vēl pōl - pakivarmītu celuttiya vēl pōla, avaḷ nōkkiyōr tiṟattu - avaḷāṟ pārkkap peṟṟār pakkal, kaṇ piḻiyala - avaḷ kaṇkaḷ tittal tappā;
3-8. celvar kaṭuntēr kuḻitta ñeḷḷal āṅkaṇ - celvar kaḷatu virintu cellum tēr kuḻitta terukkaḷiṭattē, neṭu koṭi nuṭaṅkum aṭṭavāyil - nīṇṭa koṭikaḷ aciyum aṭṭa vāyil eṉṉum ūriṭattēyuḷḷa, iru katir kaḻaṉi perukaviṉ aṉṉa - nīṇṭa katirkaḷiyuṭiya vayalkaḷiṉ periya aḻakiṉi yotta, nalam pārāṭṭi - avaḷatu aḻakiṉip pārāṭṭi, naṭi eḻil polintu - aḻakiya naṭiyāl viḷakka muṟṟu, viḻavil celīiyar vēṇṭum - avaḷuṭaṉ puṉal viḻāviṉkaṇ cellutal niṉakkut takuvatākum.
(muṭipu) taliva! kuṟumakaḷ nallaḷ amma; pōōr kiḻavōṉ paḻiyaṉ ōkkiya vēlpōla, avaḷ nōkkiyōr tiṟattuk kaṇ piḻiyala; avaḷ nalam pārāṭṭi, naṭieḻiṟ polintu, viḻaviṟ celīiyar vēṇṭum.
(vi-ri) ūral - tēmal vaki. ūralākiya titti eṉka. amma- aci. kuṟumakaḷ nallaḷ eṉṟamiyāl, yām mutumiyum aḻakiṉmiyum uṭiyēm eṉap pulantu kūṟiyavāṟu. nuṭaṅkum maṭṭavāyil eṉap pirittaṟkum iṭam uṇṭu. naṭi eḻiṟ polintu eṉṟatu, perumitattuṭaṉ iruvarum oruṅku naṭakkum naṭiyil viḷakkamuṟṟu eṉṟapaṭi. celīiyar - toḻiṟ peyar. paḻiyaṉ pakivariṭattu ōkkiya vēl piḻiyāmi `pōōr kiḻavōṉ, paḻiyaṉ vēlvāyt taṉṉaniṉ, piḻiyā naṉmoḻi'1 eṉap piṟāṇṭum varutal kāṇka. piḻiyala kaṇ eṉṟatu, nī avaḷi viṭṭu nīṅkutal aritu eṉṟapaṭi.
327. pāli
(poruḷ kaṭik kūṭṭiya 1neñciṉik kaḻaṟiyatu.)
		iṉpamum iṭumpiyum puṇarvum pirivum
		naṉpakal amiyamum iravum pōla
		vēṟuvē ṟiyala āki māṟetirntu
		uḷaveṉa uṇarntaṉi yāyiṉ orūum
	5.	iṉṉā veñcuram naṉṉaci turappat
		tuṉṉalun takumō tuṇivil neñcē
		nīcela valittaṉi yāyiṉ yāvatum
		2niṉitaluñ ceytiyō emmē kaṉikatir
		āvi avvari nīreṉa  3nacii
	10.	mātavap parikkum maraltiraṅku naṉantalik
		kaḷarkāl yātta kaṇṇakaṉ parappiṟ
		cevvarik koḻinīr kaṭuppa araviṉ
		avvari yurivi aṇavaru maruṅkiṟ
		puṟṟari yātta pularciṉi maratta
	15.	minniṟa uruviṉ maṇikkaṭ kākki
		pinniṇaṅ kavarum paṭupiṇak kavalic
		ceṉṟōr celpuṟat tiraṅkār koṉṟōr
		kōlkaḻipu iraṅkum atara
		vēypayil aḻuvam iṟanta piṉṉē.
- maruṅkūrppākic cāttaṉ pūtaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 6. tuṇivu il neñcē - uṟuti yaṟṟa neñcamē!
1-6. iṉpamum iṭumpiyum - iṉpamum tuṉpamum, puṇarvum pirivum - puṇartalum piritalum, naṉ pakal amiyamum iravum pōla - nalla pakaṟ poḻutum irāppoḻutum pōla, vēṟu vēṟu iyala āki - vēṟu vēṟu taṉmiyiṉavāki, māṟu etirntu uḷa eṉa - māṟāka etirppaṭṭu niṟpaṉa eṉa, uṇarntaṉi āyiṉ - nī uṇarntuḷā yāyiṉ, orūum iṉṉā vem curam - ivaḷi nīṅkic cellum iṉṉāmiyi yuṭiya koṭiya curaneṟiyil, nal naci turappa - nalla poruḷ vēṭki celutta, tuṉṉalum takumō - nī ceṉṟu cērtalum takuvatāmā?
7. nī cela valittaṉi yāyiṉ - nī cellutaṟkē tuṇintu viṭṭāyāyiṉ,
8-19. kaṉi katir āvi av vari - ñāyiṟṟiṉatu mikka katirāl tōṉṟum āviyāya avaṟṟiṉ alikaḷi, nīr eṉa nacii - nīr eṉṟu eṇṇi virumpi, mā tava parikkum - vilaṅkukaḷ mika ōṭi aliyum, maral tiraṅku naṉantali - maraṟceṭi vāṭum akaṉṟa iṭattiṉiyum, kaḷar kāl yātta kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ - kaḷar paranta iṭamakaṉṟa parappiṉiyum, cevvarik koḻinīr kaṭuppa - ceṅkuttāya maliyiṉiṉṟum koḻittu varum aruvi nīr pōla, araviṉ a vari urivi aṇavarum maruṅkiṉ - pāmpiṉatu aḻakiya varikaḷiyuṭiya urikkap peṟṟa tōlkaḷ poruntum pāṟi iṭaṅkaḷiyum uṭiya, puṟṟu ari yātta - puṟṟukkaḷ aṭimarattil kaṭṭap peṟṟuḷa, pular ciṉi maratta - kāynta kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya marattiṭattavāya, mi niṟa uruviṉ maṇik kaṇ kākki - peyyum mēkattiṉ niṟam pōlum uruviṉiyum nīla maṇi pōlum kaṇṇiṉiyumuṭiya kākkikaḷ, pi niṇam kavarum paṭu piṇa kavali - paciya koḻuppiṉik kavarntuṇṇum iṭamāya mikka piṇaṅkaḷ kiṭakkum kavartta neṟikaḷākiya, ceṉṟōr cel puṟattu iraṅkār koṉṟōr - vaḻic cellum vaṇikarkaḷ pōkum iṭaṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉē yiruntu irakkamuṟātu avarkaḷik koṉṟa āṟalippōr, kōl kaḻipu iraṅkum atara - taṅkaḷ ampukaḷ kaḻintataṉ poruṭṭē iraṅkum neṟikaḷiyuṭiya, vēy payil aḻuvam iṟanta piṉ - mūṅkil neruṅkiya kāṭṭiṉik kaṭanta piṉ,
7-8. emmē yāvatum niṉitalum ceytiyō - eṅkaḷic ciṟitēṉum niṉittalum ceyvāyō.
(muṭipu) tuṇivil neñcē! iṉpamum tuṉpamum puṇarvum pirivum vēṟuvē ṟiyalavāki māṟetirntu uḷa eṉa uṇarntaṉiyāyiṉ veñcuram tuṉṉalum takumō; nī celavalittaṉiyāyiṉ vēypayil aḻuvam iṟanta piṉṉē emmi yāvatum niṉitalum ceytiyō.
naṉantaliyiṉiyum parappiṉiyum maruṅkiṉiyum uṭiya aḻuvam eṉavum kavaliyākiya atarkaḷiyuṭiya aḻuvam eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka.
(vi-ri) iṉpamum iṭumpiyum māṟu etirntuḷa, puṇarvum pirivum māṟu etirntuḷa eṉka. itaṉip puṇarvum ataṉālāya iṉpamum, pirivum, ataṉālāya iṭumpiyum eṉa urittalumām. iṉpamum iṭumpiyum pakalum iravum pōla eṉa niraṉiṟiyākak koḷka. taliviyip puṇarntu iṉpam tuykkum nī, piriyiṉ tuṉpamē yuḷa eṉṟataṉi aṟintāyāyiṉ poruḷ naciyāl taliviyip pirintu veñcuram tuṉṉutal takātu eṉṟāṉ. tuṇivu - teḷivumām. nī cela valittaṉiyāyiṉ - tuṉpamātali aṟintuvittum nī cellutalit tuṇintāyāyiṉ eṉka. aḻuvam iṟantapiṉ emmi niṉitalum ceytiyō eṉṟatu, yām niṉṉuṭaṉ varuvēm allēm eṉakkūṟic celavaḻuṅkuvitta paṭiyām. kālyāttal - parattal; oru col. pāmpiṉ uri aruvi nīr pōlvatātal, `pāmpiṉ, tūṅkutōl kaṭukkun tūveḷ ḷaruvi'1 eṉpataṉālum peṟappaṭum.
328. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi colleṭuppat 2talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		vaḻiyamal aṭukkattu valaṉērpu vayiriyar
		muḻavatirn taṉṉa muḻakkattu ēṟōṭu
		uravup peyal poḻinta naḷḷeṉ yāmattu
		araviṉ pintali iṭaṟip pāṉāḷ
	5.	iraviṉ vantem 3iṭimuli poruntit
		tuṉikaṇ akala vaḷiik kaṅkuliṉ
		iṉitiṉ iyinta naṇpavar 4muṉital
		teṟṟā kutaṉaṟ kaṟintaṉa māyiṉ
		ilaṅkuvaḷi nekiḻap parantupaṭar alippayām
	10.	muyaṅkutoṟum muyaṅkutoṟum uyaṅka mukantukoṇṭu
		aṭakkuvam maṉṉō tōḻi maṭappiṭi
		maḻitavaḻ cilampiṟ kaṭuñcūl īṉṟu
		kaḻitiṉ yākki viḻikaḷiṟu tivara
		vāḻiyañ cilampiṟ ṟuñcuñ
	15.	cāral nāṭaṉ cāyaṉ mārpē.
- maturip paṇṭavāṇikaṉ 5iḷantēvaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi-,
1-9. vaḻi amal aṭukkattu - curapuṉṉi maraṅkaḷ niṟinta maliccāralil, valaṉ ērpu - (mēkamāṉatu) valamāka eḻuntu, vayiriyar muḻavu atirntaṉṉa muḻakkattu ēṟoṭu - kūttaratu muḻavam atirntāṟ pōṉṟa muḻakkattiṉi yuṭiya iṭiyēṟukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi, uravu peyal poḻinta naḷ eṉ yāmattu - mikka peyalic corinta naḷḷeṉṉum oliyiṉiyuṭiya naṭu iravil, araviṉ pi tali iṭari - pāmpiṉatu paciya taliyi iṭaṟik koṇṭu, pāl nāḷ iraviṉ vantu - antap pāti iravil vantu, em iṭimuli porunti - em muliyiṭiyē porunti, tuṉi kaṇ akala - tamatu varuttam tammiṭattu niṉṟum nīṅka, vaḷii - ākattit taḻuvi, kaṅkuliṉ - iravelām, iṉitiṉ iyinta naṇpu - iṅṅaṉam iṉimiyākap poruntiya naṇpiṉi, ilaṅkuvaḷi nekiḻa - viḷaṅkum vaḷi nekiḻavum, paṭar parantu alippa - namatu tuṉpam paravi varuttavum, avar muṉital teṟṟu ākutal naṟku aṟintaṉam āyiṉ - avar piṉ veṟuttal teḷivātali nām naṉku uṇarvōmāyiṉ,
11-15. maḻi tavaḻ cilampil - mēkam tavaḻum pakkamaliyil, maṭa piṭi - iḷiya peṇ yāṉi, kaṭuñ cūl īṉṟu - taṉatu mutaṟ cūliṉi īṉṟu, kaḻi tiṉ yākki - mūṅkilit tiṉṉum taṉ yākkiyiṉi, viḻi kaḷiṟu tivara - taṉpāl viruppa mikka taṉatu āṇ yāṉi taṭavik koṇṭirukka, vāḻi am cilampil tuñcum - vāḻikaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya ap pakka maliyil tuyilum, cāral nāṭaṉ - cāraliyuṭiya malināṭāṉāya at talivaṉatu, cāyal mārpu - meṉmiyi yuṭiya mārpiṉi,
9-11. yām - nām, muyaṅkutoṟum muyaṅkutoṟum - avar muyaṅkuntōṟum muyaṅkuntōṟum, uyaṅka mukantukoṇṭu aṭakkuvam - varunta mukantukoṇṭu nam mārpiṉkaṇṇē aṭakkiyiruppēm, maṉ - atu kaḻintatē.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! pāṉāḷ iraviṉ vantu kaṅkuliṉ iyinta naṇpu vaḷi nekiḻap paṭar alippa avar muṉital teṟṟākutal naṟku aṟintaṉamāyiṉ, cāral nāṭaṉ mārpu, muyaṅkutoṟum uyaṅka mukantukoṇṭu aṭakkuvam maṉ.
(vi-ri) mēkam eṉa varuvittu, mēkam ērpu poḻinta yāmam eṉa muṭikka. araviṉ pintali iṭaṟi eṉpatu iruḷiṉ ceṟivi uṇarttiyapaṭi. eṉavē iravuk kuṟiyiṉ ētam añcātu vantu eṉpatāyiṟṟu. pintali, pittali eṉpataṉ vikāram eṉak koṇṭu paṭattiṉiyuṭiya tali eṉṟalumām. akala aḷii eṉap piritturittalumām. naṇpiṉi muṉitalāvatu nām varunta nammip pirintiruttal. uyaṅka mukantu koṇṭu - varuntumpaṭi iṟukap paṟṟikkoṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu. muyaṅkutoṟum muyaṅka eṉap pirittu mēlum iṭiviṭātu muyaṅka eṉṟurittalumām. tōḻi colleṭuppat talimakaḷ colliyatu eṉṉum tuṟik kēṟpa, naṟku aṟintaṉamāyiṉ eṉṟu toṭaṅkit tōḻi kūṟi muṭikkumuṉ, nāṭaṉ mārpiṉi avaṉ muyaṅkutōṟum mukantu koṇṭu aṭakkuvam eṉat talivi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉṟu koḷka.
(u-ṟi) maṭappiṭi maḻi tavaḻ cilampil cūḻ īṉṟu yākki kaḷiṟu tivara cilampil tuñcum eṉṟatu, talivi, talivaṉuṭiya celvamikka maṉiyil makappeṟṟu, talivaṉ aṉpu pārāṭṭa illaṟam naṭattutali virumpuvaḷ eṉṟapaṭi.
(mē-ḷ) `orutali yurimi vēṇṭiyum’1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `vaḻiyamal aṭukkattu' eṉpataṉuḷ, mukantukoṇṭaṭakkuvam eṉa iṭiviṭātu iṉpam nukara virumpiyavāṟum, uḷḷuṟiyāṉ, illaṟam nikaḻtta virumpiyavāṟum kāṇka eṉṟār nac.
329. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu. 
pirivuṇarttiya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatūumām.)
		pūṅkaṇum nutalum pacappa nōy kūrntu
		īṅkiyāṉ varuntavum nīṅkutal tuṇintu
		vāḻtal valluna rāyiṟ 2kātalar
		kuvinta kurampi aṅkuṭic cīṟūrp
	5.	paṭumaṇi yiyampap pakaliyintu umaṇar
		koṭunukam piṇitta ceṅkayiṟ ṟoḻukip
		pakaṭayāk koḷḷum vemmuṉit tukaṭokuttu
		eṟivaḷi cuḻaṟṟum attañ ciṟitacintu
		ēkuvar kollō tāmē pāykoḷpu
	10.	3uṟuverin oṭikkuñ ciṟuvarik kuruḷi
		neṭunal yāṉi nīrnacik kiṭṭa
		kikaṟit turaṟum mitūṅku iṟumpil
		pulipukku īṉum vaṟuñcuṉip
		paṉipaṭu cimiyap paṉmali yiṟantē.
- uṟiyūr 4mutukūttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) tōḻi-,
1-3. kātalar - nam kātalar, pū kaṇum nutalum pacappa - eṉatu aḻakiya kaṇṇum neṟṟiyum pacaliyuṟa, nōy kūrntu īṅku yāṉ varuntavum - tuṉpam mikku iṅkē yāṉ varuntāniṟkavum, nīṅkutal tuṇintu - nammip piritalit tuṇintu, vāḻtal vallunar 
āyiṉ - taṉittu vāḻa vaṉmi yuṭiya rāyiṉ,
4-8. kuvinta kurampi am kuṭic cīṟūr - tali kūmpiya kuṭicikaḷākiya aḻakiya kuṭiyiruppukkaḷi yuṭiya ciṟṟūriṉkaṇ, paṭu maṇi iyampa - poruntiya maṇi olikka, pakal iyintu - pakuttal porunti, umaṇar koṭu nukam piṇitta ceṅ kayiṟṟu oḻukip pakaṭu - uppu vāṇikar vaḷinta nukattilē cevviya kayiṟṟiṉāl piṇikkappeṟṟa vaṇṭi yiḻukkum pakaṭukaḷ, ayāk koḷḷum - varunti yiḻukkum, vemmuṉi - koṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil eḻum, tukaḷ - tokuttu - puḻutiyit tiraṭṭi, eṟi vaḷi cuḻaṟṟum attam - vīcum kāṟṟuc cuḻaṟṟum cura neṟiyil, ciṟitu acintu - ciṟitu taṅki yiruntu (piṉpu),
9-14. pāy koḷpu uṟu verin oṭikkum ciṟu varik kuruḷi - pāytal koṇṭu nērmi poruntiya mutukiṉi neḷikkum kuṟukiya varikaḷiyuṭiya pulik kuṭṭi, neṭu nal yāṉi nīr  nacikku iṭṭa ki kaṟittu uraṟum - nīṇṭuyarnta nalla yāṉi nīr vēṭkiyāl cuṉiyiṉpāl iṭṭa kiyiṉik kaṭittu muḻaṅkum iṭamāya, mi tūṅku iṟumpil - mēkam tuñcum kāṭṭil, pulipukku īṉum vaṟu cuṉi - puli (nīriṉmiyiṉ) pukuntu kuṭṭiyi īṉum vaṟiya cuṉiyiṉiyuṭiya, paṉi paṭu cimiyap pal mali iṟantu - paṉi uṟinta ucciyiṉiyuṭiya pala malikaḷiyum kaṭantu, tām ēkuvar kollō - avar pōvarō.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kātalar īṅku yāṉ varuntavum nīṅkutal tuṇintu vāḻtal vallunarāyiṉ vemmuṉit tukaḷ tokuttu eṟivaḷi cuḻaṟṟum attam ciṟitu acintu paṉmali yiṟantu ēkuvar kollō.
(vi-ri) pūṅkaṇ - pūp pōlum kaṇṇum ām. pakal iyintu piṇitta pakaṭu eṉavum, maṇi iyampap pakaṭu ayāk koḷḷum eṉavum kūṭṭuka. pakal iyitalāvatu nukattiṉ iru puṟattum tammiṉ ottaṉavāya pakaṭukaḷit tērntu koḷḷal. pakal- pakaṟ poḻu teṉak koṇṭu pakalilē porunti ayāk koḷḷum  eṉṟalumām. eṟi vaḷi - cūṟāvaḷi. ciṟitu acintu - ak kāṟṟu niṟkumaḷavum taṅki yiruntu. pāy koḷpu - uṭali neṭitākkal. nacikku - naciyāl; vēṟṟumi mayakkam.
330. neytal
(talimakaṟkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kuṟinayappak kūṟiyatu.)
		kaḻippūk kuṟṟuṅ kāṉal alkiyum
		vaṇṭaṟ pāvi varimaṇal ayarntum
		iṉpuṟap puṇarntum iḷivarap paṇintum
		taṉṟuyar 1veḷippaṭat tavaṟil nantuyar
	5.	aṟiyā miyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu
		cellum aṉṉō mellam pulampaṉ
		celvōṉ 1peyarpuṟat tiraṅki muṉṉiṉṟu
		takiiya ceṉṟaveṉ niṟiyil neñcam
		eytiṉṟu kollō tāṉē eytiyum
	10.	kāmañ ceppa nāṇiṉṟu kollō
		utuva kāṇavar ūrnta tērē
		kuppi veṇmaṇaṟ kuvavumici yāṉum
		ekkart tāḻi maṭalvayi ṉāṉum
		āykoṭip pācaṭumpu ariya ūrpiḻipu
	15.	ciṟukuṭip paratavar peruṅkaṭaṉ maṭutta
		kaṭuñcelaṟ koṭuntimil pōla
		nivantupaṭu tōṟṟamo ṭikantumā yummē.	       - ulōccaṉār.
(co-ḷ) tōḻi! 6. mel am pulampaṉ - meṉṉilamākiya neytaliyuṭiya nam talivaṉ,
1-6. kaḻi pū kuṟṟum - kaḻiyiluḷḷa pūkkaḷip paṟittum, kāṉal alkiyum - kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil taṅkiyiruntum, vaṇṭal pāvi varimaṇal ayarntum - varippaṭṭa maṇalil vaṇṭaṟ pāvi ceytu viḷiyāṭiyum, iṉpu uṟa puṇarntum - iṉpam uṟap pala kāl vantu kūṭiyum, iḷivara paṇintum - ciṟumi tōṉṟap paṇi talic ceytum, tavaṟu il nam tuyar aṟiyāmiyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu - kuṟṟamillāta namatu tuyari aṟiyāmiyāl cōrnta neñcuṭaṉ, taṉ tuyar veḷippaṭac cellum - taṉatu tuyaram veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟac cellāniṟpaṉ, aṉṉō - antō!
7-10. celvōṉ peyarpuṟattu - aṅṅaṉam celkiṉṟavaṉ pōkiya ticiyil, iraṅki - irakkamuṟṟu, muṉniṉṟu takiiya - muṉṉē niṉṟu cellaṟka eṉat taṭiceya, ceṉṟa eṉ niṟi il neñcam - ceṉṟatāya eṉatu uṟutiyaṟṟa neñcamāṉatu, eytiṉṟu kollō - at talivaṉic ceṉṟu cārntatiṉṟō, eytiyum - cārntum, kāmam ceppa nāṇiṉṟu kollō - nam kāmattiṉik kūṟa nāṇiyatō;
11-17. avar ūrnta tēr utuva kāṇ - avar ēṟicceṉṟa tēr uṅkē tōṉṟalik kāṇpāyāka, kuppi veṇ maṇal kuvavu miciyāṉum - veḷḷiya maṇal tiraṇṭa kuviyalkaḷiṉ mēlum, ekkar tāḻi maṭalvayiṉāṉum - maṇal mēṭṭiṭattuḷḷa tāḻi maṭalkaḷiṉiṭattum, āy koṭi pacu aṭumpu ariya ūrpu iḻipu - aḻakiya aṭumpiṉ paciya koṭikaḷ aripaṭa ēṟiyum iṟaṅkiyum, ciṟu kaṭip paratavar peru kaṭal maṭutta - ciṟiya kuṭi vāḻkkiyi yuṭiya neytal mākkaḷ periya kaṭaliṟ celuttiya, kuṭu celal koṭutimil pōla - virintu cellutaliyuṭiya vaḷinta mīṉ paṭakupōla, nivantupaṭu tōṟṟamoṭu - uyarntu tōṉṟum tōṟṟat tuṭaṉ, ikantu māyum - ceṉṟu appāl maṟiyā niṟkiṉṟatu.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! pulampaṉ alkiyum ayarntum puṇarntum paṇintum tavaṟuil namtuyar aṟiyāmiyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu cellum; celvōṉ puṟattu iraṅkit takiiya ceṉṟa eṉ neñcam eytiṉṟu kollō; eytiyum kāmam ceppa nāṇiṉṟu kollō; avar ūrnta tēr paratavar kaṭal maṭutta timilpōla nivantupaṭu tōṟṟamoṭu ikantu māyum; utuva kāṇ.
(vi-ri) taṉ tuyar veḷippaṭac cellum eṉavum, ayarnta, neñcamoṭu cellum eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. maṉ ō eṉap pirittu acinili eṉṟalumām. pulampaṉ - neytaṟ ṟalivaṉ. neytal meṉṉilamākaliṉ neytalnilat talivaṉi mellam pulampaṉ eṉpatu vaḻakkāṟu. eytiṉṟu - etirmaṟic col. `pukkil amintiṉṟu kollō'1 eṉpuḻip pōla talivaṉ ēṟiya tēr aṇmiyil nivanta tōṟṟamuṭiyatāyiruntu cēymiyiṟ celluntōṟum maṟitalukku avvāṟu ceṉṟu maṟiyum timil uvamiyāyiṟṟu. ippāṭṭiṉit talivi kūṟṟākakkoṇṭu, talivi taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyum veḷip paṭak kūṟiyateṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. kaḷavuk kālattil, talivaṉ pāl parattimiyiṉṟākavum kātal mikutiyāl uḷatākak koṇṭu talivi ūṭutal uṇṭu eṉpatum atu pulavip pōliyā meṉpatum avar karuttākum.
(mē-ḷ) `maṟintavaṟ kāṇṭal'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ, `aṟiyāmiyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu' eṉpatu taṉvayiṉ urimi, ikantu māyum eṉpatu avaṉvayiṟ parattimi' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar nac.
331. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		nīṭunili ariya ceṅkuḻi iruppik
		kōṭukaṭin taṉṉa koḷḷi vāṉpū
		āṭuparan taṉṉa īṉal eṇkiṉ
		3cēṭuciṉi yurīi uṇṭa miccil
	5.	piṅkuḻit taḻiyar paḻiyar makaḷir
		kaṇṭiraḷ nīḷamik kaṭippiṟ ṟokuttuk
		kuṉṟakac ciṟukuṭi maṟukutoṟum maṟukumañ
		cīṟūr nāṭu palapiṟak koḻiyac
		ceṉṟōr aṉpilar tōḻi eṉṟum
	10.	aruntuṟi muṟṟiya karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻp
		pāṇar ārppap palakalam utavi
		nāḷavi yirunta naṉimakiḻ titiyaṉ
		vēḷiroṭu `1porīiya kaḻitta
		vāḷvāy aṉṉa 2vaṟuñcuram iṟantē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tōḻi-,
1-8. nīṭu nili ariya cem kuḻi iruppi - nīṇṭa niliyākiya aṭimarattiṉiyuṭiya civanta taḷirkaḷiyuṭiya iruppi maraṅkaḷiṉ, kōṭu kaṭintaṉṉa koḷḷi vāṉ pū - tantattiṉik kaṭintāṟ pōṉṟa mikutiyāṉa veḷḷiya pūkkaḷil, āṭu paranta aṉṉa - āṭukaḷ parantāl otta, īṉal eṇkiṉ cēṭu - īṉṟa peṇ karaṭikaḷiṉ tiraṭci, ciṉi urīi uṇṭa miccil - kiḷikaḷil parantu ceṉṟu uṇṭa miccilāyavaṟṟi, pi kuḻi taḻiyar paḻiyar makaḷir - paciya taḷirkaḷālāya taḻiyuṭiyarākiya eyiṉar makaḷir, kaṇ tiraḷ nīḷ amik kaṭippil tokuttu - kaṇukkaḷ tiraṇṭu nīṇṭa mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyil tiraṭṭi, kuṉṟakam ciṟu kuṭi maṟukutoṟum maṟukum - kuṉṟiṉ kaṇṇavākiya cīṟūriṉ terukkaṭōṟum cuḻaṉṟu viṟṟut tiriyum, am cīṟūr nāṭu pala piṟakku oḻiya - aḻakiya ciṟiya ūrkaḷiyuṭiya pala nāṭukaḷ piṉṉē kaḻiya,
9-14. eṉṟum - eññāṉṟum, aru tuṟi muṟṟiya karu kōṭṭu ciṟu yāḻp pāṇar ārppa - ariya icit tuṟikaḷi muṟṟa uṇarnta kariya taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya ciṟiya yāḻiṉiyuṭiya pāṇarkaḷ āravāram ceyya, pala kalam utavi - pala aṇikalaṉ kaḷi aḷittu, nāḷavi irunta naṉi makiḻ titiyaṉ - nāḷōlakkam koṇṭirunta kaḷḷiṉ makiḻviṉi yuṭiya titiyaṉ eṉpāṉ, vēḷiroṭu porīiya kaḻitta - veḷiroṭu pōr ceyaṟku uruviya, vāḷ vāy aṉṉa vaṟum curam iṟantu - vāḷiṉ vāyiṉi yotta koṭumiyi yuṭiya vaṟaṇṭa curattiṉik kaṭantu,
9. ceṉṟōr aṉpu ilar - ceṉṟavarāya nam talivar nam pāl aṉpilātār āvar (eṉ ceyvām.)
(muṭipu) tōḻi! cīṟūr nāṭu pala piṟakku oḻiya, vaṟuñcuram iṟantu  ceṉṟōr aṉpilar (eṉ ceyvām.)
(vi-ri) iruppi vāṉ pū eṉka. āṭu parantaṉṉa eṉṟatu eṇkiṉ mikutiyi uṇarttiyapaṭi. cēṭu - tiraṭci, kūṭṭam eṉṟapaṭi. urīi - uruvi eṉṟumām. uṇṭa miccilākiya pūkkaḷi eṉka. paḻiyar - kaḷ viṟpōr, eyiṉar. kaṭippu - mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyi uṇarttiṟṟu. maṟukum - tiriyum; viṟṟuttiriyum eṉṟapaṭi. aṉpilārāyc ceṉṟār eṉṟumām. aruntuṟi - paṇṇum tiṟamum ākiya iciyiṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷ. ārppa - makiḻcciyāl āravārikka. vāḷ vāy aṉṉa koṭumi yuṭiya eṉa viritturikka.
332. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻikkut 1talimakaḷ iyaṟpaṭa moḻintatu.)
		2muḷivaḷar mutala mūṅkil murukkik
		kiḷiyoṭu mēynta kēḻkiḷar yāṉi
		nīrnaci maruṅkiṉ 3niṟampārt toṭuṅkiya
		porumuraṇ uḻuvi toliccik kūrnuṉik
	5.	kurutic 4ceṅkōṭ ṭaḻituḷi kaḻāak
		5kaṉmuki aṭukkattu meṉmela iyalic
		ceṟupaki 6vāṭṭiya cemmalo ṭaṟukāl
		yāḻicip paṟavi imirap piṭipuṇarntu
		vāḻiyañ cilampiṟ ṟuñcum nāṭaṉ
	10.	niṉpurit takka cāyalaṉ eṉanī
		aṉpurit taṭaṅkak kūṟiya iṉcol
		vāyttaṉa vāḻi tōḻi vēṭṭōrkku
		amiḻtat taṉṉa kamaḻtār mārpiṉ
		vaṇṭiṭip paṭāa muyakkamum
	15.	taṇṭāk kātalum tali7nāḷ pōṉmē.
- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 12. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-9. muḷi vaḷar mutala mūṅkil murukki - muḷikaḷ vaḷarum tūṟukaḷiluḷḷa mūṅkilkaḷi oṭittu, kiḷiyoṭu mēynta kēḻ kiḷar yāṉi - taṉ iṉattuṭaṉ tiṉṟa niṟam viḷaṅkiya kaḷiṟu, nīr naci maruṅkiṉ niṟam pārttu oṭuṅkiya - nīrvēṭṭuc cellum tuṟiyarukē taṉ uruviṉip pārttut tākkaṟkup patuṅkiya, poru muraṇ uḻuvi tolicci - pōr ceyum māṟu pāṭṭiṉik koṇṭa puliyip porutu koṉṟu, kūr nuṉi kurutic cem kōṭṭu aḻi tuḷi kaḻāa - kūriya muṉiyil kuruti tōynta civanta kompiṉi mikka maḻiyāl kaḻuvikkoṇṭu. kalmuki aṭukkattu melmela iyali - kalmuṉikaḷi yuṭiya pakka maliyil mella mellac ceṉṟu, ceṟu paki vāṭṭiya cemmaloṭu - ceṟṟaṅ koṇṭa taṉ pakiyit tolitta cerukkuṭaṉ, aṟu kāl yāḻ ici paṟavi imira - yāḻ pōṉṟa iciyiṉiyuṭiya āṟu kālkaḷiyuṭiya vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum paṭi ceṉṟu, piṭi puṇarntu - taṉ piṭiyiṉik kūṭi, vāḻi am cilampil tuñcum nāṭaṉ - vāḻi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya maliyil tuyilum nāṭṭiyuṭiya talivaṉ;
10-15. niṉ puri takka cāyalaṉ eṉa - niṉatu uyarvukku ēṟṟa meṉmit taṉmiyaṉ eṉa, nī-, aṉpu urittu aṭaṅkak kūṟiya iṉcol - aṉpāl urittu eṉ maṉam amitiyuṟak kūṟiya iṉcoṟka ḷellām, vāyttaṉa - vāymiyāy muṭintaṉa, vēṭṭōrkku - taṉṉi virumpiyavarkku, amiḻtattu aṉṉa kamaḻ tār mārpiṉ - amiḻtam pōṉṟa iṉiya naṟiya tāriṉiyuṭiya taṉ mārpiṉkaṇṇē, vaṇṭu iṭippaṭāa muyakkamum - vaṇṭu iṭiyiṟ poruntalākāta ceṟinta muyakkamum, taṇṭāk kātalum - nīṅkāta kātalum, tali nāḷ pōṉm - mutaliṟ kaṇṇuṟṟa ñāṉṟē pōlac ciṟavāniṉṟaṉa.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! nāṭaṉ niṉ purit takka cāyalaṉ eṉa nī aṉpurittu aṭaṅkak kūṟiya iṉcol vāyttaṉa; avaṉ mārpiṉ muyakkamum, kātalum talināḷ pōṉm.
yāṉi uḻuvi toliccic ceṅkōṭu kaḻāa iyalip piṭipuṇarntu cilampil tuñcum nāṭaṉ eṉka.
(vi-ri) niṟam - vaṭivu; mārpumām. cemmaloṭu piṭi puṇarntu eṉka. puri - uyarcci; purittakka - uyarccikkut takka. vāyttaṉa - vāy āyiṉa. amiḻtat taṉṉa mārpu eṉka. kamaḻ tār mārpākaliṉ vaṇṭu uḷatātal peṟappaṭum. iṭip paṭā muyakkam eṉṟatu av vaṇṭu nīṅkumāṟu ceṟinta muyakkam eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-ṟi) yāṉi uḻuvi toliccik kurutic ceṅkōṭu aḻi tuḷi kaḻāac ceṟu paki vāṭṭiya cemmaloṭu yāḻicip paṟavi imirap piṭi puṇarntu vāḻiyañ cilampil tuñcum eṉṟatu, talivaṉ alar kūṟuvār vāyi aṭakki, kaḷaviṉālākum mācu tīrat, taliviyi maṇantu cuṟṟattārum pāṇar mutalāyiṉārum koṇṭāṭa vaḷam mikka maṉiyil iṉpam tuyttiruppaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
333. pāli
(piriviṭi vaṟpuṟuttun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		yāa oṇṭaḷir arakkuvitirt taṉṉaniṉ
		āka mēṉi ampacap pūra
		aḻivuperi tuṭiyi yāki avarvayiṉ
		paḻitalit tarutal vēṇṭuti moḻikoṇṭu
	5.	tāṅkal ollumō maṟṟē āṅkuniṉ
		evvam perumi urippiṉ ceyporuḷ
		vayaṅkā tāyiṉum payaṅkeṭat tūkki
		nīṭalar vāḻi tōḻi kōṭiyiṟ
		kuruttiṟupu ukka varuttam colātu
	10.	tūmpuṭit tuyttalik kūmpupu tiraṅkiya
		vēṉil veḷiṟṟuppaṉi pōlak kiyeṭuttu
		yāṉip peruniri vāṉam payirum
		maliccēṇ ikantaṉar āyiṉum nilipeyarntu
		nāḷiṭip paṭātu varuvar namareṉap
	15.	payantaru koḷkiyiṉ nayantali 1tiriyātu
		niṉvāy iṉmoḻi naṉvā yāka
		varuvar āyiṉō naṉṟē vārātu
		avaṇar kātalar āyiṉum ivaṇam
		pacali māytal eḷitumaṟ ṟilla
	20.	ceṉṟa tēettuc ceyviṉi muṟṟi
		maṟutaral uḷḷattar eṉiṉum
		kuṟukuperu naciyoṭu tūtuvarap peṟiṉē.
- kallāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 8. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-8. yāa oḷ taḷir arakku vitirttaṉṉa - yāmarattiṉ oḷḷiya taḷiril arakkup poṭiyiṉic citaṟiṉāṟpōṉṟa, niṉ ākam mēṉi am pacappu ūra - niṉ uṭampiṉatu mēṉiyiṉkaṇ aḻakiya pacali parakka, aḻivu peritu uṭiyi āki - varuttam mikutiyum uṭiyi yāki, avarvayiṉ paḻi talit tarutal vēṇṭuti - avariṭattē paḻiyic cērttali virumpukiṉṟaṉi, āṅku niṉ evvam perumi urippiṉ - aṅkē niṉatu tuṉpa mikutiyi yārēṉum ceṉṟu urippiṉ, moḻi koṇṭu tāṅkal ollumō - am moḻikaḷik kēṭṭu avarāl tuṉpam tāṅki yiruttal iyalumō, cey poruḷ vayaṅkātāyiṉum - ceyyappaṭum poruḷ muṟṟātāyiṉum, payam keṭa tūkki - iṉpap payaṉ keṭumāṟu ataṉiyē peritāka āyntu, nīṭalar - aṅkē tāḻttu viṭār;
8-14. vēṉil - vēṉiṟ kālattil, kōṭiyil kuruttu iṟupu ukka - mēlkāṟṟāl kuruttu iṟṟu utirnta, varuttam colātu - varuttam nīṅkātu, tūmpu uṭi tuy tali kūmpupu tiraṅkiya - tuḷiyuṭiya tuyyi yuṭiya ucci kūmpi vaṟṟiya, veḷiṟṟup paṉi pōl - iḷam paṉi pōla, ki eṭuttu yāṉi peru niri vāṉam payirum - kiyi uyartti yāṉiyiṉ peruṅ kūṭṭam mēkatti nōkkik kataṟum, mali cēṇ ikantaṉar āyiṉum - maliyik kaṭantu cēymikkaṇ ceṉṟaṉarāyiṉum, nili peyarntu nāḷ iṭip paṭātu namar varuvar - tām kūṟiya kāla elli peyarntiṭa nāṭkaḷ iṭippaṭāmal nam talivar varuvar;
14-17. eṉa - eṉṟiṅṅaṉam, payam taru koḷkiyiṉ nayam tali tiriyātu - payaṉi uṇṭākkum koḷkiyōṭu iṉimi nīṅkātu kūṟum, niṉ vāy iṉ moḻi naṉ vāyāka - niṉ vāyiṉ iṉiya moḻikaḷ, nalla uṇmiyāka, varuvar āyiṉ naṉṟē - vantiṭuvarāyiṉ atu mikavum nallatē (aṉṟi),
17-22. vārātu avaṇar kātalar āyiṉum - aṅṅaṉam vārāmal nam kātalar avviṭattil taṅkuvārāyiṉum, ceṉṟa tēettu - tām ceṉṟuḷa tēyattē, ceyviṉi muṟṟi - tām ceyyum toḻili muṭittukkoṇṭu, maṟutaral uḷḷattar eṉiṉum - mīḷum eṇṇamuṭiya rāyiṉum, kuṟuku peru naci
yoṭu - aṅṅaṉam mīṇṭu varum peru viruppuṭaṉ viṭukkum, tūtu varappeṟiṉ - tūtu varap peṟuvēmāyiṉ, ivaṇ nam pacali māytal eḷitu - ivviṭattu namatu pacali maṟital eḷitākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! niṉmēṉi pacappūra, aḻivuperi tuṭiyiyāki, avarvayiṉ paḻitalittarutal vēṇṭuti; niṉ evvam perumi urippiṉ moḻikoṇṭu tāṅkal ollumō; ceyporuḷ vayaṅkātāyiṉum nīṭalar; maliccēṇ ikantaṉar āyiṉum nāḷiṭippaṭātu namar varuvar eṉak kūṟum niṉ vāy iṉmoḻi naṉvāyāka varuvar āyiṉ naṉṟē; kātalar vārātu avaṇar āyiṉum, ceyviṉi muṟṟi maṟutaral uḷḷattar eṉiṉum tūtu varap peṟiṉē ivaṇ nam pacalimāytal eḷitu.
(vi-ri) taḷir arakku vitirttaṉṉa eṉpatu uru uvamam. ākamēṉi; irupeyaroṭṭu. talittarutal, oru col. maṟṟu, viṉimāṟṟu. evvam - perumi: melittal vikāram. colātu - nīṅkātu. caruccariyuṭiyatāyk karitāy nīṇṭiruttaliṉ yāṉik kikkup paṉi uvamiyākum. īṇṭu yāṉiyiṉ tuḷikki vemmiyāl tiraṅki mēl nivantiruttaliṉ ataṟkuk kuruttu iṟṟu vāṭiya tuḷiyuṭiya veḷiṟṟup paṉi uvamiyāyiṟṟu. nili peyarntu - avviṭattiṉiṉṟum peyarntu eṉṟalumām. maṉ, tilla acikaḷ, maṟutaral - mīṭci.
334. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		ōṭā nallēṟ ṟurivi tiiya
		āṭukoṇ muracam iḻumeṉa muḻaṅka
		nāṭutiṟi koṇṭaṉa māyiṉ pāka
		pāṭimiḻ kaṭaliṉ eḻunta cummiyoṭu
	5.	peruṅkaḷiṟṟut taṭakki puriyak kālvīḻttu
		irumpiṭit toḻutiyiṉ īṇṭuvaṉa kuḻīi
		vaṇaṅkiṟi makaḷir 1ayarntaṉar āṭum
		kaḻaṅkuṟaḻ āliyoṭu kataḻuṟi citaṟip
		peyaṟoṭaṅ kiṉṟāl vāṉam vāṉiṉ
	10.	vayaṅkuciṟi aṉṉattu niripaṟi kaṭuppa
		nālkuṭaṉ pūṇṭa kāṉavil puravik
		koṭiñci neṭuntēr kaṭumpari tavirātu
		iṉamayil akavuṅ kārkoḷ viyaṉpuṉattu
		nōṉcūṭ ṭāḻi īrnilam tumippa
	15.	īṇṭē kāṇak kaṭavumati pūṅkēḻp
		polivaṉa amartta uṇkaṇ
		olipal kūntal āyciṟu nutalē.
- 2maturik kūttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 3-9. pāka - pākaṉē, vāṉam - mēkamāṉatu, pāṭu imiḻ kaṭaliṉ - oli muḻaṅkum kaṭaliṉkaṇ, eḻunta cummiyoṭu - nīri mukanteḻuntatālāya muḻakkiṉoṭu, iru piṭi toḻutiyiṉ īṇṭuvaṉa kuḻīi - kariya piṭikaḷiṉ kūṭṭam pōṉṟu cērntu tiraṇṭu, peru kaḷiṟṟu taṭa ki puriya kāl vīḻttu - periya kaḷiṟṟiṉ parutta ki pōlak kāl iṟakki, vaṇaṅku iṟi makaḷir ayarntaṉar āṭum kaḻaṅku uṟaḻ āliyoṭu - vaḷinta muṉ kiyiṉiyuṭiya makaḷir virumpi viḷiyāṭum kaḻaṅkiṉi yotta paṉik kaṭṭi
yuṭaṉ, kataḻ uṟi citaṟi - viriyum tuḷikaḷic citaṟi, peyal toṭaṅkiṉṟu - peytalit toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu; (mēlum),
1-3. ōṭā nal ēṟṟu urivi tiiya - piṟakkiṭātu vellum nalla vīram mikka ēṟṟiṉatu tōlāṟ pōrtta, āṭu koḷ muracam iḻum eṉa muḻaṅka - veṟṟi koṇṭa muracamāṉatu iḻumeṉa olikka, nāṭu tiṟi koṇṭaṉam āyiṉ - pakivar nāṭṭiṉit tiṟiyākak koṇṭōm ākaliṉ,
15-17. pū kēḻ polivaṉa amartta uṇkaṇ - aḻakiya niṟattiṉāṟ polivaṉavāy māṟupaṭṭa miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum, oli pal kūntal - taḻitta palavāya kūntaliyum, āy ciṟu nutal - aḻakiya ciṟiya neṟṟiyiṉiyumuṭiya nam taliviyi, īṇṭē kāṇa - ippoḻutē kāṇṭaṟku,
9-15. vāṉiṉ vayaṅku ciṟi aṉṉattu niri paṟi kaṭuppa - vāṉattiṉkaṇ viḷaṅkum ciṟakiṉiyuṭiya aṉṉattiṉ kūṭṭam paṟantu cellali yoppa (virintu cellum), nālku uṭaṉ pūṇṭa kāl navil puravi - vēkattāṟ kāṟṟeṉak kūṟappeṟum kutirikaḷ nāṉku orucērap pūṭṭappeṟṟa, koṭiñci neṭu tēr kaṭum pari tavirātu - koṭuñciyiyuṭiya nīṇṭa - tēriṉ mikka vēkam taḷarātu, iṉa mayil akavum kārkoḷ viyaṉ puṉattu - kūṭṭamāya mayilkaḷ āṭum kār kālattiṉ taṉmiyuṭiya periya mulli nilattē, nōṉ cūṭṭu āḻi īr nilam tumippa - valiya vaṭṭakiyiṉiyuṭiya uruḷkaḷ īramāya nilattiṉik kiḻittuc cella, kaṭavumati - celuttuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) pāka! vāṉam peyal toṭaṅkiṉṟu; muracam muḻaṅka nāṭu tiṟi koṇṭaṉam āyiṉ, āy ciṟu nutali īṇṭē kāṇa, puravi nālkuṭaṉ pūṇṭa neṭuntēr kaṭumpari tavirātu viyaṉ puṉattu āḻi nilam tumippak kaṭavumati.
(vi-ri) ōṭā nallēṟṟu urivi - puliyoṭu porutu veṉṟa kollēṟṟiṉ tōl eṉpatum, ataṉāl vīramuracam pōrkkappaṭutalum, `puṉimarup paḻuntak kuttip puliyoṭu porutu veṉṟa, kaṉikura lurumuc cīṟṟak kataḻviṭi yurivi pōrtta, tuṉikural muracam'1 eṉpataṉālum, `kollēṟṟup pacuntōl cīvātu pōrtta mākkaṇ muracam' eṉpataṉālum aṟika. nāṭu tiṟikoṇṭaṉamāyiṉ eṉṟataṉāl talivaṉ aracar marapiṉaṉātal peṟṟām. aṉṉattu niri paṟi kaṭuppa eṉṟami yāl puravikaḷ veṇṇiṟa
muṭiyavātal peṟṟām. nālku - nāṉku; itaṉip peyart tiricol eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. veṇṇiṟamuṭiya kutirikaḷ ciṟantaṉavātalum, avi nāṉku pūṭṭap peṟutalum `niripaṟi aṉṉat taṉṉa viriparip pulluḷik kalimā'2 `pālpuri puravi nālkuṭaṉ pūṭṭi'1 eṉpavaṟṟāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. īṇṭu - poḻutiṉi uṇarttiṟṟu. ciṟunutal - ciṟiya nutaliyuṭiyāṭku āyiṟṟu.


335. pāli
(talimakaṉ poruḷkaṭik kūṭṭiya neñciṉik kaḻaṟic celavaḻuṅkiyatu.)
		iruḷpaṭu neñcat tiṭumpi tīrkkum
		aruṇaṉ kuṭiya rāyiṉu mītal
		poruḷil lōrkkaḵ tiyiyā tākutal
		yāṉu maṟiveṉ maṉṉē yāṉitaṉ
	5.	2koṉmarup poṭiyak kuttic ciṉañciṟantu
		iṉṉā vēṉil iṉṟuṇi yāra
		muḷiciṉi 3yāattup poḷipiḷan tūṭṭap
		pulampuvīṟ ṟirunta nilampaku veñcuram
		ariya 4vallamaṉ ṉamakkē viritār
	10.	āṭukoḷ muraciṉ aṭupōrc ceḻiyaṉ
		māṭa mūtūr matiṟpuṟan taḻīi
		nīṭuveyil uḻanta kuṟiyiṟik kaṇikkāl
		5toṭiyami palmalart tōṭupotin tiyātta
		kuṭiyō raṉṉa kōḷami yeruttiṟ
	15.	pāḷi paṟṟiḻin toḻiyap puṟañcērpu
		vāḷvaṭit taṉṉa vayiṟuṭi potiya
		nāḷuṟat tōṉṟiya nayavaru vaṉappiṉ
		ārat taṉṉa aṇikiḷar putuppū
		vāruṟu kavariyiṉ vaṇṭuṇa viriya
	20.	muttiṉ aṉṉa veḷvī tāay
		alakiṉ aṉṉa ariniṟat tāli
		nakinaṉi vaḷarkkuñ ciṟappiṟ ṟakimikap
		pūvoṭu vaḷarnta mūvāp pacuṅkāy
		nīriṉu miṉiya vākik kūreyiṟṟu
	25.	amiḻtam ūṟuñ cevvāy
		oṇṭoṭik kuṟumakaṭ koṇṭaṉam celiṉē.
- maturit tattaṅ kaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē-,
1-4. iruḷ paṭu neñcattu iṭumpi tīrkkum - vaṟumiyāl mayakkaṅ koṇṭa neñciṉ varuttattip pōkkukiṉṟa, aruḷ naṉku uṭiyar āyiṉum - aruḷiṉi mika uṭiyarāyiṉum, poruḷ illōrkku - kip poruḷ illārkku, ītal aḵtu iyiyātu ākutal - ītalākiya ac ciṟappu iyalātatātali, yāṉum aṟiveṉ - yāṉum aṟivēṉ, maṉ - ataṉāṟ peṟṟateṉ?
9-26. viri tār āṭu koḷ muraciṉ aṭu pōrc ceḻiyaṉ - virinta malart tāriṉiyum veṟṟi poruntiya muraciṉiyum vellum pōriṉiyumuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉatu, māṭam mūtūr matil puṟam taḻīi - māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya mutiya ūrāya maturiyiṉ matiṟ puṟattiṉit taḻuvi, nīṭu veyil uḻanta - nīṇṭa veppattāl varuntiya, kuṟi iṟi kaṇi kāl - kuṟiya iṟikaḷiyuṭiya tiraṇṭa aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kamukamarattiṉ, toṭi ami palmalar tōṭu potintu yātta kuṭi ōraṉṉa - toṭuttal aminta pala malarkaḷiṉ tokutiyi mūṭik kaṭṭiya kuṭiyiṉi yotta, kōḷ ami eruttil - kāyttal poruntiya erutti ṉiṭattuḷḷa, pāḷi paṟṟu iḻintu oḻiya - pāḷi paṟṟu nīṅki oḻiya, puṟam cērpu - puṟatti aṭintu, vāḷ vaṭittaṉṉa vayiṟu uṭip potiya - vāḷi vaṭittuvittāṟ pōṉṟa vayiṟṟiṉi yuṭiya potiyiṉ kaṇṇavāya, nāḷ uṟa tōṉṟiya nayavaru vaṉappiṉ - paruvam uṟṟaviṭattu veḷippaṭṭa iṉimi poruntiya aḻakiṉiyuṭiya, ārattu aṉṉa aṇi kiḷar putuppū - āram pōlum aḻaku viḷaṅkum putiya pūkkaḷ, vār uṟu kavariyiṉ vaṇṭu uṇa viriya - nīṭci poruntiya kavariyiṉ kaṇṇē vaṇṭu tēṉ uṇṇumāṟu  viriya, muttiṉ aṉṉa veḷvī tāay - muttuk kaḷip pōṉṟa veḷḷiya pūkkaḷ parantu, alakiṉ aṉṉa ari niṟattu āli - palakaṟiyi otta oḷḷiya niṟattiyuṭiya ālaṅkaṭṭi pōla, naki naṉi vaḷarkkum ciṟappiṉ - makiḻcciyi mikutiyum uṇṭākkum ciṟappuṭaṉ, takimika - aḻakumika, pūvoṭu vaḷarnta mūvāp pacu kāy - pūvoṭu vaḷartaluṟṟa muṟṟāta iḷaṅkāyiṉatu, nīriṉum iṉiya āki - nīrik kāṭṭiṉum iṉimiyuṭiyavāki, kūr eyiṟṟu amiḻtam ūṟum cevvāy - kūriya paṟkaḷiṭattē amiḻtam ūṟum civanta vāyiṉiyum, oḷ toṭi -oḷi poruntiya vaḷiyiṉiyum uṭiya, kuṟumakaḷ koṇṭaṉam celiṉē - iḷiyaḷāya taliviyi nām uṭaṉ koṇṭu celliṉ,
4-9. yāṉi - kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi, taṉ kol maruppu ōṭiyak  kutti- taṉatu kollum kōṭu oṭintiṭap pāyntu, ciṉam  ciṟantu - ciṉam mikku, iṉṉā vēṉil iṉ tuṇi āra - koṭiya vēṉilil taṉatu iṉiya tuṇiyāya piṭi uṇṇumpaṭi, muḷi  ciṉi yāattu poḷi piḷantu ūṭṭa - paṭṭa kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya yā marattiṉ paṭṭiyik kiḻittu ūṭṭa, pulampu vīṟṟirunta -  varuttam kuṭikoṇṭuḷḷa, nilam paku vem curam - nilam piḷanta vevviya curaṅkaḷum, namakku ariya alla - namakkuc cellaṟku ariyaṉa valla.
(muṭipu) neñcē! ītal poruḷillōrkku iyiyā tākutal yāṉum aṟivēṉ, maṉ; mūvāp pacuṅkāy nīriṉum iṉiyavākik kūr eyiṟṟu amiḻtam ūṟum cevvāy oṇṭoṭik kuṟumakaḷ koṇṭaṉam celiṉē, vemcuram ariya allamaṉ namakkē.
(vi-ri) iruḷ - mayakkam. pulampu vīṟṟirunta eṉpatu vēṟiṭatti lillātavāṟu tuṉpam mikkirunta eṉṟavāṟu. kaṇik kāl - tiraṇṭa kāliyuṭiya kamuka maram; ākupeyar. kamukiṉ kōḷami eruttu eṉavum, kuṭi ōraṉṉa pāḷi eṉavum kūṭṭuka. tōṭu - mikuti; tāḻi maṭalumām. kamukam pāḷi akattē toṭutta māli pōlum pūkkaḷi yuṭimiyiṉ toṭiyami paṉmalart tōṭupotin tiyātta kuṭiyō raṉṉa pāḷi eṉa uvami kūṟappaṭṭatu. pāḷi, paṟṟiḻintoḻiya eṉṟatu kamukam pāḷiyiṉ puṟa maṭal paṟṟu nīṅki oḻiya eṉṟapaṭi. poti - pīḷ, karu; atu puṟamaṭaliṉ uḷḷē vāḷpōlum vaṭiviṉi yuṭiyatā yiruttaliṉ vāḷ vaṭittaṉṉa vayiṟuṭip poti eṉappaṭṭatu. potiya putuppū eṉavum vaṉappiṉ putuppū eṉavum aṇikiḷar putuppū eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. pūviṉ kottu kavari pōlvatākaliṉ kavari eṉappaṭṭatu. putuppū viriya eṉka. tāay - parakka eṉat tirikka. pacuṅkāy - taṭpa mikutiyāl ālipōl makiḻcci viḷikkum eṉka. kamukiṉ muṟṟāta pacuṅkāyiṉ nīr iṉimi mikkuṭiyatu eṉpatu,
		`aṅkaruṅ kāli cīvi yūṟavit tamikkap paṭṭa
		ceṅkaḷi virāya kāyuñ cempaḻuk kāyum tīntēṉ
		eṅkaṇuṅ kuḷirtta iṉṉīr iḷampacuṅ kāyum mūṉṟum
		taṅkaḷi ceyyak kūṭṭit tiyalār kicey tārē.'1
eṉpataṉāl aṟika. ic ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟiya pākkiṉ mūṉṟu niliyum mūvēṟu poḻutiṟku uriyaṉavām eṉpatu,
		`piṅkaruṅ kālic ceṅkaḷi aḷii
		naṉpakaṟ kaminta antuvark kāyum
		iruṅkaṇ mālikkup perumpaḻuk kāyum
		vikaṟik kamiyak kipuṉin tiyaṟṟiya
		iṉṟēṉ aḷiiya iḷampacuṅ kāyum'2
eṉṉum peruṅkatip pakutiyāṟ peṟappaṭum. kuṟumakaḷik koṇṭaṉam celiṉ veñcuram namakku ariya allamaṉ eṉṟamiyāl, poruḷīṭṭutaṟ poruṭṭut taliviyip pirintu celvatu eṉṉāl iyalvataṉṟu; nī eṅṅaṉam cellutiyō? eṉat talimakaṉ neñciṉik kaḻaṟik kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
336. marutam
(nayappup paratti iṟ parattikkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.)
		kuḻaṟkāṟ cēmpiṉ koḻumaṭal akalilip
		pācip parappiṟ paṟaḻoṭu vatinta
		uṇṇāp piṇaviṉ uyakkañ coliya
		nāḷiri tarīiya eḻunta nīrnāy
	5.	vāḷiyō ṭuḻappat tuṟikaluḻn tamiyiṉ
		teṇkaṭ ṭēṟal mānti makaḷir
		nuṇceyal aṅkuṭam irīip paṇpiṉ
		makiḻnaṉ parattimi pāṭi yaviḻiṇark
		kāñci nīḻaṟ kuravi  yayarun
	10.	tīmperum poykit tuṟikēḻ ūraṉ
		tērtara vanta nēriḻi makaḷir
		ēcupa eṉpaveṉ nalaṉē atuvē
		pākaṉ neṭituyir vāḻtal kāyciṉak
		kolkaḷiṟ ṟiyāṉi nalkal māṟē
	15.	tāmum piṟarum uḷarpōṟ cēṟal
		muḻavimiḻ tuṇaṅki tūṅkum viḻaviṉ
		yāṉavaṇ vārā māṟē 1variṉē
		cuṭaroṭu tiritaru neruñci pōla
		eṉṉoṭu tiriyēṉ āyiṉ veṉvēl
	20.	māri yampiṉ maḻittōṟ cōḻar
		villīṇṭu kuṟumpiṉ 2 vallattup puṟamiḷi
		āriyar paṭiyiṉ uṭikaveṉ
		nēriṟi muṉki vīṅkiya vaḷiyē.
- pāvik koṭṭilār.
(co-ḷ) 1-12. kuḻal kāl cēmpiṉ koḻu maṭal akal ili- tuḷi poruntiya taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya cēmpiṉatu koḻuviya maṭaliluḷḷa akaṉṟa iliyuṭaṉ kūṭiya, pācip parappil - pāciyiṉiyuṭiya nīrp parappil, paṟaḻoṭu vatinta uṇṇāp piṇaviṉ uyakkam coliya - kuṭṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭiyirukkum uṇṇāmiyāl varuntiya peṇ nāyiṉ varuttatti nīkkutaṟku, nāḷ iri tarīiya eḻunta nīr nāy - kālip poḻutilē iriyiṉik koṇartaṟku eḻunta nīr nāy, vāḷiyōṭu uḻappa- vāḷiyip paṟṟip pōr ceytaliṉ, tuṟi kaluḻntamiyiṉ - nīrttuṟi kalaṅkiṉamiyāl, teṇ kaḷ tēṟal mānti - teḷinta kaḷḷiṉik kuṭittu, makaḷir - peṇkaḷ, nuṇ ceyal am kuṭam irīi - nuṇṇiya toḻil nalam vāynta aḻakiya kuṭattiṉi vittuviṭṭu, makiḻnaṉ paṇpu il parattimi pāṭi - tam kaṇavaratu naṟpaṇ pillāta parattimikaḷip pāṭi, aviḻ iṇar kāñci nīḻal kuravi ayarum - virinta pūṅkottukkaḷi yuṭiya kāñci marattiṉ niḻalil kuravi āṭutalic ceyyum, tīm perum poykit tuṟi kēḻ ūraṉ - iṉiya periya poykiyiṉ tuṟi poruntiya ūriyuṭiya talivaṉuṭiya, tēr tara vanta nēr iḻi makaḷir - tērkoṇṭu vara vanta parattiyarāya nēriya aṇikaḷi yaṇinta makaḷir, eṉnalaṉ ēcupa eṉpa - eṉ aḻakiṉi ēcukiṉṟaṉar eṉpar;
12-14. atu - acceyal, pākaṉ neṭitu uyir vāḻtal - pākaṉ neṭuṅkālam uyiruṭaṉ vāḻtal, kāy ciṉak kol kaḷiṟṟu yāṉi nalkal māṟē - mikka ciṉattiṉiyuṭiya kollum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi avaṉik kollātu aruḷ ceytalāl āvatu pōlum;
15-17. tāmum piṟarum uḷar pōl cēṟal - ap peṇṭirum piṟarum ciṟappuḷār pōlac cellutal, muḻavu imiḻ tuṇaṅki tūṅkum viḻavil - muḻavu olikkum tuṇaṅkik kūttu āṭum viḻāk kālattē, yāṉ avaṇ vārā māṟē - yāṉ avviṭattu vārāmiyāl āyatē;
17-23. variṉ - yāṉ avviṭattu variṉ, cuṭaroṭu tiritarum neruñci pōla - ñāyiṟṟiṉ celkiyi nōkkic cuḻalum neruñcip pūp pōla, eṉṉoṭu tiriyēṉ āyiṉ - avarkaḷi yāṉ cellumiṭa mellām eṉṉoṭu tiriyac ceyvēṉ, aṅṅaṉam ceyyēṉ āyiṉ, veṉ vēl māri ampiṉ maḻit tōl cōḻar - veṟṟi poruntiya vēliṉiyum maḻi pōṉṟa ampiṉiyum mēkam pōṉṟa tōṟkiṭukiṉiyu muṭiya cōḻaratu, vil īṇṭu kuṟumpiṉ vallattup puṟa miḷi - viṟpaṭi neruṅkiya araṇiyuṭiya vallattup puṟattēyuḷḷa kāvaṟ kāṭṭiṉkaṇ vantaṭinta, āriyar paṭiyiṉ - āriyaratu paṭipōla, eṉ nēr iṟi muṉki vīṅkiya vaḷi - eṉatu nēriya cantiṉiyuṭiya muṉ kiyil tiraṇṭa vaḷikaḷ, uṭika - citintoḻivaṉavāka.
(muṭipu) ūraṉ tērtaravanta makaḷir, eṉ nalaṉ ēcupa eṉpa; atuvē pākaṉ neṭituyir vāḻtal, kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi nalkalmāṟē, tāmum piṟarum uḷar pōṟ cēṟal, viḻaviṉ yāṉ avaṇ vārāmāṟē; variṉē cuṭaroṭu tiritaru neruñci pōla eṉṉoṭu (tiriyac ceyvēṉ) tiriyēṉāyiṉ, eṉ muṉki vīṅkiya vaḷi cōḻar vallattup puṟa miḷi āriyar paṭiyiṉ uṭika.
(vi-ri) kaṭṭēṟal - kaḷḷākiya tēṟal eṉka. paṇpiṉ - paṇpiṉālē; muṟiyālē eṉṟurittalumām. `aṟintaṟintum pākaṉiyē kollum'1 iyalpiṉatākiya yāṉi, avaṉik kollātu viṭṭiruttalē avaṉ neṭitu uyir vāḻtaṟkuk kāraṇamātal pōla, viḻavil yāṉ avaṇ vārāmiyē tāmum piṟarum uḷarpōṟ cēṟaṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu eṉṟāḷ. variṉ tiriyac ceyvēṉ, aṅṅaṉam tiriyac ceyyēṉāyiṉ eṉa virit turikka. iṟparatti mutalāyiṉāri eṉṉoṭu tiriyac ceyvēṉ eṉṟāḷeṉka. cōḻar vallattup puṟa miḷi eṉka. vallattup puṟa miḷi āriyar paṭiyiṉ uṭika eṉṟataṉāl, cōṇāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇa
tākiya vallattiṉ puṟattē orukāl āriyar paṭiyeṭuttu vantu tōṟṟōṭiṉar eṉṉum varalāṟu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. vaḷi uṭika eṉṟāḷēṉum vaḷiyi uṭippēṉ eṉṟāḷākak koḷka.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum pulavik kaṇṇum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, maṉiyōḷ ottalil taṉṉō raṉṉōr, mikipaṭak kuṟitta koḷkik kaṇṇum eṉṉum pakutikku ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ yāṉavaṇ vārā māṟē eṉat tāṉ maṉiyōḷip pōl illuṟital kūṟi, āṇṭuc celliṟ cuṭarōṭu tiriyum neruñci pōla ēṉi makaḷiri yāṉ celvuḻic cellum cēṭiyar pōlat tiriyumpaṭi paṇṇikkoḷval eṉak kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉpar nac.
`uyarntataṉ mēṟṟēyuḷḷuṅ kāli'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, uvamam uyarntatāka vēṇṭum eṉaṟku, itaṉuḷ māriyampiṉ maḻit tōṟ cōḻar eṉpataṉiyum kāṭṭi, uvami yuyarcci yāṉē uvamikkappaṭum poruṭkuc ciṟappu eytuvittavāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka eṉṟār pērā.
337. pāli
(muṉṉoru kālattup poruḷvayiṟ pirintuvanta talimakaṉ
 piṉṉum poruḷ kaṭi kūṭṭiya neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		cāral yāattu uyarciṉi kuḻitta
		māri īrntaḷi raṉṉa mēṉip
		pēramar maḻikkaṇ pulampukoṇ ṭoḻiya
		īṅkuppirin tuṟital iṉitaṉ ṟākaliṉ
	5.	avaṇa tāka poruḷeṉ ṟumaṇar
		kaṇaniri yaṉṉa palkāṟ kuṟumpoṟit
		tūtoy pārppāṉ maṭiveḷ ḷōli
		paṭiyuṭik 1kiyar varutoṭar nōkki
		uṇṇā maruṅkul iṉṉōṉ kiyatu
	10.	poṉṉā kutalum uṇṭeṉak koṉṉē
		taṭintuṭaṉ vīḻtta kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar
		tiṟaṉil citāar vaṟumi nōkkic
		ceṅkōl ampiṉar kinnoṭiyāp peyarak
		koṭiviṭu kurutit tūṅkukuṭar 2kaṟīi
	15.	varimaral iyaviṉ orunari yēṟṟi
		veṇparal imikkum kaṇpaṟi kavalik
		kaḷḷi nīḻaṟ kataṟupu vatiya
		maḻikkaṇ māṟiya veṅkāṭ ṭāriṭi
		emiyaṅ kaḻitan tōyē paṉiyiruṭ
	20.	peruṅkali vāṉan taliiya
		iruṅkuḷir vāṭiyoṭu varuntuvaḷ eṉavē.
- pāli pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. neñcē! cāral yāatta uyar ciṉi māri kuḻitta - pakka maliyiṉkaṇ yā marattiṉ uyarnta kiḷiyil mārik kālattē taḷirtta, īr taḷir aṉṉa mēṉi - kuḷirnta taḷiriyotta mēṉiyiṉiyum, pēr amar maḻi kaṇ - periya amariya kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum uṭiya talivi, pulampu koṇṭu oḻiya- taṉimi yuṟṟup pirintirukka, īṅkup pirintu uṟital - ivviṭattup pirintu taṅkiyiruttal, iṉitu aṉṟu ākaliṉ - iṉiya taṉṟu ākalāṉ, avaṇatu āka poruḷ - avaḷuḷḷa viṭattu iruppatē poruḷ āvatāka eṉṟum,
19-21. paṉi iruḷ peru kali vāṉam taliiya - naṭukkat tittarum iruḷil periya āravāramuṭiya maḻi peytamiyāl, iru kuḷir vāṭiyoṭu varuntuvaḷ eṉavē - periya kuḷir koṇṭa vāṭiyāl avaḷ varuntuvaḷ eṉṟum kūṟi,
5-19. umaṇar kaṇam niri yaṉṉa kuṟumpoṟi - uppu vāṇikaratu kūṭṭamākac cellum kaḻuti niri pōṉṟa pāṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē, palkāl tūtu oy pārppāṉ - palakālum tūtu cellum pārppāṉ, maṭi veḷ ōli - veḷḷiya ōliccuruḷuṭaṉ, varu tiṟam nōkki - varukiṉṟa iyalpiṉi nōkki, uṇṇā maruṅkul iṉṉōṉ kiyatu - uṇṇāmiyāl vāṭiya vilāviṉiyuṭiya ivaṉ kiyil uḷḷatu, poṉ ākutalum uṇṭu eṉa - poṉṉātalum kūṭum eṉak karuti, koṉṉē taṭintu uṭaṉ vīḻtta paṭiyuṭikkiyar kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar - payaṉiṉṟi appōtē koṉṟu vīḻttiya kiyiṟ paṭiyiṉiyuṭiya koṭumiyiyuṭiya maṟavar, tiṟaṉ il citāar vaṟumi nōkki - uṭukkum kūṟupāṭu illāta kantiyi uṭuttirukkum vaṟumiyip pārttu, ceṅkōl ampiṉar ki noṭiyā peyara - kurutiyāṟ civanta kōlākiya ampiṉiyuṭiya avarkaḷ kiyi noṭittup puṟam pōka, koṭi viṭu kuruti tūṅku kuṭar kaṟīi - nīḷa oḻukiya kurutiyuṭaṉ toṅkik kiṭanta kuṭalik kaṭittu, vari maral iyavil oru nari ēṟṟi - varikaḷiyuṭiya maraluḷḷa neṟiyil ōr āṇ nari, veṇ paral imikkum kaṇ paṟi kavali- veḷḷiya paraṟ kaṟkaḷ miṉṉum kaṇ oḷiyik kavarum kavartta neṟiyiṉkaṇ, kaḷḷi nīḻal kataṟupu vatiya - kaḷḷi nīḻaṟ
kaṇṇē kūkkuraliṭṭut taṅki yirukka, maḻi kaṇ māṟiya vem kāṭṭu ār iṭi - maḻi peyyā toḻinta veppamikka kāṭṭu neṟiyāya ariya iṭattē, emiyam kaḻitantōyē - (muṉpu orukāl) yām tamiyēmāka nī mīṇṭuvantā yaṉṟē.
(muṭipu) neñcē! maḻikkaṇ pulampukoṇ ṭoḻiya īṅkup pirintuṟital iṉitaṉṟākaliṉ, avaṇatāka poruḷeṉavum, iruṅkuḷir vāṭiyoṭu varuntuvaḷ eṉavum kūṟi, maḻi kaṇ māṟiya veṅkāṭṭu ār iṭi muṉpu orukāl emiyam kaḻitantōy.
(vi-ri) taliviyip piriyātu uṟitalē poruḷākum eṉṉum karuttu, `vaṭamīṉpōṟ ṟoḻutētta vayaṅkiya kaṟpiṉāḷ, taṭameṉṟōḷ piriyāmi poruḷāyi ṉallati'1 eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. avaṇatāka poruḷ eṉṟum, vāṭiyoṭu varuntuvaḷ eṉṟum kūṟik kaḻitantōy eṉak kūṭṭuka. palkāl kuṟum poṟi - pala kālkaḷ pōlum kaṟkaḷiyuṭiya pāṟi eṉṟumām. pārppāṉ ōliyuṭaṉ varutiṟam nōkki eṉavum, paṭiyuṭik kiyarākiya kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar eṉavum kūṭṭuka. uṇṇā maruṅkul eṉṟatu pārppāṉatu iyalpu kūṟiyapaṭi. tam ceykiyiṉ payaṉiṉmiyik karutik kiyi noṭittup peyarntaṉar eṉka. neñcē! muṉpu aṅṅaṉam vantāyākaliṉ, ippōtu mīṭṭum niṉ collit teḷintu niṉṉuṭaṉ pōtaru māṟu eṅṅaṉam eṉat talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
338. kuṟiñci
(alla kuṟippiṭṭa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		kuṉṟōṅku vippiṉ nāṭumīk kūṟum
		maṟaṅkeḻu tāṉi araca ruḷḷum
		aṟaṅkaṭip piṭitta ceṅkō luṭaṉamar
		maṟañcāyt teḻunta valaṉuyar tiṇitōṭ
	5.	palarpukaḻ tiruviṟ pacumpūṭ pāṇṭiyaṉ
		aṇaṅkuṭi uyarnilip poruppiṟ kavāaṉ
		ciṉiyoṇ kāntaḷ nāṟum naṟunutal
		tuṇiyīr ōti māa yōḷvayiṉ
		nuṇkōl avirtoṭi 1vaṇpuṟañ cuṟṟa
	10.	muyaṅkal iyiyā tāyiṉum eṉṟum
		vayavuṟu neñcat tuyavuttuṇi yāka
		oṉṉār tēem pāḻpaṭa nūṟum
		tuṉṉarum tuppiṉ veṉvēṟ poṟiyaṉ
		akaliruṅ kāṉattuk kolli pōlat
	15.	tavāa liyarō naṭpē avaḷvayiṉ
		aṟāa liyarō tūtē poṟāar
		2viṇpeṟak kaḻitta tiṇpiṭi yoḷvāṭ
		puṉiṟṟāṉ taraviṉ iḷiyar perumakaṉ
		tokupōrc cōḻaṉ poruṇmali pākkattu
	20.	vaḻaṅka lāṉāp peruntuṟi
		muḻaṅkiru munnīrt tiriyiṉum palavē.
- maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē! 1-10. kuṉṟu ōṅku vippiṉ mīk kūṟum  nāṭu - malikaḷ uyarnta ivvulakil ciṟappittuk kūṟum nāṭṭiṉi yuṭiya, maṟamkeḻu tāṉi aracaruḷḷum - vīram mikka paṭi kaḷiyuṭiya pēraracarkaḷuḷḷum, aṟam kaṭip piṭitta ceṅ kōluṭaṉ - aṟattiṉik kik koṇṭa ceṅkō lāṭciyuṭaṉ, amar maṟam cāyttu eḻunta - pakivaratu pōr ceyyum taṟu kaṇmiyik keṭuttu vaḷarnta, valaṉ uyar tiṇi tōḷ - veṟṟi mikka tiṇṇiya tōḷiyum, palar pukaḻ tiruviṉ - palarālum pukaḻappaṭum tiruviṉiyu muṭiyōṉākiya, pacum pūṭ pāṇṭiyaṉ- pacum pūṭ pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya, aṇaṅku uṭi  uyarnilip poruppiṉ kavā aṉ - teyvattiyuṭiya uyarnta niliyiṉip poruntiya potiyiliṉ pakka maliyiluḷḷa, ciṉi oḷ kāntaḷ nāṟum - kiḷitta oḷi poruntiya kāntaṭpūp pōla maṇakkum, naṟu nutal tuṇi īr ōti māayōḷvayiṉ - nalla neṟṟiyiṉiyum aḷavotta nīṇṭa kūntaliyumuṭiya māmi niṟattiṉaḷāya taliviyiṭattu, nuṇkōl avir toṭi vaṇ puṟam cuṟṟa muyaṅkal - avaḷatu nuṇṇiya tiraṇṭa viḷaṅkum vaḷi namatu vaḷami poruntiya mutukiṉic cuṟṟik koḷḷa muyaṅkutal, iyiyātu āyiṉum - namakkuk kiṭṭātāyiṉum,
12-14. oṉṉār tēem pāḻpaṭa nūṟum - pakivaratu tēyam pāḻpaṭa aḻikkum, tuṉ arum tuppiṉ veṉvēl poṟiyaṉ - pakivarāl kiṭṭutaṟku ariya valiyiṉiyuṭiya veṉṟi poruntiya vēliṉiyuṭiya cēraṉatu, akal iru kāṉattuk kolli pōla - akaṉṟa kariya kāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya kolli malipōla,
10-11. eṉṟum vayavu uṟu neñcattu uyavu tuṇiyāka - eññāṉṟum vēṭkiyuṟṟa neñciṟku ōr ucāt tuṇiyāka,
15. naṭpu tavā aliyar - naṭpu keṭātu nili peṟuvatāka;
16-21. poṟāar viṇ peṟa kaḻitta tiṇ piṭi oḷ vāḷ - pakivar viṇṇulaki eytumāṟu uruviya tiṇṇiya piṭi yiṉiyuṭiya oḷiporuntiya vāḷiyuṭiya, puṉiṟṟu āṉ taraviṉ iḷiyar perumakaṉ - īṉṟa aṇimiyuṭiya pacukkaḷik kavarntu varum veṭci vīrarkaḷukkut talivaṉāṉa, toku pōrc cōḻaṉ - tiraṇṭa pōriṉi mēṟkoṇṭu vellum cōḻaṉatu, poruḷ mali pākkattu vaḻaṅkal āṉā - paṇṭaṅkaḷ niṟinta pākkattiṉ kaṇ ceṉṟu mīḷal amiyāta, muḻaṅku iru munnīr peruntuṟi tiriyiṉum pala - muḻaṅkum periya kaṭaliṉ periya tuṟiyiṭat tuḷḷē alikaḷiṉum palavāka,
15-16. avaḷ vayiṉ tūtu aṟāaliyar - avaḷiṭattu viṭum tūtu oḻiyātu celvatāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! māyōḷvayiṉ muyaṅkal iyiyā tāyiṉum kolli pōla naṭput tavāliyarō; munnīrp peruntuṟit tiriyiṉum pala tūtu aṟāaliyar.
(vi-ri) eḻunta tōḷ eṉka. ceṅkōluṭaṉ tiṇi tōḷiyum tiruviṉiyumuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉak kūṭṭuka. pacumpūṭ pāṇṭiyaṉ, peyar. pūṇiṉiyum uṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṟumām. poruppu eṉṟatu potiyili. muyaṅkal iyiyātāyiṉum eṉṟamiyāl, talivaṉ allakuṟippaṭṭā ṉeṉpatu peṟṟām. naṭpu, namakku avaḷoṭu uḷatāya naṭpu eṉka. neñcattu uyavut tuṇiyāka eṉṟamiyāl, an naṭpiṉi neñcil niṉital māttiriyēṉum iṉpuṟuvēm eṉṟum, tūtu aṟā aliyar eṉṟamiyāl, palakālum tūtu celliṉ orōvaḻi avaḷi muyaṅkutalum iyivatām eṉṟum talivaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār pāṭiya ippāṭṭil mūvēntarum kūṟappaṭuvatu pōlavē, ivar makaṉārākiya nakkīraṉār pāṭiya `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum' (93) eṉṉum akappāṭṭil mūvēntarum kūṟappaṭṭiruttal aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟkuriyatu.
339. pāli
(pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		vīṅkuvicip piṇitta viripari neṭuntēr
		nōṉkatir cumanta āḻiyāḻ maruṅkiṟ
		pāmpeṉa muṭukunīr ōṭak kūmpip
		paṟṟuviṭu viraliṟ payaṟukāy ūḻppa
	5.	aṟciram niṉṟaṉṟāṟ poḻutē muṟpaṭa
		āḷviṉik keḻunta acivil uḷḷattu
		āṇmi vāṅkak kāman taṭpak
		kavipaṭu neñcaṅ kaṭkaṇ akiya
		irutalik koḷḷi yiṭiniṉṟu varunti
	10.	orutalip paṭāa uṟavi pōṉṟaṉam
		nōṅkol aḷiyaḷ tāṉē yākkikku
		1uyiriyin taṉṉa naṭpiṉ avvuyir
		vāḻtal aṉṉa kātal
		cātal aṉṉa pirivari yōḷē.
- 2narimuṭi neṭṭiyār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. viripari piṇitta vīṅku vici neṭu tēr - virinta kutirikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟa mikka viciyiyuṭiya neṭiya tēriṉ, nōṉ katir cumanta āḻi āḻ maruṅkil - valiya āriṉik koṇṭa uruḷi āḻntu cellum cuvaṭṭil, pāmpu eṉa muṭuku nīr ōṭa - pāmpu pōla vēkamākac cellum nīr ōṭavum, kūmpi paṟṟu viṭu viralil payaṟu kāy ūḻppa - kuvintu paṟṟu nīṅkiṉa viralkaḷ pōl payaṟṟuc ceṭiyil kāykaḷ muṟṟavum, poḻutu aṟciram niṉṟaṉṟu - poḻutu paṉipparuvamākap poruntiyatu;
5-10. āḷviṉikku eḻunta acivil uḷḷattu āṇmi muṟpaṭa vāṅka - tāḷāṇmi paṟṟi eḻunta taḷartalillāta neñciṉkaṇṇuḷḷa āṇmi muṉṉē iḻukkavum, kāmam 
taṭpa - kāmamāṉatu piṉṉē niṉṟu taṭukkavum, kavipaṭu neñcam - avaṟṟāl irupāṟpaṭṭa neñcamē nām, kaṇkaṇ akiya - kaṇukkaḷil tīppaṟṟi eritaliṉ, irutalik koḷḷi iṭiniṉṟu varunti - irutaliyum koḷḷiyiṉi yuṭiya oru puḻaṟ ṟaṭṭiyiṉ uḷḷē niṉṟu varunti, orutalip paṭāa uṟavi pōṉṟaṉam - oru pakkattum cella iyalātu taṭumāṟum eṟumpiṉi ottuḷēm;
11-14. yākkikku uyir iyintaṉṉa naṭpiṉ - uṭaloṭu uyir oṉṟiṉā lotta naṭpiṉiyum, a uyir vāḻtal aṉṉa kātal - avvuyir iṉpuṟṟu vāḻtal pōlum kātaliyum uṭiya, cātal aṉṉa pirivu ariyōḷ - ataṟkuc cātalpōlum tuṉpiṉit tarum pirital arumiyi uṭiya nam talivi, nōmkol - varunti yiruppāḷō, aḷiyaḷ - mikavum iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) poḻutu aṟciram niṉṟaṉṟāl; āḷviṉikku eḻunta uḷḷattu āṇmi vāṅka, kāmam taṭpa, kavipaṭu neñcamē! irutalik koḷḷi yiṭi niṉṟu varunti orutalip paṭāa uṟavi pōṉṟaṉam; naṭpiṉiyum kātaliyum piritaṟku arumiyiyum uṭiya nam talivi nōṅkol? aḷiyaḷ.
(vi-ri) tēr uruḷ kiḻittuc ceṉṟa cuvaṭṭil pāmpu pōl nīr ōṭum eṉṟa ik karuttu, `nēmi, taṇṇila maruṅkiṟ pōḻnta vaḻiyuḷ, niricel pāmpiṉ viripu nīrmuṭuka' (324) eṉa muṉṉarum vantuḷḷami kāṇka. nīr ōṭap, payaṟukāy ūḻppap poḻutu aṟciram niṉṟaṉṟu eṉka. āṇmi muṟpaṭa vāṅka eṉṟamiyiṉ kāmam piṉṉiṉṟu taṭpa eṉka. cellutal tavirtal eṉṟa iraṇṭil oṉṟiṟpaṭāmiyiṉ, kavipaṭu neñcamāyiṟṟu. neñcam, viḷi. uṟavi - eṟumpu. yākkikku uyir iyintaṉṉa naṭpu eṉṟatu, oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāta naṭpu eṉṟapaṭi. `cātaliṉ iṉṉāta tilli'1 eṉpavākaliṉ cātal aṉṉa pirivu eṉṟatu cātalpōlum iṉṉāmiyuṭiya pirivu eṉpatāyiṟṟu. eṉavē avvuyir vāḻtal aṉṉa kātal eṉpataṟku, avvuyir iṉpuṟṟu vāḻtal pōlum iṉpatti nalkum kātal eṉap poruḷ koḷka.
340. neytal
(pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ tōḻi talimakaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		paṉṉāḷ evvan tīrap pakalvantu
		puṉṉiyam potumpiṉ iṉṉiḻaṟ kaḻippi
		māli mālkoḷa nōkkip paṇṇāyntu
		valavaṉ vaṇṭēr iyakka nīyum
	5.	celavuvirup puṟutal oḻikatil amma
		cellā nallicip polampūṭ ṭiriyaṉ
		palpūṅ kāṉaṟ pavattiri aṉavivaḷ
		1nalleḻil iḷanalam toliya olleṉak
		kaḻiyē ōtam malkiṉṟu vaḻiyē
	10.	vaḷḷeyiṟ ṟaravoṭu vayamīṉ koṭkum
		ceṉṟōr maṉṟa māṉṟiṉṟu poḻuteṉa
		niṉṟiṟat tavalam vīṭa iṉṟivaṇ
		cēppiṉ evaṉō pūkkēḻ pulampa
		pacumīṉ noṭutta 2veṇṇelmāt tayirmiti
	15.	3mitavi māvār kunavē niṉakkē
		vaṭavar tanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭam
		kuṭapula uṟuppiṟ kūṭṭupu nikaḻttiya
		vaṇṭimir 4naṟuñcān taṇikuvam tiṇṭimil
		elluttoḻiṉ maṭutta valviṉip paratavar
	20.	kūrvaḷik 5kaṭuvici maṇṭaliṟ pāyntuṭaṉ
		kōṭcuṟāk kiḻitta koṭumuṭi neṭuvali
		taṇkaṭal acivaḷi eṟitoṟum viṉiviṭṭu
		muṉṟiṟ ṟāḻit tūṅkum
		teṇkaṭaṟ parappiṉem uṟiviṉ ūrkkē.
- nakkīrar.
(co-ḷ) 13. pū kēḻ pulampa - pūkkaḷ poruntiya kaṭaṟ kariyi yuṭiya talivaṉē,
6-12. cellā nalici polam pūṇ tiriyaṉ - eṉṟum keṭāta nalla cīrttiyiṉiyum poṟpūṇiṉiyumuṭiya tiriyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, pal pū kāṉal pavattiri  aṉa ivaḷ - pala pūkkaḷi yuṭiya cōliyiṉiyuṭiya pavattiri eṉum ūr pōṉṟa ivaḷ, nal eḻil iḷa nalam toliya - taṉatu nalla aḻaku vāynta iḷamic cevvi toliyumāṟu, vaḻi - vaḻiyāṉatu, kaḻi ol eṉa ōtam malkiṉṟu - kaḻi olleṉum oliyuṭaṉ nīr perukap peṟṟuḷatu, vaḷ eyiṟṟu aravoṭu vayamīṉ koṭkum - mēlum kūriya paṟkaḷiyuṭiya pāmpiṉoṭu cuṟāmīṉ tiriyappeṟum, poḻutu maṉṟa māṉṟiṉṟu - poḻutu orutaliyāka mayaṅkiyatu, ceṉṟōr - iṅṅaṉamākavum nam talivar ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉarē, eṉa - eṉṟu kūṟi, niṉtiṟattu avalam vīṭa - niṉṉiṭattuk koḷḷum tuṉpam oḻiya,
18-24. tiṇ timil ellu toḻil maṭutta val viṉi paratavar - tiṇṇiya paṭakuṭaṉ ceṉṟu pakalilē mīṉ piṭikkum toḻili mēṟkoṇṭa valiya ceyaliyuṭiya mīṉ piṭippōr, kūr vaḷi kaṭu vici maṇṭaliṉ - kaṭiya kāṟṟiṉ mikka viciyuṭaṉ cellutaṟ
kaṇ, kōḷ cuṟā pāyntu uṭaṉ kiḻitta koṭumuṭi neṭuvali - kolit toḻiliyuṭiya cuṟā mīṉkaḷ oruṅkē pāyntu kiḻitta vaḷinta muṭikaḷik koṇṭa nīṇṭa valikaḷ, viṉiviṭṭu - toḻil oḻintu, taṇ kaṭal acivaḷi eṟitoṟum - kuḷirnta kaṭaliṉiṉṟu acintuvarum kāṟṟu vīcuntōṟum, muṉṟil tāḻi tūṅkum - maṉi muṟṟaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tāḻi marattiṉ mītu kiṭantu aciyā niṟkum, teḷkaṭal parappiṉ em uṟivu iṉ ūrkku - teḷinta kaṭaṟ parappiṉiyuṭiya ematu uṟitaṟkiṉiya ūriṉkaṇ,
12-13. iṉṟu ivaṇ cēppiṉ evaṉō - nī iṉṟu emmiṭattē taṅkic celliṉ varum kuṟi yātu?
14-18. pacu mīṉ noṭutta veṇ nel mā tayir miti mitavi - paciya mīṉi viṟṟu māṟṟiya veṇṇelliṉ māvit tayiriṭṭup picinta kūḻiṉi, mā ārkun - kutirikaḷ uṇpaṉavākum, niṉakku, vaṭavar tanta vāṉ kēḻ vaṭṭam - vaṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷār koṇarnta veḷḷiya niṟattiyuṭiya vaṭṭak kallil, kuṭa pula uṟuppil kūṭṭupu nikaḻttiya - kuṭamaliyāya potiyiṟ cantaṉak kaṭṭiyāl piṟa maṇap poruḷkaḷiyum kūṭṭi yuṇṭākkiya, vaṇṭu imir naṟu cāntu aṇikuvam - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum naṟiya cāntiṉi aṇivippēm;
1-5. pal nāḷ evvam tīra pakal vantu - pala nāḷum niṉatu varuttam nīṅkap pakaṟ poḻutilē vantu, puṉṉi am potumpiṉ iṉ niḻal kaḻippi - puṉṉi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya cōliyiṉ iṉiya niḻalil poḻutik kaḻittu, māli māl koḷa nōkki - mālip pōḻtu vara ataṉi mayakkam tōṉṟap pārttu, valavaṉ paṇ āyntu vaṇ tēr iyakka - niṉ pākaṉ tēriṉip pūṭṭutali ārāyntu aḻakiya at tēriṉic celutta, nīyum celavu viruppuṟutal oḻika - nīyum cellutaṟku virumputali oḻivāyāka.
(muṭipu) pulampa! ivaḷ, taṉ nalam toliya, vaḻiyē kaḻi ōtam malkiṉṟu; aravoṭu vayamīṉ koṭkum; poḻutu maṉṟa māṉṟiṉṟu; ceṉṟōr; eṉa niṉ tiṟattuk koḷḷum avalam vīṭa, em uṟivu iṉ ūrk kaṇ ivaṇ iṉṟu cēppiṉ evaṉō? mā mitavi ārkuna; niṉakku naṟuñ cāntu aṇikuvam; ātalāl nīyum celavu viruppuṟutal oḻikatil.
(vi-ri) oḻikatil: til viḻiviṉkaṇ vantatu. amma, aci. ivaḷ iḷa nalam toliya niṉ tiṟattuk koḷḷum avalam eṉa viritturikka. pacumīṉ noṭutta veṇṇel - mīṉik koṭuttu ataṟku māṟākappeṟṟa nel eṉka. vaṭavar tanta vaṭṭam - vaṭavar vaṭamaliyiṉiṉṟum koṇṭuvanta vaṭṭak kal eṉka. vaṭamali yil uṇṭākiya kallum teṉmaliyil uṇṭākiya cantaṉak kaṭṭiyum ciṟantaṉavākap paṇṭiyōr koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu, `vaṭavar tan vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭam, teṉpula maruṅkiṟ cāntoṭu tuṟappa'1 `vaṭamalip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattut, teṉmalip piṟanta cantaṉam maṟuka'2 eṉpaṉa vaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. potiyiṉmali kuṭamali eṉavum paṭumākaliṉ, ataṉik kuṭapulam eṉakkoṇṭu ataṉiluṇṭākiya kaṭṭiyi uṟuppu eṉṟār. aṉṟi, kuṭapula uṟuppu eṉpataṟku yavaṉa tēyattiluṇ ṭākiya virikku uṟuppām poruḷkaḷ eṉṟalumām. `kuṭatici maruṅkiṉ veḷḷayir'3 eṉa varuvatu kāṇka.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'4 eṉṉum cūttirattu, vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya viṭattu eṉṉum pakutikku, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ taṉakkum puravikkum koṭuppaṉa kūṟit taṭutta vāṟu kāṇka eṉpar nac.
341. pāli
(piriviṉ kaṭ ṭōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		uytaki yiṉṟāl tōḻi pipayak
		kōṅkuṅ koykuḻi yuṟṟaṉa kuyilum
		tēmpāy māat tōṅkuciṉi viḷikkum
		nāṭār kāvirik kōṭutōy malirniṟit
	5.	kaḻiyaḻi nīttam cāaya vaḻināḷ
		maḻikaḻin taṉṉa mākkāl mayaṅkaṟal
		patavumēyal aruntu tuḷaṅkimil nallēṟum
		matavunaṭi nākko ṭacivīṭap parukik
		kuṟuṅkāṟ kāñcik kōti melliṇarp
	10.	poṟṟaki nuṇṭātu uṟippat tokkuṭaṉ
		kuppi vārmaṇal ekkart tuñcum
		yāṇar vēṉil maṉṉitu
		māṇalam nukarun tuṇiyuṭi yōrkkē.
- āvūr mūlaṅkiḻār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. tōḻi-, kōṅkum pi paya koy kuḻi uṟṟaṉa- kōṅkum mella mellak koyyappaṭum taḷirkaḷi eytiṉa, kuyilum tēm pāy māattu ōṅku ciṉi viḷikkum - kuyilum tēṉ perukum māmarattil uyarnta kiḷiyiliruntu kūvum;
4-11. nāṭu ār kāviri kōṭu tōy malir niṟi - nāṭukaḷ niṟitaṟ kētuvāya kāviriyiṉ kariyip poruntiya mikka perukkākiya, kaḻi aḻi nīttam - ōṭakkōl maṟiyum veḷḷam, cāaya vaḻināḷ - vaṟṟiya piṉṉāḷil, maḻi kaḻintaṉṉa mā kāl mayaṅku aṟal - maḻi peytu kaḻintā lotta periya vāykkāl kaḷiṟ ṟaṅkiya nīriṉi, patavu mēyal aruntu tuḷaṅku imil nal 
ēṟum - aṟukam pullāya uṇaviṉit tiṉṟa aciyum koṇṭiyiṉi yuṭiya nalla ēṟukaḷum, matavu uṭi nā koṭu aci vīṭa paruki - valimiyuṭiya nāviṉāl taḷarcci nīṅkak kuṭittu, kuṟukāl kāñci kōti mel iṇar poṉ taki nuṇ tātu uṟippa - kuṟiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kāñci marattiṉ māli pōṉṟa melliya pūṅ kottukkaḷiṉiṉṟu utirum poṉṉiṉ aḻakuṭiya nuṇṇiya poṭikaḷ mēlē utirntiṭa, tokku uṭaṉ kuppi vār maṇal ekkar tuñcum - oruṅku kūṭi neṭiya maṇaliṉ kuppiyākiya mēṭṭilē tuyilum,
12-13. itu - iṅṅaṉamāya ikkālam; māṇ nalam nukarum tuṇiyuṭiyōrkku - ciṟanta iṉpatti nukarum tuṇiyiṉip pakkalil uṭiyārkku, yāṇar vēṉil - aḻakiya vēṉiṟ paruvamākum, maṉ - ataṉāṟ peṟṟa teṉ?
1. uytaki iṉṟu - tuṇiyip pirintuḷḷa nām uyyumāṟu illiyē.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kōṅkum koy kuḻi yuṟṟaṉa; kuyilum viḷikkum; nallēṟum aṟal paruki nuṇtātu uṟippa ekkart tuñcum; itu tuṇiyuṭiyōrkku yāṇar vēṉil maṉ; nām uytaki iṉṟu.
(vi-ri) aṟal paruki eṉak kūṭṭuka. nā koṭu - nāviṉik koṇṭu; nāviṉāl. tuñcum eṉpataṉip peyareccamākak koṇṭu tuñcum itu eṉṟurittalumām. yāṇar vēṉil - iṉpattit tarum vēṉil eṉka.
342. kuṟiñci
(alla kuṟippaṭṭa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		oṟuppa ōvali niṟuppa nillali
		puṇarntōr pōlap pōṟṟumati niṉakkiyāṉ
		kiḷiñaṉ allaṉō neñcē teṉāatu
		velpōrk kavuriyar naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷati
	5.	maṇkoḷ puṟṟat taruppuḻi tiṟappiṉ
		ākoḷ mūtūrk kaḷvar perumakaṉ
		ēval iḷiyar talivaṉ mēvār
		aruṅkuṟum peṟinta āṟṟaloṭu paruntupaṭap
		palceruk kaṭanta celluṟaḻ taṭakkik
	10.	keṭāa nallicit teṉṉaṉ toṭāa
		nīriḻi maruṅkiṟ kallaḷik karantavav
		variyara makaḷiriṉ ariyaḷ
		avvari alkul aṇiyāk kālē.
- maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 3-4. neñcē-, teṉāatu - teṟkiṉ kaṇṇē yuḷḷatākiya, vel pōr kavuriyar nal nāṭṭu uḷḷatu - pōr vellum pāṇṭiyaratu nalla nāṭṭiluḷḷatāya,
5-13. maṇ koḷ puṟṟattu - maṇṇālāya puṟṟiṉiyuṭiya, aruppu uḻi tiṟappiṉ - kāṭṭaraṇiṉ iṭattit tiṟattalōṭu, ā koḷ mūtūr kaḷvar perumakaṉ - pakivar ākkaḷik kavarntu koḷḷum paḻiya ūriṉarāya kaḷvarkaṭku mutalvaṉum, ēval iḷiyar talivaṉ - ēvutalic ceyyum vīrarkaṭkut talivaṉum, mēvār aru kuṟumpu eṟinta āṟṟaloṭu - pakivaratu ariya araṇkaḷi aḻitta valimiyuṭaṉ, paruntupaṭa pal ceru kaṭanta - paruntukaḷ vantu kūṭap pakivaratu pala pōriyum veṉṟa, cel uṟaḻ taṭa ki - iṭiyuṭaṉ māṟupaṭum periya kiyiṉiyum, keṭāa nal ici - eṉṟum keṭāta nalla cīrttiyiṉiyumuṭi yāṉum ākiya, teṉṉaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉatu, toṭāa nīr iḻi maruṅkil - tōṇṭappaṭāta aruvi viḻum poykiyiṉi yuṭiya, kalaḷi - maliyiṉ kukiyil, karanta - maṟinta, variyara makaḷiriṉ - variyara makaḷirpōla, ariyaḷ - ariyaḷāya, a vari alkul - aḻakiya varikaḷiyuṭiyaḷāya talivi, aṇiyākkāl - nammi aṇiyap peṟātaviṭattu,
1-3. oṟuppa ōvali - uṉṉi vaṉ coṟ kūṟi oṟukkavum oḻivāy alli, niṟuppa nillali - iṉcoṟ kūṟi niṟuttavum niṟpāy alli, niṉakku yāṉ kiḷiñaṉ allaṉō - uṉakku yāṉ uṟaviṉaṉ allaṉō, puṇarntōrpōla pōṟṟumati - uḷḷam oṉṟiya naṇparippōla yāṉ kūṟuvataṉip pōṟṟu vāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! variyara makaḷiriṉ ariyaḷ avvari alkul aṇiyākkāl, oṟuppa ōvali; niṟuppa nillali; niṉakku yāṉ kiḷiñaṉ allaṉō; puṇarntōrpōlap pōṟṟumati.
kaḷvar perumakaṉum iḷiñar talivaṉum taṭakkiyum nalliciyum uṭiyōṉumākiya teṉṉaṉ eṉka.
(vi-ri) oṟuttal - vaṉcoṟ kūṟik kaḻaṟutal. mēl oṟuppa eṉṟamiyāl niṟuppa eṉpataṟku iṉcoṟ kūṟi niṟuttavum eṉka. ivvirutiṟattāṉum aṟivuṟuttal kiḷiñar ceyalātaliṉ, niṉakku yāṉ kiḷiñaṉ allaṉō eṉṟāṉ. alla kuṟippaṭṭa talimakaṉ talivi peṟutaṟkariyaḷ ākaliṉ āṇṭuc cellutal oḻika īṅkē nil eṉṟu urittum neñcu kēḷātu cellutaliṉ ataṉi nōkki iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
teṉāatu - kuṟippumuṟṟu eccamākiyatu. uḷḷati - ai cāriyi. naṉṉāṭṭu uḷḷatākiya kal aḷi eṉṟu kūṭṭuka. aruppam - aruppu eṉṟāyatu. aruppam - kāṭṭaraṇ. mēvār - vēḷirum vēntarumākiya pakivar. vēḷiratu kuṟumpu eṟinta eṉṟum, vēntaratu pal ceruk kaṭanta eṉṟum koḷka. vēḷir - kuṟunila maṉṉar. toṭā maruṅku - tōṇṭappaṭāta iṭam; iyaṟkiyāya nīrnili; itu poyki eṉappaṭum.
343. pāli
(talimakaṉ iṭiccurattu mīḷak karutiya neñciṉik kaḻaṟip pōyatu.)
		vāṅkami puriyum vīṅkiṟip paṇittōḷ
		cilcuṇaṅ kaṇinta palpūṇ meṉmuli
		nalleḻil ākam pullutal nayantu
		1maraṅkōḷ umaṇmakaṉ peyarum parutip
	5.	puṉṟali cititta vaṉṟali naṭukaṟ
		kaṇṇi vāṭiya maṇṇā maruṅkuṟ
		kūruḷi kuyiṉṟa kōṭumāy eḻuttav
		āṟucel vampalar vēṟupayam paṭukkum
		kaṇpori kavaliya kāṉat tāṅkaṇ
	10.	naṉantali yāat tantaḷirp peruñciṉi
		ilpōl nīḻaṟ celveyil oḻimār
		neṭuñcevik kaḻutik kuṟuṅkāl ēṟṟip
		puṟaniṟi paṇṭattup poṟiyacāak 2kaḷinta
		peyarpaṭi koḷḷārk kuyavuttuṇi yāki
	15.	uyarnta āḷviṉi purintōy peyarntuniṉṟu
		uḷḷiṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē kaḷḷiṉ
		makiḻiṉ makiḻnta arimatar maḻikkaṇ
		ciṉmoḻip polinta 1tuvarvāyp
		paṉmāṇ pētiyiṟ 2pirinta nīyē.
- maturi maruta ṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ)  16-19. eṉ neñcē vāḻi-, kaḷḷiṉ makiḻiṉ makiḻnta- kaḷḷāl ākiya makiḻvupōla makiḻtaṟku ētuvākiya, ari matar maḻikaṇ - cevvari paṭarnta matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum, cil moḻip polinta tuvar vāy - cilavāya moḻikaḷāṟ polivuṟṟa pavaḷampōṉṟa vāyiṉiyum, pal māṇ - palavāya māṇpu kaḷiyum uṭiya, pētiyiṉ pirinta nī - nam taliviyip pirintuvanta nī,
4-15. maram kōḷ umaṇ makaṉ peyarum paruti - vaṇṭiyiṉik koṇṭa uppu vāṇikaṉatu peyarntu cellum uruḷiṉ, puṉ tali - polivillāta pūṇ, cititta vaṉ tali naṭukal - citiyac ceyta valiya pāṟiyiluḷḷa naṭukalliṉ, kaṇṇi vāṭiya maṇṇā maruṅkul - iṭappeṟṟa kaṇṇi vāṭappeṟṟatum nīrāṭṭap peṟātatumākiya iṭattil, kūr uḷi kuyiṉṟa kōṭumāy eḻuttu - kūriya uḷiyāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟa kīṟṟukkaḷ maṟinta eḻuttukkaḷ, a āṟu cel vampalar vēṟu payam paṭukkum - av vaḻiyilē cellum putiyarkku vēṟu poruḷiṉavākap piṟaḻntu kāṇappaṭum, kaṇ pori kavaliya kāṉattu āṅkaṇ - iṭaṅkaḷ porinta kavartta neṟikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭākiya aṅkuḷḷa, naṉantali yāattu am taḷir peru ciṉi - akaṉṟa iṭattiṉkaṇṇē yā marattiṉ aḻakiya taḷirkaḷiyuṭiya periya kiḷikaḷiṉ, il pōl nīḻal cel veyil oḻimār - illiṉ kaṇ iruppatu pōṉṟa niḻalil tām vanta veyiliṉ veppam oḻitaṟku, neṭu cevi kaḻuti kuṟukāl ēṟṟi - nīṇṭa ceviyiṉiyum kuṟiya kāliṉiyumuṭiya āṇ kaḻutiyiṉ, puṟam niṟi paṇṭattup poṟi acāak kaḷinta - puṟattē niṟintuḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ pārattālāya taḷarviṉip pōkkiyavarāki, peyarpaṭi koḷḷārkku - mīṇṭum puṟappāṭu ceyyātiruntārkku, uyavu tuṇiyāki - ucāviyirukkum vaḻittuṇiyāki, uyarnta āḷviṉi purintōy - mikka muyaṟciyiṉi virumpi niṉṟaṉi, (āyiṉum ippōtu),
1-3. vāṅku ami puriyum vīṅku iṟi paṇi tōḷ - vaḷinta mūṅkili okkum mikka iṟiyiṉiyuṭiya perutta tōḷiḷiyum, cil cuṇaṅku aṇinta pal pūṇ meṉ muli - cilavāya tēmali aṇinta pala pūṇkaḷiyuṭiya metteṉṟa muliyiṉiyumuṭiya taliviyiṉ, nal eḻil ākam pullutal nayantu - nalla aḻakiya ākattiṉit taḻuvutali virumpi,
15-16. peyarntu niṉṟu uḷḷiṉi - mīṇṭu niṉṟu immuyaṟciyiṉik kiviṭa niṉittuḷāy; itu takumā?
(muṭipu) eṉ neñcē! vāḻi! pētiyiṟ pirinta nī kāṉattu āṅkaṇ uyarnta āḷviṉipurintōy āyiṉum, ippōtu, meṉmuli ākam pullutal nayantu peyarntu niṉṟu uḷḷiṉi; itu takumā?
(vi-ri) maram kōḷ : maram eṉpatu ataṉālāya vaṇṭiyik kuṟittatu; marakkalattik kuṟittalpōla. atu marak kalattik kuṟittal. `peruṅ kaṭal nīntiya maram valiyuṟukkum'1 `marattārim māṇāk kuṭip piṟantār'2 `illi maram pōkkik kūli koṇṭār'3 eṉpavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. paruti - vaṇṭiyiṉ uruḷ. payam - poruḷ. peyarpaṭi - peyarpu aṭi; peyar talip poruntal. talivi ucāttuṇi yiṉṟi varunta avaḷip pirintu, curattilē cellum paṇṭa vāṇikarkku ucāt tuṇiyākip periya muyaṟciyi mēṟkoṇṭa nī ippoḻutu iṭic curattē mīṇṭu niṉṟu taliviyi niṉippatu eṉṉi? eṉat talivaṉ neñciṉik kaḻaṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
344. mulli
(viṉi muṟṟiya talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		vaḷamaḻi poḻinta vāṉiṟak kaḷari
		uḷartaru taṇvaḷi uṟutoṟum nilaveṉat
		tokumuki virinta muṭakkāṟ piṭaviṉ
		viyēr vāleyiṟ ṟoṇṇutal makaḷir
	5.	kimāṇ tōḷi kaṭuppap piyeṉa
		mayiliṉam payilum marampayil kāṉam
		elliṭi yuṟāa vaḷavi vallē
		4kaḻaloli nāvil teṇmaṇi kaṟaṅka
		5niḻalolip paṉṉa nimirparip puravi
	10.	vayakkuṟu koṭiñci poliya vaḷpāyntu
		iyakkumati vāḻiyō kiyuṭi valava
		pacappuṟu paṭaraṭa varuntiya
		nayappiṉ kātali nakimukam peṟavē.
- maturi aḷakkar ñāḻār makaṉār 1 maḷḷaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11-13. ki uṭi valava - tēr ūrtalil ki tērnta pākaṉē! vāḻi-!, pacappu uṟu paṭar aṭa varuntiya - pacali uṟṟa tuṉpam varutta varuntiya, nayappu iṉ kātali - aṉpu mikka iṉiya kātaliyiṉ, naki mukam peṟa - nakiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya mukattiṉi aṭiya,
1-6. vaḷa maḻi poḻinta - vaḷantarum maḻi corinta, vāl niṟa kaḷari - veḷḷiya niṟattiṉiyuṭiya kaḷar nilamākiya kāṭṭil, uḷartaru taṇ vaḷi uṟutoṟum - vīcum kuḷirnta kāṟṟu mikun toṟum, nilavu eṉa toku muki virinta muṭa kāl piṭaviṉ - nilavoḷi eṉṉumāṟu tokka arumpukaḷ virinta vaḷinta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya piṭā marattiṉ kaṇṇē, vi ēr vāl eyiṟṟu oḷ nutal makaḷir ki māṇ tōḷi kaṭuppa - kūriya aḻakiya veḷḷiya paṟkaḷiyum oḷi taṅkiya neṟṟiyiṉiyumuṭiya makaḷiratu oḻuṅku māṭcimippaṭṭa tōḷi eṉṉum viḷi yāṭṭi yoppa, pi eṉa mayil iṉam payilum maram payil kāṉam - melleṉa mayiṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ iyalum maraṅkaḷ aṭarnta kāṭṭu neṟiyil,
7. el iṭi uṟā aḷavi - iruḷ iṭippaṭā muṉṉarē,
7-11. vallē - virintu, kaḻal oli nāviṉ teṇ maṇi  kaṟaṅka - kaḻalākiya olikkum nāviṉiyuṭiya teḷinta maṇi olikka, niḻal olippu aṉṉa nimir parip puravi - oḷiyiṉ taḻittali yotta nimirnta celaviṉiyuṭiya kutirikaḷi, vaḷpu āyntu - kaṭivāḷattiṉi āyntu celutti, vayakku uṟu koṭiñci poliya - viḷakkamuṟum koṭiñci polivuṟṟuk kāṇa ivarntu, iyakkumati - tēriṉic celuttuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) valava! vāḻi! kātali nakimukam peṟa, kāṉam el iṭi uṟā aḷavi, puravi vaḷpu āyntu koṭiñci poliya (ivarntu tēriṉi) iyakkumati.
(vi-ri) kaḷari - kaḷar nilam, piṭaviṉ kaṇ mayiliṉam payilum kāṉam eṉka. tōḷi makaḷir viḷiyāṭṭuḷ oṉṟu. itu palvarik kūttuḷ oṉṟātalic cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟu kāti uriyāṉ aṟika. tōḷi vīci āṭutaliṉ tōḷi eṉappaṭṭatu. ki māṇ - ki vīcutal māṭciyuṟṟa eṉṟumām. tiruvācakattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa tōṇōkkam eṉpatum ituvēpōlum. kārkālattil piṭaviṉ mītu mayilkaḷ uvakiyuṭaṉ ulāvutalukku oṇṇutal makaḷiratu tōḷ vīccu uvamam āyiṟṟu.
345. pāli
(tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		vicumputaḷi poḻintu vemmi 1nīṅkit
		taṇpatam paṭutal celkeṉap paṉmāṇ
		nāñcela viḻintaṉa māka ōṅkupukaḻk
		kāṉamar celvi aruḷaliṉ veṇkāṟ
	5.	palpaṭip puravi eytiya tollici
		nuṇaṅkunuṇ paṉuvaṟ pulavaṉ pāṭiya
		iṉamaḻi tavaḻum ēḻiṟ kuṉṟattuk
		karuṅkāl vēṅkic cempūm piṇiyal
		aitēn talkul yāmaṇin tuvakkuñ
	10.	ciṉṉāḷ kaḻika eṉṟu muṉṉāḷ
		nammoṭu poyttaṉar āyiṉun tammoṭu
		tiruntuvēl iḷiyar curumpuṇa malimār
		māmuṟi īṉṟu marakkom pakippa
		uṟikaḻin tulanta piṉṟip poṟiya
	15.	ciṟuveḷ ḷaruvit tuvaliyiṉ malarnta
		karuṅkāl nuṇaviṉ peruñciṉi vāṉpūc
		cemmaṇaṟ ciṟuneṟi kammeṉa varippak
		kāṭukaviṉ peṟuka tōḻi āṭuvaḷikku
		olkunili iṟṟi orutaṉi neṭuvīḻ
	20.	kalkaṇ cīkkum attam
		alkuveyil nīḻal acintaṉar celavē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 18. tōḻi-,
1-3. vicumpu taḷi poḻintu - vāṉam tuḷiyic corintu, vemmi nīṅki - veppam kaḻintu, taṇ patam paṭutal - kuḷirnta nilimi eytutaliṉ, celka eṉa - emmiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu celvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi, nām pal māṇ celaviḻintaṉam āka - nām palamuṟiyum uṭaṉ cella virumpi vēṇṭiṉēmāka,
3-11. ōṅku pukaḻ kāṉ amar celvi - mikka pukaḻ vāynta koṟṟavi, aruḷaliṉ - aruḷ kūrntu aḷittaliṉ, veṇkāl pal paṭi puravi eytiya - veḷḷiya kāliṉiyum pala paṭiyiṉi yum uṭiya kutirikaḷi aṭinta, tol ici nuṇaṅku nuṇ paṉuval pulavaṉ pāṭiya - paḻamiyāṉa pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya mika nuṇṇiya ceyyuṭkaḷi iyaṟṟiya pulavaṉāṟ pāṭappeṟṟa, iṉam maḻi tavaḻum ēḻil kuṉṟattu - kūṭṭamāya mēkaṅkaḷ tavaḻum ēḻil eṉum kuṉṟattiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa, karu kāl vēṅki cem pū piṇiyal - kariya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya vēṅki marattiṉ civanta pūkkaḷālāya māliyi, aitu ēntu alkul yām aṇintu uvakkum - aḻakiyatāya uyarnta alkulil yām aṇintu makiḻum paruvamāya, cil nāḷ kaḻika eṉṟu - cila nāḷ kaḻivatāka eṉṟu, muṉ nāḷ nammoṭu poyttaṉar āyiṉum - muṟkālattē nampāl poy kūṟic ceṉṟuviṭṭārāyiṉum,
18-21. āṭu vaḷikku olku nili iṟṟi oru taṉi neṭu vīḻ - aciyum kāṟṟiṉāl taḷarum nili vāynta iṟṟi marattiṉ oṉṟākit taṉitta nīṇṭa viḻutu, kal kaṇ cīkkum attam - maliyiṉ iṭattiṉit tuṭikkum cura neṟiyil, alku veyil nīḻal acintaṉar cela - poruntiya veyiṟkāka niḻaliṉkaṇ taṅkiṉa rākic celaṟku,
11-18. tammoṭu tiruntu vēl iḷiyar - tammuṭaṉē tiruntiya vēliṉiyuṭiya ēvalāḷarum, curumpu uṇa malimār - vaṇṭukaḷ uṇṇumpaṭi tarittukkōṭaṟku, mā muṟi īṉṟu marakkompu akippa - ciṟanta taḷirkaḷi īṉṟu marattiṉ kompukaḷ taḷirkku māṟu, uṟi kaḻintu ulanta piṉṟi - maḻi peytu nīṅkiya piṉpu, poṟiya ciṟu veḷ aruvit tuvaliyiṉ malarnta - kuṉṟukaḷiṉiṭat tavāya ciṟiya veḷḷiya aruvikaḷiṉ nīrt tuḷiyāl malarnta, karu kāl nuṇaviṉ peru ciṉi vāṉ pū - kariya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya nuṇā marattiṉ periya kiḷikaḷiṟ pūtta veḷḷiya pūkkaḷ, cemmaṇal ciṟu neṟi kam eṉa varippa - civanta maṇaliyuṭiya ciṟiya vaḻikaḷilē kam meṉṟu maṇakkumāṟu utirntu aḻaku ceya, kāṭu kaviṉ  peṟuka - kāṭu aḻaku peṟuvatāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! paṉ māṇ nām cela viḻintaṉamāka, vēṅkip pūm piṇiyal yām aṇintu uvakkum ciṉṉāḷ kaḻika eṉṟu muṉṉāḷ poyttaṉarāyiṉum, attam niḻal acintaṉar cela, mā muṟi īṉṟu marakkompu akippa, nuṇaviṉ vāṉpū ciṟu neṟi kammeṉa varippak, kāṭu kaviṉ peṟuka.
