(vi-ri) paṭutaliṉ uṭaṉ koṇṭu celkeṉa viritturikka. `kāṉamar celvi aruḷaliṉ..... puravi eytiya..... pulavaṉ pāṭiya ēḻiṟ kuṉṟam' eṉpataṉāl, ēḻiṟ kuṉṟattip pāṭiya ciṟanta pulavaṉ oruvaṉukkuk kāṉamar celvi kutiri aḷittatoru varalāṟu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṭi - kutirikkalaṉi, ēḻiṟ kuṉṟam - naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉatu; mēl kaṭaṟ pakkattatu. tammoṭu iḷiyar malimār - tāmum iḷiyarum malitaṟku eṉka. talivar ippoḻutu nammip poyttu akaṉṟaṉarāyiṉum paṉṉāḷum namakku iṉpam viḷittavarākaliṉ, avar cellum neṟi niḻalil iḷippāṟic cellutaṟkuk kāṭu kaviṉ peṟuvatāka eṉat tōḻiyi nōkkit talivi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
346. marutam
(tōḻi talimakaṟku vāyiṉ maṟuttatu.)
		nakinaṉ ṟamma tāṉē iṟimici
		māric cutiyiṉ īrmpuṟat taṉṉa
		kūraṟ kokkiṉ kuṟumpaṟic cēval
		veḷḷi veṇṭō ṭaṉṉa kayalkuṟittuk
	5.	kaḷḷār uvakik kalimakiḻ uḻavar
		kāñciyaṅ kuṟuntaṟi kuttit tīñcuvi
		meṉkaḻik karumpiṉ naṉpala miṭintu
		peruñcey nelliṉ pācaval potti
		varuntik koṇṭa valvāyk koṭuñciṟi
	10.	mītaḻi kaṭunīr nōkkip pippayap
		pārval irukkum payaṅkēḻ ūra
		yāmatu pēṇiṉṟō ilamē nīniṉ
		paṇṇami nalyāḻp pāṇaṉoṭu vicipiṇi
		maṇṇār muḻaviṉ kaṇṇatirn tiyampa
	15.	makiḻtuṇic cuṟṟamoṭu maṭṭu mānti
		emmaṉi vārā yāki muṉṉāḷ
		nummaṉic cērnta ñāṉṟi yammaṉik
		kuṟuntoṭi maṭanti uvantaṉaḷ neṭuntēr
		iḻiyaṇi yāṉip paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ
	20.	māṭamali maṟukiṟ kūṭal āṅkaṇ
		veḷḷat tāṉiyoṭu 1vēṟupulat tiṟutta
		kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ nallamar cāayk
		kaṭumparip puraviyoṭu kaḷiṟupala vavvi
		ētiṉ maṉṉar ūrkoḷak
	25.	kōti mārpaṉ uvakiyiṟ peritē.
- nakkīrar.
(co-ḷ) 1-11. māri cutiyiṉ iṟimici īr puṟattu aṉṉa - mārikkālattē iṟappiṉ mēluḷḷa cuti pūciya kuḷirnta mēliṭattiyotta, kūral kuṟum paṟi kokkiṉ cēval - iṟakiṉiyuṭiya kuṟukak kuṟukap paṟakkum  kokkiṉ cēvalāṉatu, veḷḷi veṇ tōṭu aṉṉa kayal  kuṟittu - veḷḷiyālāya veḷḷiya itaḻ pōṉṟa kayal mīṉip peṟal karuti, kaḷ ār uvaki kali makiḻ uḻavar - kaḷḷiṉiyuṇṭa makiḻvālāya mikka cerukkiṉik koṇṭa uḻavar, kāñci kuṟu taṟi kutti - kāñci marattiṉ kuṟiya tuṇṭukaḷi naṭṭu, tīm cuvi mel kaḻi karumpiṉ nal pala miṭintu - iṉiya cuviyuṭiya melliya taṇṭiṉi yuṭiya karumpiṉ ciṟanta pala kaḻikaḷik kaṭṭik kuṟukkē vittu aṭittu, peru cey nelliṉ pacu aval potti - periya neṟpayiriyuṭiya ceyyākiya paciya paḷḷaṅkaḷil nīrit tēkki, varuntik koṇṭa valvāyk koṭu ciṟi - varuttamuṟṟu iyaṟṟiya valiya iṭattiṉiyuṭiya vaḷinta aṇiyiṉ, mītu aḻi kaṭu nīr nōkki - mēlē tēṅki vaḻiyum kaṭiya nīriṉip pārttu, pi paya pārval irukkum payam kēḻ ūra - mellamellac ceṉṟu nōkkiyirukkum payaṉ poruntiya ūriṉi yuṭiyavaṉē,
12-17. nī niṉ paṇ ami nalyāḻ pāṇaṉoṭu - nī niṉatu paṇṇiṉ tiṟam aminta nalla yāḻiṉiyuṭiya pāṇaṉuṭaṉ, vicipiṇi maṇār muḻaviṉ kaṇ atirntu iyampa - iṟukiya kaṭṭiṉiyuṭiya mārccaṉi aminta muḻaviṉ kaṇkaḷ muḻaṅki olikka, makiḻtuṇic cuṟṟamoṭu maṭṭu mānti - makiḻcciyuṟṟa niṉakkut tuṇiyākiya cuṟṟattuṭaṉ kaḷḷuṇṭu, em maṉi vārāy āki - ematu maṉiyiṉkaṇ vārā toḻintu, muṉ nāḷ - muṉṉi nāḷil, num maṉi cērnta ñāṉṟi - numatu maṉiyāya ap paratti illiṉ kaṇ cērntirunta pōḻtu,
17-25. am maṉi kuṟu toṭi maṭanti - ammaṉiyiluḷḷa ciṟiya toṭiyiṉiyaṇinta niṉ paratti, neṭu tēr iḻiaṇi yāṉi paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ - nīṇṭa tēriṉiyum iḻiyi aṇinta yāṉiyiṉiyumuṭiya paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ eṉpāṉi, māṭam mali maṟukiṉ kūṭal āṅkaṇ - māṭaṅkaḷ mikka terukkaḷiyuṭiya kūṭalākiya aṅkē, veḷḷam tāṉiyoṭu vēṟupulattu  iṟutta - mikka cēṉiyuṭaṉ vēṟṟuppulattē pōr ceya vantu taṅkiyirunta, kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ nal amar cāay - kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ nalla pōriṉkaṇ cāyttu, kaṭum parip puraviyoṭu kaḷiṟu pala vavvi - kaṭiya celaviṉiyuṭiya kutirikaḷuṭaṉ pala yāṉikaḷiyum paṟṟik koṇṭu, ētil maṉṉar ūr koḷa - paki maṉṉaratu ūriṉip paṟṟik koḷḷa, kōti mārpaṉ uvakiyiṉ peritu - kōti mārpaṉ eṉṉum cēraṉ eytiya makiḻviṉum peritāka, uvantaṉaḷ - makiḻntaṉaḷ aṉṟō.
12. yām atu pēṇiṉṟō ilam - yāṅkaḷ ataṉik karutiyatu ilam;
1. naki naṉṟu amma - aṅṅaṉamākavum nī maṟuppatu mikka nakiyi uṇṭākkuvatākum.
(muṭipu) payam kēḻ ūra! nī niṉ pāṇaṉoṭu muḻaviṉ kaṇ iyampa makiḻtuṇic cuṟṟamoṭu maṭṭu mānti em maṉi vārāyāki num maṉic cērnta ñāṉṟi, ammaṉik kuṟuntoṭi maṭanti, kōtimārpaṉ uvakiyiṟ peritu uvantaṉa ḷaṉṟō! yām ataṉip pēṇiṉṟō ilam. naki naṉṟu amma.
(vi-ri) kūral - ciṟaku. cēval kayal kuṟittuk koṭuñ ciṟi mītaḻi kaṭunīr nōkkip pārvalirukkum eṉka. pārval iruttal - patuṅkip pārttiruttal. karumpiṉi miṭintu eṉṟatu maruta nilattiṉ vaḷamikuti kūṟiyavāṟu. nelliṉ ceyyākiya aval eṉka. ciṟimītu vaḻiyum nīril kayal mīṉkaḷ ēṟi varumākaliṉ, kokkiṉ cēval ataṉi nōkkiyirukkum eṉka. paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ - mōkūrilirunta oru kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ; pāṇṭiyaṉ paṭit talivaṉ kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉik kūṭaṟkaṇṇē amaril cāyttup puraviyoṭu kaḷiṟu pala vavvi ūrkoḷa eṉka. ip paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉik kūṭaliyāṇṭa pēraracaṉākak karutu vārum uḷar. (irākaviyaṅkār `tamiḻ varalāṟu' pakkam (51) kāṇka.) kuṟuntoṭi maṭanti uvantaṉaḷ, yām atu pēṇiṉṟu ilam, aṅṅaṉamākavum, yām vēṟu yāriyum aṟiyēm eṉa nī kūṟutal peritum naki viḷivikkiṉṟatu eṉat tōḻi talivaṉi mikak kaḻaṟit taliviyiṉ ūṭal nīṅkumāṟu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `peṟaṟkarum perum poruḷ muṭintapiṉ vanta' 1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `piḻittuvan tirunta kiḻavaṉi neruṅki, iḻittāṅ kākkik koṭuttaṟ kaṇṇum' eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ, `parattiyar maṉikkaṇ taṅki vantu akaṉakar pukutātu puṟattirunta talivaṉi mikak kaḻaṟic cila moḻikaḷik kūṟi, itaṉāṉē talivi maṉattiṉkaṇ ūṭal nīṅkum taṉmi uḷatākkik kūṭṭum' eṉṟurittu ataṟku ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭiṉar nac.
(u-ṟi) kokkiṉ cēval kayal kuṟittup pārval irukkum eṉṟatu talivaṉ parattiyari nōkkiyiruppaṉ eṉṟapaṭi.
347. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		tōḷun tolkaviṉ toliya nāḷum
		nalaṅkavar pacali nalkiṉṟu naliyac
		cālperun tāṉic cēra lātaṉ
		mālkaṭal ōṭṭik kaṭampaṟut tiyaṟṟiya
	5.	paṇṇami muraciṉ kaṇṇatirn taṉṉa
		kavvi tūṟṟum vevvāyc cēri
		ampal mūtūr alarnamak koḻiyac
		ceṉṟaṉa rāyiṉuñ ceyviṉi avarkkē
		vāykkatil vāḻi tōḻi vāyātu
	10.	maḻikaran toḷitta kaḻitiraṅ kaṭukkattu
		oṇkēḻ vayappuli pāynteṉak kuvavaṭi
		veṇkōṭ ṭiyāṉi muḻakkici verīik
		kaṉṟoḻit tōṭiya puṉṟali maṭappiṭi
		kitali vitta miyal vituppoṭu
	15.	keṭumakap peṇṭiriṟ ṟērum
		neṭumara maruṅkiṉ maliyiṟan tōrē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9-16. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, vāyātu maḻi karantu oḷitta kaḻi tiraṅku aṭukkattu - maḻi peyyātu maṟintu oḷittamiyāl mūṅkilkaḷ vāṭiya pakka maliyil, oḷkēḻ vaya puli pāynteṉa - oḷḷiya niṟamuṭiya valiya puli pāyntatāka, kuvavu aṭi veṇ kōṭṭu yāṉi muḻakku ici verīi - tiraṇṭa aṭiyiṉiyum veḷḷiya kompiṉiyum uṭiya kaḷiṟu muḻakkiya oliyiṉik kēṭṭu añci, kaṉṟu oḻittu ōṭiya pul tali maṭa piṭi - taṉ kaṉṟiṉi viṭṭu ōṭiya pulleṉṟa taliyiṉiyuṭiya iḷiya piṭiyāṉi, ki tali vitta miyal vituppoṭu - kiyit talimītu vittukkoṇṭa mayakkam taṅkiya virivuṭaṉ, keṭu maka peṇṭiriṉ tērum - tam makaviṉik kāṇā toḻinta peṇṭir pōla ak kaṉṟiṉit tēṭit tiriyum, neṭu mara maruṅkiṉ mali iṟantōr - nīṇṭa maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya pakkattiṉiyuṭiya maliyik kaṭantu ceṉṟavarāya nam talivar,
1-2. tōḷum tol kaviṉ toliya - tōḷkaḷ paḻiya aḻaku keṭavum, nāḷum nalam kavar pacali nalku iṉṟu naliya - ovvoru nāḷum aḻakiṉik kavarntukoḷḷum pacaliyāṉatu aruḷutaliṉṟi varuttavum,
3-8. cāl peru tāṉic cēralātaṉ - niṟinta periya cēṉiyiṉiyuṭiya neṭuñcēralātaṉ eṉpāṉ, māl kaṭal ōṭṭik kaṭampu aṟuttu iyaṟṟiya - periya kaṭaliṉkaṇ pakivar
kaḷi ōṭṭi avartam kāvaṉ maramāya kaṭampiṉi aṟuttuc ceyta, paṇ ami muraciṉ kaṇ atirntaṉṉa - paṇṇutal aminta muraciṉatu kaṇ muḻaṅkiṉāṟpōṉṟa, mūtūr kuvi tūṟṟum vevvāyc cēri ampal alar - mūtūriṉkaṇ paḻiyiṉit tūṟṟum koṭiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya cērip peṇṭiruṭiya ampalum alarum, namakku oḻiya - nammiṭattē taṅkiyirukkavum, ceṉṟaṉar āyiṉum - nammip pirintu ceṉṟār āyiṉum,
8-9. avarkkuc ceyviṉi vāykka - avarkkuc ceyyum viṉi ki kūṭuvatāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! neṭu mara maruṅkiṉ maliyiṟantōr, tōḷ kaviṉ toliya, pacali naliya, ampal alar namakku oḻiyac ceṉṟaṉarāyiṉum, avarkkuc ceyviṉi vāykka til.
(vi-ri) tōḷum eṉpatiluḷḷa ummiyit toliyavum eṉa māṟiyum. naliya, oḻiya eṉpuḻi ummi virittum urikka. cēralātaṉ imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ. avaṉ mēlkaṭaṟkaṇṇē pakivari ōṭṭi avaratu kaṭampiṉit taṭintu muraciyaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu patiṟṟup pattiṉ iraṇṭām pattāl aṟiyappaṭum. innūluḷḷē. `valampaṭu muraciṟ cēralātaṉ, munnī rōṭṭik kaṭampaṟuttu'1 eṉa muṉ vantiruttalum kāṇka. kavvi tūṟṟutalē iyalpākavuṭiya vevvāy eṉka. ampalum alarum eṉka. ampalākiya alar eṉṟumām. vāykka til; til viḻiviṉkaṇ vantatu.
348. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ 2ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi colleṭuppat 
talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		eṉṉā vatukol tāṉē muṉṟil
		tēṉtēr cuviya tiraḷari māattuk
		kōṭik kūḻtta kamaḻnaṟun tīṅkaṉi
		payirppuṟu palaviṉ etirccuḷi yaḷii
	5.	iṟāloṭu kalanta vaṇṭumū cariyal
		neṭuṅkaṇ āṭamip paḻunik kaṭuntiṟaṟ
		pāppukkaṭup paṉṉa tōppi vāṉkōṭṭuk
		kaṭavuḷ ōṅkuvarik kōkkik kuṟuvar
		muṟittaḻi makaḷir maṭuppa mānti
	10.	aṭukkal ēṉal irumpuṉam maṟantuḻi
		yāṉi vavviṉa 1tiṉiyeṉa nōṉātu
		iḷiyarum mutiyaruṅ kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic
		ciliyāyntu tiritarum nāṭaṉ
		niliyā naṉmoḻi tēṟiya neñcē.
- maturi iḷampālāciriyaṉ cēntaṉ kūttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-10. muṉṟil - muṉṟiliṉkaṇṇuḷḷa, tēṉ tēr cuviya - tēṉ eṉattakum cuviyiṉavākiya, tiraḷ ari māttuk kōṭikku ūḻtta kamaḻ naṟu tīm kaṉi - tiraṇṭa aṭiyiṉiyu ṭiya māmarattiṉ kōṭikkālattē mutirnta naṉ maṇaṅ kamaḻum iṉiya kaṉikaḷuṭaṉ, payirppu uṟu palaviṉ etir cuḷi aḷii - piciṉiyuṟṟa palaviṉatu oḷi poruntiya cuḷikaḷiyiṭṭu, iṟāloṭu kalanta - tēṉuṭaṉ kūṭṭiyākkiya, vaṇṭu mūcu ariyal - vaṇṭu moykkum ariyalākiya, neṭu kaṇ āṭu ami paḻuni - aciyum mūṅkiliṉ nīṇṭa kaṇuviṭiyuḷḷa kuḻāyil neṭi tiruntu mutirtaliṉ, kaṭutiṟal pāppu kaṭuppu aṉṉa tōppi - kaṭiya vēkam koṇṭa pāmpiṉatu vekuṭciyiyotta kaḷḷiṉi, vāṉ kōṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ ōṅku varikku ōkki - uyarnta cimiyatti yuṭiya teyvam uṟiyum uyarnta malikkup paṭittup piṉ, muṟit taḻi makaḷir maṭuppa - taḷirālāya taḻiyuṭi yaṇinta makaḷir uṇpikka, kuṟavar mānti - puṉaṅ kākkum kuṟavar ataṉi uṇṭu, aṭukkal ēṉal irumpuṉam maṟantuḻi - pakka maliyiluḷḷa periya tiṉippuṉaṅkāvali maṟantaviṭattu.
11-14. yāṉi tiṉi vavviṉa eṉa - yāṉikaḷ tiṉip puṉattik kavarntuṇṭaṉavāka, nōṉātu - ataṉip poṟātu, iḷiyarum mutiyarum kiḷiuṭaṉ kuḻīi - iḷiyarum mutiyarumāya cuṟṟamelām oruṅkē kūṭi, cili āyntu tiritarum nāṭaṉ - villi ārāyntu koṇṭu tiriyum nāṭṭi yuṭiyōṉākiya nam talivaṉatu, niliyā nalmoḻi tēṟiya neñcē - uṟutiyallāta iṉiya colli meyyeṉat teḷinta neñcamē,
1. eṉ āvatu kol - iṉi namakku eṉṉa tīṅku uṟumō.
(muṭipu) nāṭaṉ niliyā naṉmoḻi tēṟiya neñcē! eṉ āvatu kol. tōppi makaḷir maṭuppak kuṟavar māntip puṉam maṟantuḻi, tiṉi yāṉi vavviṉa eṉa, kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic cili āyntu tiritarum nāṭu eṉka.
(vi-ri) tēṉ tēr cuvi - tēṉō eṉa ārāyum cuvi. etirccuḷi - cuvi mikka cuḷi eṉṟumām. matuviṉ kaḷippu mikutikkup pāmpiṉ vekuḷiyiyum, pāmpu tēḷ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ nañciṉ vēkattiyum uvami kūṟuvar, `pāmpu vekuṇ ṭaṉṉa tēṟal'1 `tēṭkaṭup paṉṉa nāṭpaṭu tēṟal'2 eṉpaṉa kāṇka. ariyalākiya tōppi eṉka. mānti am mayakkattāl maṟantuḻi eṉa viritturikka.
349. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		arampōḻ avvaḷi ceṟinta muṉki
		varintutām piṇitta tolkaviṉ toliya
		evaṉāyn taṉarkol tōḻi ñemaṉṉ
		terikōl aṉṉa ceyirtīr cemmoḻi
	5.	ulinta okkal pāṭunar celiṉē
		uraṉmali uḷḷamoṭu muṉipāḻ āka
		aruṅkuṟum peṟinta peruṅkala veṟukki
		cūḻātu curakkum naṉṉaṉ naṉṉāṭṭu
		ēḻiṟ kuṉṟattuk kavāaṟ 3kēḻkoḷat
	10.	tiruntari nivanta karuṅkāl vēṅki
		erimaruḷ kavaḷam māntik kaḷiṟutaṉ
		varinutal vitta valitēmpu taṭakki
		kallūr pāmpiṟ ṟōṉṟuñ
		colpeyar tēetta curaṉiṟan tōrē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 3-14. tōḻi-, ñemaṉṉ terikōl aṉṉa - poruḷiṉ aḷavi aṟiyum karuviyāya tulākkōliyotta, ceyirtīr cemmoḻi - kuṟṟamaṟṟa meymmoḻiyiṉiyuṭiya ṉākiya, ulinta okkal pāṭunar celiṉē - vaṟumiyāl aḻinta cuṟṟattuṭaṉ pāṇar mutaliyōr celliṉ, uraṉ mali uḷḷamoṭu - ūkkam mikka neñcattuṭaṉ, muṉipāḻ āka - māṟṟār pōrmuṉi pāḻpaṭa, aru kuṟumpu eṟinta - avaratu ariya araṇit tolittuk koṇṭa, peru kala veṟukki - vili mikka aṇikalaṉ mutalāya celvaṅkaḷi, cūḻātu curakkum - appāṭunar takuti mutaliya ārāyātu mika aḷikkum, naṉṉaṉ nal nāṭṭu - naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉatu nalla nāṭṭiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa, ēḻil kuṉṟattuk kavāaṉ - ēḻil eṉṉum maliccāralil, kēḻkoḷa - naṉṉiṟam porunta, tiruntu ari nivanta - cevvitāya aṭimaram uyarnta, karuṅkāl vēṅki - kariya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya vēṅki marattiṉ, eri maruḷ kavaḷam mānti - tīyi yotta pūkkaḷāya uṇaviṉi uṇṭu, kaḷiṟu - āṇ yāṉi, taṉ varinutal vitta vali tēmpu taṭa ki - taṉatu varikaḷ taṅkiya neṟṟiyil vitta valimi kuṉṟiya periya ki, kal ūr pāmpil tōṉṟum - kuṉṟattē ēṟum pāmpu pōlat tōṉṟum, col peyar tēetta curaṉ iṟantōr - moḻi vēṟupaṭṭa tēyattiṭattavākiya curaneṟiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟōr,
1-3. aram pōḻ a vaḷi ceṟinta muṉ ki - arattāṟ piḷakkappeṟṟa aḻakiya vaḷikaḷ ceṟinta muṉ kiyiṉi, tām varintu piṇitta - tām varintukoṇṭu paṟṟiyatālāya, tol kaviṉ toliya - paḻiya aḻakukeṭa, evaṉ āyntaṉar - eṉṉa poruḷik karutiṉarō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! curaṉ iṟantōr, tol kaviṉ toliya evaṉ āyntaṉar?
(vi-ri) piṇitta - paṟṟiya. muṉ kiyip paṟṟi varinta eṉa māṟuka. varinta tolkaviṉ - maṇantamiyāl aññāṉṟu eytiya aḻaku. kippaṟṟi variyum vaḻakkiṉi, `nēriṟi muṉki paṟṟi numartara, nāṭaṟi naṉmaṇam ayarkam'1 eṉpataṉāl aṟika. ñemaṉkōl eṉak kūṭṭuka. terital - poruḷkaḷi ārāyntu aṟital. cemmoḻiyiyuṭiya naṉṉaṉ eṉavum, cūḻātu curakkum naṉṉaṉ eṉavum taṉittaṉi iyiyum. kaḷiṟu curattil, vēṟu takka kavaḷam kiṭikkap peṟāmiyāl, vēṅkip pūvi uṇṭu paci taṇiyātu vali kuṉṟiyirukkum eṉka.
350. neytal
(pakaṟkuṟi vantu nīṅkun talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		kaḻiyē, ciṟukura ṉeytaloṭu kāvi kūmpa
		eṟitiri yōtan taralā ṉātē
		tuṟiyē, maruṅkiṟ pōkiya mākkavi maruppiṉ
		iruñcēṟ ṟīraḷi alavaṉ nivappa
	5.	vaḻaṅkunar iṉmiyiṟ pāṭāṉ ṟaṉṟē
		koṭunukam nuḻinta kaṇikkāl attiri
		vaṭimaṇi neṭuntēr pūṇa ēvātu
		ēnteḻiṉ maḻikkaṇ ivaḷkuṟi yākac
		cērntaṉi ceṉmō perunīrc cērppa
	10.	ilaṅkirum parappiṉ eṟicuṟā nīkki
		valampuri mūḻkiya vāṉṟimiṟ paratavar
		olitalip paṇilam ārppak kalleṉak
		kalikeḻu koṟki etirkoḷa iḻitaruṅ
		kuvavumaṇal neṭuṅkōṭ ṭāṅkaṇ
	15.	uvakkāṇ tōṉṟumeñ ciṟunal lūrē.
- cēntaṉ kaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. peru nīrc cērppa - periya kaṭaṟkariyiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
1-5. kaḻiyē ciṟu kural neytaloṭu kāvi kūmpa - kaḻiyiṭattē ciṟiya pūṅkottukkaḷiyuṭiya neytaṟ pūvum karuṅkuvaḷip pūvum kuviya, ōtam eṟi tiri taral āṉātu - kaṭal vīcum tiriyit tarutal amiyātu, tuṟiyē - tuṟi, maruṅkil pōkiya mā kavi maruppiṉ - pakkattē nīṇṭa periya kavartta kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya, iru cēṟṟu īr aḷi alavaṉ - kariya cēṟṟiṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa īramuṭiya vaḷiyilirukkum ñeṇṭu, nivappa - mēlē veḷippaṭa, vaḻaṅkunar iṉmiyiṉ pāṭu āṉṟaṉṟu - celvōr illāmiyāl oli aṭaṅkiyatu,
10-15. ilaṅku iru parappiṉ - viḷaṅkum periya kaṭaṟ parappil, eṟi cuṟā nīkki - eṟiyum cuṟā mīṉi otukki, valampuri mūḻkiya - valampuriyiṉ muttiṉi mūḻkiyeṭutta, vāṉ timil paratavar - periya paṭakiṉiyuṭiya paratavar, oli talip paṇilam kal eṉa ārppa - oliyit taṉṉiṭattēyuṭiya caṅkukaḷ kalleṉa ārkkumpaṭi, kalikeḻu koṟki etirkoḷa - āravāra mikka koṟkiyōr etirkoḷḷumāṟu, iḻitarum - paṭakiṉiṉṟu iṟaṅkum, kuvavu maṇal neṭu kōṭṭu āṅkaṇ - tiraṇṭa maṇali yuṭiya nīṇṭa kariyāya avviṭattē, em ciṟu nal ūr uvak kāṇ tōṉṟum - ematu ciṟiya nalla ūr uṅkē tōṉṟāniṟkum, kāṇpāyāka;
6-9. koṭu nukam nuḻinta kaṇi kāl attiri - vaḷinta nukattiṟ pūṭṭap pukka tiraṇṭa kāliṉiyuṭiya kōvēṟu kaḻutiyi, vaṭi maṇi neṭu tēr pūṇa ēvātu - vaṭitta maṇiyiṉiyuṭiya nīṇṭa tēriṉiṟ pūṇumpaṭi niṉ pākaṟku āṇi ceyyātu, ēntu eḻil maḻi kaṇ ivaḷ kuṟiyāka - mikka aḻaku poruntiya kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya ivaḷ kāriyamākac, cērntaṉi ceṉmō - iṉṟiravu ivvūril taṅkic celvāyāka.
(muṭipu) cērppa! kaḻiyē tiri ōtam taral āṉātu, tuṟiyē vaḻaṅkunar iṉmiyiṉ pāṭāṉṟaṉṟu, em ciṟu nal ūr uvakkāṇ tōṉṟum. attiri neṭuntēr pūṇa ēvātu ivaḷ kuṟiyākac cērntaṉi ceṉmō.
(vi-ri) kaḻiyilē tiri ōtam taral āṉātu, tuṟi pāṭāṉṟaṉṟu eṉpaṉa, pakaṟpoḻutu kaḻintamiyi uṇarttuva āyiṉa. neytal nilattup pōtarum talivaṉ attiri ūrntu varutal uṇṭeṉpatu, `kaḻiccuṟā eṟinta puṭṭāḷ attiri, neṭunīr iruṅkaḻip parimelin tacii'1 eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. īṇṭu, attiri pūṇṭa tēr ūrtal kūṟappaṭṭatu. attiri tēr pūṇap pākaṉi ēvātu eṉa viritturikka. kuṟi - muṭikkappaṭum kāriyam. valampuri mūḻkiya - valampuric caṅkiṉi mūḻki eṭutta eṉṟumām. paratavar ārppa iḻitarum neṭuṅkōṭ ṭāṅkaṇ ūr tōṉṟum eṉka.
351. pāli
(poruṇmuṟṟi maṟuttarāniṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		vēṟṟunāṭ ṭuṟiyuḷ viruppuṟap pēṇip
		peṟalaruṅ kēḷir piṉvantu viṭuppap
		poruḷakap paṭutta pukaṉmali neñcamoṭu
		kuṟiviṉi muṭitta niṟiviṉ iyakkam
	5.	aṟivuṟūuṅ kollō tāṉē katirteṟak
		kaḻalili yukutta kālporu tāḻciṉi
		aḻalakin taṉṉa aṅkuḻip potumpiṟ
		puḻalvī yiruppip puṉkāṭ ṭattam
		maṟutara luḷḷamoṭu kuṟukat tōṟṟiya
	10.	ceykuṟi āḻi vikaṟō ṟeṇṇi
		eḻutucuvar 2naṉinta aḻutuvār maḻikkaṇ
		vilaṅkivīḻ arippaṉi polaṅkuḻit teṟippat
		tiruntiḻi muṉki aṇalacit tūṉṟi
		iruntaṇi mītu 3poruntuḻik kiṭakki
	15.	varuntutōṭ pūcal kaḷiyu marunteṉa
		uḷḷutoṟu paṭūum palli
		puḷḷuttoḻu tuṟivi cevimuta lāṉē.
- poruntil iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē! 1-4. vēṟṟunāṭṭu uṟiyuḷ viruppu uṟa pēṇi - vēṟu nāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu taṅkutali viruppam mikak kuṟikkoṇṭu, peṟal arum kēḷir piṉvantu viṭuppa- peṟutaṟ kariya cuṟṟattār piṉ vantu viṭitarap pōntu, poruḷ akap paṭutta pukalmali neñcamoṭu - poruḷi īṭṭiya viruppumikka neñciṉuṭaṉ, kuṟiviṉi muṭitta niṟivuiṉ iyakkam - muṭikkappaṭum viṉiyic ceytu muṭittamiyāl ākiya niṟivuṭaṉ kūṭiya iṉiya celaviṉi,
5-9. katir teṟa - ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir vetuppa, kaḻalili ukutta kālporu tāḻciṉi - kaḻaṉṟa ilikaḷi utirtta kāṟṟu alikkum tāḻnta kiḷikaḷil, aḻal akintaṉṉa am kuḻi potumpil - tī kappu viṭṭālotta aḻakiya taḷirkaḷiyuṭiya marac ceṟivil, puḻal vī iruppi pul kāṭṭu attam - uṭṭuḷi poruntiya pūkkaḷiyuṭiya polivaṟṟa iruppi maraṅkaḷi yuṭiya kāṭṭu neṟiyil, maṟutaral uḷḷamoṭu kuṟuka - yāṉ mīḷum eṇṇamuṭiyēṉāki vantu koṇṭirukkavum,
9-17. tōṟṟiya ceykuṟi āḻi - cuḻitta ceykuṟiyāya vaṭṭatti, vikaltōṟu eṇṇi - nāḷtōṟum eṇṇi, eḻutu cuvar - avi eḻutappaṭṭa cuvar, naṉinta - naṉitaṟku ētuvāya, aḻutu vār maḻikkaṇ vilaṅki vīḻ arip paṉi - aḻutalāl nīṇṭa kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiliruntu vilaki viḻum aitākiya nīrāṉatu, polam kuḻi teṟippa - kātilaṇinta poṟ kuḻiyiṟ ṟeṟittuviḻa, tiruntu iḻi muṉki aṇal acittu ūṉṟi - tiruntiya aṇi
kaḷiyuṭiya muṉkiyik kavuḷil poruntavittu, aṇimītu iruntu - aṇiyiṉmītu iruntu, kiṭakki poruntuḻi - piṉ paṭukkiyiṟ kiṭattal poruntiya viṭattu, varuntu tōḷ pūcal kaḷiyum maruntu eṉa - varuntum tōḷiṉ varuttattip pōkkum maruntu itueṉa, uḷḷutoṟu paṭūum palli - niṉikkuntōṟum olikkum palliyāṉatu, puḷḷut toḻutu uṟivi - nimittattip paraviyirukkum taliviyiṉ, cevimutalāṉa - kātiṉ akattē,
5. aṟivuṟūum kollō - aṟivuṟuttumō.
(muṭipu) neñcē! kuṟiviṉi muṭitta niṟivu iṉ iyakkattiṉi, puḷḷut toḻutu uṟivi cevimutalāṉē, palli, pūcal kaḷiyum marunteṉa aṟivu ṟūuṅ kollō.
(vi-ri) kēḷir - tōḻi mutalāyiṉār. akappaṭutta, muṭitta eṉṉum peyareccaṅkaḷ kāraṇap poruḷaṉa. kuṟi - muṭikkap paṭum poruḷ. itaṉip `payakkuṟi yillit tām vāḻum nāḷē'1 eṉpataṉ uriyāl aṟika. niṟivu - uḷḷa niṟivu. aṟivuṟū
uṅkol - aṟivuṟuttuṅkol, piṟaviṉi maṟutaral uḷḷamoṭu kuṟukat tōṟṟiya - mīḷum uḷḷattuṭaṉ kuṟukutalit terivittaṟku eṉṟumām; itaṟkut tōṟṟiya eṉpatu ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeccamākum. aṇimītu iruntu kiṭakki poruntuḻi eṉa māṟuka. uḷḷutoṟu paṭūum palli eṉṟatu, palliyiṉ iyaṟki kūṟiyapaṭi. puḷḷut toḻutu uṟivi, cuṭṭu; avaḷ eṉṟapaṭi.
352. kuṟiñci
(varintu eytiya piṉṟi maṇamaṉikkaṭ ceṉṟa tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu. varivu malintu colliya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatūumām.)
		1muṭavumutir palaviṉ kuṭamaruḷ perumpaḻam
		palkiḷit talivaṉ kallāk kaṭuvaṉ
		pāṭimiḻ aruvip pāṟi maruṅkiṉ
		āṭumayil muṉṉa tākak kōṭiyar
	5.	viḻavukoḷ mūtūr viṟali piṉṟi
		muḻavaṉ pōla akappaṭat taḻīi
		iṉṟuṇip payiruṅ kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ
		kuṭinaṉku uṭiyaṉ kūṭunarp piriyalaṉ
		keṭunā moḻiyalaṉ aṉpiṉaṉ eṉanī
	10.	valla kūṟi vāyvatiṟ puṇarttōy
		nalli kāṇil kātalan 2tōḻi
		kaṭumparip puravi neṭuntēr añci
		nallici niṟutta nayavaru paṉuval
		tollici niṟīiya uricāl pāṇmakaṉ
	15.	eṇṇumuṟi niṟutta paṇṇi ṉuḷḷum
		putuvatu puṉinta tiṟattiṉum
		vatuvi nāḷiṉum iṉiyaṉāl emakkē.
- 3añciyatti makaḷ nākiyār.
(co-ḷ) 11. kātal am tōḻi - kātal mikka aḻakiya tōḻiyē, kāṇil nalli - ārāyumiṭattu nī mikavum naṉmiyiyuṭiyi;
1-7. muṭavu mutir palaviṉ kuṭam maruḷ peru paḻam - vaḷital mikka palāmarattiṉ kuṭam pōṉṟa periya paḻattiṉi, pal kiḷit talivaṉ kallāk kaṭuvaṉ - pala cuṟṟattiṟkut talivaṉākiya kallāta āṇkuraṅku, pāṭu imiḻ aruvi pāṟi maruṅkiṉ - mika olikkum aruviyiyuṭiya kaṟpāṟi yiṭattē, āṭum mayil muṉṉatu āka- āṭukiṉṟa mayil oṉṟu taṉakku muṉṉē niṟka, kōṭiyar viḻavu koḷ mūtūr viṟali piṉṟi - kūttar viḻāviṉik koṇṭāṭum mutiya ūril viṟaliyiṉ piṉpu niṟkum, muḻavaṉ pōla akappaṭat taḻīi - muḻavu iyampuvōṉ pōlat taṉṉakattē poruntat taḻuvikkoṇṭu, iṉ tuṇi payirum kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ - iṉiya tuṇiyāya peṇ kuraṅkiṉi aḻikkum malināṭṭiyuṭiya nam talivaṉ,
8-10. kuṭi naṉku uṭiyaṉ - uyar kuṭippiṟappu uṭiyavaṉ, kūṭunar piriyalaṉ - taṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭukiṉṟavarkaḷip piriyāṉ, nā keṭu moḻiyalaṉ -nāvāl keṭumoḻikaḷik kūṟuvāṉallaṉ, aṉpiṉaṉ - aṉpu uṭiyavaṉ, eṉa nī valla kūṟi vāyvatiṟ puṇarttōy - eṉṟu nī avaṉ ciṟappukkaḷik kūṟip porutta muṟak kūṭṭivittaṉi, aṅṅaṉamē,
12-17. kaṭum pari puravi neṭu tēr añci - kaṭiya vēkattiṉi yuṭiya kutirikaḷ pūṇṭa nīṇṭa tēriṉiyuṭiya atiyamāṉañci yiṉ, tol ici niṟīiya uricāl pāṇ makaṉ - paḻiya pukaḻi nilipeṟac ceyta pukaḻcāṉṟa pāṇ makaṉāṉavaṉ, nal ici niṟutta nayavaru paṉuval - nalla icikaḷi variyaṟi ceyta iṉimi mikka nūliṉ, eṇṇu muṟi niṟutta - eṇṇiṉ muṟippaṭi iyaṟṟiya, paṇṇiṉuḷḷum - paṇṇik kāṭṭiṉum, putuvatu puṉinta tiṟattiṉum - avaṉ putumiyāka iyaṟṟiya tiṟattik kāṭṭiṉum, vatuvi nāḷiṉum - vatuvi ceyta nāḷiṉum, emakku iṉiyaṉ - namakku iṉiyaṉāka vuḷḷāṉ.
(muṭipu) kātalam tōḻi! kuṉṟanāṭaṉ kuṭinaṉku uṭiyaṉ, kūṭunarp piriyalaṉ, keṭunā moḻiyalaṉ, aṉpiṉaṉ eṉanī valla kūṟip puṇarttōy. ākaliṉ, nalli. añciyiṉ pāṇmakaṉ paṇṇiṉuḷḷum tiṟattiṉum vatuvi nāḷiṉum emakku iṉiyaṉ.
(vi-ri) kaṭuvaṉ palaviṉ perum paḻatti muḻavaṉ pōlat taḻuvikkoṇṭu tuṇiyi aḻikkum kuṉṟam eṉka. āṭukiṉṟa mayiliṉ piṉṉē kaṭuvaṉ palāp paḻattit taḻuvi niṟpatu, āṭukiṉṟa viṟaliyiṉ piṉpu muḻaviṉiyuṭiyavaṉ niṟpatu pōlum eṉa uvami koḷka. viṟali - viṟalpaṭa āṭupavaḷ. kuṭippiṟantār iyalpilē pala nalaṅkaḷum uṭiyarāvar ākaliṉ, kuṭinaṉkuṭiyaṉ eṉa ataṉi muṟkūṟiṉāḷ. keṭu moḻi - poymmoḻi. añciyip pāṭi avaṉatu tol iciyi niṟuttiya pāṇmakaṉ eṉka. nayavaru paṉuval eṉṟatu icit tamiḻ nūli. eṇṇumuṟi niṟutta - alaku muṟippaṭi amitta. alaku - curuti. paṇkaḷuḷḷē eḻu narampāṉ iyaṉṟatu paṇ eṉavum, āṟu, aintu, nāṉku eṉak kuṟinta narampukaḷāṉ iyaṉṟatu tiṟam eṉavum paṭum eṉpatu, `niṟi narampiṟṟē paṇṇeṉalākum' `kuṟi narampiṟṟē tiṟameṉappaṭumē' eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. iṉi putuvatu puṉinta tiṟattiṉum eṉṟataṟku, niṉ putumiyuṟak ki ceyta vakiyiṉum eṉṟurittalum ām.
(u-ṟi) palkiḷit talivaṉākiya kaṭuvaṉ palāp paḻattit taḻuvit tuṇiyi aḻikkum kuṉṟanāṭaṉ eṉṟatu, talivaṉ pala cuṟṟattiyum pēṇit, talivipāl mikka aṉpuṭiyaṉāka iruppaṉ eṉpatu uṇarttiyavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `avaṉaṟi vāṟṟa vaṟiyu mākaliṉ'1 eṉṉum talivi kūṟ ṟil, niṟuttaṟkaṇṇum eṉṉum pakutiyil, talivaṉatu paṇpiṉit tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟṟāl tāṉ niṟuttuk kūṟutaṟ kaṇṇum eṉṟurittu, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭiṉar iḷam.
353. pāli
(muṉṉoru kālattup poruḷvayiṟ pirinta talimakaṉ poruṇmuṟṟi vantirunta kālattu mīṇṭum poruḷ kaṭāviṉa neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		āḷviṉip piritalum uṇṭō piriyiṉum
		kēḷiṉi vāḻiya neñcē nāḷum
		kaṉavuk kaḻintaṉiya vāki naṉaviṉ
		nāḷatu celavum mūppiṉatu varavum
	5.	aritupeṟu ciṟappiṟ kāmat tiyaṟkiyum
		innili aṟiyā yāyiṉuñ cennili
		amiyā ṭaṅkaḻi tīṇṭik kalleṉa
		ñemiyili utirtta erivāyk kōṭi
		neṭuveṇ kaḷari nīṟumukantu cuḻalak
	10.	kaṭuveyil tirukiya vēṉil veṅkāṭṭu
		uyaṅkunaṭi maṭappiṇi taḻīiya vayaṅkupoṟi
		aṟukōṭ ṭeḻiṟkali aṟukayam nōkkit
		teṇṇīr vēṭṭa ciṟumiyiṟ ṟaḻimaṟantu
		uṇṇīr iṉmiyiṉ olkuvaṉa taḷara
	15.	maraniḻal aṟṟa iyaviṟ curaṉiṟantu
		uḷḷuvi yalliyō maṟṟē uḷḷiya
		viruntoḻi vaṟiyāp peruntaṇ pantar
		varunti varunar ōmpit taṇṇeṉat
		tātutukaḷ utirtta tāḻiyaṅ kūntal
	20.	vīḻitaḻ alari mellakañ cērtti
		makiḻaṇi muṟuval māṇṭa cēkki
		nammoṭu naṉmoḻi navilum
		pommal ōtip puṉiyiḻi kuṇaṉē.
- maturi aḷakkar ñāḻalār makaṉār 2 maḷḷaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. neñcē-, vāḻiya-, āḷviṉip piritalum uṇṭō - poruḷīṭṭum muyaṟciyāl nām iṉip piritalum kūṭumō, piriyiṉum iṉi kēḷ - aṉṟip pirivatāyiṉum ippoḻutu yāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēḷ;
2-6. nāḷum kaṉavu kaḻintaṉiya vāki - nāṭōṟum kaṉavilē poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟi maṟinta at taṉmiyavāki, naṉaviṉ - naṉaviṉ kaṇṇē, nāḷatu celavum - nāḷiṉ kaḻitalum, mūppiṉatu varavum - mūppiṉ varukiyum, aritu peṟu ciṟappiṉ kāmattu iyaṟkiyum - aritākap peṟum ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya kāmattiṉ taṉmiyum āya ivaṟṟi, i nili aṟiyāy āyiṉum - ippoḻutu aṟintilāyāyiṉum,
6-15. cennili ami āṭu, am kaḻi tīṇṭi - cevviya niliyiṉiyuṭiya mūṅkiliṉ aciyum taṇṭiṉit tākki, kal eṉa ñemi ili utirtta erivāyk kōṭi - kalleṉṉum oliyuṇṭāka ñemi marattiṉ ilikaḷi utirtta veppam poruntiya kōṭik kāṟṟu, neṭu veṇ kaḷari nīṟu mukantu cuḻala- nīṇṭa veḷḷiya kaḷar nilattuḷḷa puḻutiyi mukantukoṇṭu cuḻaṉṟiṭa, kaṭuveyil tirukiya vēṉil vem kāṭṭu - koṭiya veyyil muṟukiya vēṉiṟkālattu vevviya kāṭṭil, uyaṅku naṭi maṭa piṇi taḻīiya - varuntiya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya iḷiya piṇiyi uṭaṉkoṇṭa, vayaṅku poṟi aṟu kōṭṭu eḻil kali- viḷaṅkum puḷḷikaḷiyum aṟalpaṭṭa kompiṉiyum uṭiya aḻakiya kalimāṉ, teḷ nīr vēṭṭa ciṟumiyiṉ - teḷinta nīr vēṭṭa tuṉpattāl, taḻi maṟantu - taḻi uṇṭalik kiviṭṭu, aṟu kayam nōkki - nīr aṟṟa kuḷattiṉi nōkkic ceṉṟu, uṇ nīr iṉmiyiṉ olkuvaṉa taḷara - āṅku uṇṇum nīr iṉmiyiṉ melintu taḷara, maram niḻal aṟṟa iyaviṉ curaṉ iṟantu - maraṅkaḷ niḻal aṟṟaṉavāyuḷa neṟiyiṉiyuṭiya pāliyik kaṭantu,
16-23. uḷḷiya viruntu oḻivu aṟiyā peru taṇ pantar - virumpi varum viruntiṉar oḻital aṟiyāta periya kuḷirnta pantariṉkaṇ, varunti varunar ōmpi - vaṟumiyāl varunti varuvōrip pātukāttu, taṇ eṉa - kuḷircciyāka, tāḻi - tāḻiyiṉ, tātu tukaḷ utirtta - tātākiya poṭiyi appiya, kūntal - kūntaliṉ, mel akam - melliya piṉṉalil, vīḻ itaḻ alari cērtti - virumpappaṭum pūviṉ itaḻi vittup piṉṉi, makiḻ aṇi muṟuval - makiḻcciyiṉi aṇipeṟak koṇṭa puṉṉakiyuṭaṉ, māṇṭa cēkki - māṇpuṟṟa paḷḷiyiṉkaṇ ṇiruntu, nammoṭu naṉmoḻi navilum - nammuṭaṉ nalla moḻikaḷik kūṟum, pommal ōti puṉi iḻi kuṇaṉ - polivu peṟṟa kūntaliyum aḻakiya aṇikaḷiyum uṭiya nam taliviyiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷi,
16. uḷḷuvi alliyō - niṉittu mayaṅkuvāy alliyō.
(muṭipu) neñcē! vāḻiya! piritalum uṇṭō? nāḷatu celavum mūppiṉatu varavum kāmattiyaṟkiyum innili aṟiyāyāyiṉum, curaṉ iṟantu puṉiyiḻi kuṇaṉ uḷḷuvi alliyō.
(vi-ri) kaṉavilē poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟik kaḻitalāvatu tōṟṟa māttiriyāṉē maṟital. atupōl nāṭkaḷ virintu celvaṉa eṉṟāṉ. nāḷatu celavum mūppiṉatu varavum kāmattu iyaṟkiyum aṟiyāy eṉṟatu. `nāḷatu ciṉmiyum iḷamiya tarumiyum ...... aṉpiṉa takalamum'1 oṉṟāmi kūṟip poruḷvayiṟ cellutali vilakkiyavāṟu. `iḷamiyuṅ kāmamum niṉpāṇi nillā'2 eṉavum, `iḷamiyum kāmamum ōrāṅkup peṟṟār, vaḷami viḻitakka tuṇṭō'3 eṉavum pōntaṉa īṇṭu oppu nōkkaṟ pālaṉa. kōṭi cuḻala, kali olkuvaṉa taḷara, mara niḻalaṟṟa iyaviṟ curaṉ eṉka. viruntōmputalum vaṟiyārkku ītalum mutalāya illaṟam puritalākiya taliviyiṉ ceykiyum, kūntal alari cērtti muṟuval māṇṭa avaḷatu mēṉi vaṉappum cēkkiyil nammoṭu naṉmoḻi navilutalākiya moḻiyiṉ iṉimiyum pēriṉpam viḷippaṉa
vākaliṉ, tuṉpam tarum koṭiya kāṭṭiṭic celvuḻi, av viṉpatti niṉittu mīḷvi eṉpāṉ `curaṉ iṟantu uḷḷuvi alliyō...... puṉiyiḻi kuṇaṉē' eṉṟāṉ eṉka.
354. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṟku uḻiyar colliyatu.)
		matavali yāṉi maṟaliya pācaṟi
		4iṭiyumiḻ muracam porukaḷat tiyampa
		veṉṟukoṭi yeṭuttaṉaṉ vēntaṉum kaṉṟoṭu
		5kaṟavip palliṉam puṟavuto ṟukaḷak
	5.	kuḻalvāy vittaṉar kōvalar valvirintu
		iḷiyar ēkuvaṉar pariya viriyuḷik
		kaṭunaṭip puravi vaḻivāy ōṭa
		valavaṉ vaḷpuvali yuṟuppap pulavar
		pukaḻkuṟi koṇṭa polantār akalattut
	10.	taṇkamaḻ cāntam nuṇṭukaḷ aṇiya
		veṉṟiko ḷuvakiyoṭu pukutal vēṇṭiṉ
		yāṇṭuṟi vatukol tāṉē māṇṭa
		pōtuṟaḻ koṇṭa uṇkaṇ
		tīti lāṭṭi tirunutaṟ pacappē.
- maturit tamiḻk kūttaṉ kaṭuvaṉ maḷḷaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. taliva! matavali yāṉi maṟaliya pācaṟi - mikka valiyiyuṭiya yāṉi pōr vēṭṭu māṟupaṭum pācaṟik kaṇṇē, iṭi umiḻ muracam poru kaḷattu iyampa - iṭiyeṉa olikkum veṉṟi muracam pōrkkaḷattē olikka, vēntaṉum veṉṟu koṭi eṭuttaṉaṉ - aracaṉum pōr veṉṟu koṭiyi uyarttiṉaṉ, pal kaṟavi iṉam kaṉṟoṭu puṟavu toṟu ukaḷa - palavākiya kaṟakkum āṉiṉaṅkaḷ kaṉṟoṭu kāṭutōṟum tāvi vara, kōvalar vāy kuḻal vittaṉar - āyarkaḷ tam vāyiṟ kuḻal vittu ūtuvārāyiṉar;
5-11. iḷiyar val virintu ēkuvaṉar pariya - niṉ ēvalar mika virintu ēkuvārāy muṉcella, viri uḷi kaṭu naṭi puravi vaḻivāy ōṭa - virinta piṭari mayiriṉiyum kaṭiya naṭiyiṉiyum uṭiya kutiri neṟiyiṉkaṇṇē ōṭumāṟu, valavaṉ vaḷpu vali uṟuppa -pākaṉ kaṭivāḷattiṉi valintu piṭittuc celutta, pulavar pukaḻ kuṟi koṇṭa - pulavar pukaḻum paṭiyiṉ kuṟiyāya viḻuppuṇ koṇṭa, polam tār akalattu - aḻakiya māliyiṉit taritta mārpil, taṇ kamaḻ cāntam nuṇ tukaḷ aṇiya - taṇṇiya kamaḻum cāntiṉoṭu nuṇṇiya poṭikaḷi aṇiya, veṉṟi koḷ uvakiyoṭu pukutal vēṇṭiṉ - veṟṟi koṇṭa uvakiyuṭaṉ niṉ maṉiyiṟ pukutali nī virumpiṉamiyāṉ,
12-14. māṇṭa pōtu uṟaḻ koṇṭa uṇkaṇ - māṇpuṟṟa nīla malaroṭu māṟupaṭṭa miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya, tītilāṭṭi- tītaṟṟavaḷāya taliviyiṉ, tiru nutal pacappu - aḻakiya neṟṟiyiṟ paṭarnta pacali, yāṇṭu uṟivatukol - iṉi eṅkut taṅkum?
(muṭipu) taliva! vēntaṉum veṉṟu koṭi eṭuttaṉaṉ. kōvalar kuḻal vāyvittaṉar. veṉṟikoḷ uvakiyoṭu pukutal vēṇṭiṉ, tītilāṭṭi tirunutaṟ pacappu yāṇṭu uṟivatukol!
(vi-ri) mata - valiyeṉap poruḷpaṭum uriccol. matavali- oruporu ḷirucol, mikka valiyuṭiya eṉṟapaṭi. muracam iyampa veṉṟu, pācaṟikkaṇ koṭi eṭuttaṉaṉ eṉka. vēntaṉum eṭuttaṉaṉ eṉṟamiyāl, talimakaṉ vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻip pirintāṉāyiṟṟu. kuḻal vāy vittaṉar kōvalar eṉṟatu mīṇṭu ēkaṟkuriya mālip poḻutu vantamiyik kuṟittapaṭi. puravi ōṭumāṟu vaḷpu valiyuṟuppa eṉavum, iḷiyar ēkuvaṉar pariyavum valavaṉ vaḷpu valiyuṟuppavum cāntam nuṇtukaḷ aṇiyavum pukutal vēṇṭiṉ eṉavum kūṭṭi muṭikka. pulavar pukaḻ kuṟi eṉṟatu mārpiṟ paṭṭa viḻuppuṇ. cāntam nuṇ tukaḷ aṇiya eṉṟatu etirvu paṟṟiyatu. vēṇṭiṉ - vēṇṭiṉiyākaliṉ.
355. pāli
(pirivuṇarttiya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		māvum vaṇṭaḷir īṉṟaṉa kuyilum
		iṉṟīm palkuraṟ kompar nuvalum
		mūtili yoḻitta pōtaviḻ peruñciṉi
		vallōṉ tivarum vaḷḷuyirp pāli
	5.	narampārt taṉṉa vaṇṭiṉam muralun
		tuṇikayan tuṉṉiya tūmaṇal ekkart
		tātuku taṇpoḻil alkik kātalar
		ceḻumaṉi maṟakkuñ cevvi vēṉil
		tāṉē vantaṉṟu āyiṉ āṉātu
	10.	ilaṅkuvaḷi 1nekiḻnta evvaṅ kāṭṭip
		pulantaṉam varukam  2ceṉmō tōḻi
		yāmē emiyam āka nīyē
		poṉṉayan taruḷili yāki
		iṉṉi yākutal ottaṉṟāl eṉavē.
-3taṅkāṟ poṟ kollaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi-,
1-9. māvum vaṇ taḷir īṉṟaṉa - māmaraṅkaḷum aḻakiya taḷirkaḷi īṉṟaṉa, kuyilum kompar iṉ tīm palkural nuvalum - kuyilum avaṟṟiṉ kompukaḷiliruntu mika iṉiya kuralāl pala kālum kūvāniṟkum, mutu ili oḻitta pōtu aviḻ peru ciṉi- mutirnta ilikaḷi utirtta malarkaḷ virinta periya kiḷikaḷil, vallōṉ tivarum narampu vaḷ uyir pāli ārttaṉṉa - yāḻ vallōṉ taṭavum narampu iṉiya icikoṇṭa pālip paṇṇi olittā lotta iciyuṭaṉ, vaṇṭu iṉam muralum - vaṇṭiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ olikkum, tuṇi kayam tuṉṉiya - teḷinta nīr niliyi aṭutta, tātu uku taṇ poḻil - pūntukaḷ utirum kuḷirnta cōliyil uḷḷa, tū maṇal ekkar - tūya maṇal mēṭṭil, alki- taṅki, kātalar ceḻu maṉi maṟakkum - kātalarāyiṉār tamatu vaḷaviya maṉiyiṉi maṟantirukkum, cevvi vēṉil vantaṉṟu - iḷavēṉiṟ kālam vantuḷatu, āyiṉ - ātalāṉ, (nām talivaṉ pāṟ ceṉṟu),
9-14. ilaṅku vaḷi āṉātu nekiḻnta evvam kāṭṭi - viḷaṅkum vaḷi palakālum nekiḻntu vīḻum tuṉpiṉi nam talivaṟkuk kāṭṭi, yāmē emiyam āka - yām tamiyēm āka, nīyē poṉ nayantu aruḷ iliyāki - nī poruḷi virumpi aruḷ illāy āki, iṉṉi ākutal ottaṉṟu - it taṉmiyi yuṭiyi ātal niṉ takutikku ottatu aṉṟu, eṉa - eṉṟu kūṟi, pulantaṉam varukam - avaṉi veṟuttu varutaṟku, ceṉmō - nām cellakkaṭavēm.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kātalar taṇ poḻil alkic ceḻumaṉi maṟakkum iḷavēṉil vantaṉṟu. ātalāṉ, nām talivaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu nam vaḷi nekiḻnta evvam kāṭṭi, yām emiyam āka nī poṉ nayantu aruḷili yāki, iṉṉiyākutal ottaṉṟu eṉap pulantaṉam varukam ceṉmō.
(vi-ri) peruñciṉi vaṇṭiṉam muralum taṇ poḻil eṉavum tuṇikayam tuṉṉiya taṇpoḻil eṉavum kūṭṭuka. tuṇikayam, viṉittoki; nīr teḷinta kayam eṉka. maṟakkum - maṟattaṟku ētuvākiya. āyiṉ - ātalāl eṉka. nekiḻnta evvam - nekiḻtaṟku ētuvākiya evvam. nekiḻtta evvam eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku, nekiḻac ceyta evvam eṉṟurikka. ceṉmō eṉpatu vikāram.
356. marutam
(piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṟkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi talimakaḷik kuṟi nayappak kūṟiyatu.)
		mēṟṟuṟik koḷīiya kaḻāliṟ kīḻttuṟi
		ukuvār aruntap pakuvāy yāmi
		kampuḷ iyavaṉ āka vicipiṇit
		teṇkaṭ kiṇiyiṟ piṟaḻum ūraṉ
	5.	iṭineṭun teruviṟ katumeṉak kaṇṭeṉ
		poṟṟoṭi muṉki paṟṟiṉaṉ āka
		aṉṉāy eṉṟaṉeṉ avaṉkiviṭ ṭaṉaṉē
		toṉṉaci 1kāvāmi naṉṉaṉ paṟampiṟ
		ciṟukā rōṭaṉ payiṉoṭu cērttiya
	10.	kaṟpōl nāviṉēṉ āki maṟṟatu
		ceppaleṉ maṉṉāl yāykkē naṟṟērk
		kaṭumpakaṭ ṭiyāṉic cōḻar marukaṉ
		neṭuṅkatir nelliṉ vallam kiḻavōṉ
		1nallaṭi yuḷḷā ṉākavum 2oṉṉār
	15.	3katuva muyaṟalum muyalpa atāaṉṟu
		olipal kūntal namvayiṉ aruḷātu
		koṉṟaṉaṉ āyiṉuṅ kolipaḻu taṉṟē
		aruvi yāmpal kalitta muṉṟuṟi
		naṉṉaṉ 4āypaṟam paṉṉa
	20.	miṉṉīr ōti eṉṉiniṉ kuṟippē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 18-20. aruvi āmpal kalitta muṉ tuṟi - aruvi nīr perukumiṭattu āmpal taḻittuḷḷa muṉ tuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya, naṉṉaṉ āy paṟampu aṉṉa - naṉṉaṉatu aḻakiya paṟampu pōṉṟa, miṉ īr ōti - miṉṉukiṉṟa kuḷirnta kūntaliyuṭiyāḷē!
1-4. mēl tuṟi koḷīiya kaḻāliṉ - mēlit tuṟiyil kaḷḷiṉik koṇṭa kalaṅkaḷik kaḻuvutaliṉ, kīḻ tuṟi ukuvār arunta - kīḻt tuṟiyiṉ oḻukum kaḷḷiṉ kalaṅkal nīri aruntiṭa, paku vāy yāmi - piḷanta vāyiṉiyuṭiya yāmiyāṉatu, kampuḷ iyavaṉ āka - campaṅkōḻi vācciyakkāraṉ āka, vici piṇi teḷ kaṇ kiṇiyiṉ piṟaḻum ūraṉ - iṟukap piṇitta teḷinta kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kiṇip paṟi pōlap piṟaḻum ūriyuṭiyōṉ,
5-8. iṭi neṭu teruvil katumeṉak kaṇṭu - nīṇṭateruviṉ naṭuvē virintu eṉṉik kaṇṭu, eṉ poṉ toṭi muṉ ki paṟṟiṉaṉ āka - eṉatu poṉ vaḷiyiṉi yaṇinta muṉ kiyip piṭittāṉāka, aṉṉāy eṉṟaṉeṉ - aṉṉāy eṉṟu kūviṉēṉ, tol naci kāvāmi - nīṇṭa nāḷāka varum viruppiṉi nīkkāmalē, avaṉ kiviṭṭaṉaṉ - avaṉ eṉ kiyi viṭṭuviṭṭāṉ;
8-11. naṉṉaṉ paṟampil - naṉṉaṉatu paṟampu maliyil, ciṟu kārōṭaṉ - ciṟiya paṇi ceyvōṉ, payiṉoṭu cērttiya kal pōl - arakkoṭu cērttu iyaṟṟiya cāṇik kalliṉip pōlum, nāviṉēṉ āki - vaṉmi aminta nāviṉēṉ āki, atu yāykkuc ceppaleṉ - an nikaḻcciyi nam tāykkuk kūṟiṉēṉ allēṉ. (eṉṉi yeṉiṉ),
11-17. nal tēr kaṭu pakaṭṭu yāṉi cōḻar marukaṉ - nalla tēriṉiyum kaṭiya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉikaḷiyu muṭiya cōḻaratu vaḻi vantōṉākiya, neṭu katir nelliṉ vallam kiḻavōṉ - nīṇṭa neṟ katirkaḷiyuṭiya vallattiṉ talivaṉ, nal aṭi uḷḷāṉākavum- naṉmiyuḷḷavaṉākavum, oṉṉār katuva muyaṟalum muyalva - avaṉ pakivar avaṉip paṟṟa muyalvatum ceyvaraṉṟē, atāaṉṟu - atu kiṭakka, oli pal kūntal - taḻitta palavāya kūntaliyuṭiyāy, nam vayiṉ aruḷātu - nampāl aruḷ ceyyātu, koṉṟaṉaṉ āyiṉum - tuṉpuṟuttiṉaṉ āyiṉum, koli paḻutu aṉṟu - atu kuṟṟamuṭittu ākātu; ākaliṉ,
20. eṉṉi niṉ kuṟippē - niṉ karuttu yātō kūṟuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) miṉ īr ōti! ūraṉ, iṭineṭun teruvil eṉ muṉki paṟṟiṉaṉāka, aṉṉāy eṉṟaṉaṉ. naci kāvāmi avaṉ kiviṭṭaṉaṉ. maṟṟu atu kaṟpōl nāviṉēṉ āki yāykkuc ceppalaṉ. avaṉ nam vayiṉ aruḷātu koṉṟaṉaṉ āyiṉum, koli paḻutu aṉṟu, niṉ kuṟippu eṉṉi?
(vi-ri) koḷīiya - koṇṭa kalaṅkaḷ, viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar. uku vār - cintukiṉṟa kalaṅkalnīriṉ oḻukku. yāmi iṅṅaṉam kaḷḷiṉ kalaṅkali uṇṭal, `vaḷḷukir yāmi..... akaṉṟuṟip, pakuvāy niṟiya nuṅkiṟ kaḷḷiṉ, ukuvār aruntum......'1 eṉa muṉṉarum pōntuḷḷami kāṇka. arunta eṉṉum eccam paku eṉpatu koṇṭu muṭintatu. yāmi kiṇiyiṟ piṟaḻum ūr eṉka. yāmiyum kiṇiyiṉ kaṇṇum vaṭivāṉ ottiruttaliṉ kiṇiyiṉ eṉpatu meyyuvamattiṉkaṇ vantatu. `kayattuvāḻ yāmi kāḻ kōt taṉṉa, nuṇkōṟ ṟakitta teṇkaṇ mākkiṇi'2 `āmi yakaṭupōl aṅkaṭ ṭaṭāri'3 eṉpaṉa īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālaṉa. kāvāmi - varutalik kāvāmal, nīkkāmal eṉṟapaṭi. toṉṉaci cālāmi eṉṟu pāṭaṅ koḷḷiṉum ituvē poruḷāmeṉka. ciṟukā rōṭaṉ payiṉōṭu cērttiya kaṟpōla eṉṉum ittoṭar innūliṉ mutaṟceyyuḷilum vantuḷḷami kāṇka. cōḻar marukaṉākiya vallaṅkiḻavōṉ eṉka. nallaṭi - naṉmi, atu piṟarkkut tīṅku iyaṟṟāmi. atāaṉṟu eṉpatu atu pōlvatu itu eṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭutaṟkaṇ vantatu. muṉpu ki viṭṭa avaṉ nammi aruḷātu tuṉpuṟuttiṉaṉāyiṉum, paḻaṅ kaṇṇōṭṭa
muṭiya nām ataṉit tīṅkākak koḷḷamāṭṭōm eṉpāḷ koṉṟaṉaṉāyiṉum koli paḻutaṉṟē eṉṟāḷ. eṉṉi niṉ kuṟippu eṉṟatu avaṉ kuṟiyiṉi virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷalē takkatu eṉṟu uṇarttiyapaṭiyām.
357. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.)
		koṭumuḷ īṅkic cūraloṭu miṭinta
		vāṉmuki iṟumpiṉ vayavoṭu vatinta
		uṇṇāp piṇaviṉ uyakkam tīriya
		taṭamarup piyāṉi valampaṭat tolicci
	5.	viyalaṟi civappavāṅki muṇaṅku nimirntu
		pulavuppuli puraṇṭa pulcāy ciṟuneṟi
		payiliruṅ kāṉattu vaḻaṅkal cellātu
		peruṅkaḷiṟ ṟiṉaniri 1kitoṭūup peyarum
		tīñcuḷip palaviṉ ṟoḻuti umpaṟ
	10.	peruṅkā ṭiṟantaṉa rāyiṉum yāḻaniṉ
		tiruntiḻip paṇittōḷ varunta nīṭi
		uḷḷā tamitalō ilarē 2 nalkuvara
		mikupeyal niliiya tīnīrp poyki
		aṭiyiṟan taviḻnta taṇkamaḻ nīlam
	15.	kāloṭu tuyalvan taṉṉaniṉ
		āyitaḻ maḻikkaṇ amartta nōkkē.
- erukkāṭṭūrt tāyaṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-10. (tōḻi! nam talivar) koṭu muḷ īṅki cūraloṭu miṭinta - vaḷinta muḷḷiṉiyuṭiya iṇṭañceṭi pirampuṭaṉ ceṟintuḷḷa, vālmuki iṟumpiṉ - veḷḷiya arumpukaḷi yuṭiya kuṟuṅkāṭṭiṉkaṇ, vayavoṭu vatinta - cūl vēṭkiyuṭaṉ kiṭanta, uṇṇāp piṇaviṉ uyakkam tīriya - uṇṇāta peṇpuliyiṉ varuttatti nīkka, taṭa maruppu yāṉi valam paṭa tolicci - periya kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyi valap pakkattē viḻak koṉṟu, viyal aṟi civappa vāṅki - akaṉṟa pāṟikaḷ kurutiyāṟ civakkumpaṭi kiḻit tiḻuttuvantu, muṇaṅku nimirntu - mūri nimirntu, pulavu puli puraṇṭa - pulālnāṟum puli kiṭantupuraṇṭatāl, pulcāy ciṟuneṟi payil iru kāṉattu - puṟkaḷ cāyntu kiṭakkum ciṟiya neṟikaḷ mikka periya kāṭṭilē, vaḻaṅkal cellātu - iyaṅkutal poruntātu, peru kaḷiṟṟu iṉa niri - periya kaḷiṟṟiṉ kūṭṭamāya niri, ki toṭūup peyarum - tam kikaḷāṟ ṟōṇṭit tiṉṟu pōkum, tīm cuḷip palaviṉ toḻuti - iṉiya cuḷikaḷiyuṭiya palā marattiṉ kūṭṭatti yuṭiya, umpal peru kāṭu iṟantaṉar āyiṉum - periya umpaṟ kāṭṭik kaṭantu ceṉṟuḷārāyiṉum,
10-11. niṉ tiruntu iḻi paṇi tōḷ varunta nīṭi - niṉatu tiruntiya aṇikaḷi yaṇinta mūṅkil pōṉṟa perutta tōṭkaḷ varunti yiḷikkumāṟu tāḻttu,
12-16. miku peyal niliiya tī nīr poyki - mikka maḻi nilipeṟṟamiyāl perukiya iṉiya nīriṉiyuṭiya poyki yiṉ kaṇ, aṭi iṟantu aviḻnta taṇ kamaḻ nīlam - ilikaḷi viṭṭu mēleḻuntu virinta taṇṇiya kamaḻkiṉṟa nīlappū, kāloṭu tuyal vantaṉṉa - kāṟṟiṉāl acintālotta, niṉ āy itaḻ maḻi kaṇ amartta nōkku - niṉatu aḻakiya itaḻ poruntiya kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiṉ amariya pārviyiṉi, nalku vara - aruḷuṇṭāka, uḷḷātu amital ilar - niṉiyātiruttal ilarāvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talivar, umpaṟ peruṅkāṭu iṟantaṉarāyiṉum, niṉ paṇit tōḷ varunta nīṭi, niṉ maḻikkaṇ amartta nōkku, nalkuvara uḷḷātu amital ilar.
(vi-ri) tīriya - ceyyiya eṉṉum eccam. tīriya tolicci eṉka. peyarum kāṭu eṉavum palaviṉ toḻutiyiyuṭiya kāṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka. peyarum kāṭu - peyartaṟku iṭaṉāyirunta kāṭu. cēraṉāṭṭakattu umpaṟkāṭu eṉap peyariya iṭam oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉpatu, `umpaṟkāṭṭu ainnūṟūr' (iraṇṭām pattu) `umpaṟ kāṭṭit taṉ kōṉiṟīi' (mūṉṟām pattu) `umpaṟkāṭṭu vāriyiyum' (aintām pattu) eṉap patiṟṟuppattiṉ patikaṅ kaḷil varutalāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. umpaṟkāṭeṉpatu yāṉiyi mikutiyāka uṭimiyāṟ peṟṟa peyar pōlum. nalkuvara - aruḷ uṇṭātal porunta, nalkuvar eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, aruḷvar eṉa muṟṟākkuka.


358. kuṟiñci
(pakalē ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.)
		nīlat taṉṉa niṟaṅkiḷar eruttiṟ
		kāmar pīli āymayiṟ ṟōki
		iṉṟīṅ kurala tuvaṉṟi meṉcīr
		āṭutaki eḻiṉalaṅ kaṭuppak kūṭik
	5.	kaṇṇēr itaḻa taṇṇaṟuṅ kuvaḷik
		kuṟuntoṭar aṭicciya naṟumpal kūḻi
		nīṭunīr neṭuñcuṉi āyamo ṭāṭāy
		uyaṅkiya maṉatti yākip pulampukoṇṭu
		iṉṉi yākiya niṉṉiṟam nōkki
	10.	aṉṉi 1viṉaviṉa ḷāyiṉ aṉṉō
		eṉṉeṉa urikkō yāṉē 2 tuṉṉiya
		peruvari iḻitarum neṭuveḷ ḷaruvi
		ōṭi yāṉi uyarmici yeṭutta
		āṭukoṭi kaṭuppat tōṉṟum
	15.	kōṭuyar veṟpaṉ uṟīiya nōyē.
- 3maturi marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-10. tōḻi! nīlattu aṉṉa niṟam kiḷar eruttiṉ - nīla maṇiyi yotta niṟam viḷaṅkum kaḻuttiṉiyum, kāmar pīli - aḻakiya pīliyākiya, āy  mayil tōki - ciṟanta tōkiyi yuṭiya mayilkaḷ, iṉ tīm kurala tuvaṉṟi - mikka iṉiya kuraliṉiyuṭiyavāy neruṅki, mel cīr āṭutaki eḻil nalam kaṭuppa - melliya tāḷa aṟuti koṇṭu āṭum takutiyāya aḻakiṉ ciṟappiṉi oppa, kaṇ nēr itaḻa taṇ naṟu kuvaḷi - kaṇṇi yotta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya kuḷirnta naṟiya kuvaḷi malariṉ, kuṟu toṭar aṭicciya naṟu pal kūḻi - kuṟiya māliyiṉic cerukiya naṟiya palavāya kūntal parakka, nīṭu nīr neṭu cuṉi āyamoṭu kūṭi āṭāy - āḻnta nīriṉiyuṭiya nīṇṭa cuṉiyiṉ kaṇ niṉ āyattuṭaṉ kūṭi āṭātē, uyaṅkiya maṉatti āki - varuntiya maṉattiṉiyuṭiyiyāki, pulampu koṇṭu - taṉimi koṇṭu, iṉṉi ākiya niṉ niṟam nōkki - it taṉmiyiyākiya niṉatu mēṉi vēṟupāṭṭip pārttu, aṉṉi viṉaviṉaḷ āyiṉ - nam aṉṉi (ataṉ kāraṇattik) kēṭṭaṉaḷāyiṉ,
11-15. tuṉṉiya peru vari iḻitarum neṭu veḷ aruvi - poruntiya periya maliyiṉiṉṟum oḻukum nīṇṭa veḷḷiya aruviyāṉatu, ōṭi yāṉi uyarmici eṭutta āṭu koṭi kaṭuppa tōṉṟum - neṟṟip paṭṭattiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ mītu tūkkiya aciyum veṇtukiṟ koṭiyiṉi yoppat tōṉṟum, kōṭu uyar veṟpaṉ uṟīiya nōy - cikaram uyarnta maliyiyuṭiya talivaṉ nammi aṭivitta innōyiṉi, aṉṉō - antō, yāṉ eṉ eṉa urikku - yāṉ yātiṉāṉ nikaḻntateṉṟu colluvēṉ?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! aṉṉi, uyaṅkiya maṉattiyākip pulampu koṇṭu iṉṉiyākiya niṉṉiṟam nōkki, viṉaviṉaḷāyiṉ, veṟpaṉ uṟīiya nōy aṉṉō! yāṉ eṉṉeṉa urikku.
(vi-ri) pīli ākiya tōkiyiyuṭiya mayil eṉak kūṭṭuka. iṉtīm - oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi. mayil pala kūṭi iṉiya kuraliyuṭiyavāyt tōkiyivirittu āṭutalpōla nīyum āyattuṭaṉ kūṭik kūntal parakkac cuṉiyil āṭutal ceyyāy eṉa uvamiyum poruḷum ottu niṉṟu makiḻcci viḷittal kāṇka. uvamiyil iṉṟīṅkurala eṉpataṟ kēṟpap poruḷilum iṉiya kuraluṭaṉ pāṭikkoṇṭu āṭāy eṉa viritturikka.
359. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		paṉivār uṇkaṇum pacanta tōḷum
		naṉipiṟar aṟiyac cāaya nāḷuṅ
		karantaṉam uṟiyum nampaṇ paṟiyār
		nīṭiṉar maṉṉō kātalar eṉanī
	5.	evaṉki yaṟṟaṉi ikuḷi avarē
		vāṉa varampaṉ veḷiyat taṉṉanam
		māṇalan tammoṭu koṇṭaṉar muṉāatu
		aruñcurak kavali yaciiya kōṭiyar
		peruṅkal mīmici iyameḻun tāṅku
	10.	vīḻpiṭi keṭutta neṭuntāḷ yāṉi
		cūrpukal aṭukkattu maḻimāṟu muḻaṅkum
		poyyā nallici māvaṇ pulli
		kavikkatir varakiṉ yāṇarp pintāḷ
		muticcuvaṉ mūḻkiya kāṉcuṭu kurūuppuki
	15.	aruvit tuvaliyoṭu mayaṅkum
		peruvari attam iyaṅki yōrē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. ikuḷi - tōḻi! paṉi vār uṇ kaṇum - nīr oḻukum miyuṇ kaṇkaḷum, pacanta tōḷum - pacaliyuṟṟa tōḷkaḷum, naṉi piṟar aṟiyac cāaya - piṟar mikavum aṟiyumpaṭi vāṭutaluṟa, nāḷum karantaṉam uṟiyum nam paṇpu aṟiyār - nāṭō ṟum avaṟṟi maṟittu vatiyum nam iyalpiṉi aṟiyārāy, kātalar nīṭiṉar eṉa - nam kātalar vārātu mikat tāḻttaṉar eṉṟu, nī evaṉ kiyaṟṟaṉi - nī ceyalaṟṟatu eṉṉiyō?
7-16. muṉāatu - muṉpu uḷḷatākiya, aru cura kavali aciiya kōṭiyar iyam - ariya curattiṉ kavartta neṟikaḷiṟ ṟaṅkiya kūttaratu vācciya oli, peruṅ kal mī mici eḻuntāṅku - periya maliyiṉ mēliṭattē tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōla, vīḻ piṭi keṭutta neṭu tāḷ yāṉi - virumpappaṭum piṭiyiṉi iḻanta nīṇṭa kāliyuṭiya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi, cūr pukal aṭukkattu maḻi māṟu muḻaṅkum - teyvam virumpiyuṟiyum malippakkattē iṭiyoṭu māṟupaṭa muḻaṅkum iṭamāṉa, poyyā nal ici mā vaṇ pulli - paricilarkkup poyyāta nalla pukaḻiṉiyum ciṟanta vaṇmiyiyumuṭiya pulli eṉpāṉatu, kavi katir varakiṉ yāṇar pin tāḷ - kiḷitta katiriṉiyuṭiya varakiṉatu aḻakiya paciya taṭṭikaḷiyuṭiya, mutic cuval mūḻkiya - paḻaṅ kolli yākiya mēṭṭu nilattic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa, kāṉ cuṭu kurūup puki - kāṭṭic cuṭuvatā leḻum viḷaṅkum puki, aruvit tuvaliyoṭu mayaṅkum - aruviyiṉ nuṇṇiya tuḷikaḷuṭaṉ viravit tōṉṟum, peru vari attam iyaṅkiyōr - periya mali poruntiya kāṭṭilē ceṉṟōr ākiya
5-7. avar - nam talivar, vāṉa varampaṉ veḷiyattu aṉṉa - vāṉa varampaṉatu veḷiyam eṉumiṭatti yotta, nam māṇ nalam tammoṭu koṇṭaṉar - namatu ciṟanta aḻakiṉit tammuṭaṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar.
(muṭipu) ikuḷi! kātalar nam paṇpu aṟiyār nīṭiṉar maṉṉō eṉa nī evaṉ kiyaṟṟaṉi? māvaṇ pulli peruvari attam iyaṅkiyōr (ākiya) avar, vāṉa varampaṉ veḷiyattu aṉṉa nam māṇ nalam tammoṭu koṇṭaṉar.
(vi-ri) nāḷum cāaya eṉṟurittalumām. iyaṅkiyōr avar eṉavum kōṭiyar iyam peruṅkal mīmici eḻuntāṅku eṉavum koṇṭu kūṭṭuka. muṉāatu paḻiyatākiya eṉṟumām; paḻiyatākiya namatu māṇalam eṉak kūṭṭiyurittalumām. iyam kal mici eḻutal eṉṟatu, maliyil etiroli yuṇṭātal. pulliyiṉ peruvari eṉka. mūḻkiya kāṉ - cūḻnta kāṭu. avar nam māṇalam tammoṭu koṇṭaṉar eṉṟatu, avar mīṇṭu varuṅkāl, namatu nalamum vantuviṭum eṉak kūṟi āṟṟuvittavāṟām.
360. neytal
(pakaṟkuṟi vanta talimakaṟkut tōḻi pakaṟkuṟi maṟuttu iravukkuṟi nērntatu.)
		palpūn taṇpoḻiṟ pakaluṭaṉ kaḻippi
		orukāl ūrtip parutiyañ celvaṉ
		kuṭavayiṉ māmali maṟiyak koṭuṅkaḻit
		taṇcēṟ ṟaṭiiya kaṇikkāl neytal
	5.	nuṇṭā tuṇṭu vaṇṭiṉam tuṟappa
		veruvaru kaṭuntiṟal iruperun teyvattu
		uruvuṭaṉ iyinta tōṟṟam pola
		anti vāṉamoṭu kaṭalaṇi koḷāa
		vanta māli peyariṉ maṟṟivaḷ
	10.	perumpulam piṉaḷē teyya ataṉāl
		pāṇi piḻiyā māṇviṉik kalimā
		tuñcūr yāmattut teviṭṭal ōmpi
		neṭuntēr akala nīkkip piyeṉak
		kuṉṟiḻi kaḷiṟṟiṟ kuvavumaṇal nīnti
	15.	iraviṉ vammō uravunīrc cērppa
		iṉamīṉ aruntum nāriyoṭu paṉimici
		aṉṟil cēkkum muṉṟiṟ poṉṉeṉa
		naṉmalar naṟuvī tāam
		puṉṉi naṟumpoḻiṟ ceytanaṅ kuṟiyē.
- maturik kaṇṇattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. uravu nīr cērppa - valiya kaṭaṟkariyiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
1-10. pal pū taṇ poḻil pakal uṭaṉ kaḻippi - pala pūkkaḷi yuṭiya kuḷirnta poḻilil pakal muḻuvatum pōkki, oru kāl ūrti paruti am celvaṉ - oṟṟi uruḷ pūṇṭa tēriṉiyuṭiya ñāyiṟākiya aḻakiya celvaṉ, kuṭavayiṉ mā mali maṟiya - mēṟkil uḷḷatākiya periya maliyil maṟiya, koṭu kaḻi taṇ cēṟṟu aṭiiya kaṇi kāl neytal - vaḷinta kaḻiyiluḷḷa kuḷirnta cēṟṟil poruntiya tiraṇṭa kāliṉiyuṭiya neytaṟ pūviṉ, nuṇ tātu uṇṭu vaṇṭu iṉam tuṟappa - nuṇṇiya poṭiyi uṇṭu vaṇṭiṉ kūṭṭam ataṉi viṭṭēka, veruvaru kaṭu tiṟal iru peru teyvattu - añcattakum mikka valiyuṭiya iru perun teyvaṅkaḷāya civaṉum tirumālum āya ivarkaḷatu, uru uṭaṉ iyinta tōṟṟam pōla - cenniṟamum karuniṟamum oruṅku poruntiya tōṟṟattip pōla, anti vāṉamoṭu kaṭal aṇi koḷāa - anti vāṉattiṉ aḻakuṭaṉ kaṭaliṉ aḻakiyum koṇṭu, vanta māli - vanta mālik kālattē, peyariṉ - nī nīṅkiṉ, ivaḷ perum pulampiṉaḷ - ivaḷ mikka taṉimiyuṟṟaṉaḷāki varuntuvaḷ,
10-14. ataṉāl-, pāṇi piḻiyā māṇviṉik kalimā - tāḷatti ottu naṭattal tappāta māṇpuṟṟa viṉi valla cerukkuṟum kutiri, tuñcu ūr yāmattu teviṭṭal ōmpi - ūr tuyilum yāmattil olittalip pātukāttu, neṭutēr akala nīkki - nīṇṭa tēriṉic cēymiyil nīkki niṟutti, pi eṉa kuṉṟu iḻi kaḷiṟṟil kuvavu maṇal nīnti - melleṉak kuṉṟiṉ iḻintu cellum kaḷiṟu pōlat tiraṇṭa maṇal mēṭṭiṉik kaṭantu,
16-19. iṉam mīṉ aruntum nāriyoṭu - mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷi uṇṇum nārikaḷuṭaṉ, paṉimici - paṉimarattiṉmītu, aṉṟil cēkkum muṉṟil - aṉṟil taṅkiyirukkum muṟṟattē, poṉ eṉa naṉ malar naṟuvī tāam - poṉ pōla nalla malarākiya naṟiya pūkkaḷ utirntu paravum, puṉṉi naṟu poḻil ceyta - puṉṉi maraṅkaḷuḷḷa naṟiya cōliyiṟ ceyta, nam kuṟi - namatu kuṟiyiṭattiṟku,
15. iraviṉ vammō - iraviṉkaṇ varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) uravu nīrc cērppa! nī taṇpoḻil pakal uṭaṉ kaḻippi, māli peyariṉ maṟṟu ivaḷ perum pulampiṉaḷ. ataṉāl neṭuntēr akala nīkki, kuvavu maṇal nīnti, puṉṉi naṟum poḻiṟkaṇ ceyta namkuṟi iraviṉ vammō.
(vi-ri) pakal uṭaṉ kaḻippi, māli peyariṉ eṉa iyiyum. paruti am celvaṉ maṟiya, neytal uṇṭu vaṇṭiṉam tuṟappa anti vāṉamoṭu kaṭalaṇi koṇṭu vanta māli eṉka. anti vāṉam - antik kālattuc cevvāṉam. teyya - acic col. ūrtuñcu yāmam eṉa māṟuka. iravuk kuṟikku iṭiyīṭu nērātapaṭi varutal vēṇṭum eṉpāḷ, kutiri teviṭṭal ōmpi, tēr akala nīkki, pi yeṉa maṇal nīnti varuka eṉṟāḷ. aṉṟil cēkkum naṟum poḻil eṉavum muṉṟil naṟuvī tāam naṟum poḻil eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
361. pāli
(poruḷvayiṟ pirintu pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		tūmalart tāmarip pūviṉ aṅkaṇ
		māyitaḻk kuvaḷi malarpiṇit taṉṉa
		tirumukat talamarum perumatar maḻikkaṇ
		1aṇivaḷi muṉki āyitaḻ maṭanti
	5.	vārmuli muṟṟattu nūliṭi vilaṅkiṉum
		kavavuppulan tuṟiyuṅ kaḻiperuṅ kāmattu
		iṉpuṟu nukarcciyiṟ ciṟantatoṉ ṟilleṉa
		aṉpāl moḻinta eṉmoḻi koḷḷāy
		poruḷpuri vuṇṭa maruḷi neñcē
	10	kariyāp pūviṟ periyōr āra
		aḻaleḻu tittiyam maṭutta yāmi
		niḻaluṭi neṭuṅkayam pukalvēṭ ṭāaṅku
		1uḷḷutal ōmpumati iṉinī muḷḷeyiṟṟuc
		ciṉmoḻi arivi tōḷē paṉmali
	15.	vevvaṟi maruṅkiṉ viyaṉcuram
		evvaṅ kūra iṟantaṉam yāmē.
- eyiṉanti makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-9. tū malar tāmarip pūviṉ aṅkaṇ - tūya malarāya tāmarip pūviṭattē, mā itaḻ kuvaḷi malar piṇit taṉṉa - kariya itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya kuvaḷimalar iraṇṭiṉip piṇittu vittāṟpōṉṟa, tiru mukattu alamarum peru matar maḻi kaṇ - aḻakiya mukattiṭattē cuḻalum periya matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum, aṇivaḷi muṉki - aḻakiya vaḷikaḷiyuṭiya muṉ kiyiṉiyum, āy itaḻ maṭanti - aḻakiya vāy itaḻiṉiyu muṭiya nam taliviyiṉ, vārmuli muṟṟattu kavavu - vār aṇinta mulip parappiṉ muyakkattiṉi, nūl iṭi vilaṅkiṉum - oru nūl iṭiyē taṭuppiṉum, pulantu uṟiyum - ataṉi veṟuttu uṟiyum, kaḻi peruṅ kāmattu iṉpu uṟu nukarcciyil - mikap periya kātaloṭu iṉpam tuykkum nukarcciyik kāṭṭiṉum, ciṟantatu oṉṟu il eṉa - mēmpaṭṭatōr iṉpam illi eṉa, aṉpāl moḻinta eṉ moḻi koḷḷāy - aṉpuṭaṉ kūṟiya eṉ moḻiyiṉ ēṟṟuk koḷḷāyāki, poruḷ purivuṇṭa maruḷi neñcē - poruḷi virumpi vanta mayakkattiṉiyuṭiya neñcamē,
14-16. pal mali vev aṟi maruṅkiṉ viyaṉ curam - pala malikaḷiyum vevviya pāṟikaḷiyumuṭiya nīṇṭa curattiyum, evvaṅ kūra iṟantaṉam yām - tuṉpam mika yām kaṭantu vantuḷē mākaliṉ, 
10-12. kariyā pūvil periyōr āra - vāṭāta pūviṉiyuṭiya tēvar uṇṭaṟ poruṭṭu, aḻal eḻu tittiyam maṭutta yāmi - aḻal ōṅkiya vēḷvik kuṇṭattiṉkaṇ iṭappeṟṟa yāmi, niḻal uṭi neṭu kayam pukal vēṭṭāṅku - tāṉ muṉpirunta niḻal poruntiya periya poykiyiṉkaṇ pōtali virumpuvatu pōla,
13-14. muḷ eyiṟṟu cilmoḻi arivi tōḷ - muḷ pōṉṟa paṟkaḷiyum cilavāya moḻikaḷiyu muṭiya nam taliviyiṉ tōḷiṉi, iṉi nī uḷḷutal ōmpumati - ippōtu nī niṉittali oḻivāyāka.
(muṭipu) kaḻi peruṅ kāmattu iṉpuṟu nukarcciyiṟ ciṟantatoṉṟilleṉa moḻinta eṉ moḻi koḷḷāy ākip poruḷ purivuṇṭa maruḷi neñcē! yām paṉmali viyaṉ curam evvam kūra iṟantaṉam (ākaliṉ) tittiyam maṭutta yāmi neṭuṅkayam pukal vēṭṭāṅku, arivitōḷ iṉi nī uḷḷutal ōmpumati.
(vi-ri) mukamum kaṇkaḷum muṟiyē tāmarip pūvum kuvaḷi malarum pōl viḷaṅkum eṉṉum ivvuvami `tāmarip pōtiṟ pūtta taṇṇaṟuṅ kuvaḷip pūppōṟ, kāmaru mukattiṟ pūtta karumaḻit taṭaṅkaṇ'1 eṉat tiruttakka tēvarālum kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. nūl iṭi vilaṅkiṉum eṉṟatu - nūl iṭiyē ūṭaṟuttu muyakkatti nekiḻvippatāyiṉum eṉṟapaṭi. pulantu uṟital - avviṭiyīṭṭi  veṟuttu uṟital. nekiḻā muyakkiṉi virumpuvār talivaṉ talivi iruvarum ākaliṉ, pulantuṟitalum iruvarkkum koḷka. `vīḻum iruvark kiṉitē vaḷiyiṭi, pōḻap paṭāa muyakku'2 eṉpatu kāṇka. ciṟantatu - ciṟanta iṉpam; poruḷ mutaliyaṉavumām. aṭutta yāmi eṉap pirittu vēḷvik kuṇṭattic cāra vikkap peṟṟuḷḷa yāmi eṉṟalumām. ikkaruttu, `neṭumaṇi yūpat tiṭṭa tavaḻnaṭi yāmi nīṇīrt, toṭumaṇik kuvaḷip paṭṭam tuṇiyoṭu niṉippatē pōl'3 eṉat tēvarālum āḷap peṟṟuḷatu. tittiya maṭutta yāmi kayam pukal vēṭṭāṅku eṉṟa uvami, arivi tōḷ ippoḻutu kūṭutaṟku aritu eṉpataṉi viḷakkiyavāṟām. pala malikaḷiṉ vev vaṟi maruṅkiṉ eṉṟurittalumām.
362. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi colliyatu.)
		pāmpuṭi viṭara paṉinīr iṭṭuttuṟit
		tēṅkalan toḻuka yāṟuniṟin taṉavē
		veṇkōṭ ṭiyāṉi poruta puṇkūrntu
		piṅkaṇ valliyam kallaḷic ceṟiya
	5.	murukkarum paṉṉa 4vaḷḷukir vayappiṇavu
		kaṭikoḷa vaḻaṅkār āṟē āyiṭi
		elliṟ ṟeṉṉāṉ veṉvēl ēnti
		nacitara vanta naṉṉa rāḷaṉ
		neñcupaḻu tāka vaṟuviyaṉ peyariṉ
	10.	iṉṟip poḻutum yāṉvā ḻalaṉē
		evaṉkol vāḻi tōḻinam iṭimulic
		cuṇaṅkaṇi muṟṟat tāram pōlavuñ
		cilampunīṭu cōlic citartūṅku naḷirppiṉ
		ilaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi pōlavum
	15.	nilaṅkoṇ ṭaṉavāl tiṅkaḷaṅ katirē.
- veḷḷi vītiyār.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-2. pāmpu uṭi viṭara - pāmpukaḷiyuṭiya mali viṭar kaḷiṉiṉṟu varum, paṉinīr - kuḷirnta nīr, iṭṭut tuṟi - oṭuṅkiya tuṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka, tēm kalantu oḻuka - tēṉuṭaṉ kūṭi oḻukaliṉ, yāṟu niṟintaṉavē - yāṟukaḷ niṟivuṟṟaṉa;
3-6. veṇ kōṭṭu yāṉi poruta puṇ kūrntu - veḷḷiya kompiṉiyuṭiya yāṉi pāyntu kuttiya puṇ mikuntu, pi kaṇ valliyam kal aḷi ceṟiya - paciya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya āṇ puli kaṟkukiyiṉuḷḷē oṭuṅki yirukka, murukku arumpu aṉṉa vaḷ ukir vaya piṇavu kaṭi koḷa - cemmurukkiṉ arumpiṉi yotta kūriya nakattiṉiyuṭiya valiya peṇpuli ataṉik kāval ceytiruttaliṉ, āṟu vaḻaṅkār - (yāvarum) anneṟiyil iyaṅkār;
6-10. āyiṭi elliṟṟu eṉṉāṉ - anniliyil iruḷ vantu viṭṭatu eṉṟu eṇṇātavaṉāki, veṉ vēl ēnti - veṟṟiyit tarum vēliṉik kiyiṟ koṇṭu, naci tara vanta naṉṉarāḷaṉ - nampāluḷḷa kātal koṇṭu varutalāl vanta naṉmiyāḷaṉākiya nam talivaṉ, neñcu paḻutāka vaṟuviyaṉ peyariṉ - taṉ eṇṇam payaṉilatāka nammik kūṭātu mīḷiṉ, iṉṟu ippoḻutum yāṉ vāḻalaṉ - iṉṟu iv viravum yāṉ uyir tarittirēṉ,
11-15. nam iṭi muli cuṇaṅku aṇi muṟṟattu āram pōlavum - namatu muliyiṭamāya cuṇaṅku aṇinta parappil kiṭakkum muttumāli pōlavum, cilampu nīṭu cōli citar tūṅku naḷirppiṉ ilaṅku veḷ aruvi pōlavum - maliyiṉiṭattu nīṇṭa cōliyil tuḷikaḷ citaṟum kuḷircciyiṉiyuṭiya viḷaṅkum veḷḷiya aruvi pōlavum, tiṅkaḷ am katir nilam koṇṭaṉa - tiṅkaḷiṉ aḻakiya katirkaḷ maliyiṭattē paraviṉa;
evaṉ kol - nām ceyvatu eṉṉiyō.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻi! yāṟu niṟintaṉa. vayap piṇavu kaṭi koḷa āṟu vaḻaṅkār. āyiṭi veṉ vēl ēnti nacitara vanta naṉṉarāḷaṉ vaṟuviyaṉ peyariṉ, ippoḻutum  yāṉ vāḻalaṉ . tiṅkaḷ am katir nilam koṇṭaṉa. evaṉkol?
(vi-ri) pāmpuṭi viṭara paṉinīr eṉṟatu nīriṉatu tīmiyiyum, iṭṭut tuṟi eṉṟatu, tuṟiyiṉatu tīmiyiyum, yāṟu niṟintaṉa eṉṟatu yāṟṟiṉatu tīmiyiyum uṇarttiyavāṟām. kaṭi koḷa eṉṟataṉi, kaṭi koḷāniṉṟaṉa eṉa virittu, yāṟu niṟintaṉa, vayap piṇavu kaṭikoḷā niṉṟaṉa; ākaliṉ āṟu vaḻaṅkār eṉa urikka. tiṅkaḷaṅ katir nilaṅ koṇṭaṉa eṉṟamiyāṉ, nilavu veḷippāṭu iravuk kuṟik kūṭṭattiṟku iṭiyīṭātal peṟṟām. vaṟuviyaṉ peyariṉ eṉpataṟku ētuk kūṟuvāḷ tiṅkaḷaṅ katir nilaṅ koṇṭaṉa eṉṟāḷ. ellā vakiyāṉum naṉmi yuṭiyāṉākiya talivaṉ, nammēluḷḷa aṉpiṉ mikutiyāl, vaḻiyarumi nōkkātu iravukkuṟik
kaṇ vantāṉākaliṉ, avaṉ kūṭṭam peṟātu peyariṉ yāṉ uyir vāḻtaṟku oruppaṭēṉ. āyiṉ, nilavu veḷip pāṭṭiṉāl kūṭṭamum iyivataṉṟu. itaṟku nāṉ eṉṉa ceyvēṉ eṉpāḷ pōṉṟu, talivaṉ virivil varintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉat tōḻi ciṟip
puṟamāka uṇarttiṉā ḷeṉka. vāḻalaṉ - aṉ vikuti taṉmikkaṇ vantatu.
363. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		niricelal ivuḷi virivuṭaṉ kaṭii
		akaliru vicumpiṟ pakalcelac ceṉṟu
		maḻukucuṭar maṇṭilam māmali maṟiya
		poḻutukaḻi malariṟ puṉiyiḻi cāay
	5.	aṇiyaṇin tiṉiyi yākal kaṇiyarip
		pullili nellip pukaril pacuṅkāy
		kallatar maruṅkiṟ kaṭuvaḷi yutirppap
		polañcey kāciṟ poṟpat tāam
		attam naṇṇi atarpārt tirunta
	10.	koliveṅ koḷkik koṭuntoḻiṉ maṟavar
		āṟuceṉ mākkaḷ aruniṟat teṟinta
		eḵkuṟu viḻuppuṇ kūrntōr 1 eytiya
		vaḷivāyp paruntiṉ vaḷḷukirc cēval
		kiḷitaru 2teḷviḷi keḻumuṭip payirum
	15.	iṉṉā veñcuram iṟantōr muṉṉiya
		ceyviṉi valatta rāki ivaṇayantu
		eytavan taṉarē tōḻi miyeḻil
		tuṇiyēr etirmalar uṇkaṇ
		piṇiyēr nōkkam peruṅkaviṉ koḷavē.
- maturip poṉcey kollaṉ veṇṇākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 17. tōḻi-,
5-15. kaṇi ari pul ili nelli pukar il pacu kāy - tiraṇṭa ariyiṉiyum ciṟiya iliyiṉiyum uṭiya nelliyiṉ vaṭuvaṟṟa paciya kāykaḷ, kal atar maruṅkiṉ kaṭu vaḷi utirppa - kaṟkaḷiyuṭiya neṟikaḷiṭattē kaṭiya kāṟṟu utirttaliṉ, polaṉ cey kācil poṟpat tāam - poṉṉāṟ ceyta kācu pōl aḻaku peṟap paravikkiṭakkum, attam naṇṇi atarpārt tirunta - kāṭṭiṉi aṭintu aṅku vaḻivaruvārip pārttuk koṇṭirunta, koli vem koḷki koṭu toḻil maṟavar - koliyi virumpum kōṭpāṭṭiṉiyum koṭiya toḻiliyu muṭiya maṟavar, āṟu cel mākkaḷ aru niṟattu eṟinta - vaḻic cellum makkaḷatu ariya mārpilē eṟinta, eḵku uṟu viḻu puṇ kūrntōr eytiya - vēlāluṟṟa ciṟanta puṇṇi mikak koṇṭu paṭṭōri aṭinta, vaḷi vāy vaḷ ukir paruntiṉ cēval - vaḷinta vāyiṉiyum kūriya nakattiṉiyum uṭiya paruntiṉ cēval. keḻu muṭi - āṇṭup poruntiya ūṉ uṇṇaṟku, kiḷi taru teḷ viḷi payirum - taṉ kiḷiyiṉit tarukiṟkum teḷinta kuralāl aḻikkum, iṉṉā vem curam iṟantōr - iṉṉāmiyit tarum koṭiya curattik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam talivar,
15-19. muṉṉiya ceyviṉi valattar āki - tām karutic ceṉṟa poruḷīṭṭum toḻilil veṟṟiyuṭiyar āki, mi eḻil tuṇi ēr etir malar uṇkaṇ piṇi ēr nōkkam - kariya aḻakiya iṇi yotta etir etir poruntiya malarkaḷip pōṉṟa miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiṉ peṇmāṉi yotta niṉ pārvi, peru kaviṉ koḷa - mikka aḻakiṉiyuṟa, ivaṇ nayantu eyta vantaṉar - nammi virumpi iṅkē vantu cērntaṉar; ātaliṉ,
1-3. niri celal ivuḷi virivuṭaṉ kaṭii - variciyākac cellum kutirikaḷi virivākac celutti, akal iru vicumpil pakal cela ceṉṟu - akaṉṟa periya vāṉil pakaṟ poḻutu kaḻiyac ceṉṟu, maḻuku cuṭar maṇṭilam mā mali maṟiya - maḻuṅkiya oḷiyiṉiyuṭiya ñāyiṟu periya maliyil maṟintiṭa,
4-5. puṉi iḻi - aḻakiya aṇiyuṭiyāḷē, poḻutu kaḻi malaril cāay - pakaṟpoḻutu kaḻiyavē kuviyum malar pōla vāṭi, aṇi aṇintu - paḷḷiyi aṭintu, iṉiyi ākal - ittaṉmi yuṭiyi ākātēkoḷ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! iṉṉā veñcuram iṟantōr muṉṉiya ceyviṉi valattarāki, niṉ piṇiyēr nōkkam kaviṉ koḷa ivaṇ eyta vantaṉar. maṇṭilam māmali maṟiya, malariṟcāay aṇi aṇintu iṉiyi ākal.
(vi-ri) parutiyiṉ tēriṟ pūṭṭiya kutirikaḷ ēḻu eṉpa ākaliṉ, niri celal ivuḷi eṉṟār. maṇṭilam kaṭii vicumpiṟ ceṉṟu mali maṟiya eṉka. iṉiyi ākal - iṅṅaṉam varuntātē eṉṟapaṭi. viḻuppuṇ kūrntōr - viḻuppuṇ paṭṭu iṟantōri eṉka. eyta vantaṉar - aṇuka vantaṉar eṉṟumām.
364. mulli
(paruvaṅ kaṇṭaḻinta talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		mātiram putiyap pāayk kālvīḻttu
		ēṟuṭip perumaḻi poḻinteṉa avaṟōṟu
		āṭukaḷap paṟiyiṉ varinuṇal kaṟaṅka
		āypoṉ aviriḻi tūkki yaṉṉa
	5.	nīṭiṇark koṉṟi 1kaviṉpeṟak kāṭuṭaṉ
		cuṭarpuri tōṉṟip putaṟalik koḷāa
		mulli illamoṭu malarak kalla
		pakuvāyp piñcuṉi māvuṇa malirak
		kārtoṭaṅ kiṉṟē kāli kātalar
	10.	veñciṉa vēntaṉ viyaṉperum pācaṟi
		veṉṟi vēṭkiyoṭu nammum uḷḷār
		2yātucey vāṅkol tōḻi nōtakak
		kolikuṟit taṉṉa māli
		tuṉitaru pōḻtiṉ nīntalō aritē.
- maturi marutaṅkiḻār makaṉār peruṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 12. tōḻi! 1-9. mātiram putiya pāay kāl vīḻttu- tici yellām maṟiyap paravik kāl iṟaṅki, ēṟu uṭi peru maḻi poḻinteṉa - iṭiyēṟṟiṉiyuṭiya periya maḻi corintatāka, aval tōṟu āṭu kaḷa paṟiyiṉ vari nuṇal kaṟaṅka- paḷḷaṅka ṭōṟum āṭum kaḷattē olikkum paṟi pōla varikaḷiyuṭiya tēri olikka, āy poṉ avir iḻi tūkki aṉṉa - ciṟanta poṉṉālāya viḷaṅkum aṇikaḷit toṅka viṭṭāṟ pōṉṟa, nīṭu iṇar koṉṟi kaviṉ peṟa - nīṇṭa pūṅkottukkaḷi yuṭiya koṉṟi aḻaku peṟa, kāṭu uṭaṉ - kāṭu muḻuvatum, cuṭar puri tōṉṟi putal tali koḷāa - tōṉṟic ceṭi viḷakkup pōṉṟa pūkkaḷit taṉṉiṭattē koḷḷa, mulli illamoṭu malara - mullip pūkkaḷum tēṟṟāviṉ pūkkaḷum malaravum, kalla paku vāy pi cuṉi mā uṇa malira - maliyiṭattaṉavākiya akaṉṟa vāyiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya cuṉikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ nīr uṇṇumāṟu nīr niṟiya, kāli kār toṭaṅkiṉṟu - (iṅṅaṉam), ikkālam kārpparuvattit toṭaṅkiyatu,
9-14. kātalar vem ciṉa vēntaṉ viyaṉ perum pācaṟi - nam kātalarō koṭiya ciṉattiṉiyuṭiya aracaṉatu mikap periya pācaṟikkaṇṇē, veṉṟi vēṭkiyoṭu nammum uḷḷār - pakivari veṉṟi koḷḷum viruppattāl nammi niṉittalum ceytilar, nōtaka kolikuṟit taṉṉa māli - nām varunta nammik kollutal karuti varuvatu pōṉṟa māli, tuṉitaru pōḻtiṉ nīntalō aritu - virintu nampāl varuṅkāl ataṉik kaṭantu cellal aritāku maṉṟō, yātu ceyvām - yām eṉ ceyvām.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāli kār toṭaṅkiṉṟu. kātalar vēntaṉ pācaṟi veṉṟi vēṭkiyoṭu nammum uḷḷār. māli tuṉitaru pōḻtiṉ, nīntalō aritu. yātu ceyvām.
(vi-ri) tēri yolikku vācciya oli oppātal, `neṭunī ravala pakuvāyt tēri, ciṟupal liyattiṉ neṭuneṟik kaṟaṅka'1 `tēri yoliyiṉ māṉac cīramittuc, cillari kaṟaṅkum ciṟu pal liyattoṭu'2 eṉa muṉṉarum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kāṭuṭaṉ, eṉpataṉi iṭinili viḷakkākak koṇṭu, kaviṉ peṟa, talik koḷḷa, malara eṉpavaṟṟōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. cuṭar puri pū eṉa virittut talik koḷāa eṉpataṉit talikkoḷḷa eṉat tirikka. kāli eṉpatu - kālam eṉṉum poruṭṭātali. `kaḻakattuk kāli pukiṉ'3 eṉpataṉālum, kātalarip pirintārkku māli kolvatu pōl varum eṉpataṉi, `kātalar ilvaḻi māli kolikkaḷattu, ētilar pōla varum'4 eṉpataṉālum aṟika.
365. pāli
(talimakaṉ iṭiccurattu niṉṟu colliyatu.)
		akalvāy vāṉam āliruḷ parappap
		pakalāṟṟup paṭutta piyeṉ tōṟṟamoṭu
		ciṉaval pōkiya puṉkaṇ māli
		attam naṭukal āḷeṉa utitta
	5.	kāṉa yāṉik katuvāy vaḷḷukir
		irumpaṉi itakkiyiṉ oṭiyum āṅkaṇ
		kaṭuṅkaṇ āṭavar 1ēmuyal kiṭakki
		varunar iṉmiyiṟ kaḷiyunark kāṇā
		eṉṟūḻ veñcuram tanta nīyē
	10.	tuyarcey tāṟṟā yākip peyarpāṅku
		uḷḷiṉi 2vāḻiya neñcē veṉvēl
		māvaṇ kaḻuvuḷ 3kāmūr āṅkaṇ
		pūtan tanta poriyari vēṅkit
		taṇkamaḻ putumalar nāṟum
	15.	añcil ōti āymaṭat takiyē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. neñcē-, vāḻiya-,
1-3. akal vāy vāṉam āl iruḷ parappa - akaṟci vāynta vāṉattiṉkaṇ mikka iruḷ parakka, pakal āṟṟuppaṭutta - ñāyiṟṟip pōkkiya, pi eṉ tōṟṟamoṭu - paciya tōṟṟat
tuṭaṉ, ciṉaval pōkiya - ciṉattal mikka, puṉkaṇ māli - tuṉpattic ceyyum mālik kālattē,
4-9. attam naṭu kal āḷ eṉa utitta - kāṭṭu neṟiyiluḷḷa naṭukalli āḷ eṉa niṉittu utitta, kāṉa yāṉi - kāṭṭu yāṉiyiṉ, katuvāy vaḷ ukir - citivuṟṟa vaḷaviya nakam, irupaṉi itakkiyiṉ oṭiyum āṅkaṇ - periya paṉi nuṅkiṉ tōṭu pōla oṭintiṭum avviṭattē, kaṭuṅkaṇ āṭavar ē muyal kiṭakki - vaṉkaṇmiyuṭiya āṟalippōr ampu eyyum muyaṟciyoṭu patuṅki yiruttaṟkaṇ, varunar iṉmiyiṉ kaḷiyunar kāṇā - vaḻivaruvār illāmiyāl tam vaṟumi kaḷivōrik kāṇalākāta iṭamāya, eṉṟūḻ vem curam tanta nī - veppam mikka koṭiya cura neṟiyil eṉṉi aḻittu vanta nī,
10-15. tuyar ceytu āṟṟāy āki - eṉakkut tuyariṉic ceytu nīyum āṟṟāyāki, peyarpu - mīṇṭu, veṉvēl mā vaṇ kaḻuvuḷ - veṉṟi vēliṉiyum ciṟanta vaṇmiyiṉiyumuṭiya kaḻuvuḷ eṉpāṉatu, kāmūr āṅkaṇ - kāmūrākiya avviṭattu, pūtam tanta pori ari vēṅki - pūtattāl tarappaṭṭa porinta aṭimarattiṉiyuṭiya vēṅkiyiṉ, taṇ kamaḻ putu malar nāṟum - taṇṇitāki maṇakkum putu malar pōla nāṟum, am cil ōti - aḻakiya cilavākiya kūntaliyuṭiya nam taliviyiṉ, āy maṭat taki - aḻakiya maṭappamākiya kuṇattiṉi, uḷḷiṉi - niṉippāy āyiṉi.
(muṭipu) neñcē! puṉkaṇmāli veñcuram tanta nī, tuyarceytu āṟṟāyāki, peyarpu, añcil ōtiyiṉ āymaṭat takiyiṉi uḷḷiṉi.
(vi-ri) āl iruḷ - niṟinta iruḷ, ālutal - niṟital, māl eṉap pirittalumām. pi eṉ tōṟṟam - pacanta tōṟṟam. pōkiya - mikka. māliyil veñcuram tanta eṉka. iṉi, māliyil naṭukal āḷ eṉa utitta eṉavum, māliyil tuyar ceytu āṟṟāyāki eṉṟum muṭittalumām. katuvāy - citivu, utittalāl citivuṟṟa ukir eṉka. itakki - paṉi nuṅkiṉ tōṭu. varunar iṉmiyiṉ eṉṟatu, kāṭṭiṉ koṭumiyāl varuvār illāmi
yāl eṉka. curattiṉ koṭumi karutātu eṉṉi aḻittu vanta nīyē, iṭic curattil, mīṇṭu niṉṟu, taliviyiṉ nalattiṉi uḷḷi mīḷukiṉṟaṉi; niṉ aṟivu iruntavāṟu eṉṉiyō eṉat talivaṉ neñciṉi nōkkik kaḻaṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
366. marutam
(parattiyiṟ pirintuvanta talimakaṉ vāyil 
vēṇṭiyaviṭattut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		tāḻciṉi marutam takipeṟak kaviṉiya
		nīrcūḻ viyaṉkaḷam poliyap pōrpaḻittuk
		kaḷḷār kaḷamar pakaṭu1taḷi māṟṟik
		kaṭuṅkāṟ ṟeṟiyap pōkiya turumpuṭaṉ
	5.	kāyaṟ ciṟutaṭi kaṇkeṭap pāytaliṉ
		2irunīrp parappiṟ paṉittuṟip paratavar
		tīmpoḻi veḷḷuppuc cititaliṟ ciṉiik
		kaḻaṉi uḻavaroṭu māṟetirntu mayaṅki
		iruñcēṟ ṟaḷḷal eṟiceruk kaṇṭu
	10.	narimū tāḷar kipiṇi viṭuttu
		naṉimutir tēṟal nuḷiyark kīyum
		polampūṇ evvi nīḻal aṉṉa
		nalampeṟu paṇittōḷ naṉṉutal ariviyoṭu
		maṇaṅkamaḻ taṇpoḻil alki neruni
	15.	nītaṟ piḻittami aṟintu
		kaluḻnta kaṇṇaḷem aṇaṅkaṉ ṉāḷē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) taliva! 1-5. tāḻ ciṉi marutam taki peṟak kaviṉiya - tāḻnta kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya maruta maram ciṟakka aḻaku peṟṟirukkum, nīr cūḻ viyaṉ kaḷam poliya pōrpu aḻittu - nīr cūḻnta akaṉṟa kaḷam polivuṟap pōriṉip pirittuk kaṭāviṭṭu, kaḷ ār kaḷamar - piṉ kaḷḷuṇṭu vanta uḻavar, pakaṭu taḷi māṟṟi - ak kaṭākkaḷik kaṭṭaviḻttu viṭuttu, kaṭu kāṟṟu eṟiya- kaṭiya kāṟṟil nelliṉit tūṟṟa, pōkiya turumpu uṭaṉ - paṟantu pōṉa turumpukaḷ muḻuvatum, kāyal ciṟu taṭi kaṇ keṭa pāytaliṉ - uppaḷatti luḷḷa ciṟiya pāttikaḷil iṭamillātu eṅkum vīḻntu parattaliṉ,
6-9. iru nīr parappiṉ paṉi tuṟi paratavar - periya kaṭaṟ parappil kuḷirnta tuṟikkaṇ ṇuḷḷa nuḷiyarkaḷ, tīm poḻi veḷ uppu cititaliṉ - iṉimi mikka veḷḷuppu keṭṭamiyiṉ, ciṉii - ciṉantu, kaḻaṉi uḻavaroṭu māṟu etirntu - vayal uḻavaroṭu māṟupaṭṭu etirttu, mayaṅki iru cēṟṟu aḷḷal eṟiceru kaṇṭu - kikalantu mikka cēṟṟuk kuḻampiṉi eṟintu ceyyum pōriṉik kaṇṭu,
10-11. nari mūtāḷar ki piṇi viṭuttu - naritta mutiyōrākiya maruta nila makkaḷ pōr ceyyum kip piṇippiṉi viṭuttu vilakki, naṉi mutir tēṟal - muṟṟiya tēṉākiya kaḷḷiṉ teḷivi, nuḷiyarkku īyum - paratavarkku aḷikkum iṭamākiya,
12-16. polam pūṇ evvi nīḻal aṉṉa - poṟ pūṇ aṇinta evvi eṉpāṉatu nīḻal eṉṉum ūrpōṉṟa, nalam peṟu paṇi tōḷ nalnutal ariviyoṭu - aḻaku peṟṟa mūṅkil pōlum tōḷum nalla neṟṟiyum uṭiya parattiyoṭu, maṇam kamaḻ taṇ poḻil alki - maṇam nāṟum kuḷirnta cōliyilē taṅki, neruni - nēṟṟu, nī taṉ piḻittami aṟintu - nī taṉakkut tīṅku ceytamiyi yuṇarntu, em aṇaṅku aṉṉāḷ - em teyvam pōṉṟavaḷāya talivi, kaluḻnta kaṇṇaḷ - aḻuta kaṇṇiṉi yuṭiyaḷ āyiṉāḷ.
(muṭipu) taliva! nī evvi nīḻal aṉṉa ariviyoṭu neruni taṇpoḻil alki, taṟpiḻittami aṟintu, em aṇaṅku aṉṉāḷ kaluḻnta kaṇṇaḷ āyiṉaḷ; eṉ ceytaṉi.
paratavar, uḻavaroṭu māṟetirntu mayaṅki, aḷḷal eṟi ceruk kaṇṭu, nari mūtāḷar, ki piṇi viṭuttu, tēṟal nuḷiyarkku īyum nīḻal eṉka.
(vi-ri) pōrpu - neṟkatiriṉ pōr. aḷḷal eṟi ceru - aḷḷali ṉiṉṟu tākkum ceru eṉṟumām. nuḷiyar pakkal tīṅku iṉmiyiṉ, nari mūtāḷar, avarkkut tēṟal nalki amiti ceyvārāyiṉar eṉka. īyum eṉṉum peyareccam nīḻal eṉṉum iṭappeyar koṇṭatu. paṉittuṟip paratavarum kaḻaṉi uḻavarum, iṅṅaṉam cerucceytaṟ kiṭamākiya nīḻal eṉṟamiyāl, avvūr neytalum marutamum mayaṅkiyatātal peṟṟām. `yāḻici maṟukiṉ nīṭūr kiḻavōṉ, vāyvāḷ evvi'1 eṉa muṟ pōntamiyiṉ, nīḻal eṉpatu nīṭal eṉpataṉ tiripātaluṅ kūṭum. kaṇṇaḷ - kaṇṇaḷ āyiṉaḷ eṉa ākkac col virittu urikka.
367. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ vaṟpuṟukkum tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		ilaṅkucuṭar maṇṭilam pulantali peyarntu
		palkatir maḻukiya kalcēr amiyattu
		alantali mūtēṟu āṇkural viḷippa
		maṉivaḷar nocci mācērpu vatiya
	5.	2muṉiyuḻi yirunta aṅkuṭic cīṟūrk
		karuṅkāl vēṅkic ceñcuval varakiṉ
		mikupatam niṟinta tokukūṭ ṭoruciṟik
		kuviyaṭi verukiṉ piṅkaṇ ēṟṟi
		ūṉacip 3piṇaviṉ uyaṅkupaci kaḷiiyar
	10.	taḷirpuri koṭiṟṟiṟ ceṟimayir eruttiṟ
		katirtta ceṉṉik kavirppū aṉṉa
		neṟṟic cēval aṟṟam pārkkum
		pulleṉ māliyum iṉitumaṉ ṟamma
		nallaka vaṉamuli aṭiyap pullutoṟu
	15.	uyirkuḻip paṉṉa cāyaṟ
		ceytīr iṉṟuṇip puṇarntici ṉōrkkē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) tōḻi, 14-16.  nal akam vaṉa muli aṭiya pullutoṟu - nalla mārpakattē yuḷḷa aḻakiya muliyiṉip poruntap pulluntoṟum, uyir kuḻippu aṉṉa - uyir kuḻivatu pōlum, cāyal - meṉmi vāynta, ceyirtīr iṉ tuṇi - kuṟṟamaṟṟa iṉiya tuṇivari, puṇarnticiṉōrkku - kūṭiyiruppavarkaṭku,
1-13. ilaṅku cuṭar maṇṭilam - viḷaṅkum oḷi vāynta ñāyiṟu, pulam tali peyarntu - vāṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, pal katir maḻukiya kal cēr amiyattu - taṉatu palavākiya katirkaḷum vemmi kuṟiyap peṟṟa mēṟku maliyiṉi aṭiyum pōḻtil, alantali mutu ēṟu - kalakka muṟṟa mutiya ēṟu, āṇkural viḷippa - taṉatu āṇ kural tōṉṟa aḻittiṭa, maṉi vaḷar nocci mā cērpu vatiya - maṉiyic cūḻntu vaḷarum nocciyiṉkaṇ māṉkaḷ cērntu taṅkiyirukka, muṉi uḻi irunta am kuṭi ciṟu ūr - kāṭṭaraṇkaḷiṉ pakkattēyirunta aḻakiya kuṭiyirup pukkaḷiyuṭiya ciṟṟūriṉkaṇṇē, karu kāl vēṅki ceñ cuval varakiṉ - kariya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya vēṅkimaram cūḻnta civanta mēṭṭu nilattē viḷinta varakākiya, miku patam niṟinta toku kūṭṭu oru ciṟi - mikka tāṉiyam niṟinta tokutiyākavuḷḷa kūṭṭiṉ oru pakkattē, kuvi aṭi piṅ kaṇ verukiṉ ēṟṟi - kuvinta aṭiyiṉiyum paciya kaṇṇiṉiyu muṭiya kāṭṭup pūṉiyiṉ āṇ, ūṉ naci piṇaviṉ uyaṅku paci kaḷiiyar - ūṉi virumpiyuḷḷa peṇ pūṉiyiṉ varuttum paciyiṉi nīkkumāṟu, taḷirpuri koṭiṟṟiṉ - taḷiriyotta kaṉṉat tiṉiyum, ceṟi mayir eruttiṉ - neruṅkiya mayiriṉiyuṭiya kaḻuttiṉiyum, katirtta ceṉṉi - nimirtta taliyiṉkaṇ, kavir pū aṉṉa neṟṟi - murukkam pūviṉi yotta cūṭṭiṉiyu muṭiya, cēval aṟṟam pārkkum - kōḻic cēvalip paṟṟum amiyattiṉip pārttirukkum, pul eṉ māliyum - polivaṟṟa im mālip pōtum, maṉṟa iṉitu - uṟutiyāka iṉitākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! iṉ tuṇip puṇarnticiṉōrkkup pulleṉ māliyum maṉṟa iṉitu.
(vi-ri) maṇṭilam peyarntu kal cēr amiyam eṉavum, pal katir maḻukiya amiyam eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. alantali- kalakkam; mutumiyālāyatu; vēṭṭuvarāṟ peṇmāṉukkut tīṅkuṇṭākumō eṉṟa kalakkamumām; alantatali eṉpataṉ vikāramumām. ēṟu viḷittalāl mā cērpu vatiya eṉka. viḷippa eṉṉum eccam kāraṇap poruṭṭāyatu. nocci mā cērpu vatiya, verukiṉ ēṟṟi cēval aṟṟam pārkkum pulleṉ māli eṉka. veruku mālip poḻutil kōḻiyip paṟṟi yuṇṇum iyalpiṉatu eṉpatu, `kōḻik kuṟuṅkāṟ pēṭi, vēli verukiṉam māli yuṟṟeṉa, pukumiṭa ṉaṟiyātu tokupuṭaṉ kuḻīip, pitaṟ piḷḷik kiḷipayirn tāaṅku'1 eṉṟataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. māliyum, eṉṟa ummi iḻivu ciṟappu. maṉṟa eṉpataṉ īṟu tokkatu. puṇarnticiṉōrkku māliyum  iṉitu eṉṟataṉāl, talivaṉip pirintuḷḷa yāṉ, ittakiya mālip poḻutil eṅṅaṉam āṟṟiyiruppēṉ eṉa vaṟpuṟukkum tōḻikkut talimakaḷ etiraḻintu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
368. kuṟiñci
(pakalē ciṟippuṟamākat talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyt tōḻi colliyatu.)
		toṭutōṟ kāṉavaṉ cūṭuṟu viyaṉpuṉam
		karipuṟaṅ kaḻīiya perumpāṭ ṭīrattut
		tōṭuvaḷar pintiṉi nīṭukural kākkum
		oṇṭoṭi makaḷirkku ūcal āka
	5.	āṭuciṉi yoḻitta 1kōṭiṇar kañaliya
		kuṟumpoṟi ayalatu neṭuntāḷ vēṅki
		maṭamayiṟ kuṭumiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum nāṭaṉ
		uyarvari maruṅkiṟ kāntaḷañ cōlik
		kuraṅkaṟi vārā marampayil iṟumpiṟ
	10.	kaṭicuṉit teḷinta maṇimaruḷ tīnīr
		piṭipuṇar kaḷiṟṟiṉ emmo ṭāṭip
		2paṉṉāḷ umparp peyarntu 3ciṉṉāḷ
		kaḻiyā miyē vaḻivaḻip peruki
		ampaṇi viḷinta tēkkaṭ ṭēṟal
	15.	vaṇṭupaṭu kaṇṇiyar makiḻum cīṟūr
		evaṉkol vāḻi tōḻi koṅkar
		maṇiyari yāttu maṟukiṉ āṭum
		uḷḷi viḻaviṉ aṉṉa
		alarā kiṉṟatu palarvāyp paṭṭē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 16. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-7. toṭu tōl kāṉavaṉ cūṭu uṟu viyaṉ puṉam - kālilē ceruppiṉi aṇinta vēṭaṉ cuṭṭeritta akaṉṟa kolliyil, kari puṟam kaḻīiya perumpāṭṭu īrattu - karinta iṭaṅkaḷik kaḻuvic ceṉṟa mikka peyalāya maḻi īrattil, tōṭu vaḷar pin tiṉi nīṭu kural kākkum - itaḻkaḷ vaḷarnta paciya tiṉiyiṉ nīṇṭa katirkaḷik kāttirukkum, oḷ toṭi makaḷirkku ūcal āka āṭu ciṉi oḻitta - oḷi poruntiya vaḷiyali aṇinta makaḷir tamakku ūcal ākak koṇṭu āṭum kiḷikaḷip pōkkiya, kōṭu iṇar kañaliya - kompilē pūṅkottukkaḷ neruṅkiya, kuṟum poṟi ayalatu - kuṉṟiṉ pakkattiluḷḷatākiya, neṭu tāḷ vēṅki - nīṇṭa aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya vēṅkimaram, maṭa mayil kuṭumiyil tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ - iḷiya mayiliṉ ucciyiluḷḷa kuṭumi pōlat tōṉṟum mali nāṭṭiyuṭiya nam talivaṉ,
8-13. uyar vari maruṅkil kāntaḷ am cōli - uyarnta malip pakkattēyuḷḷa kāntaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya cōliyākiya, kuraṅku aṟi vārā maram payil iṟumpil - kuraṅkukaḷum ēṟi aṟital illāta nīṇṭa maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta kāṭṭil, kaṭi cuṉi teḷinta maṇi maruḷ tīm nīr - viḷakkam poruntiya cuṉiyil uḷḷa teḷinta paḷiṅkiṉi yotta iṉiya nīril, piṭi puṇar kaḷiṟṟiṉ emmoṭu palnāḷ āṭi - piṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya kaḷiṟu pōla nammuṭaṉ pala nāḷum āṭi, umpar peyarntu - nammip pirintu vēṟiṭam ceṉṟu, ciṉṉāḷ kaḻiyāmiyē - cila nāḷ kaḻitaṟku muṉpē, vaḻivaḻi peruki - an nikaḻcci muṟimuṟiyākap peruki,
14-15. am paṇi viḷinta tēṉ kaḷ tēṟal - aḻakiya mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyil muṟṟiya tēṉākiya kaḷḷiṉ teḷivi, vaṇṭu paṭu kaṇṇiyar makiḻum cīṟūr - vaṇṭu moykkum kaṇṇiyi yuṭiyārāy uṇṭu makiḻum ciṟṟūriṉkaṇṇē,
16-19. palar vāy paṭṭu - palar vāyilum pēcappaṭṭu, koṅkar maṇi ari yāttu maṟukiṉ āṭum - koṅku nāṭṭiṉar maṇiyiṉi iṭiyiṟ kaṭṭik koṇṭu teruvil āṭum, uḷḷi viḻaviṉ aṉṉa - uḷḷi viḻaviṉ āravāram pōṉṟa, alar ākiṉṟatu - alarākā niṉṟatu; evaṉkol - iḵteṉṉiyō!
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻi! vēṅki maṭamayir kuṭumiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum nāṭaṉ, cuṉit tī nīr paṉṉāḷ emmoṭāṭi, peyarntu ciṉṉāḷ kaḻiyāmi, annikaḻcci, vaḻivaḻip peruki, palar vāyppaṭṭu, uḷḷiviḻaviṉ aṉṉa alarākiṉṟatu evaṉkol.
(vi-ri) āṭu ciṉi, pakkaṅkaḷiṟ paṭinta kiḷi yeṉṟum, kōṭu, mēlē nivanta kompeṉṟuṅ koḷka. pintiṉi kākkum makaḷirkku ūcalāka āṭu ciṉi yoḻitta eṉṟatu, iṉit tām tiṉippuṉam kākkumāṟu illi eṉṟataṉik kuṟippiṉāl uṇarttiyapaṭiyām. iṇar kañaliya vēṅki eṉavum kuṟum poṟi ayalatākiya vēṅki eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. cōliyākiya iṟumpu eṉka. aṉṟi, cōlikkaṇṇatākiya iṟumpu eṉṟalumām. kuraṅkum eṉṉum ummi tokkatu. aṟivārā - aṟiyalākāta. an nikaḻcci vaḻivaḻip peruki yeṉa varuvitturikka. koṅkar - koṅku nāṭṭiṉar. talivaṉ kēṭṭu varintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṉum karuttāl alarākiṉṟatu evaṉkol eṉat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷpōṟ kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
369. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevili colliyatu.)
		kaṇṭiciṉ 1makaḷē keḻīi iyiveṉi
		oṇṭoṭi ceṟitta muṉki ūḻkoḷpu
		maṅkiyar palapā rāṭṭac centārk
		kiḷḷiyun tīmpāl uṇṇā mayiliyaṟ
	5.	cēyiḻi makaḷir āyamum ayarā
		tāḻiyum malarpala aṇiyā kēḻkoḷa
		2kāḻpuṉin tiyaṟṟiya vaṉappami nōṉcuvarp
		pāviyum paliyeṉap peṟāa nōypora
		ivikaṇ ṭiṉivataṉ taliyum niṉivilēṉ
	10.	koṭiyōḷ muṉṉiya tuṇarēṉ toṭiyōy
		iṉṟuniṉ olikural maṇṇal eṉṟataṟku
		eṟpulan taḻintaṉa ḷākit taṟṟakak
		kaṭalan tāṉik kivaṇ cōḻar
		keṭalaru nallici uṟanti yaṉṉa
	15.	nitiyuṭi naṉṉakarp putuvatu puṉintu
		tamarmaṇaṉ ayaravum ollāḷ kavarmutal
		ōmi nīṭiya ulavi nīḷiṭi
		maṇiyaṇi palaki mākkāḻ neṭuvēl
		tuṇivuṭi uḷḷamoṭu tutinta muṉpiṉ
	20.	aṟiyāt tēettu aruñcuram maṭutta
		ciṟiyōṟ kottaveṉ perumaṭat takuvi
		ciṟappuñ cīrum iṉṟic cīṟūr
		nalkūr peṇṭiṉ pulvēy kurampi
		3ōrā yātta oru tūṇ muṉṟil
	25.	ētil vaṟumaṉic cilampuṭaṉ kaḻīi
		mēyiṉaḷ kolleṉa nōval yāṉē.
- nakkīrar.
(co-ḷ) 1. makaḷē-, keḻīi iyiveṉi - avaḷuṭaṉ porunti iyintiruntēṉuṭiya niliyiṉi, kaṇṭiciṉ - kāṇ pāyāka;
2-8. oḷ toṭi ceṟitta muṉ ki ūḻ koḷpu - oḷi poruntiya vaḷiyalic ceṟitta muṉ kiyil muṟiyāṉē koṇṭu, maṅkiyar pala pārāṭṭa - cūḻa niṉṟa maṅkiyar palavāṟākap pārāṭṭa, centārk kiḷḷiyum - civanta irēkiyiṉi
yuṭiya kiḷikaḷum, tīmpāl uṇṇā - iṉiya pāli uṇkiṉṟila, mayil iyal cēy iḻi makaḷir āyamum - mayil pōlum cāyaliyuṭiya civanta aṇikaḷi yaṇinta tōḻiyar kūṭṭaṅ
kaḷum, ayarā - viḷiyāṭukiṉṟila, tāḻiyum malar pala aṇiyā - pūñceṭi yuṭiya tāḻikaḷum pala malarkaḷik koṇṭila, kēḻ koḷa kāḻ puṉintu iyaṟṟiya vaṉappu ami nōṉcuvar pāviyum pali eṉa peṟāa - aḻakiya niṟam porunta muttuvaṭam mutaliya puṉintu ceyta aḻaku aminta valiya cuvariṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa pāvikaḷum pali eṉa etaṉiyum koṇṭila,
8-9. ivi kaṇṭu nōy pora iṉivataṉ taliyum - ivikaḷik kaṇṭu nōy varutta varuntuvataṉ mēlum,
9-12. niṉivilēṉ - cūḻcci yillēṉākiya yāṉ, koṭiyōḷ muṉṉiyatu uṇarēṉ - koṭiyaḷākiya avaḷ eṇṇiyati uṇarēṉāki, toṭiyōy - toṭiyaṇinta makaḷē, iṉṟu niṉ oli kural maṇṇal eṉṟataṟku - iṉṟu niṉatu taḻitta kūntalip puṉika eṉṟataṟku, eṉ pulantu aḻintaṉaḷ āki - eṉṉi veṟuttu maṉam uṭintavaḷāki,
12-16. taṉ taka - avaḷ taṉ celva nilikkēṟṟa paricu, kaṭal tāṉi ki vaṇ cōḻar - kaṭal pōṉṟa cēṉiyiṉiyum kivaṇ miyiyum  uṭiya cōḻaratu, keṭal arum nal ici uṟanti aṉṉa - nīṅkutal illāta nalla pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya uṟiyūr pōṉṟa, niti uṭi nal nakar putuvatu puṉintu - celvamuṭiya nalla maṉiyil putuvatāka oppaṉi ceytu, tamar maṇaṉ ayaravum ollāḷ - taṉ tamarāvār maṇam ceyvikkavum maṉam poruntāta
vaḷāki,
16-21. kavar mutal ōmi nīṭiya ulavi nīḷ iṭi - kavartta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ōmi maraṅkaḷ uyarntuḷḷa kāṭākiya nīṇṭa iṭattil, maṇi aṇi palaki - maṇikaḷ patitta pariciyiṉiyum, mā kāḻ neṭu vēl - ciṟanta kāmpiṉiyuṭiya nīṇṭa vēliṉi yum, tuṇivu uṭi uḷḷamoṭu - tuṇivu taṅkiya neñcamuṭaṉ, tutinta muṉpiṉ - mikka mey valiyiṉiyum uṭiya, aṟiyā tēettu aru curam maṭutta - aṟiyappaṭāta tēyattiṉkaṇ ariya cura neṟiyil koṇṭupōya, ciṟiyōṟku otta eṉ peru maṭat takuvi - iḷiyāṉukkup poruntiya periya maṭappamum takutiyumuṭiya eṉ makaḷ,
22-26. ciṟappum cīrum iṉṟi - naṉku matippum uyarvum illātu, cīṟūr nalkūr peṇṭiṉ - ciṟiya ūril vaṟumiyuṟṟa peṇṭiṉatu, pul vēy kurampi - pul vēynta kuṭilāya, ōr ā yātta - oru pacu kaṭṭiyuḷḷa, oru tūṇ muṉṟil - oṟṟit tūṇ koṇṭa mukappiṉiyuṭiya, ētu il vaṟu maṉi - iyipillāta vaṟiya maṉiyil, cilampu uṭaṉ kaḻīi mēyiṉaḷ kol eṉa - cilampu kaḻittu avaṉuṭaṉ maṇam poruntiṉaḷō eṉṟu, yāṉ nōval - yāṉ varuntuvēṉ.
(muṭipu) makaḷē! keḻīi iyiveṉik kaṇṭiciṉ; kiḷḷiyum tīmpāl uṇṇā; āyamum ayarā; tāḻiyum malar pala aṇiyā; pāviyum paliyeṉap peṟāa; ivikaṇṭu iṉivataṉ ṟaliyum, niṉivilēṉ uṇarēṉ, olikural maṇṇal eṉṟataṟku aḻintaṉaḷāki, tamar maṇaṉ ayaravum ollāḷ, aruñ curam maṭutta ciṟiyōrkku otta eṉ peru maṭat takuvi, cīṟūr ētil vaṟumaṉic cilampuṭaṉ kaḻīi mēyiṉaḷ kol leṉa yāṉ nōval.
(vi - ri) makaḷē eṉṟatu tōḻiyi. keḻīi iyiveṉi taliviyuṭaṉ keḻutakimi pūṇṭu poruntiyirunta eṉatu niliyiṉi eṉka. pārāṭṭa - pārāṭṭavum, kiḷḷi pāl uṇṇāmi mutaliyaṉa taliviyiṉ pirivāl nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷ, ōraṟivu uyirākiya tāḻip pūñceṭi mutal teyvam īṟākiya  aṉittuyirum taliviyiṉ pirivāl varuntiṉami uṇarttiya vāṟu kāṇka. ivikaṇṭu iṉivataṉṟaliyum, mēyiṉaḷ kolleṉa nōval eṉak kūṭṭuka. toṭiyōy eṉṟatu talimakaḷic cevili viḷittatu. maṇṇal - maṇṇuka eṉa viyaṅkōḷ. taṟṟakat tamar maṇam ayaravum eṉka. nakarp putuvatu puṉintu - maṉiyip putuvatāka oppaṉi ceytu eṉṟumām. palakiyiṉiyum vēliṉiyum uḷḷamoṭu muṉpiṉiyum uṭiya ciṟiyōṉ eṉavum ollāḷ ciṟiyōṟku otta eṉavum kūṭṭuka. ciṟappu - naṉku matippu; cīr - uyarvu. `cīrmi ciṟappoṭu- nīṅkum'1 eṉpuḻip parimēlaḻakar urittavāṟu kāṇka. cilampuṭaṉ kaḻīi eṉpataṟkuk 2`kūṭa laṉṉataṉ, aruṅkaṭi viyaṉakarc cilampuṅ kaḻiyāḷ' eṉpuḻi uritta viḷakkavuri kāṇka.
370. neytal
(pakalē ciṟippuṟamākat talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyt tōḻi colliyatu.)
		vaḷivāyk kōtiyar vaṇṭal tii
		iḷiyōr celpa ellum elliṉṟu
		akalilip puṉṉip pukaril nīḻaṟ
		pakalē emmo ṭāṭi iravē
	5.	kāyal vēynta tēyā nallil
		nōyoṭu vikuti yāyiṉ nunti
		aruṅkaṭip paṭuvalum eṉṟi maṟṟunī
		cellal eṉṟalum āṟṟāy celiṉē
		vāḻaleṉ eṉṟi yāyiṉ ñāḻal
	10.	vaṇṭupaṭat tatinta kaṇṇi neytal
		taṇṇarum pintār tuyalvara antik
		kaṭalkeḻu celvi kariniṉ ṟāṅku
		nīyē kāṉal oḻiya yāṉē
		veṟikoḷ pāviyiṟ polintaveṉ aṇituṟantu
	15. 	āṭumakaḷ pōlap peyartal
		āṟṟēṉ teyya alarkaviv vūrē.
- ammūvaṉār.
(co-ḷ) tōḻi! 1-2. vaḷi vāy kōtiyar iḷiyōr - vaḷivu koṇṭa māliyi yuṭiya iḷamakaḷir, vaṇṭal tii  celpa - vaṇṭal viḷiyāṭṭiṉic ceytu mīṇṭu cellā niṟpar, ellum elliṉṟu - pakalum oḷi kuṟintatu,
3-7. pakalē - pakaṟpoḻutil, akal ili puṉṉi pukar il nīḻal - akaṉṟa ilikaḷiyuṭiya puṉṉiyiṉ puḷḷi yillāta niḻalil, emmoṭu āṭi - eṅkaḷuṭaṉ viḷiyāṭi, iravē - irāp poḻutil, kāyal vēynta tēyā nal il - kāynta pullāl vēyap peṟṟa vaḷaṅ kuṉṟāta nalla namatu illiṉkaṇ, nōyoṭu vikuti yāyiṉ - varuttattuṭaṉ porunti yiruppā yāyiṉ, nunti aruṅ kaṭi paṭuval eṉṟi - uṉatu tantiyiṉ ariya kāvaṟ paṭuvēṉ eṉkiṉṟaṉi,
7-9. maṟṟu nī cellal eṉṟalum āṟṟāy - aṉṟi nī illiṟkuc celka eṉṟu nāṉ kūṟutaliyum poṟāyāki, celiṉē vāḻaleṉ eṉṟi - celliṉ uyir vāḻēṉ eṉkiṉṟaṉi, āyiṉ - aṅṅaṉa māyiṉ, 
9-16. ñāḻal vaṇṭu paṭa tatinta kaṇṇi - pulinakak koṉṟiyatu vaṇṭu moykka malarnta malark kaṇṇiyuṭaṉ, neytal taṇ arum pintār tuyalvara - neytaliṉ taṇṇiya ariya paciya malarmāli aciya, anti - antikkālattē, kaṭal keḻu celvi kari niṉṟāṅku - kaṭaṟ ṟeyvam kariyiṉkaṇ niṟpatupōla, nī kāṉal oḻiya - nī ik kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil taṉittu niṟka, yāṉ, veṟi koḷ pāviyil polinta eṉ aṇi tuṟantu - veṟiyāṭṭam mēviya makaḷ pōṉṟu polivuṟṟa eṉatu aḻakiṉit tuṟantu, āṭu makaḷ pōlap peyartal āṟṟēṉ - avaḷ piṉ vaṟitē peyartal pōla nīṅkutal iyalātavaḷākiṉṟēṉ, i ūr alarka - ivvūr alar kūṟiṉum kūṟuka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! puṉṉi nīḻal pakal emmoṭāṭi, iravu nal il nōyoṭu vikutiyāyiṉ, nunti aruṅkaṭip paṭuval eṉṟi, maṟṟu, celiṉ vāḻaleṉ eṉṟi, āyiṉ kaṭalkeḻu celvi kari niṉṟāṅku nī kāṉal oḻiya yāṉ eṉ aṇi tuṟantu āṭumakaḷ pōlap peyartal āṟṟēṉ. ivvūr alarka.
(vi-ri) kōtiyar āya iḷiyōr eṉka. iḷiyōr eṉṟatu ēṉi āyattāri. iḷiyōr celpa, ellum elliṉṟu eṉṟatu nāmum cella vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku ētuk kūṟiyapaṭi. 
kāyal - kāynta pul; al, peyar vikuti. celpa; elliṉṟu; ākaliṉ pakal emmoṭu āṭiya nī iravu nallil cellutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻikku iravu illiṟ ceṉṟu nōyoṭu taṅkuvēṉ āyiṉ, eṉ tantiyiṉ kāvaliṟ paṭuvēṉ eṉṟu talivi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. vikuvē ṉāyiṉ enti aruṅkaṭip paṭuval eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟit tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, ataṟkēṟpa vikutiyāyiṉ eṉavum, nunti eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. paṭuvalum - ummi acinili. cellal eṉṟalum āṟṟāy eṉṟatu, celiṉ vāḻaleṉ eṉpataṟku aṭiyāy vantatu. talivaṉit talippeyyātu celliṉ uyir vāḻēṉ eṉṟāḷākak koḷka. nīyē, yāṉē eṉpavaṟṟu ēkāraṅkaḷ kaṭṭuric cuvipaṭa niṉṟaṉa, piriniliyumām. veṟikoḷ pāvi - veṟiyāṭum makaḷ. āṭu 
makaḷ - peyar māttiriyāy niṉṟatu. teyya - aciccol.
371. pāli
(poruḷ vayiṟ pirintu pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		avviḷim purīiya viciyami nōṉcilic
		cevvāyp pakaḻic ceyirnōk kāṭavar
		kaṇiyiṭak kaḻintataṉ vīḻtuṇi uḷḷik
		kuṟuneṭun tuṇiya maṟipuṭi yāṭap
	5.	puṉkaṇ koṇṭa tirimarup pirali
		mēypatam maṟutta ciṟumiyoṭu nōykūrntu
		neytalam paṭuviṟ ciṉṉīr uṇṇātu
		eḵkuṟu māntariṉ iṉintukaṇ paṭukkum
		pitaṟa vempiya pāḻcēr attam
	10.	1emiyam nīntum emmiṉum paṉivārntu
		eṉṉa āṅkol tāmē teṇṇīr
		āycuṉi nikarmalar pōṉmeṉa nacii
		vītēr paṟavi viḻiyum
		pōtār kūntaṉaṅ kātali kaṇṇē.
- eyiṉanti makaṉ iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē-, 11-14. teḷ nīr āy cuṉi nikar malar pōṉmeṉa - teḷinta nīriṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya cuṉiyiluḷḷa oḷi poruntiya malarip pōlum eṉa, naci i vī tēr paṟavi viḻiyum - tēṉi virumpip pūkkaḷi ārāyum vaṇṭukaḷ viruppuṟum, pōtu ār kūntal nam kātali kaṇ - malar niṟinta kūntaliyuṭiya nam kātaliyiṉ kaṇkaḷ,
1-9. a viḷimpu urīiya vici ami nōṉ cili - aḻakiya viḷimpiṉi uruvi nāṇ ēṟṟiya vici aminta valiya villiṉi yum, cev vāy pakaḻi - kurutiyāṟ civanta vāyiṉiyuṭiya ampiṉiyum uṭiya, ceyir nōkku āṭavar - ciṉam taṅkiya pārviyiṉi yuṭiya maṟavar, kaṇi iṭa kaḻinta - ampiṉi eytiṭalāṉ iṟanta, taṉ vīḻ tuṇi uḷḷi - taṉatu kātalmikka piṇiyiṉi niṉittu, kuṟu neṭu tuṇiya maṟi puṭi āṭa - kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum ākiya aḷaviṉiyuṭiya taṉ kuṭṭikaḷ (ētam uṇarāmiyiṉ) taṉ pakkalil viḷiyāṭa, puṉkaṇ koṇṭa tiri maruppu irali - tuṉpiṉiyaṭinta muṟukkuṇṭa kōṭṭiṉi yuṭiya āṇmāṉ, mēy patam maṟutta ciṟumiyoṭu nōy kūrntu - mēyum uṇaviṉi veṟutta tuṉpattuṭaṉ varuttam mikku, neytal paṭuvil - kaḷar nilattiluḷḷa ciṟiya kuḻiyil taṅkiya, cil nīr uṇṇātu - ciṟiya nīrum parukātu, eḵku uṟum māntariṉ iṉintu kaṇ paṭukkum - ampu tikkappeṟṟa makkaḷip pōla varuntik kaṇṇayarntu kiṭakkum, pitu aṟa vempiya pāḻ cēr attam - pacumiyaṟak kotitta pāḻpaṭṭa cura neṟiyiṉi,
10-11. emiyam nīntum emmiṉum - tamiyēmāyk kaṭantu cellum nammik kāṭṭiṉum, paṉi vārntu - nīr oḻukap peṟṟu, eṉṉa āṅkol - ettaṉmiyāṉa tuṉpattiṉi eytā niṟkumō.
(muṭipu) neñcē! nam kātali kaṇkaḷ pāḻcēr attam emiyam nīntum emmiṉum, paṉivārntu eṉṉa ām kol.
(vi-ri) maṟi puṭi āṭa - kuṭṭikaḷ iṟanta peṇmāṉiṉ pakkattē viḷiyāṭa eṉṟumām. uḷḷip puṉkaṇ koṇṭa irali nōy kūrntu ciṉṉīr uṇṇātu iṉintu kaṇpaṭukkum attam eṉka. taliviyip pirintu koṭiya kāṭṭiṉit tamiyamāyk kaṭantu cellutalāl nām eytum tuṉpattik kāṭṭilum nammip pirintuṟiyum kātaliyiṉ kaṇkaḷ eṉṉa tuṉpattiṉi eytumō eṉat taliviyip pirintu ēkutaliṉ iṉṉāmi pulappaṭat talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
372. kuṟiñci
(alla kuṟippaṭṭup pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ, taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		1arunteṟaṉ marapiṟ kaṭavuḷ kāppap
		peruntēṉ tūṅkum nāṭukāṇ naṉantali
