		aṇaṅkuṭi varippiṟ pāḻi āṅkaṇ
		vēṇmutu mākkaḷ viyaṉakark karanta
	5.	aruṅkala veṟukkiyiṉ ariyōḷ paṇpuniṉintu
		varuntiṉam mātō eṉiṉumaḵ tollāy
		irumpaṇit toṭutta palarā ṭūcal
		ūrntiḻi kayiṟṟiṟ celavara varunti
		neṭuneṟik kutirik kūrvēl añci
	10.	kaṭumuṉi 2yalitta koṭuvi lāṭavar
		3ākoḷ pūcaliṟ pāṭuciṟan teṟiyum
		peruntuṭi vaḷpiṉ vīṅkupu nekiḻā
		mēymaṇi yiḻanta pāmpiṉ nīnaṉi
		tēmpiṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē vēntar
	15.	4kōṇṭaṇi eyiliṟ kāppuc ciṟantu
		īṇṭaruṅ kuriyaṇam aṇaṅki yōḷē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ)  14. vāḻi eṉ neñcē - eṉ neñcē vāḻi!
1-6. aru teṟal marapiṉ kaṭavuḷ kāppa - piṟarāl teṟutaṟku ariya muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiya kaṭavuḷ kāttaliṉ, peru tēṉ tūṅkum nāṭu kāṇ naṉantali - periya tēṉ kūṭukaḷ toṅkuvatum nāṭṭiṉ elliyiṉik kāṇṭaṟkuriya uyarcciyiṉiyum akaṉṟa iṭattiṉiyum uṭiyatumākiya, aṇaṅku uṭi varippiṉ - accam poruntiya pakkamaliyiṉiyuṭiya, pāḻi āṅkaṇ - pāḻimaliyiṭattē, vēḷ mutu mākkaḷ viyaṉ nakark karanta - paḻiya vēḷkuṭi makkaḷ tamatu akaṉṟa ūriṉkaṇ maṟittu vitta, aruṅkala veṟukkiyiṉ ariyōḷ paṇpu niṉintu - ariya aṇikalamākiya celvattippōlap peṟutaṟku arumiyuṭiyōḷatu iṉpatti niṉintu, varuntiṉam  eṉiṉum - nām vāḷātē varuntiyuḷḷōm eṉṟu yāṉ kūṟavum, aḵtu ollāy - ataṉi nī ki viṭutaṟkup poruntāyāy,
7-14. iru paṇi toṭutta palar āṭu ūcal - periya kiḷiyil kaṭṭap peṟṟa palarum āṭum ūcaliṉatu, ūrntu iḻi kayiṟṟil cela vara varunti - ēṟiyum iṟaṅkiyum āṭum kayiṟṟiṉip pōṉṟu celkiyālum varukiyālum varunti, neṭu neṟi kutiri kūr vēl añci - nīṇṭa vaḻiyiṉiyuṭiya kutirimalikkut talivaṉākiya kūriya vēliyuṭiya añci eṉpāṉatu, kaṭu muṉi alitta koṭu vil āṭavar - kaṭiya pakippulattiṉi varuttiya vaḷinta villiyuṭiya vīrar, ā koḷ pūcalil pāṭu ciṟantu eṟiyum - tam pakivar nirikaḷik kavarum pōril oli mika aṭikkum, peru tuṭi vaḷpiṉ vīṅkupu nekiḻā - periya uṭukkiyiṉ vārip pōlac ceṟintum nekiḻntum, mēy maṇi iḻanta pāmpiṉ- mēytaṟporuṭṭu umiḻntu vitta maṇiyiṉi iḻanta pāmpip pōla, nī naṉi tēmpiṉi - nī mikavum vāṭutaluṟṟaṉi,
14-16. nam aṇaṅkiyōḷ - nammi varuttiya kātali, vēntar kōṇ taṇi eyilil - paki vēntaratu māṟupāṭu taṇitaṟku ētuvākiya matil pōla, kāppu ciṟantu - kāvalmikku, īṇṭu aruṅkuriyaḷ - eytutaṟku arumiyi yuṭiyavaḷ aṉṟō.
(muṭipu) eṉ neñcē vāḻi! ariyōḷ paṇpu niṉintu varuntiṉam eṉiṉum, aḵtu ollāy, celavara varunti, vīṅkupu nekiḻā, maṇiyiḻanta pāmpiṉ nī naṉi tēmpiṉi, nam aṇaṅkiyōḷ kāppuc ciṟantu īṇṭu aruṅ kuriyaḷ.
(vi-ri) aruntiṟal eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, ariya āṟṟaliyuṭiya eṉka. kaṭavuḷ kāttalāl piṟarāṟ kavarappaṭātu tēṉiṟāl tūṅkum eṉka. nāṭu kāṇ naṉantali - `taṉmēl ēṟi nāṭṭik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟutaṟku ētuvākiya ōkka'mum akaṟciyu
muṭiya iṭam eṉka. `kōṭu pala virinta nāṭukāṇ neṭuvari'1 eṉpataṉ paḻiya uri kāṇka. pāḻimaliyiṭattatāya nakarilē vēḷmutumākkaḷ karantu vitta veṟukki eṉka. aḵtu ollāy - ataṉik kiviṭutaṟkup poruntāyāy eṉa viritturikka. neṭu neṟik kutiri - uyarntu cellum neṟiyiyuṭiya kutiri; āvatu kutirimali; itu veḷippaṭi. kaṭumuṉi alitta - muṉ pala pōrkaḷil pakivari varuttiya eṉka. añciyiṉ āṭavar māṟṟār āviṉik kavarntu varum pūcalil eṟiyum tuṭi eṉka. pāmpu mēyuṅkāl maṇiyiṉi umiḻum eṉṉum karuttu, 2`nāmanal larāk katirpaṭa umiḻnta, mēymaṇi viḷakkil' eṉa muṉṉarum pōntuḷatu kāṇka.
373. pāli
(pirintupōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ iṭiccurattu taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		muṉikavarntu koṇṭeṉak kalaṅkip pīreḻuntu
		maṉipāḻ paṭṭa maricēr maṉṟattup
		1paṇittāḷ yāṉi parūuppuṟa 2muriñcac
		cetukāḻ cāynta mutukāṟ potiyil
	5.	aruñcuram nīntiya varuttamoṭu kiyaṟṟup
		perumpuṉ māli pulampuvan tuṟutara
		mīḷi uḷḷañ celavuvali yuṟuppat
		tāḷki pūṭṭiya taṉinili yirukkiyoṭu
		taṉṉili yuḷḷum nannili yuṇarāḷ
	10.	irumpal kūntaṟ cēyiḻi maṭanti
		kaṉiyiruḷ naṭunāḷ aṇiyoṭu porunti
		veytuṟṟup pulakkum neñcamo ṭituyirā
		āyitaḻ maḻikkaṇ malka nōykūrntu
		peruntōḷ naṉikkuṅ kaluḻntuvār arippaṉi
	15.	melviral ukiriṟ ṟeṟiyiṉaḷ veṉvēl
		aṇṇal yāṉi aṭupōr vēntar
		oruṅkakap paṭutta muravuvāy ñāyil
		ōreyiṉ maṉṉaṉ pōlat
		tuyiṟuṟan taṉaḷkol aḷiyaḷ tāṉē
- pāṇṭiyaṉ ēṉāti neṭuṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē! 1-9. muṉi kavarntu koṇṭeṉa - pōrmuṉi akappaṭuttikkoṇṭatāka, kalaṅki - kalakkamuṟṟu, pīr eḻuntu - pīrkkup paṭarntu, maṉi pāḻpaṭṭa mari cēr maṉṟattu - maṉikaḷ pāḻpaṭṭup pōṉa marimā vantaṭiyum maṉṟattiṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa, paṇitāḷ yāṉi parūup puṟam uriñca- parutta kāliṉi yuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ pariya mutuku uriñcutalāl, cetu kāḻ cāynta mutu kāl potiyil - cōrnta viṭṭam vīḻnta mutiya tūṇkaḷi yuṭiya ampalattē, aru curam nīntiya varuttamoṭu kiyaṟṟu - ariya curattik kaṭantu vanta varuttattāl ceyalaṟṟu, peru puṉ māli pulampu vantu uṟutara - periya puṟkeṉṟa mālik kālattē taṉimi vantaṭiya, mīḷi uḷḷam celavu valiyuṟuppa - tiṇmiyi yuṭiya nam uḷḷam mīṇṭu cellutali vaṟpuṟutta, tāḷ ki pūṭṭiya taṉinili irukkiyoṭu - muḻantāḷkaḷik kiyāṟ pūṭṭiya taṉitta niliyiṉatākiya iruppuṭaṉ, taṉ nili uḷḷum - taṉatu niliyiṉiyē eṇṇiyirukkum, nam nili uṇarāḷ - namatu iyalpiṉi aṟiyāḷāki,
10-19. iru pal kūntal cēy iḻi maṭanti - nīṇṭa palavāya kūntaliyum civanta aṇiyiṉiyumuṭiya nam talivi, kaṉi iruḷ naṭu nāḷ - ceṟinta iruḷiyuṭiya naṭu iravil, aṇiyoṭu porunti - aṇiyiṉkaṇ kiṭantu, veytu uṟṟu pulakkum neñcamoṭu - kotippuṟṟu veṟukkum uḷḷattuṭaṉ, aitu uyirā - metteṉa uyirttu, āy itaḻ maḻi kaṇ - aḻakiya pūvitaḻ pōṉṟa kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ,  malka nōy kūrntu - nīr peruka varutta mikku, peru tōḷ naṉikkum kaluḻntu vār ari paṉi - periya tōḷiṉi naṉiyac ceyyumāṟu aḻutaliṉ oḻukum nīrt tuḷikaḷi, mel viral ukiriṉ teṟiyiṉaḷ - melliya viral nakattāl teṟittu, veṉ vēl aṇṇal yāṉi aṭu pōr vēntar - veṉṟi vēliṉiyum talimi koṇṭa yāṉiyiṉi yum aṭum pōriṉiyum uṭiya aracar palar, oruṅku akappaṭutta - oruṅku kūṭic cūḻntu koṇṭatāl, muravu vāy ñāyil ōr eyil maṉṉaṉ pōla - muṟital poruntiya cūṭṭiṉiyuṭiya oru matiliṉkaṇ akappaṭṭa aracaṉip pōla, tuyil tuṟantaṉaḷ kol - tuyil oḻintuḷāḷō, aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! nām aruñ curam nīntiya varuttamoṭu kiyaṟṟu, potiyiliṉkaṇ māli pulampu vantuṟutara, uḷḷam celavu valiyuṟuppa, taṉi nili yirukkiyoṭu taṉṉili yuḷḷum namnili uṇarāḷ, maṭanti, naṭunāḷ aṇiyoṭu porunti uyirā, nōy kūrntu, tuyil tuṟantaṉaḷkol, aḷiyaḷ.
(vi-ri) muṉi kavarntu koṇṭeṉa - pakivar oruvarōṭoruvar pōr ceytalāl eṉṟapaṭi. kalaṅkutal - muṉnili māṟutal. pīr eḻuntu - pīrkku muḷittup paṭara. pāḻpaṭṭa maṉṟam, mari cēr maṉṟam eṉka. maṉṟattup potiyilil irukkiyoṭu eṉak kūṭṭuka. kāḻ eṉṟatu īṇṭu viṭṭatti; `yāṉi, verin ōṅku ciṟupuṟam uriña volki, iṭṭiki neṭuñcuvar viṭṭam vīḻnteṉa' 1 eṉa muṉṉarp pōntami kāṇka. perum puṉ māli - periya varuttattic ceyyum māli eṉṟalumām. pulampu vantu uṟutara - taliviyip pirinta taṉimi tōṉṟi varuttutalāl eṉka. tāḷ ki pūṭṭiya - tāḷkaḷi maṭakki nimirttu iru kiyāṉum kōtta eṉka. āḻnta cintaṉiyiliruppār aṅṅaṉam tāḷkaḷik kiyāṟ kōttiruttal marapu. maṭantiyākiya aḷiyaḷ eṉak kūṭṭi yurittalumām. nam nili uṇarāḷ pulakkum neñcamoṭu eṉka. ōr eyil maṉṉaṉ eṉṟatu, kuṟu nila maṉṉaṉi.
374. mulli
(pācaṟi muṟṟiya talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		mākkaṭaṉ mukantu mātirat tiruḷi
		malartali yulakam putiya valaṉērpu
		paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa koḻumpal koṇmūp
		pōḻnta pōlap palavuṭaṉ miṉṉit
	5.	tāḻnta pōla 1naṇinaṇi vantu
		cōrnta pōlac corivaṉa payiṟṟi
		iṭiyum muḻakkum iṉṟip pāṇar
		vaṭiyuṟu nalyāḻ narampicit taṉṉa
		iṉkural aḻituḷi talii naṉpala
	10.	peyalpeytu kaḻinta pūnāṟu vikaṟic
		ceṟimaṇal nivanta kaḷartōṉṟu iyavil
		kuṟumōṭṭu mūtāy kuṟukuṟu ōṭi
		maṇimaṇṭu pavaḻam pōlak kāyā
		aṇimiku cemmal oḷippaṉa maṟiyak
	15.	kārkaviṉ koṇṭa 2kāmar kālic
		celka tērē nalvalam peṟuna
		peruntōḷ nuṇukiya nucuppil
		tiruntiḻi arivi viruntetir koḷavē.
- iṭikkāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 16. nal valam peṟuna - nalla tiṟaṉ vāynta pākaṉē!
1-10. mā kaṭal mukantu - periya kaṭal nīri mukantu, mātirattu iruḷi - ticikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu iruṇṭu, malartali ulakam putiya valaṉ ērpu - akaṉṟa iṭattiṉi yuṭiya ulakam maṟiya valamāka eḻuntu ceṉṟu, paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa koḻumpal koṇmū - poṟiyāl varuntutalik koṇṭa vaḷaviya pala mēkaṅkaḷ, pōḻnta pōla pala uṭaṉ miṉṉi - vāṉip piḷantaṉa pōlap palavum oruṅkē tōṉṟa miṉṉutalic ceytu, tāḻnta pōla naṇi naṇi vantu - iṟaṅkuvaṉa pōla neruṅka neruṅka vantu, cōrnta pōla corivaṉa payiṟṟi - kaviḻntaṉa pōla mikkup poḻintu, iṭiyum muḻakkum iṉṟi  - iṭittalum kumuṟutalum iṉṟi, pāṇar vaṭi uṟu nal yāḻ narampu icittaṉṉa - pāṇaratu vaṭittal uṟṟa nal yāḻ narampu olippatu pōṉṟa, iṉ kural aḻi tuḷi talii - iṉiya kuraluṭaṉ mikka tuḷiyip porunti, naṉ pala peyal peytu kaḻinta - naṉṟākiya pala maḻikaḷip peytu kaḻinta, pū nāṟu vikaṟi - pūkkaḷ nāṟum viṭiyaṟkālattē,
11-15. ceṟi maṇal nivanta kaḷar tōṉṟu iyavil - ceṟinta maṇal mēṭu paṭṭatum kaḷarmaṇ tōṉṟuvatumākiya neṟiyil, kuṟu mōṭṭu mūtāy - ciṟu vayiṟṟiṉi yuṭiya tampalap pūcci, kuṟukuṟu ōṭi - kuṟukakkuṟukac ceṉṟu, maṇi maṇṭu pavaḻam pōla - nīlamaṇiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya pavaḻam pōla, kāyā aṇi miku cemmal oḷippaṉa maṟiya - kāyāviṉ aḻaku mikka vāṭaṟpūvil oḷintu maṟiya, kār kaviṉ koṇṭa kāmar kāli - kārpparuvamāṉatu aḻaku peṟṟa viruppam poruntiya kālattē,
16-18. perutōḷ nuṇukiya nucuppil - periya tōḷiṉiyum nuṇṇiya iṭiyiṉiyum, tiruntu iḻi - tiruntiya aṇikaḷiyum uṭiya, arivi viruntu etirkoḷa - nam talivi viruntāka nammi ēṟṟuk koḷḷa, tēr celka - nam tēr (virintu) celvatāka.
(muṭipu) nal valam peṟuna! kār kaviṉkoṇṭa kāmar kāli, arivi viruntu etirkoḷa, tēr celka.
(vi-ri) paḻaṅkaṇ - tuṉpam; poṟi āṟṟāta tuṉpam. pōḻnta pōla, tāḻnta pōla, cōrnta pōla eṉpaṉa taṟkuṟip piṉpāṟ paṭuvaṉa. kuṟu kuṟu - kuṟukak kuṟuka, `kuṟu kuṟu naṭantu'1 eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka. mukantu, iruḷi, ērpu koṇṭa koṇmū eṉavum; koṇmū miṉṉi, vantu corivaṉa payiṟṟi, aḻi tuḷi talii, peytu kaḻinta vikaṟi eṉavum; vikaṟi, iyavil, mūtāy, ōṭi, maṟiya, kārkaviṉ koṇṭa kāli eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
(mē-ḷ) `karaṇattiṉ amintu'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `pērici ūrtip pākar pāṅkiṉum' eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ, talivaṉ tāṉ peṟṟa iṉpattip pākaṟkuk kūṟiyatu eṉpataṟku, `celka tērē nalvalam peṟuna' eṉpataṉi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar nac.
375. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		ceṉṟu nīṭunar allar avarvayiṉ
		iṉital āṉāy eṉṟiciṉ ikuḷi
		amputoṭi yamiti kāṇmār vampalar
		kalaṉilar āyiṉuṅ koṉṟupuḷ ḷūṭṭuṅ
	5.	kallā iḷiyar kalitta kavalik
		kaṇanari iṉaṉoṭu kuḻīi niṇaṉaruntum
		neyttōr āṭiya mallal mociviral
		atta eruvic cēval cērnta
		aricēr yātta veṇṭiraḷ 1viṉiviṟal
	10.	eḻāat tiṇitōṭ cōḻar perumakaṉ
		viḷaṅkupukaḻ niṟutta iḷamperuñ ceṉṉi
		kuṭikkaṭaṉ ākaliṟ kuṟiviṉi muṭimār
		cempuṟaḻ puricip pāḻi nūṟi
		vampa vaṭukar pintali cavaṭṭik
	15.	koṉṟa yāṉik kōṭṭiṟ ṟōṉṟum
		añcuvaru marapiṉ veñcuram iṟantōr
		nōyilar peyartal aṟiyiṉ
		āḻala maṉṉō tōḻiyeṉ kaṇṇē.
- 2 iṭiyaṉ cēntaṅ koṟṟaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. ikuḷi - tōḻiyē! ceṉṟu nīṭunar allar - nam talivar poruḷ īṭṭutaṟkuc ceṉṟu nīṭṭippār allar, avarvayiṉ iṉital āṉāy - avartiṟattu varuntutal ami
vāyāka, eṉṟiciṉ - eṉkiṉṟāy.
3-6. ampu toṭi amiti kāṇmār - tām ampu viṭuttaliṉ ciṟappiṉik kāṇumporuṭṭu, vampalar kalaṉ ilar āyiṉum - putiyarāy varuvōr aṇikalaṉ illātava rāyiṉum, koṉṟu puḷ ūṭṭum - avarkaḷik koṉṟu paṟavikaṭku uṇpikkum, kallā iḷiyar - āṟalittali yaṉṟip piṟatoḻili aṟiyāta iḷiyar, kalitta kavali - cerukkit tiriyum kavartta neṟikaḷil, kaṇa nari iṉaṉoṭu kuḻīi - kūṭṭamāya narikaḷiṉ iṉattoṭu kūṭi, niṇaṉ aruntum - niṇattiṉi uṇṇum.
7-15. neyttōr āṭiya mallal moci viral - kuruti paṭinta vaḷamuṭiya neruṅkiya viralkaḷiyuṭiya, atta eruvic cēval cērnta - kāṭṭiluḷḷa āṇparuntu taṅkiyirukkum, ari cēr yātta veṇ tiraḷ - aṭitiraṇṭa yāmarattiṉ veḷḷiya tiraṇṭa kompāṉatu, viṉi viṟal eḻāa tiṇi tōḷ - pōriṉ veṟṟi nīṅkāta tiṇṇiya tōḷiṉi yuṭiya, cōḻar perumakaṉ - cōḻar perumāṉākiya, viḷaṅku pukaḻ niṟutta iḷam perum ceṉṉi - eṉṟum viḷaṅkum pukaḻiṉi nilināṭṭiya iḷam peruñ ceṉṉi eṉpāṉ, kuṭikkaṭaṉ ākaliṉ kuṟi viṉi muṭimār - taṉatu kuṭikku āṟṟum kaṭaṉākaliṉ muṭikkappaṭum poruḷāya pōrviṉiyi muṭittaṟku, cempu uṟaḻ purici pāḻi nūṟi - cempiṉi otta matili yuṭiya pāḻi  eṉṉum araṇi aḻittu, vampa vaṭukar pintali cavaṭṭi - putiya vaṭukaratu paciya taliyit taṟittu, koṉṟa yāṉi kōṭṭil tōṉṟum - koṉṟa yāṉikaḷiṉ kōṭupōlat tōṉṟum,
16-18. añcu varu marapiṉ vem curam iṟantōr - accam varum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya koṭiya curaneṟiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam talivar, nōy ilar peyartal aṟiyiṉ - tīṅkilarāy mīṇṭu varutali aṟivēṉāyiṉ, tōḻi - tōḻiyē, eṉ kaṇ āḻala - eṉ kaṇkaḷ aḻamāṭṭā, (maṉ - aṟintilaṉē.)
(muṭipu) ikuḷi! (nam talivar) nīṭunar allar, avarvayiṉ niṉital āṉāy, eṉṟiciṉ. veñcuram iṟantōr (ākiya avar) nōyilar peyartal aṟiyiṉ eṉ kaṇ(kaḷ) āḻala.
(vi-ri) kaṇa nari iṉaṉoṭu kuḻīi niṇaṉ aruntum eruvic cēval cērnta yā maram eṉka. aṉṟi, nari iṉaṉoṭu kuḻīi niṇaṉ aruntum aricēr yā, eruvic cēval cērnta yā, eṉat taṉittaṉi kūṭṭi muṭittalumām. yātta - yāmarattiṉuṭiya. tiraḷ - tiraṇṭa kompu - āku peyar. viḷi viṟal eḻūut  tiṇi tōḷ eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, veṟṟiyuṇṭākum eḻuup pōlum tiṇṇiyatōḷ eṉka. eḻūu - eḻu, kaṇiya maram. cavaṭṭi 
`kaṭicol lillik kālattup paṭiṉē'1 eṉpataṉāṟ pōntatu.
376. marutam
(kātaṟ paratti pulantu colliyatu.)
		cellal makiḻnaniṟ ceykaṭaṉ uṭiyeṉmaṉ
		kallā yāṉi kaṭipuṉal kaṟṟeṉa
		malipuṉal poruta marutōṅku paṭappi
		olikatirk kaḻaṉik kaḻāar muṉṟuṟik
	5.	kalikoḷ cuṟṟamoṭu karikāl kāṇat
		taṇpataṅ koṇṭu tavirnta iṉṉici
		oṇpoṟip puṉikaḻal cēvaṭip puraḷak
		karuṅkaccu yātta kāṇpiṉ avvayiṟṟu
		2irumpolam  pāṇṭil maṇiyoṭu teḷirppap
	10.	puṉaṉayan tāṭum atti aṇinayantu
		kāviri koṇṭoḷit tāṅku maṉṉō
		numvayiṟ pulattal cellēm emvayiṉ
		pacantaṉṟu kāṇṭiciṉ nutalē acumpiṉ
		antūmpu vaḷḷi aḻaṟkoṭi mayakki
	15.	vaṇṭōṭṭu nelliṉ vāṅkupīḷ viriyat
		tuyttali muṭaṅkiṟāt teṟikkum poṟpuṭik
		kuraṅkuḷip puravik kuṭṭuvaṉ
		1maranti aṉṉaveṉ nalantantu ceṉmē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1. makiḻna - talivaṉē! cellal - cellātē,
2-5. kallā yāṉi kaṭi puṉal kaṟṟeṉa - pākaṉāṟ payiṟṟappaṭāta yāṉi kāvali yuṭiya nīril viḷiyāṭalik kaṟṟatāka, malipuṉal poruta marutu ōṅku paṭappi - ataṉāl mikkeḻum nīr mōtiya maruta maraṅkaḷ uyarnta tōṭṭattiṉiyum, oli katir kaḻaṉi - taḻitta katirkaḷi yuṭiya vayalkaḷiyu muṭiya, kaḻāar muṉtuṟi - kaḻāar eṉṉum patiyiṉ muṉpu, kali koḷ cuṟṟamoṭu karikāl kāṇa - āravāram poruntiya cuṟṟattiṉaruṭaṉ karikālvaḷavaṉ kaṇṇuṟṟu makiḻa.
6-13. taṇ patam koṇṭu - puṉal viḻāk koṇṭu, tavirnta iṉ ici - iṉiya ici taṅkiya, oḷ poṟi puṉi kaḻal cē aṭi puraḷa - oḷḷiya poṟikaḷi uṭiya aḻakiya vīrakkaḻal civanta aṭiyil puraḷa, karu kaccu yātta kāṇpu iṉ a vayiṟṟu maṇiyoṭu- kariya kacciṉik kaṭṭiya kāṭci iṉitākiya aḻakiya vayiṟṟil kaṭṭappeṟṟa maṇiyuṭaṉ, irumpolam pāṇṭil teḷirppa - periya poṉṉālākiya kañca tāḷam olikka, puṉal nayantu āṭum atti aṇi nayantu - puṉaliṉkaṇ virumpi āṭiya atti eṉpāṉatu aḻakiṉi virumpi, kāviri koṇṭu oḷittāṅku - kāviri avaṉi vuvikkoṇṭu kaṭalil oḷittāṟ pōlac cērip paratti niṉṉik kavarntuceṉṟāḷāka, num vayiṉ pulattal cellēm - numpāl yām veṟuttal illēm, em vayiṉ pacantaṉṟu kāṇṭiciṉ nutal - aṅṅaṉamākavum emmiṭattu nutal pacali yuṟṟatu kāṇpāyāka,
13-18. acumpiṉ - cēṟṟiluḷḷa, am tūmpu vaḷḷi aḻal koṭi mayakki - aḻakiya tuḷiyuṭiya vaḷḷiyāya oḷḷiya koṭiyip piṉṉuvittu, vaḷ tōṭṭu nelliṉ vāṅku pīḷ  viriya - vaḷam poruntiya itaḻiṉiyuṭiya nelliṉatu vaḷinta katir virintiṭa, tuy tali muṭaṅku iṟā teṟikkum - tuyyiṉit taliyil uṭiya vaḷinta iṟā mīṉ pāyum iṭamāya, poṟpu uṭi kuraṅku uḷi puravi kuṭṭuvaṉ maranti aṉṉa - aḻaku poruntiya vaḷinta piṭari mayiriṉiyuṭiya kutiriyi yuṭiya kuṭṭuvaṉatu maranti eṉṉum ūr pōṉṟa, eṉ nalam tantu ceṉmē - eṉ aḻakiṉit tantu celka.
1. niṟ cey kaṭaṉ uṭiyeṉmaṉ - niṉpāṟ ceyyum kaṭamikaḷi mikavum uṭiyēṉ.
(muṭipu) makiḻna! cellal, kaḻāar muṉṟuṟi, karikāl kāṇa, puṉal nayantāṭum atti aṇi nayantu kāviri koṇṭoḷittāṅku (cēripparatti niṉṉik kavarntu ceṉṟāḷāka), num vayiṟ pulattal cellēm emvayiṉ nutal pacantaṉṟu, kāṇṭiciṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ maranti aṉṉa eṉ nalam tantu ceṉmē, niṟ ceykaṭaṉ uṭiyēṉ, maṉ.
(vi-ri) yāṉi kaṭi puṉal kaṟṟeṉap puṉal nayan tāṭum atti eṉak kūṭṭi yurittalumām. ip poruṭku yāṉikkum maṇi teḷirttal koḷka. kali koḷ cuṟṟam - viḻaviṉ āravārattik koṇṭa cuṟṟam. tavirtal - taṅkutal. taṇ patam koṇṭu tavirnta atti, puṉal nayantāṭum atti eṉak kūṭṭi yurittalumām. itaṟku, puṉalāṭum atti cuṟṟattuṭaṉ ēkātu, taṅkiviṭṭāṉāka, avaṉatu aṇi nayantu kāviri koṇ ṭoḷittāṟ pōla, eṉṟurittuk koḷka.  kāviri koṇ ṭoḷittāṅku, cērip paratti niṉṉik kavarntu ceṉṟāḷāka eṉa viritturittuk koḷka. maṉ oḻiyicip poruṭṭumām.
377. pāli
(poruḷ kaṭikkūṭṭiya neñciṟkut talimakaṉ colliyatu.)
		kōṭi nīṭaliṉ 1vāṭupulat tukka
		ciṟupul luṇavu neṟipaṭa maṟuki
		nuṇpal 2 eṟumpu koṇṭaḷic ceṟitta
		vittā valci vīṅkucili maṟavar
	5.	3pallūḻ pukkup payaṉiri kavarak
		koḻuṅkuṭi pōkiya perumpāḻ maṉṟattu
		narimū tāḷar atirtali yiṟakkik
		kavimaṉat tiruttum valluvaṉap paḻiya
		variniṟac citali arittaliṟ pulleṉṟu
	10.	perunalañ citinta pēemutir potiyil
		iṉṉā oruciṟit taṅki iṉṉakic
		ciṟumeṉ cāyal perunalam 1 uḷḷi
		vampala rākiyuṅ kaḻipa maṉṟa
		nacitara vantōr irantavi
	15.	icipaṭap peytal āṟṟu vōrē
- 2māṟōkkattuk kāmakkaṇi nappālattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē! 14-15. naci tara vantōr - poruḷ vēṭkiyāṉatu koṇṭu varutalāl vantuṟṟa iravalar, irantavi - iranta poruḷkaḷi, ici paṭap peytal āṟṟuvōr - pukaḻ uṇṭāka aḷittalic ceyya vēṇṭuvōr.
1-6. kōṭi nīṭaliṉ - kōṭiyiṉ vemmi mikkamiyāl vāṭu pulattu ukka - vaṟaṇṭa nilattē utirnta, ciṟu pul uṇavu - ciṟiya pullariciyi, nuṇ pal eṟumpu - ciṟiya palavāya eṟumpukaḷ, neṟi paṭa maṟuki - oḻuṅku peṟac ceṉṟu, koṇṭu aḷic ceṟitta - koṇṭu vantu tam vaḷiyil tokuttu vittaṉavākiya, vittā valci - (tām) vitittu viḷikkāta avvuṇaviṉik koḷḷum, vīṅku cili maṟavar - perutta villiyuṭiya maṟavarkaḷ, pal ūḻ pukku payaṉ niri kavara - pala muṟi pukuntu pāṟpacuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷik kavarntamiyiṉ, koḻu kuṭi pōkiya peru pāḻ maṉṟattu - vaḷamuṟṟirunta ūriṉ kuṭikaḷ pōyviṭṭa iṭamāya periya pāḻpaṭṭa maṉṟattiṉ kaṇṇē.
7-13. nari mūtāḷar - nariyiṉi yuṭiya mutiyōr, atirtali iṟakki - naṭuṅkum tamatu taliyik kaviḻttu, kavi maṉattu iruttum - tamatu kavartta maṉattilē irutti vallup pōr puriyum, vallu vaṉappu aḻiya - vallut tariyiṉ aḻaku keṭa, vari niṟac citali arittaliṉ - varikaḷ poruntiya niṟamuṭiya kaṟiyāṉ arittamiyāṉ, pul eṉṟu - polivaṟṟu, peru nalam citinta pēem mutir potiyil - periya aḻaku keṭṭa accam mikka potiyiliṉ, iṉṉā oru ciṟi taṅki - iṉṉāmi yuṭiya oru puṟattē taṅki yiruntu, iṉ naki ciṟu meṉ cāyal peru nalam uḷḷi - iṉiya nakiyiyum ciṟiya meṉmiyākiya cāyaliṉiyum uṭiyāḷatu pēraḻaki niṉittu, vampalar ākiyum - ētilar ākiyum, kaḻipa maṉṟa - oru taliyāka mēlum cellā niṟpar. (nī aṅṅaṉam cellutaṟku valliyō.)
(muṭipu) nacitara vantōr irantavi icipaṭap peytal āṟṟuvōr, perumpāḻ maṉṟattup perunalam citinta pēem mutir potiyil iṉṉā oru ciṟit taṅki, iṉṉakic ciṟumeṉ cāyal perunalam uḷḷi, vampala rākiyum kaḻipa maṉṟa. (nī aṅṅaṉam cellutaṟku valliyō)
(vi-ri) eṟumpu koṇṭu aḷic ceṟitta valci, vittā valci eṉat taṉittaṉik kūṭṭuka. itaṉāl maṟavarkaḷ tam uṇaviṉi vitittu viḷivittaliṉṟic ciṟṟeṟumpukaḷ aritiṉ muyaṉṟu aḷiyiṟ ceṟittu vittavaṟṟi akaḻntu eṭuttu uṇṇum iyalpiṉarātal peṟṟām. maṉattu iruttum vallu - maṉattilē cūḻntu viḷiyāṭum vallu eṉka. vallu - cūtu karuvi; īṇṭuc cūtāṭum iṭattiṟkāyiṟṟu. nari mūtāḷar potiyiliṉ kaṇ cūtāṭuvār eṉpatu, `pali kaṇ māṟiya pāḻpaṭu potiyil, narimū tāḷar nāyiṭak kuḻinta, valliṉ nallakam'1 eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. āṟṟuvōr kaḻipa eṉṟatu, nī iṭiccurattil taliviyiṉ nalattiṉi uḷḷi mīḷvāyeṉak kuṟippittavāṟu āyiṟṟu.
378. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi colleṭuppat talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		nitiyan tuñcum nivantōṅku varippiṉ
		vatuvi makaḷir kūntal kamaḻkoḷa
		vaṅkūḻ āṭṭiya aṅkuḻi vēṅki
		naṉpoṉ aṉṉa naṟuntātu utirak
	5.	kāmar pīli āymayiṟ ṟōki
		vēṟuvē ṟiṉatta varivāḻ varuṭik
		kōṭumuṟ ṟiḷantakar pāṭuviṟan tiyala
		āṭukaḷa vayiriṉ iṉiya ālip
		pacumpuṟa meṉcīr ociya vicumpukantu
	10.	iruṅkaṇ āṭamit tayaṅka irukkum
		peruṅkal nāṭaṉ pirinta pulampum
		uṭaṉṟa aṉṉi amarā nōkkamum
		vaṭanti tūkkum varupaṉi aṟciram
		cuṭarkeḻu maṇṭilam maḻuṅka ñāyiṟu
	15.	kuṭakaṭal cērum paṭarkūr māliyum
		aṉittum aṭūuniṉṟu 2naliya nīmaṟṟu
		yāṅṅaṉam vāḻti eṉṟi tōḻi
		nīṅkā vañciṉañ ceytunat tuṟantōr
		uḷḷār āyiṉum uḷaṉē avarnāṭṭu
	20.	aḷḷilip palaviṉ kaṉikavar kiya
		kallā manti kaṭuvaṉō ṭukaḷuṅ
		kaṭuntiṟal aṇaṅkiṉ neṭumperuṅ kuṉṟattup
		pāṭiṉ aruvi cūṭi
		vāṉṟōy cimiyam tōṉṟa lāṉē.
- kāvaṭṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 17. tōḻi-,
1-11. nitiyam tuñcum nivantu ōṅku varippiṉ - mikka poruḷ taṅkum uyarntōṅkum maṉiyakattē, vatuvi makaḷir kūntal kamaḻ koḷa - kaliyāṇam ceyta makaḷirtam kūntal pōla maṇam poruntumāṟu, vaṅkūḻ āṭṭiya am kuḻi vēṅki naṉ poṉ aṉṉa naṟu tātu utira - kāṟṟu acittalāl aḻakiya taḷirkaḷi yuṭiya vēṅki marattiṉ poṉ pōṉṟa naṟiya tātu utirtalāl, kāmar pīli āy mayiltōki - viruppan tarum pīli poruntiya ciṟanta tōkiyi yuṭiya mayil, vēṟu vēṟu iṉatta vari vāḻ varuṭi - vēṟuvēṟu vakiyiṉavākiya maliyil vāḻum varuṭi kaḷiṉ, kōṭu muṟṟu iḷa takar pāṭu viṟantu iyala - kompukaḷ poruntiya iḷiya kaṭākkaḷ oliyik kēṭṭu añci iriya, āṭu kaḷa vayiriṉ iṉiya āli - āṭum kaḷattē yolikkum kompu pōla iṉiyaṉavāka olittu, vicumpu ukantu - vāṉiṟ paṟantu ceṉṟu, iru kaṇ āṭu ami - nīṇṭa kaṇukkaḷi yuṭiya aciyum mūṅkiliṉ kaṇ, pacu puṟa meṉ cīr ociya - paciya puṟattiṉiyuṭiya melliya aḻakiya iṭam vaḷiya, tayaṅka irukkum peru kal nāṭaṉ - viḷaṅkat taṅkiyirukkum periya mali poruntiya nāṭṭi yuṭiyavaṉākiya talivaṉ, pirinta pulampum - pirintamiyāl ākiya taṉimiyum,
12. uṭaṉṟa aṉṉi amarā nōkkamum - māṟupaṭṭa aṉṉiyiṉ poruntāta pārviyum,
13-15. vaṭanti tūkkum varu paṉi aṟciram - vāṭiyāl acikkap peṟum peyyum paṉikkālattē, cuṭar keḻu maṇṭilam maḻuṅka - oḷi poruntiya taṉatu maṇṭilam maḻuṅkiṭa, ñāyiṟu kuṭa kaṭal cērum paṭar kūr māliyum - ñāyiṟu mēlik kaṭalil maṟiyum tuṉpam mikka mālippoḻutum āya,
16-17. aṉittum aṭūu niṉṟu naliya - yāvum kūṭi niṉṟu varuttavum, nī yāṅṅaṉam vāḻti eṉṟi - nī eṅṅaṉam uyir vāḻkiṉṟāy eṉṟu viṉavukiṉṟaṉi,
18-19. nīṅkā vañciṉam ceytu nam tuṟantōr - nīṅkāmik kuriya cūḷuṟavu ceytu piṉ nammit tuṟantōrāya avar, uḷḷār āyiṉum - nammi niṉiyārāyiṉum,
19-24. avar nāṭṭu aḷ ili palaviṉ - avar nāṭṭil neruṅkiya ilikaḷiyuṭiya palāviṉ, kaṉi kavar kiya - paḻattiṉik kavarnteṭutta kikaḷiyuṭiya, kallā manti kaṭuvaṉōṭu ukaḷum - aṟiyāta peṇ kuraṅku āṇ kuraṅkiṉōṭu tuḷḷit tiriyum, kaṭu tiṟal aṇaṅkiṉ neṭu peru kuṉṟattu - mikka valiyaminta teyvattiyuṭiya nīṇṭa periya maliyakattu, pāṭu iṉ aruvi cūṭi- ōci iṉitākiya aruviyik koṇṭu, vāṉ tōy cimiyam tōṉṟalāṉ - vāṉi aḷāviya ucci mali kāṇap peṟutaliṉ; uḷaṉ - uḷḷēṉ ākā niṉṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! peruṅkal nāṭaṉ pirinta pulampum, aṉṉi amarā  nōkkamum, paṭarkūr māliyum naliya, nī yāṅṅaṉam vāḻti eṉṟi. vañciṉam ceytu tuṟantōr uḷḷār āyiṉum, avar nāṭṭu neṭum peruṅ kuṉṟattu vāṉṟōy cimiyam tōṉṟalāṉē uḷaṉ. 
(vi-ri) kūntal kamaḻ koḷa - kūntal pōla maṇattal ceyya. kamaḻ koḷa utira eṉka. kāmar eṉpataṟku aḻaku poruntiya eṉa virittu, utira eṉṉum eccattiṟku muṭipu ākkuka. 
varuṭi - maliyil vāḻum oruvaki vilaṅku. itu mutukil kāluṭiyatum eṭṭukkāl uṭiyatum eṉap pala tiṟappaṭutalāl, vēṟu vēṟu iṉatta varuṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. `māvum mākkaḷum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, pērāciriyar `kiḷi yeṉpaṉa eṇkāl varuṭiyum kuraṅkum pōlvaṉa. eṇ kāla vāyiṉum mā veṉap paṭutaliṉ, varuṭi, kiḷi āyiṟṟu' eṉṟurittatum, `mīmicik koṇṭa kavarparik koṭuntāḷ, varivāḻ varuṭi'2 eṉṉu miṭattu, `mutukiṭattē koṇṭa, nilattik kikkoḷḷum celaviṉi yuṭiya, vaḷinta kāli yuṭiya, variyiṭattē vāḻum eṇ kāl varuṭi' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittatum kāṇka. viṟantu - añci. āli - ālittu, olittu. pulampum nōkkamum māliyum naliya eṉka. uñaṟṟi eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, avaṟṟip poṟuttalic ceytu eṉṟurikka. nammit tuṟantu ceṉṟa talivar nammi niṉintu mīṇṭila rāyiṉum avaratu maliyiṉ ucci tōṉṟutalāl ataṉip pārttu yāṉ āṟṟiyuḷēṉ eṉat talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. talivaṉip pirinta talivi avaṉatu maliyip pārttu āṟṟi yiruttal, `avarnāṭṭuk, kuṉṟam nōkkiṉeṉ ṟōḻi, paṇṭi yaṟṟō kaṇṭiciṉ nutalē'3 eṉpataṉālum aṟika. uḷaṉē - aṉ vikuti taṉmikkaṇ vantatu.
379. pāli
(muṉṉoru kālattup poruṇmuṟṟi vanta talimakaṉ piṉṉum poruḷ kaṭikkūṭṭiya neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		nannayan tuṟivi toṉṉalam aḻiyat
		teruḷā miyiṉ tītoṭu keḻīi
		aruḷaṟa nimirnta muṉpoṭu poruḷpurintu
		āḷviṉik ketiriya mīḷi neñcē
	5.	niṉiyiṉi yāyiṉ eṉava kēṇmati
		viritiri munnīr maṇṭiṇi kiṭakkip
		paritiyañ celvam potumi yiṉṟi
		naṉaviṉ iyaṉṟa tāyiṉuṅ kaṅkuṟ
		kaṉavi ṉaṟṟataṉ kaḻivē ataṉāṉ
	10.	viravuṟu paṉmalar vaṇṭucūḻpu aṭiccic
		cuvaṉmici aciiya nilitayaṅ kuṟumuṭi
		īṇṭupaṉ ṉāṟṟam vēṇṭuvayiṉ uvappac
		ceyvuṟu viḷaṅkiḻip polinta tōḷcērpu
		eytiya kaṉituyil ēṟṟoṟun tiruki
	15.	meypuku vaṉṉa kikavar muyakkiṉ
		mikutikaṇ ṭaṉṟō vilaṉē nīniṉ
		palporuḷ vēṭkiyiṟ colvari nīvic
		celavuvali yuṟuttaṉi yāyiṟ kāloṭu
		1kaṉiyeri nikaḻnta iliyil aṅkāṭṭu
	20.	uḻippuṟat taṉṉa puḷḷi nīḻal
		aciiya poḻutiṟ pacii vantivaṇ
		maṟapparum palkuṇam niṟattuvan tuṟutara
		orutiṟam niṉittal cellāy tiripuniṉṟu
		uṟupuli yuḻanta vaṭumarup poruttaṟkup
	25.	piṭiyiṭu pūcaliṉ aṭipaṭak kuḻinta
		nirampā nīḷiṭit tūṅki
		iraṅkuvi yalliyō uraṅkeṭa melintē.
- pāli pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. nam nayantu uṟivi - nammi virumpi iruppavaḷāya nam taliviyiṉ, tol nalam aḻiya - paḻiya aḻaku keṭa, teruḷāmiyiṉ tītoṭu keḻīi - aṟivu teḷiyap peṟāmiyiṉ tīmiyuṭaṉ porunti, aruḷ aṟa - irakkam nīṅka, nimirnta muṉpoṭu poruḷ purintu - mikka vaṉmiyuṭaṉ poruḷi virumpi, āḷviṉikku etiriya mīḷi neñcē - atupaṟṟi muyalutaṟku muṟpaṭṭa valiya neñcamē! niṉiyiṉi yāyiṉ eṉava kēṇmati - cintippāyāyiṉ yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟik kēṭpāyāka.
6-9. viri tiri munnīr - virinta alikaḷiyuṭiya kaṭal cūḻnta, maṇ tiṇi kiṭakki - maṇ tiṇinta iv vulaka muḻuvatum, potumi iṉṟi - piṟarkkup potu illiyāka, paruti am celvam - tikiri yuruṭṭum celvamāṉatu, naṉaviṉ iyaṉṟatu āyiṉum - uṇmiyākavē ki kūṭiyatāyiṉum, ataṉ kaḻivu kaṅkul kaṉaviṉ aṟṟu - ataṉ pōkku iravil kaṉaviṟ ṟōṉṟi maṟiyum attaṉmiyatu.
9-16. ataṉāṉ - ataṉāl, viravu uṟu pal malar - kalappuṟṟa pala malarkaḷi, vaṇṭu cūḻpu aṭicci - vaṇṭu moykkumpaṭi cūṭi, cuval mici aciiya nili tayaṅku uṟu muṭi - piṭariṉ mītu taṅkiya niliyiṉiṉṟum acinta periya kūntaliṉ, īṇṭu pala nāṟṟam - ceṟinta palavāya nāṟṟam, vēṇṭuvayiṉ uvappa - vēṇṭu miṭattup peṟṟu makiḻa, ceyvu uṟu viḷaṅku iḻi polinta tōḷ cērpu - ceyyappeṟṟa viḷaṅkum aṇikaḷāṟ polivuṟṟa tōḷi aṇintu, eytiya kaṉi tuyil ēṟṟoṟum - poruntiya mikka tuyili ēṟkumtoṟum, tiruki mey pukuvu aṉṉa ki kavar muyakkiṉ mikuti - māṟupaṭṭu meyyiṉuḷ mey pukutalotta kikaḷāl virumpik koḷḷum muyakkattiṉum mēmpaṭṭa poruḷi, kaṇṭaṉṟō ilaṉ - yāṉ kaṇṭatillēṉ.
16-18. nī niṉ pal poruḷ vēṭkiyiṉ - nī niṉ pala vāya poruḷ viruppāl, col vari nīvi - eṉ colliṉ aḷavik  kaṭantu,  celavu valiyuṟuttaṉiyāyiṉ - cellutali vaṟ puṟuttuvi yāyiṉ,
18-27. kāloṭu kaṉi eri nikaḻnta ili il kāṭṭu - kāṟṟiṉāl mikkeḻum tī mēviyatāya iliyaṟṟa kāṭṭil, uḻi puṟattu aṉṉa puḷḷi nīḻal - māṉiṉ puṟattuḷḷa puḷḷipōṉṟa puḷḷikaḷi yuṭiya mara niḻalil, aciiya poḻutil - taṅki yirukkuṅkāl, ivaḷ maṟappu aru pal kuṇam niṟattu vantu uṟutara- ivaḷatu maṟattaṟkariya pala kuṇaṅkaḷum niṉ neñcattu vantupuka, pacii - virumpi, oru tiṟam niṉittal cellāy - oru kūṟṟiṉi niṉittal māṭṭāyāy, tiripu niṉṟu - vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟu, uram keṭa melintu - tiṭpam aṟa melintu, uṟu puli uḻanta vaṭu maruppu oruttaṟku - periya puliyuṭaṉ pōr ceytu varuntiya vaṭup poruntiya kompiṉiyuṭiya kaḷiṟṟiṉ poruṭṭu, piṭi pūcaliṉ iṭu aṭipaṭa kuḻinta - peṇ yāṉi āravārattuṭaṉ iṭṭēkum aṭikaḷ patitalāl paḷḷamāya, nirampā nīḷ iṭi - ceṉṟu toliyāta nīṇṭa curattiṉ iṭiyē, tūṅki - taṅki, iraṅkuvi alliyō - varuntuvāy alliyō.
(muṭipu) poruḷ purintu āḷviṉikku etiriya neñcē! kēṇmati, paritiyañ celvam naṉaviṉ iyaṉṟatāyiṉum ataṉ kaḻivu kaṉaviṉ aṟṟu. ataṉāl, (taliviyiṉ) tōḷ cērpu, kikavar muyakkiṉ mikuti kaṇṭaṉṟō ilaṉ. nī poruḷ vēṭkiyiṉ celavu valiyuṟuttaṉiyāyiṉ, ivaḷ pal kuṇam niṟattu vantuṟutara, oru tiṟam niṉittal cellāy nirampā nīḷiṭit tūṅki iraṅkuvi alliyō.
(vi-ri) paruti añ celvam - tikiri yuruṭṭum celvam; eṉṟatu puvi muḻutāḷum celvam. naṉaviṉ iyaṉṟatāyiṉum eṉṟatu ataṉ arumiyi viḷakkiṟṟu. kaṉaviṉ aṟṟu eṉṟatu kaṉaviṉippōl virivilē tōṉṟi maṟiyum iyalpiṟṟu eṉṟapaṭi. cūḻpu - cūḻa eṉat tirikka. tayaṅkutal - acital. muṭi - kūntal. tōḷic cērntu tuyil ēṟkuntoṟum muyaṅkum muyakkattiṉum mēmpaṭṭa poruḷik kaṇṭaṉṟō ilaṉ eṉat talivaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. `tām vīḻvār meṉṟōṭ ṭuyiliṉ iṉitukol, tāmarik kaṇṇāṉ ulaku'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. oru tiṟam niṉittal cellāy - cellutal, mīḷutal eṉṉum iru kūṟṟiṉil oṉṟiṉit tuṇiyamāṭṭāyāy. aṭu maruppu eṉṟu piritturittalumām. piṭi pūcaliṉ iṭu aṭi eṉa māṟuka.
380. neytal
(piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi 
talimakaṭkuk kuṟi nayappak kūṟiyatu.)
		tērcēṇ nīkkit tamiyaṉ vantunum
		ūryā teṉṉa naṇinaṇi otuṅki
		muṉṉāṭ pōkiya tuṟivaṉ neruni
		akalili nāval uṇṭuṟi utirtta
	5.	kaṉikaviṉ citiya vāṅkik koṇṭutaṉ
		tāḻi vēraḷi vīḻtuṇik kiṭuum
		alavaṟ kāṭṭi naṟpāṟ ṟituveṉa
		niṉinta neñcamoṭu neṭitupeyarn tōṉē
		2utukkāṇ tōṉṟum tērē iṉṟum
	10.	nāmetir koḷḷā māyiṉ tāṉatu
		tuṇikuvaṉ pōlām nāṇumika vuṭiyaṉ
		veṇmaṇal neṭuṅkōṭṭu maṟikō
		1 amma tōḻi kūṟumati nīyē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 13. tōḻi-, amma - nāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēṭpāyāka.
1-3. tēr cēṇ nīkki - taṉatu tēriṉic cēymiyil niṟutti, tamiyaṉ vantu - taṉiyaṉāy vantu, num ūr yātu eṉṉa - numatu ūr yāteṉṟu viṉavi, naṇi naṇi otuṅki - kuṟukak kuṟuka naṭantu, muṉ nāḷ pōkiya tuṟivaṉ - muṉṉāḷil ceṉṟa tuṟivaṉ,
3-8. neruni - nēṟṟu, akal ili nāval uṇ tuṟi utirtta - akaṉṟa iliyiṉiyuṭiya nāval maram nīr uṇṇum tuṟik kaṇṇē corinta, kaṉi kaviṉ citiya vāṅkikkoṇṭu - kaṉiyiṉi ataṉ aḻakukeṭa iḻuttukkoṇṭu ceṉṟu, tāḻi vēr aḷi taṉ vīḻ tuṇikku iṭūum alavaṉ kāṭṭi - tāḻiyiṉ vērppakkaluḷḷa vaḷiyiluḷḷa taṉatu aṉpu poruntiya peṇ ñeṇṭiṟkut tarum ōr āṇ ñeṇṭiṉik kāṭṭi, nal pāṟṟu itu eṉa - itu nalla pāṉmi yuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟi, neṭitu niṉinta neñcamoṭu peyarntōṉ - neṭitu cintitta neñcuṭaṉ mīṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ,
9-11. iṉṟum utu kāṇ tēr tōṉṟum - iṉṟum utōpār avaṉatu tēr tōṉṟā niṟkum, nām etir koḷḷām āyiṉ - nām avaṉi etirkoṇṭu ēṟkāmāyiṉ, nāṇu mika uṭiyaṉ - mikka nāṇa muṭiyaṉ ākalāṉ, tāṉ atu tuṇikuvaṉ pōlām- avaṉ ataṉi mey yeṉat tuṇivāṉ pōlum,
12-13. veṇ maṇal neṭu kōṭṭu maṟikō - nāṉ veḷḷiya neṭiya maṇaṟ kariyil maṟintu koḷvēṉā, nī kūṟumati - nī uṉ karuttiṉik kūṟuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! tēr cēṇ nīkki vantu muṉṉāṭpōkiya tuṟivaṉ, neruni, alavaṟ kāṭṭi neṭitu niṉinta neñcamoṭu peyarntōṉ. iṉṟum tēr utukkāṇ tōṉṟum, nām etirkoḷḷām āyiṉ, tāṉ atu tuṇikuvaṉ pōlām, neṭuṅkōṭṭu maṟikō, nī kūṟumati.
(vi-ri) yātu eṉṉa - yātu eṉṟu viṉavi. naṇi naṇi otuṅki- mella mella naṭantu eṉka. naṟpāṟṟu - nalla pāṉmiyuṭiyatu; pāṉmi - ūḻ. itu pōṉṟu eṉ kātaliyi uṇpittu iṉpuṟum pēṟṟiṉip peṟṟilēṉē eṉṟu neṭitu niṉinta neñcam eṉka. iṉṟum eṉpataṉi iṭinili viḷakkākak koṇṭu iṉṟum etirkoḷḷāmāyiṉ eṉavum kūṭṭiyurikka. atu tuṇikuvaṉ pōlām eṉṟatu, nām etir koḷḷāmiyiṉ avaṉpāl aṉpilēm eṉṟu tuṇintu oḻikuvaṉ pōlum eṉṟapaṭi. nāṇam mikavuṭiyaṉ eṉṟatu, nāṇ aḻiyap piṉṉum palkāl varuvāṉ allaṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyapaṭiyām. iṉi, atu tuṇikuvaṉ pōlām eṉṟatu maṭa lēṟutal mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟit tuṇikuvaṉ pōlum eṉṟurit talumām. maṟikō eṉṟatu, nī avaṉatu kuṟiyiṉi muṭippāy eṉak kuṟippiṟ kūṟiyatu.
		1`nīkaṇ ṭaṉiyeṉiṉ vāḻali nēriḻi ampalattāṉ
		cēykaṇ ṭaṉiyaṉ ceṉṟāṅkō ralavaṉṟaṉ cīrppeṭiyiṉ
		vāyvaṇ ṭaṉiyatōr nāvaṟ kaṉinaṉi nalkakkaṇṭu
		pēykaṇ ṭaṉiyatoṉ ṟākiniṉ ṟāṉap peruntakiyē.'
eṉṉum ceyyuḷil itaṉ karuttu amintiruttal aṟintu makiḻtaṟ kuriyatu.
381. pāli
(talimakaṉ iṭic curattut taṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		āḷi naṉmāṉ aṇaṅkuṭi yoruttal
		mīḷi vēḻattu neṭuntaki pulampa
		ēntal veṇkōṭu vāṅkik 2kuruttaruntum
		3añcuvarat takuna āṅkaṇ mañcutapa
	5.	aḻalkāṉṟu tiritarum alaṅkukatir maṇṭilam
		niḻalcūṉ ṟuṇṭa nirampā nīḷiṭik
		kaṟṟurik kuṭampik katanāy vaṭukar
		viṟciṉan taṇinta veruvaru kavalik
		kuruti yāṭiya pulavu4nā ṟiruñciṟi
	10.	eruvic cēval īṇṭukiḷit toḻuti
		paccūṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟip paṟinivantu
		cekkar vāṉiṉ vicumpaṇi koḷḷum
		5aruñcuram nīntiya nammiṉum poruntār
		muṉiyaraṇ kaṭanta viṉival tāṉit
	15.	tēṉimir naṟuntār vāṉava ṉuṭaṟṟiya
		oṉṉāt tevvar maṉṉeyil pōlap
		perumpāḻ koṇṭa mēṉiyaḷ neṭituyirttu
		varuntuṅko laḷiyaḷ tāṉē curumpuṇa
		neṭunīr payanta niriyitaḻk kuvaḷi
	20.	etirmala riṇippō taṉṉataṉ
		arimatar maḻikkaṇ teṇpaṉi koḷavē.
- maturi iḷaṅkucikaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē! 1-13. āḷi nalmāṉ aṇaṅku uṭi oruttal - āḷiyākiya nalla vilaṅkiṉatu varuttutaliyuṭiya ēṟu, mīḷivēḻattu neṭuntaki pulampa - valiyuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ talivaṉāṉa kaḷiṟu varunta, ēntal veṇkōṭu vāṅki - ataṉ nimirnta veḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉip paṟittu, kuruttu aruntum - kuruttiṉit tiṉṉum, añcu varat takuna āṅkaṇ - accam uṇṭākat takka curattiṭattē, mañcu tapa - mēkam peyyātoḻiya, aḻal kāṉṟu tiritarum alaṅku katir maṇṭilam - eriyiṉik kakkic cellum aciyum katirkaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟu, niḻal cūṉṟu uṇṭa nirampā nīḷ iṭi - niḻaliṉi akaḻntu uṇṭa cellat toliyāta nīṇṭa iṭattiluḷḷa, kaṟṟu urik kuṭampik katanāy vaṭukar - kaṉṟiṉ tōlālāya kūṭṭiṉiyum ciṉam poruntiya nāyiṉiyumuṭiya vaṭukar, vil ciṉam taṇinta veruvaru kavali - tamatu villāṟ porum vekuḷi nīṅkiya accam varum kavartta neṟikaḷil, kuruti āṭiya pulavu nāṟu iruciṟi eruvic cēval - kuruti paṭinta pulāl nāṟum periya ciṟakiṉiyuṭiya āṇ paruntiṉatu, īṇṭu kiḷit toḻuti - kiḷiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya kūṭṭam, pacu ūṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟi paṟi nivantu - paciya ūṉiṉ mikutiyit terivittup paṟanteḻuntu, cekkar vāṉiṉ vicumpu aṇi
koḷḷum - cevvāṉampōla vicumpiṉi aḻaku ceyyum, arum curam nīntiya nammiṉum - ariya curattiṉik kaṭantu vanta nammik kāṭṭiṉum, (nam talivi)
13-21. poruntār muṉi araṇ kaṭanta viṉi val tāṉi - pakivaratu pōr ceyyum araṇi veṉṟa pōrttoḻil valla cēṉiyiṉiyum, tēṉ imir naṟu tār - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum naṟiya māliyiṉiyumuṭiya, vāṉavaṉ - cēraṉ, uṭaṟṟiya - pōr purintu aḻitta, oṉṉāt tevvar maṉ eyil pōla - māṟupaṭṭa pakivaratu poruntiya matilpōla, perum pāḻ koṇṭa mēṉiyaḷ - mikka pāḻpaṭṭa mēṉiyaḷāki, curumpu uṇa neṭunīr payanta niri itaḻ kuvaḷi - vaṇṭu tēṉip paruka āḻamāṉa nīriṉāl tarappaṭṭa niriyākavuḷḷa itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya kuvaḷiyatu, etir malar iṇi pōtu aṉṉa - putiya malarāya iraṇṭu pūkkaḷip pōṉṟa, taṉ arimatar maḻi kaṇ - taṉatu cevvari paranta matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ, teṇ paṉi koḷa - teḷinta nīrik koḷḷa, neṭitu uyirttu - perumūcceṟintu, varuntum kol - varuntiyiruppaḷō, aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! aruñcuram nīntiya nammiṉum (nam talivi) perum pāḻ koṇṭa mēṉiyaḷ (āki) neṭitu uyirttu taṉ kaṇ teṇ paṉi koḷa varuntuṅkol, aḷiyaḷ.
(vi-ri) āḷiyāṉatu yāṉik kompip paṟittu uṇṇum eṉpatu, `mūrittāḷ āḷiyāṉit, talinilam puraḷaveṇ kōṭuṇṭatē pōṉṟu'1 eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. kuruku eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkum kuruttu eṉpatē poruḷākum. kāṉṟu cūṉṟu eṉpaṉa ilakkaṇi vaḻakku. vampalar maṭintu oḻintamiyiṉ vaṭukar ciṉan taṇintār āyiṟṟu. viṟciṉam - viṟkoṇṭu porum ciṉam. kiḷiyiyuṭiya cēvaliṉatu toḻuti eṉka. paccūṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟutalāvatu tām paṟattalāl ūṉ mikutiyā yiruttali ēṉōr aṟiyac ceytal. kuruti paṭinta ciṟakuṭaṉ vicumpilē vaṭṭamiṭutalāl, eruvic cēvaliṉ toḻuti cev vāṉam pōlvatāyiṟṟu. nīḷiṭiyil uḷḷa kavaliyil kurutiyāṭiya eruvic cēval vicumpu aṇi koḷḷum aruñcuram eṉka. etir malar - appoḻutu alarnta malar.
382. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.)
		piṟaruṟu viḻumam piṟarum nōpa
		tammuṟu viḻumam tamakkō tañcam
		kaṭampukoṭi yāttuk kaṇṇi cūṭṭi
		vēṟupal kurala orutūk kiṉṉiyam
	5.	kāṭukeḻu neṭuvēṭ 2pāṭukoḷik kēṟpa
		3aṇaṅkayar viyaṉkaḷam poliyap piyat
		tūṅkutal purintaṉar namareṉa āṅkavaṟku
		aṟiyak kūṟal vēṇṭum tōḻi
		aruvi pāynta karuviral manti
	10.	ceḻuṅkōṭ palaviṉ paḻampuṇi yākac
		cāraṟ pērūr muṉṟuṟi iḻitarum
		vaṟaṉuṟal aṟiyāc cōli
		viṟaṉmali nāṭaṉ coṉṉayan tōyē.		- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 8-13. tōḻi-,  aruvi pāynta karuviral manti - aruviyiṉkaṭ pāynta kariya viraliyuṭiya peṇ kuraṅku, ceḻu kōḷ palaviṉ paḻam - vaḷamuṟak kāyttalik koḷḷum palāviṉ paḻattiṉi, puṇiyāka - teppamākak koṇṭu, cāral pēr ūr muṉtuṟi iḻitarum - pakka maliyiluḷḷa periya ūriṉ kaṇ tuṟi muṉpu vantiṟaṅkum, vaṟaṉ uṟal aṟiyāc cōli viṟal mali nāṭaṉ - vaṟaṭci yuṟutali aṟiyāta cōliyiyuṭiya veṟṟi poruntiya mali nāṭṭiṉ talivaṉatu, col nayantōy - colli virumpi ēṟṟaṉi;
1-2. piṟar uṟu viḻumam piṟarum nōpa - piṟarkku uṟum tuṉpam kāṇiṉ nallōr tām piṟarē yāyiṉum nōvāniṉṟu vilakka muyalvar, (aṉṟi), tam uṟu viḻumam tamakkō tañcam - tamakku uṟṟa tuṉpiṉit tamakkup peritākak koḷḷātu eḷitākak koḷvar, (itu cāṉṟōr iyaṟki.)
3-8. namar - nammavar, kaṭampu koṭi yāttu - kaṭampiṉkaṭ koṭiyiṉik kaṭṭi, kaṇṇi cūṭṭi - māli cūṭṭi, vēṟu pal kurala oru tūkku iṉ iyam - pal vēṟu kurali uṭiyaṉavākiya tāḷattiṉ vaḻippaṭum oru tūkkiṉiyuṭiya iṉiya vācciyaṅ
kaḷik koṇṭu, kāṭu keḻu neṭu vēḷ pāṭu koḷikku ēṟpa - kāṭṭiṟ poruntiya neṭiya murukaṉip pāṭum pāṭṭiṟkup porunta, aṇaṅku ayar viyaṉ kaḷam poliya - veṟiyāṭṭuc ceyyum periya kaḷam ciṟappuṟa, piya tūṅkutal purintaṉar - mella āṭutali virumpiṉar, eṉa - eṉṟu, āṅku avaṟku aṟiyak kūṟal vēṇṭum - talivaṉik kāṇumiṭattu avaṉukku nī uṇarak kūṟutal takkatu.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! mali nāṭaṉ col nayantōy, piṟaruṟu viḻumam piṟarum nōpa, tammuṟu viḻumam tamakkō tañcam, namar neṭuvēṭ pāṭu koḷik kēṟpak kaḷam poliyat tūṅkutal purintaṉar eṉa avaṟku aṟiyak kūṟal vēṇṭum.
(vi-ri) piṟaruṟṟa tuṉpattiṟku varuntutalum tammiyuṟṟa tuṉpattip poṟuttalum cāṉṟōr iyalpākalāl, nam talivar nām uṟṟa tuṉpatti aṟiyiṉ virintu ataṉip pōkkutaṟku muṟpaṭuvar eṉat tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. oru tūkku iṉṉiyam - tāḷaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻivarum centūkku mutalākiya eḻuvakit tūkkiṉuḷ oṉṟiṉip poruntiya iṉṉiyam eṉka. kāṭu eṉṟatu īṇṭuk kuṟiñciyi. tōḻi eṉṟatu taliviyi.
383. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya tāy colliyatu.)
		1taṟpuran teṭutta eṟṟuṟan tuḷḷāḷ
		ūruñ cēriyum ōrāṅ kalareḻak
		2kāṭuṅ kāvum avaṉoṭu tuṇintu
		nāṭun tēyamum naṉipala iṟanta
	5.	ciṟuvaṉ kaṇṇik kērtē ṟuvareṉa
		vāṭiṉi vāḻiyō vayali nāṭoṟum
		3palkiḷik koṭikkom palamara malarnta
		alkul 4taḻikkūṭṭu aṅkuḻi utaviya
		viṉiyami varaṉīr viḻuttoṭi tattak
	10.	kamañcūṟ peruniṟi tayaṅka mukantukoṇṭu
		āymaṭak kaṇṇaḷ tāymukam nōkkip
		peycilam polippap peyarvaṉaḷ vikalum
		āranīr ūṭṭip purappōr
		yārmaṟṟup peṟukuvi aḷiyi nīyē.
- kayamaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. vayali - vayalik koṭiyē, vāḻiyō - nī vāḻi, taṉ purantu eṭutta - taṉṉi vaḷartteṭutta, eṉ tuṟantu uḷḷāḷ - eṉṉiyum niṉiyāḷāyt tuṟantu, ūrum cēriyum ōrāṅku alar eḻa - ūriṉum cēriyiṉum oru peṟṟiyē alar mika, kāṭum kāvum avaṉoṭu tuṇintu - kāṭum cōliyum at talivaṉoṭu pōkat tuṇintu, nāṭum tēyamum naṉipala iṟanta - mikap palavāya nāṭukaḷiyum tēyaṅkaḷiyum kaṭanta, ciṟu vaṉ kaṇṇikku - iḷiya vaṉkaṇmi yuṭiyāṭku, ēr tēṟuvar  eṉa - aḻaku ceyvippār pōla, vāṭiṉi - vāṭiyuḷḷāy;
6-14. nāḷ toṟum - ovvoru nāḷum, pal kiḷi koṭi kompu - palakiḷiyāya oḻukiya kompukaḷ, alamara - pāram tāṅkātu cuḻala, malarnta - virinta, alkul taḻik kūṭṭu am kuḻi utaviya - alkulil taḻi yuṭikkuk kūṭṭum aḻakiya taḷirkaḷi nī tarumāṟu, āy maṭa kaṇṇaḷ - aḻakiya maṭappam tōṉṟum kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya eṉ makaḷ, viḻu toṭi tatta - ciṟanta vaḷiyal aciyavum, pey cilampu olippa - kāliṟ peyta cilampu olikkavum, viṉi ami varal nīr - toḻiṟpaṭṭa mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyiṉ vaḻiyāka vantu viḻum nīriṉi, kamam cūl peru niṟi tayaṅka mukantu koṇṭu- niṟinta nīriṉik koḷḷum periya cālil aciya mukantu eṭuttuk koṇṭu vantu ūṟṟi, tāy mukam nōkki - tāyip pōla niṉ mukattiṉip pārttu, vikalum - nāṭōṟum, peyarvaṉaḷ - peyarvāḷāvaḷ aṉṟō, (avaḷ pōla) āra nīr ūṭṭi - niṟiya nīriṉi uṇpittu, purappōr maṟṟu yār peṟukuvi - purappār yāri iṉi nī peṟuvāy; nī aḷiyi - nī iraṅkat takkāy.
(muṭipu) vayali vāḻi! kāṭum kāvum avaṉoṭu tuṇintu nāṭum tēyamum iṟanta ciṟu vaṉkaṇṇikku ēr tēṟuvareṉa vāṭiṉi. nāṭoṟum am kuḻi utaviya, āymaṭak kaṇṇaḷ ami varal nīr peru niṟi tayaṅka mukantu koṇṭuvantu ūṟṟit tāy mukam nōkkip peyarvaṉaḷ aṉṟō. (avaḷ pōla) iṉi nīrūṭṭip purappōr yār peṟukuvi nī; aḷiyi.
(vi-ri) cēri - ūriṉ oru pakuti.  `ūruñ cēriyum uṭaṉiyin talareḻa'1 eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka. kāṭum kāṉamum eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ kāṭum curamum eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka. tēyam - nāṭu pala cērntatu. tēṟuvar - teḷipavar; teḷintu ceyvippavar eṉṉum poruṭṭu. malarnta kuḻi utaviya eṉavum, amivaral nīr niṟi tayaṅka mukantu koṇṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka. utaviya - utavumāṟu; ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeccam. ami mūṅkiṟ kuḻāy. niṟi - cāl; nīrccāl vayiṟu puṭit tiruttaliṉ kamañ cūṟ peru niṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. tayaṅkal - acital. tāy mukam nōkki - tāypōla mukam nōkki eṉa virinturikka. āra - niṟiya, uṇṇa eṉṟumām. maṟṟu - viṉimāṟṟiṉkaṇ vantatu.
384. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉ varavukaṇṭu uḻiyar colliyatu.)
		irunta vēntaṉ aruntoḻil muṭitteṉap
		purinta kātaloṭu peruntēr yāṉum
		ēṟiya taṟintaṉ ṟallatu vantavāṟu
		naṉiyaṟin taṉṟō ilaṉē tāay
	5.	muyaṟpaṟaḻ ukaḷum mulliyam puṟaviṟ
		kavikkatir varakiṉ cīṟūr āṅkaṇ
		melliyal arivi ilvayiṉ niṟīi
		iḻimiṉ eṉṟaniṉ moḻimaruṇ ṭiciṉē
		vāṉvaḻaṅ kiyaṟki vaḷipūṭ ṭiṉiyō
	10.	māṉuru vākaniṉ maṉampūṭ ṭiṉiyō
		urimati  vāḻiyō valava eṉattaṉ
		varimaruḷ mārpiṉ naḷippaṉaṉ muyaṅki
		maṉikkoṇṭu pukkaṉaṉ neṭuntaki
		viruntēr peṟṟaṉaḷ tiruntiḻi yōḷē.
1okkūr mācāttiyār.
(co-ḷ) 11. valava - pākaṉē, vāḻi- vāḻvāyāka.
1-11. irunta vēntaṉ - pācaṟikkaṇ ṇirunta nam aracaṉ, arum toḻil muṭitteṉa - ariya pōriṉi veṉṟi koṇṭu muṭittāṉāka, purinta kātaloṭu - virumpiya kātaloṭu, yāṉum-, perum tēr ēṟiyatu aṟintaṉṟu allatu - periya tēriṉkaṇ ēṟiyamarntati aṟintatē yaṉṟi, vantavāṟu - iṅku vantuṟṟa pariciṉi, naṉi aṟintaṉṟō ilaṉ - naṉku aṟintilēṉ, muyal paṟaḻ tāay ukaḷum mulli am puṟavil - muyaṟ kuṭṭikaḷ tāvik kutikkum mulliyāya aḻakiya kāṭṭil, kavi katir varakiṉ ciṟu ūr āṅkaṇ - kavartta katiriṉik koṇṭa varakiṉiyuṭiya ciṟiya ūriṭattē, mel iyal arivi ilvayiṉ niṟīi - meṉmit taṉmi vāynta taliviyiṉ illiṉkaṇ tēriṉi niṟutti, iḻimiṉ eṉṟa niṉ moḻi maruṇṭiciṉ - iṟaṅkuka eṉṟa niṉ coṟkēṭṭu maruṭciyuṟṟēṉ, vāṉ vaḻaṅku iyaṟki vaḷi - vāṉil iyaṅkum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya kāṟṟiṉi, māṉ uruvāka - kutiriyiṉ vaṭivāka, pūṭṭiṉiyō - pūṭṭi vantaṉiyō, (aṉṟi), niṉ maṉam pūṭṭiṉiyō - niṉatu maṉattiṉi (aṅṅaṉam) pūṭṭi vantaṉiyō, urimati - kūṟuvāyāka, eṉa - eṉṟu kūṟi,
13. neṭuntaki - peruntakimiyuṭiya talivaṉ,
11-13. taṉ vari maruḷ mārpiṉ naḷippaṉaṉ muyaṅki - taṉatu maliyi yotta mārpiṉakattē (avaṉic) ceṟip pōṉāyt taḻuvi, maṉi koṇṭu pukkaṉaṉ - taṉ maṉikkaṇ uṭaṉ koṇṭu pukuntaṉaṉ;
14. tiruntu iḻiyōḷ - tiruntiya aṇikaḷiyuṭiya avaṉ talivi, viruntu ēr peṟṟaṉaḷ - viruntōmpum ciṟappiṉip peṟṟāḷ.
(muṭipu) valava! vāḻi! yāṉum peruntēr ēṟiyatu aṟintaṉṟalatu, vantavāṟu aṟintaṉṟō ilaṉ, cīṟūr āṅkaṇ ilvayiṉ niṟīi iḻimiṉ eṉṟa niṉmoḻi maruṇṭiciṉ, nītāṉ vaḷiyiṉi māṉuruvākap pūṭṭiṉiyō, aṉṟi, niṉ maṉattiṉi aṅṅaṉam pūṭṭiṉiyō, urimati eṉa neṭuntaki taṉ mārpiṉ muyaṅki maṉik koṇṭu pukkaṉaṉ; tiruntiḻi viruntu ēr peṟṟaṉaḷ.
(vi-ri) vēntaṉ aruntoḻil muṭittu eṉa - vēn taṉuṭiya ariya toḻili yāṉ muṭittēṉāka eṉṟurittalum ām. naṉi aṟital - naṉku aṟital, moḻi maruṇṭiciṉ - moḻiyik kēṭṭu maruṇṭēṉ. vāṉ vaḻaṅ kiyaṟki vaḷipūṭ - ṭiṉiyō, māṉuruvāka niṉ maṉam pūṭṭiṉiyō urimati - eṉṟatu piṟaporuṭkaṇ tōṉṟiya ākkam paṟṟiya viyappu. naḷippaṉaṉ - ceṟippōṉāki, naḷi eṉṉum uriyaṭiyākap piṟanta viṉi. viruntō peṟṟaṉaḷ eṉṟu pāṭam koḷḷutalumām, itaṟku ōkāram ciṟappu.
(mē-ḷ) `putumi perumi'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `.......peruntēryāṉum....... maruṇṭiciṉē' eṉpataṉi maruṭki eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉar, iḷam.
`karaṇattiṉ amintu muṭinta kāli'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, pērici ūrtip pākar pāṅkiṉum eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉāṟ pākaṉ ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟum, `viṉivayiṟ pirintōṉ'3 eṉṉum cūttirattu, itaṉuḷ `purinta kātaloṭu........ māṉuruvāka niṉ maṉam pūṭṭiṉiyō, urimati vāḻiyō valava' eṉa `uḷḷam pōla uṟṟuḻi utaviṟṟu eṉat talivaṉ kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
385. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		taṉṉō raṉṉa āyamum mayiliyal
		eṉṉō raṉṉa tāyaruṅ - kāṇak
		kival yāṉik kaṭuntērc cōḻar
		kāvirip paṭappi uṟanti yaṉṉa
	5.	poṉṉuṭi neṭunakarp puriyōr ayara
		naṉmāṇ viḻavil takaram maṇṇi
		4āmpala puṇarppac cellāḷ kāmpoṭu
		nelli nīṭiya kallaṟik kavāaṉ
		atta ālattu alantali neṭuvīḻ
	10.	tittik kuṟaṅkil tirunta uriña
		vaḷiyuṭi muṉki aḷiik kiḷiya
		payilirum piṇiyaṟ pacuṅkāḻk kōvi
		akalami yalkul paṟṟik kūntal
		āṭumayiṟ pīliyiṟ poṅka naṉṟum
	15.	tāṉamar tuṇivaṉ ūkka ūṅki
		uḷḷātu kaḻinta muḷḷeyiṟṟut tuvarvāyc
		ciṟuvaṉ kaṇṇi cilampukaḻīi
		aṟiyāt tēettaḷ ākutal koṭitē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-7. mayil iyal - mayilpōlum cāyaliyuṭiya eṉ makaḷ, taṉ ōraṉṉa āyamum - taṉṉi yotta tōḻimārum, eṉ ōraṉṉa tāyarum kāṇa - eṉṉi yotta tāymārum kaṇṭu makiḻa, kival yāṉi kaṭu tēr cōḻar - valiya kiyiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyiyum kaṭiya tēriṉiyumuṭiya cōḻaratu, kāvirip paṭappi uṟanti aṉṉa - kāviriyiṉ pakkattatākiya uṟiyūri yotta, poṉ uṭi neṭu nakar - celvam mikka periya maṉiyil, puriyōr ayara - mēlōr vatuvi ceyvikka (nikaḻum), nal māṇ viḻavil - nalla ciṟappuṟṟa maṇaviḻāvil, takaram maṇṇi - avaṭku mayirc cāntiṉip pūci, ām pala puṇarppa cellāḷ - maṟṟum poruntiya pala ciṟappukkaḷiyum ceyvikka maṇantu cellāḷāy,
7-18. kāmpoṭu nelli nīṭiya kal aṟi kavāaṉ - mūṅkiluṭaṉ nellimaram uyarnta kaṟpāṟikaḷiyuṭiya pakka
maliyilē, attam ālattu alantali neṭuvīḻ - neṟiyiluḷḷa ālamarattiṉ vāṭi yacikiṉṟa neṭiya viḻutu, titti kuṟaṅkil tirunta uriña - taṉatu tēmal poruntiya tuṭiyil naṉku urāyntiṭa, kūntal āṭu mayil pīliyil poṅka - kūntalāṉatu āṭum mayiliṉ tōki pōlap poṅkip parakka, tāṉ amar tuṇivaṉ - taṉṉāl virumpappeṟṟa tuṇivaṉ, vaḷiyuṭi muṉki aḷii - taṉatu vaḷi poruntiya muṉ kiyiṉip paṟṟiyum, kiḷiya payil irum piṇiyal pacu kāḻ kōvi - pala kiḷiyiṉavāy neruṅkiya periya māliyāya aḻakiya vaṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa mēkali yaṇinta, akal ami alkul paṟṟi - akaṟci yaminta alkuli aṇittum, naṉṟum ūkka - ūṅki - peritum ūkkamuṟutta ūkkaṅ koṇṭu, uḷḷātu kaḻinta - vaḻivaruttam eṇṇātu kaḻinta, muḷ eyiṟṟu tuvar vāy ciṟu vaṉkaṇṇi - muṭpōṉṟa kūriya paṟkaḷiyum  pavaḷam pōṉṟa vāyiṉiyu muṭiya añcāmiyuṭiyaḷāya iḷiyaḷ, cilampu kaḻīi aṟiyāt tēettaḷ ākutal - aṟiyappaṭāta tēyattilē cilampu kaḻittu vatuvi ceytiruttal, koṭitu - iṉṉāmiyuṭiyatu.
(muṭipu) mayiliyal, āyamum tāyarum kāṇa, uṟanti yaṉṉa neṭu nakarilē, puriyōr ayara, viḻavil, takaram maṇṇi, ām pala puṇarppac cellāḷāy, kal aṟi kavāṉ, tāṉamar tuṇivaṉ ūkka ūṅkik kaḻitta ciṟuvaṉkaṇṇi, cilampu kaḻīi, aṟiyāt tēettaḷ ākutal koṭitu.
(vi-ri) kāvirip paṭappi uṟanti - kāviriyiṉ tōṭṭak kūṟu cūḻnta uṟiyūr eṉṟumām. ām pāl puṇarppa eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, ākum ūḻ puṇarkka eṉṟu urikka. alantali - alanta tali eṉṟataṉ vikāramumām. muṉ ki kiḷittalāl kūntal mayiṟpīli pōlat tāḻntu alkul paṟṟip poṅka eṉṟurittalumām. ūkka ūṅki - celuttac ceṉṟu eṉṟumām. aṟiyāt tēettaḷāyc cilampu kaḻīi iruttal eṉa māṟuka.
386. marutam
(tōḻi vāyiṉ maṟuttatu. talimakaḷ takuti colliyatūumām.)
		poyki nīrnāyp pulavunāṟu irumpōttu
		vāḷi nāḷiri tērum ūra
		nāṇiṉeṉ peruma yāṉē pāṇaṉ
		mallaṭu mārpiṉ valiyuṟa varunti
	5.	etirtalik koṇṭa āriyap porunaṉ
		1niṟittiraṇ muḻavuttōḷ kiyakat toḻinta
		tiṟaṉvēṟu kiṭakki nōkki naṟpōrk
		kaṇiyaṉ nāṇi yāṅku maṟiyiṉaḷ
		mella vantu nalla kūṟi
	10.	miyīr ōti maṭavōy yāṉuniṉ
		cēri yēṉē ayali lāṭṭiyēṉ
		nuṅki yākuveṉ niṉakkeṉat taṉkit
		noṭumaṇi melviral taṇṇeṉat tivara
		nutalum kūntalum nīvip
	15.	pakalvantu peyarnta vāṇutaṟ kaṇṭē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. poyki pulavu nāṟu nīr nāy irum pōttu - poykikkaṇ ṇuḷḷa pulāl nāṟum periya nīr nāyiṉ āṇ, nāḷ iri vāḷi tērum ūra - nāṭkālattē vāḷiyāya iriyiṉi ārāyum ūriyuṭiya taliva!
3. peruma - perumāṉē!
8-15. maṟiyiṉaḷ mella vantu - maṟintu melleṉa vantu, nalla kūṟi - mukamaṉurikaḷ kūṟi, mi īr ōti maṭavōy - kariya periya kūntali yuṭiya maṭavōḷē, yāṉum niṉ cēriyēṉē-yāṉum niṉ cēriyil uḷḷavaḷē, ayal illāṭṭiyēṉ - ayaṉ maṉikku uriyēṉ, niṉakku nuṅki ākuveṉ - uṉakkut taṅki yāvēṉ, eṉa - eṉṟu kūṟi, taṉ ki toṭu maṇi mel viral taṇ eṉa tivara nutalum kūntalum nīvi - taṉ kiyiṉ mōtiram aṇinta melliya viralāl taṇṇeṉṟu porunta neṟṟiyiṉiyum kūntaliyum taṭavi, pakal vantu peyarnta vāḷ nutal kaṇṭu - pakaṟ pōtil vantu mīṇṭa oḷḷiya neṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiya niṉ parattiyik kaṇṭu,
3-8. pāṇaṉ mal aṭu mārpiṉ vali uṟa varunti - pāṇaṉ eṉpāṉatu maṟpōr ceytalil valla mārpiṉatu valimi mikalāṉ varunti, etir talik koṇṭa - avaṉōṭu etir niṉṟu porutali mēṟ koṇṭa, āriyap porunaṉ - āriyap porunaṉ eṉpāṉatu, niṟi tiraṇ muḻavu tōḷ - vali niṟitalik koṇṭa tiraṇṭa muḻavu pōṉṟa tōḷkaḷ, ki akattu oḻinta tiṟaṉ vēṟu kiṭakki nōkki - ap pāṇaṉ kiyakappaṭṭu oḻiyak kiṭanta vēṟu vakiyākiya kiṭakkiyip pārttu, nal pōr kaṇiyaṉ nāṇiyāṅku- nalla pōr valla kaṇiyaṉ eṉpāṉ nāṇiyatu pōla,
3. yāṉ nāṇiṉeṉ - yāṉ nāṇamuṟṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) ūra! peruma! maṟiyiṉaḷ vantu nalla kūṟi nutalum kūntalum nīvi, pakal vantu peyarnta vāṇutaṟ kaṇṭu, pāṇaṉoṭu poruta āriyap porunaṉ muḻavut tōḷkaḷ avaṉ kiyakappaṭṭu oḻiyak kiṭanta kiṭakkiyi nōkkik kaṇiyaṉ nāṇiyāṅka yāṉ, nāṇiṉeṉ.
(vi-ri) `pāṇaṉ...... kaṇiyaṉ nāṇi yāṅku' eṉṉum pakutiyiṟ kūṟiya varalāṟu: maṟpōril valla pāṇaṉ eṉpāṉum āriyap porunaṉ oruvaṉum maṟpōr poruta kāliyil, porunaṉ tōḷkaḷ pāṇaṉ kiyakattu akappaṭṭu aṟṟu oḻiya avaṉ iṟantu kiṭantataṉi nōkkik kaṇiyaṉ eṉpāṉ nāṇiṉāṉ eṉpatu. iṅkuk kūṟappeṟṟa kaṇiyaṉ eṉpāṉ āriyap porunaṉukku naṇpaṉākavēṉum avaṉē velvāṉ eṉac cūḷurittavaṉāka
vēṉum iruttal vēṇṭum. tiṟaṉ vēṟu kiṭakki eṉṟatu, uyir nīṅkik kiṭakkum kiṭakkiyi. oḻinta kiṭakki - tōḷ oḻiya uṭal kiṭanta kiṭakki eṉka. tōṭki yakattu eṉpatoru pāṭam iruppiṉ, pāṇaṉāṉavaṉ āriyap porunaṉuṭiya kiyil akappaṭṭu oḻintāṉ eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷal takum. aṅṅaṉa māyiṉ kaṇiyaṉ pāṇaṉukku naṭpāḷaṉ ātal vēṇṭum. maṭavōy eṉṟatu paratti taliviyi viḷittatu. nuṅki yākuvēṉ eṉṟataṟku niṉakkut tōḻi yāvēṉ eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar poruḷ koḷvar. maṇi - maṇi patitta mōtiram. toṭutal - aṇital. pakal vantu peyarnta eṉṟataṉāl paratti palaruṅ kāṇak kūcātu vantāḷ eṉpatum, nutalum kūntalum nīvi eṉṟataṉāl avaḷ taliviyip pēṇātu oḻukiṉāḷ eṉpatum kūṟi, ataṟkut talivi nāṇiṉāḷ eṉpāḷ nāṇiṉeṉ yāṉē eṉa ataṉit taṉ mēl ēṟaṭṭut tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
(u-ṟi) nīr nāyiṉ pulavu nāṟum pōttu nāṭ kālattē vāḷi iri tērum ūra eṉṟatu, talivaṉ taṉ puṟattoḻukkattāl ūrellām alareḻac ceytu, viṭiyalil taliviyi nāṭi varukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟapaṭi.
(mē-ḷ) `peṟaṟkarum perumporuṇ muṭintapiṉ vanta'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, pēṇā oḻukkam nāṇiya poruḷiṉum eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ yāṉ niṉakkut tōḻi yāvēṉ eṉap paratti nīviya pēṇā oḻukkattiṟkut talivi nāṇiyatu kaṇṭu, tāṉ nāṇiṉēṉeṉṟu talivaṟkut tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟār nac.
387. pāli
(talimakaḷatu kuṟippaṟintu tōḻi talimakaṉic celavaḻuṅkac colliyatu.)
		tiruntiḻi nekiḻntu peruntōḷ cāay
		arimatar maḻikkaṇ kaluḻac celvīr
		varuvīr ākutal urimiṉ maṉṉō
		2uvaruṇap paṟinta ūṉṟalic ciṟāaroṭu
	5.	avvarik koṉṟa kaṟicēr vaḷḷukirp
		paciviraṟ pulitti neṭitupicin tūṭṭiya
		pūntukil imikkum polaṉkāḻ alkul
		avvari citiya nōkki vevviṉip
		3payilariṟ kiṭanta vēṭṭuviḷi verīi
	10.	varippuṟa italiṉ maṇikkaṭ pēṭi
		nuṇpoṟi aṇinta eruttiṟ kūrmuṭ
		ceṅkāṟ cēvaṟ payirum āṅkaṇ
		villīṇ ṭaruñcaman tatiya nūṟi
		nallici niṟutta nāṇuṭi maṟavar
	15.	nirinili naṭukaṟ porunti imiyātu
		irinaciik kiṭanta mutuvāyp palli
		ciṟiya 4teṟṟuva tāyiṟ periya
		ōṭi yāṉi uyarntō rāyiṉum
		niṉṟāṅkup peyaruṅ kāṉañ
	20.	ceṉṟōr maṉṉeṉa irukkiṟ pōrkkē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. tiruntu iḻi nekiḻntu - ivaḷ tiruntiya aṇi nekiḻavum, peru tōḷ cāay - periya tōḷ meliyavum, ari matar maḻi kaṇ kaluḻa - ari paranta matartta kaṇkaḷil nīroḻukavum, celvīr - cellum talivarē,
4-8. uvar uṇa paṟinta ūṉ talic ciṟāaroṭu - uvar maṇ arittalāl mayir kaḻinta ūṉ poruntiya taliyiṉiyuṭiya ciṟuvaruṭaṉ, a vari koṉṟa kaṟi cēr vaḷ ukir - āṭiyiṉ maṟukkaḷic cititta kaṟi poruntiya kūriya nakattiṉiyum, paci viral pulatti - kañcip paci koṇṭa viraliyu muṭiya vaṇṇātti, neṭitu picintu ūṭṭiya pū tukil imikkum - neṭum poḻutu picintu kañci ūṭṭiya aḻakiya āṭi oḷirum, polaṉ kāḻ alkul - poṉ vaṭam aṇinta alkuliṉkaṇ, a vari citiya nōkki - aḻakiya titali aḻiya ataṉip pārttu,
8-12. vevviṉi payil aril kiṭanta - koṭuntoḻili yuṭiyarum piṇakkam poruntiya kāṭukaḷil taṅkikkiṭap pāru mākiya, vēṭṭu viḷi verīi - vēṭṭuvaratu kūppīṭṭik kēṭṭu añci, vari puṟa italiṉ maṇi kaṇ pēṭi - puṟattē varikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭiyiṉ nīlamaṇi pōṉṟa kaṇṇiyuṭiya pēṭi, nuṇpoṟi aṇinta eruttiṉ - nuṇṇiya puḷḷikaḷi aṇinta kaḻuttiṉiyum, kūrmuḷ ceṅkāl - kūriya muṭpōṉṟa viralik koṇṭa civanta kāliyumuṭiya, cēval payirum āṅkaṇ - taṉ cēvali aḻikkum curattē,
13-20. vil īṇṭu aru camam tatiya nūṟi - viṟpaṭi neruṅkiya ariya pōriṉi aḻiyak koṉṟu, nal ici niṟutta nāṇ uṭi maṟavar - nalla pukaḻi nilināṭṭip paṭṭa māṉam mikka vīraratu, niri nili naṭukal porunti - variciyāka niṟṟaliyuṭiya naṭukallip porunti yiruntu, imiyātu iri naciik kiṭanta mutuvāyp palli - kaṇṇimiyātu uṇavi vēṭṭuk kiṭanta mutumi vāynta palli, ciṟiya teṟṟuvatu āyiṉ - ciṟṟaḷavil taṭippaṭuttuvatāyiṉ, periya ōṭi yāṉi uyarntōr āyiṉum - periya neṟṟip paṭṭam aṇinta yāṉiyiṟ cellum aracarāyiṉum, āṅku niṉṟu - avviṭattiṉiṉṟu mēṟ cellātu, peyarum kāṉam ceṉṟōr maṉ eṉa - mīḷum kāṉattiṉkaṇ nam talivar ceṉṟōr eṉṟu kūṟi,
20. irukkiṟpōrkku - āṟṟiyirukka vallārkku,
3. varuvīr ākutal urimiṉ - nīr virivil vantuviṭu vīrātalik kūṟumiṉ.
(muṭipu) iḻi nekiḻntu tōḷ cāay kaṇ kaluḻac celvīr! italiṉ pēṭi vēṭṭu viḷi verīi cēvaṟ payirum āṅkaṇ, palli naṭukaṟ porunti ciṟiya teṟṟuvatāyiṉ uyarntōrāyiṉum peyarum kāṉam ceṉṟōrmaṉ eṉak kūṟi, pūntukil imikkum alkul avvari citiya nōkki irukkiṟ pōrkku, varuvīrākutal urimiṉ.
(vi-ri) vari - āṭiyiṉ maṟu. koṉṟa kaṟi cēr - pōkkutalāl kaṟi poruntiya. paci - kañcip paci; `paci kol melviraṟ peruntōṭ pulitti'1 eṉa muṉvantami kāṇka. vil  villiyuṭiya tāṉikku āku peyar. maṟavar - karanti maṟavar. teṟṟuvatu - koṭṭuvatu eṉṟalumām. virintu varuvēṉeṉṟu kūṟip piriyaluṟṟa talimakaṟku, `kāṉañ ceṉṟōr eṉak kūṟi av vari citiya nōkki, āṟṟiyirup pōrkku, nīr mīṇṭu varuvīrātali urimiṉ' eṉat tōḻi kūṟic celavaḻuṅkuvittāḷ eṉka. āṟṟiyirēṉ eṉpatu kuṟippu. talivikkum taṉakkum vēṟupāṭu iṉmiyiṉ iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāḷ. `cellāmi yuṇṭēl eṉakkuri maṟṟuniṉ, valvaravu vāḻ vārk kuri'2 eṉpatum ataṟkup parimēlaḻakar uritta uriyum kāṇka.
388. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ciṟippuṟamākat talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu. tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colliyatumām.)
		amma vāḻi tōḻi nammali
		amiyaṟut tiyaṟṟiya vevvāyt taṭṭiyiṉ
		naṟuviri āram aṟaveṟin tuḻuta
		3uḷikkuraṟ ciṟutiṉi kavartaliṟ kiḷiyamal
	5.	peruvari yaṭukkattuk kurīi ōppi
		ōṅkiruñ cilampiṉ oḷḷiṇar naṟuvī
		vēṅkiyaṅ kavaṭṭiṭi nivanta itaṇattup
		poṉmaruḷ naṟuntā tūtum tumpi
		iṉṉici ōrā iruntaṉam āka
	10.	miyīr oti maṭanal līrē
		novviyaṟ pakaḻi pāynteṉap puṇkūrntu
		4evvamoṭu vanta uyarmarup poruttalnum
		puṉattuḻip pōka luṟumō maṟṟeṉac
		ciṉavukkoḷ ñamali ceyirttuppuṭi yāṭac
	15.	collik kaḻinta valviṟ kāḷi
		cāntār akalamum takiyum mikanayantu
		īṅkunām uḻakkum evvam uṇarāḷ
		naṉṉar neñcamoṭu mayaṅki veṟiyeṉa
		aṉṉi tanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ
	20.	emmiṟi aṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉṟu innōy
		taṇimarun taṟival eṉṉu māyiṉ
		viṉaviṉ evaṉō maṟṟē kaṉalciṉa
		miyal vēḻam meyyuḷam pōka
		ūṭṭi yaṉṉa ūṉpuraḷ ampoṭu
	25.	kāṭṭumāṉ aṭivaḻi 1oṟṟi
		vēṭṭañ cellumō nummiṟi eṉavē.
- ūṭṭiyār.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, amma - yāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēṭpāyāka.
1-9. nam mali ami aṟuttu iyaṟṟiya vevvāy taṭṭiyiṉ - namatu maliyiṉ mūṅkili aṟuttuc ceyta vevviya ōci poruntiya taṭṭiyāl, naṟu viri āram aṟa eṟintu uḻuta uḷi kural ciṟu tiṉi kavartaliṉ - naṟiya maṇam koṇṭa cantaṉa maratti muḻuvatum veṭṭi uḻutu vititta tiṉiyiṉ tuyyiyuṭiya ciṟu katirkaḷik kavartalāl, kiḷi amal peruvari aṭukkattu kurīi ōppi - mūṅkilkaḷ neruṅkiya periya malic cāraliṉ kaṇṇē kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭi, ōṅku iru cilampiṉ oḷ iṇar naṟu vī vēṅki am kavaṭṭu iṭi nivanta itaṇattu - ōṅkiya periya maliyil oḷi poruntiya kottukkaḷil naṟiya malarkaḷik koṇṭa vēṅki marattiṉ aḻakiya kaviyiṭattē uyarak kaṭṭiya paraṇil, poṉ maruḷ naṟu tātu ūtum tumpi - poṉṉi yotta naṟiya pūm poṭiyiṉi ūtum vaṇṭiṉ, iṉ ici ōrā iruntaṉamāka- iṉiya iciyic ceviyuṟṟu iruntēmāka,
10-15. mi īr ōti maṭa nallīrē - kariya nīṇṭa kūntaliyuṭiya maṭappam vāynta maṅkiyarē, novvu iyal pakaḻi pāynteṉa - viriyum iyaluṭiya ampu pāyntatāka, puṇ kūrntu - puṇmikku, evvamoṭu vanta uyar maruppu oruttal - tuṉpattuṭaṉ vanta uyarnta kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya kaḷiṟu, num puṉattuḻip pōkal uṟumō - uṅkaḷ puṉattiṉkaṇ pōtaluṟṟatō, eṉa - eṉṟu, 
ciṉavu koḷ ñamali ceyirttup puṭiyāṭa - kōpittalik koṇṭa nāy kaṟuvit taṉ pakkalilē toṭara, colli kaḻinta val vil kāḷi - viṉavic ceṉṟa valiya villiyuṭiya kāḷiyāya talivaṉatu,
16-17. cāntu ār akalamum takiyum mika nayantu - cāntam poruntiya mārpiṉiyum aḻakiṉiyum mika virumpi, īṅku nām uḻakkum evvam uṇarāḷ - iṅkē nām varuntum tuṉpiṉi uṇarāḷāy,
18-19. aṉṉi naṉṉar neñcamoṭu mayaṅki - nam aṉṉi naṉṟākiya taṉ neñcattil piṟaḻa uṇarntu, veṟi eṉa tanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ - veṟiyāṭal vēṇṭumeṉa aḻittu vanta mutumi vāynta vēlaṉ,
20-21. em iṟi aṇaṅkaliṉ i nōy vantaṉṟu - em iṟivaṉāṉa murukavēḷ varuttaliṉ inta nōy vantuḷatu,  taṇi maruntu aṟival eṉṉum āyiṉ - itaṉit taṇivikkum maruntiṉi yāṉ aṟivēṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvāṉāyiṉ,
22-26. num iṟi - numatu iṟivaṉāya murukavēḷ, kaṉal ciṉa miyal vēḻam mey uḷam pōka - kotikkum ciṉamum mata mayakkamuṭiya kaḷiṟṟiṉ uṭaliṉuḷ uruvic cella, ūṭṭi aṉṉa ūṉ puraḷ ampoṭu - cenniṟam pūciṉāṟpōṉṟa ūṉ poruntiya ampiṉuṭaṉ, kāṭṭu māṉ aṭi vaḻi oṟṟi - kāṭṭiṉ vilaṅkukaḷ ceṉṟa aṭiccuvaṭṭiṉit toṭarntu, vēṭṭam cellumō eṉavē - vēṭṭiyāṭaṟkuc cellutalum uṇṭō eṉṟu, viṉaviṉ evaṉō - nām viṉaviṉ eṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! amma!  nam mali, ciṟu tiṉi kavartaliṉ, peru vari yaṭukkattu kurīi ōppi, itaṇattut tumpi iṉṉici ōrā iruntaṉamāka, maṭa nallīrē! evvamoṭu vanta uyar maruppu oruttal num puṉattu vaḻip pōkaluṟumō eṉac collik kaḻinta kāḷi akalamum takiyum nayantu īṅku nām uḻakkum evvam uṇarāḷ, aṉṉi mayaṅki veṟi eṉat tanta vēlaṉ `emmiṟi aṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉṟu innōy, taṇi maruntaṟival' eṉṉumāyiṉ, num iṟi vēḻam meyyuḷam pōka ampoṭu kāṭṭu māṉ aṭi vaḻi oṟṟi vēṭṭam cellumō eṉavē viṉaviṉ evaṉō.
(vi-ri) taṭṭiyiṉ ōppi eṉṟu iyiyum. uḻutal kuralākiya kāriyattiṟku vaḻi vaḻik kāraṇam. uḻuta kural - `pakaṭu naṭantakūḻ'1 eṉpatupōla niṉṟatu. nayantu uḻakkum evvam - nayattalāl varuntum tuṉpatti. naṉṉar neñcam - aṉpuṭiya neñcam. innōy vantaṉṟu eṉavum in nōy taṇi maruntu eṉavum īriṭattum kūṭṭuka. mey uḷam - meyyiṉ uḷḷiṭam. ūṭṭi aṉṉa eṉpataṟku, `ūṭṭi yaṉṉa oṇṭaḷirc ceyali’2 eṉpuḻi urittataṉi nōkkuka. aṉṉi nam evvattiṉ kāraṇatti uṇarāḷāy vēlaṉi aḻikka, avaṉ itu teyvattāl vantatu, taṇi maruntu aṟivēṉ eṉpaṉāyiṉ eṉṟa taṉ karuttu, iṉi aṉṉiyāl iṟ ceṟikkappaṭuvēm ākaliṉ, iṉit talivaṉik kaḷaviṟkūṭutal iyalāteṉpataṉit talivaṉukku uṇarttu talākum. talivaṉ uṇarntu virintu varintu koḷvāṉāvatu itaṉ payaṉ.
389. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ vaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		aṟiyāy vāḻi tōḻi neṟikural
		cāntār kūntal uḷarip pōtaṇintu
		tēṅkamaḻ tirunutal tilakam tiiyum
		pallitaḻ etirmalar kiḷḷi vēṟupaṭa
	5.	nalliḷa vaṉamuli alliyo ṭappiyum
		peruntōḷ toyyil varittum ciṟuparaṭṭu
		añceñ cīṟaṭip pañci ūṭṭiyum
		eṟpuṟan tantu niṟpā rāṭṭip
		palpūñ cēkkiyiṟ pakalum nīṅkār
	10.	maṉivayiṉ iruppavar maṉṉē tuṉitantu
		irappōr ēntuki niṟiyap purappōr
		pulampil uḷḷamoṭu putuvatan tuvakkum
		arumporuḷ vēṭṭam eṇṇik kaṟuttōr
		ciṟupuṉ kiḷavic cellal pāḻpaṭa
	15.	nallici tamvayiṉ niṟumār valvēl
		vāṉa varampaṉ naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
		vēṉil nīṭiya veṅkaṭaṟ ṟaṭimutal
		āṟucel vampalar vēṟupirin talaṟak
		kolivem miyiṉ nilipeyarn tuṟiyum
	20.	peruṅkaḷiṟu tolicciya iruṅkēḻ ēṟṟi
		cempula maruṅkiṟ ṟaṉkāl vāṅki
		valampaṭu veṉṟiyoṭu cilampakañ cilampap
		paṭumaḻi urumiṉ muḻaṅkum
		neṭumara maruṅkiṉ maliyiṟan tōrē.
- nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, aṟiyāy - nī aṟiyātu kūṟukiṉṟaṉi,
1-10. neṟi kural cāntu ār kūntal uḷari - neṟinta kottāka vuḷḷa mayirccāntaṇinta kūntali āṟṟi, pōtu aṇintu - malari aṇintum, tēm kamaḻ tiru nutal tilakam tiiyum - iṉimiyuṟa maṇakkiṉṟa aḻakiya neṟṟiyil poṭṭu aṇintum, pal itaḻ etir malar kiḷḷi vēṟupaṭa alliyōṭu nal iḷa vaṉamuli appiyum - pala itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya putiya malarkaḷik kiḷḷi avvitaḻ kaḷuṭaṉ niṟam vēṟupaṭa avvavaṟṟiṉ poṭikaḷiyum nalla iḷiya aḻakiya mulikaḷil appiyum, peru tōḷ toyyil varittum - periya tōḷil toyyil eḻutiyum, ciṟu paraṭṭu am cem ciṟu aṭi pañci ūṭṭiyum - ciṟiya paraṭṭiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya civanta ciṟiya aṭiyil cempañcuk kuḻampiṉit taṭaviyum, eṉ puṟantantu - ivvāṟellām eṉṉip pātukāttum, niṉ pārāṭṭi - niṉṉip pārāṭṭip pēciyum, pal pū cēkkiyil pakalum nīṅkār - palavāya malarkaḷip parappiya paḷḷiyil pakaṟ pōḻtum nīṅkārāki, maṉivayiṉ iruppavar - nam maṉikkaṇṇē taṅki yiruppavarāya nam talivar,
10-15. irappōr ēntu ki niṟiya - irappavarkaḷatu ēntum ki niṟintiṭa, purappōr - avarip purakkiṉṟa īkiyāḷarkaḷ, pulampu il uḷḷamoṭu - varuttam illāta uḷḷattuṭaṉ, tuṉi
tantu - virintu vantu, putuva tantu uvakkum - putiya poruḷkaḷit tantu makiḻuvataṟkuriya, arum poruḷ vēṭṭam eṇṇi - ariya poruḷi īṭṭivarali virumpi, kaṟuttōr - pakivaratu, ciṟu puṉ kiḷavi cellal - ciṟiya pulliya iḻi collākiya tuṉpam, pāḻpaṭa - aḻiyavum, tam vayiṉ nal ici niṟumār - tampāl nalla pukaḻiṉi niṟuttavum,
15-24. val vēl vāṉa varampaṉ nal nāṭṭu umpar - valiya vēliṉiyuṭiya vāṉavarampaṉatu nalla nāṭṭiṉ appāluḷḷa, vēṉil nīṭiya vem kaṭaṟṟu aṭi mutal - veppam mikka koṭiya kāṭṭiṉi aṭinta iṭattē, āṟu cel vampalar vēṟu pirintu alaṟa - vaḻic celvōrāya putiyar vēṟu vēṟākap pirintu pōy alaṟa, koli vemmiyiṉ nilipeyarntu uṟiyum - kollum koṭumi nōkkit taṉ niliyi viṭṭu vēṟiṭattut taṅkiya, peru kaḷiṟu tolicciya - periya kaḷiṟṟiṉik koṉṟa, iru kēḻ ēṟṟi - periya niṟam poruntiya āṇ puli, cempula maruṅkil - kurutiyāṟ civanta nilattiṭattē, taṉ kāl vāṅki - taṉ kāli vaḷittut tāvi, valam paṭu veṉṟiyoṭu - valattē vīḻttiya veṟṟiyāl, cilampu akam cilampa - veṟpiṉiṭam etir oli ceyya, paṭu maḻi urumiṉ muḻaṅkum - poruntiya mēkattiṉiṭattē iṭipōla muḻaṅkum, neṭu mara maruṅkiṉ mali iṟantōrē - neṭiya maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya mali vaḻikaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉarē.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! aṟiyāy. eṟ puṟantantu niṟ pārāṭṭi pakalum nīṅkār maṉivayiṉ iruppavar, kaṟuttōr kiḷavic cellal pāḻpaṭa, tamvayiṉ nallici niṟumār, irappōr ēntuki niṟiyat tantuvakkum poruḷ vēṭṭam eṇṇi, vāṉavarampaṉ naṉṉāṭṭu umpar, veṅ kaṭaṟṟu aṭi mutal kaḷiṟu tolicciya iruṅkēḻ ēṟṟi cilampakam cilampa urumiṉ muḻaṅkum mali iṟantōr.
(vi-ri) aṇintu, aṇintum eṉa ummi virikka. `eṟ puṟantanta' eṉṟu pāṭamiruppiṉ, ivvāṟellām eṉṉi oppittup pātukātta - niṉṉi eṉṟurikka. niṉ eṉṟatu tōḻiyi. putuva - tām tēṭiya putiya poruḷkaḷ. purappōr tuṉitantu uvakkum eṉak kūṭṭuka. tantu uvakkum - poruḷi īntu ataṉip peṟṟavar uvappatu kaṇṭu uvakkum eṉka. uvakkum arum poruḷ - uvattaṟku ētuvākiya arum poruḷ. pakivar poruḷiṉmipaṟṟi ikaḻnturikkum collit talimakkaḷ poṟār eṉpati, `kaṟuttōr, eḷḷal neñcattu ēec col nāṇi'1 eṉa muṉṉarum pōntuḷatu. vāṉa varampaṉ - cēraṉukku uriyatōr peyar : vāṉulakukāṟum taṉ āṇi cela niṉṟavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷatākum. `vāṉavarampaṉi nīyō peruma'2 `vāṉa varampaṉeṉap pēriṉitu viḷakki'3 eṉpaṉa kāṇka. ivvāṟellām eṉṉip puṟantantum niṉṉip pārāṭṭiyum pakaliṉum cēkkiyil akalātirunta talivar, ippoḻutu nām taṉittirukka nammip pirintu ētamikka koṭiya kāṭṭu neṟiyiṟ ceṉṟuḷārākaliṉ yāṉ āṟṟiyiruppatu eṅṅaṉam eṉat taṉṉi āṟṟiyirukkumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttiya tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiṉāḷeṉka.
390. neytal
(talimakaṉ pāṅkaṟkuc colliyatu; neñciṟkuc colliyatūumām.)
		uvarviḷi uppiṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟi
		4atarpaṭu pūḻiya cēṭpulam paṭarum
		tatarkōl umaṇar pōkum neṭuneṟik
		kaṇaniri 1vāḻkkitāṉ naṉṟu kollō
	5.	vaṇarcuri mucci muḻutumaṟ puraḷa
		aitakal alkul kaviṉpeṟap puṉinta
		palkuḻit toṭali olkuvayiṉ olki
		nellum uppum nērē ūrīr
		koḷḷī rōveṉac 2cēritoṟum nuvalum
	10.	avvāṅ kunti amittō ḷāyniṉ
		meyvāḻ uppiṉ viliyey yāmeṉac
		ciṟiya vilaṅkiṉa mākap periyataṉ
		arivēy uṇkaṇ amarttaṉaḷ nōkki
		yārī rōvem vilaṅki yīireṉa
	15.	mūral muṟuvalaḷ pērvaṉaḷ niṉṟa
		ciṉṉiri vālvaḷip polinta
		3paṉmāṇ pētik koḻinteṉ neñcē.
- ammūvaṉār.
(co-ḷ) pāṅka! 1-4. uvar viḷi uppiṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟi- uvarnilattē viḷinta uppiṉatu viliyik kūṟi, atarpaṭu pūḻiya- neṟikaḷiṟ paṭum puḻutiyi yuṭiya, cēṇ pulam paṭarum - neṭiya cēymikkaṇ cellum, tatar kōl umaṇar pōkum - ceṟinta kōlkaḷik koṇṭu cellum uppuvāṇikar pōvatākiya, neṭu neṟi - nīṇṭa neṟikaḷil, kaṇa niri vāḻkki - ak kūṭṭattuṭaṉ cērntu vāḻum vāḻkki, naṉṟu kol - ciṟantatu pōlum;
5-12. vaṇar curi mucci muḻutum maṉ puraḷa - vaḷintu curinta kūntaliṉ muṭi muḻuvatum peritum aliyak koṇṭu, aitu akal alkul kaviṉ peṟa puṉinta pal kuḻi toṭali - melliya akaṉṟa alkul aḻakupeṟat taritta pala taḷiruṭaṉ kūṭiya taḻiyuṭi, olkuvayiṉ olki - aciyumiṭattu acintu naṭantu, ūrīr - ūravarē, nellum uppum nērē - nellum uppum vili oppākum, koḷḷīrō eṉa - koḷvīrāka eṉṟu, cēri toṟum nuvalum - cērikaṭōṟum kūṟi viṟkum, a vāṅku unti ami tōḷāy - aḻakiya vaḷinta untiyiṉiyum mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōḷiṉiyu muṭiyāy! niṉ mey vāḻ uppiṉ vili eyyām eṉa - niṉatu uṭaliṉkaṇ uḷatām iṉpattiṟku viliyiṉi aṟintilamē eṉṟu yām kūṟi, ciṟiya vilaṅkiṉam āka - ciṟitu taṭippaṭuttiṉē māka;
12-17. taṉ periya ari vēy uṇ kaṇ amarttaṉaḷ nōkki - taṉatu periya aripaṭarnta miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉāl māṟupaṭṭaṉaḷ pōla nōkki, em vilaṅkiyīr ārīrō eṉa - emmit taṭuppīr nīvir yāvirō eṉṟu kūṟi, mūral muṟuvalaḷ pērvaṉaḷ niṉṟa - iḷanaki yuṭiyāḷāyc ciṟitu peyarntu niṉṟa, cil niri vāl vaḷi polinta - cilavāya nirinta veḷḷiya vaḷikaḷāṟ polivuṟṟa, pal māṇ pētikku - pala māṇpumuṭiya pētiyiṉ poruṭṭu, eṉ neñcu oḻintatu - eṉ neñcam taṉ valiyiṉi iḻantuviṭṭatu.
(muṭipu) pāṅka! uppiṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟic cēṭpulam paṭarum umaṇar pōkum neṭu neṟi, kaṇa niri vāḻkkitāṉ naṉṟukol! nellum uppum nērē, ūrīr! koḷḷīrō eṉa nuvalum amit tōḷāy! niṉ mey vāḻ uppiṉ vili eyyām eṉac ciṟiya vilaṅkiṉamāka, yārīrō em vilaṅkiyīreṉa, muṟuvalaḷ pērvaṉaḷ niṉṟa pētikku eṉ neñcu oḻintatu.
(vi-ri) uvar - kaḷar nilam. uppiṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟic cēṭ pulam paṭarum umaṇar eṉka. aṉṟi, umaṇarpōkum neṭu neṟik kaṇ uppiṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟic cēṭ pulam paṭarum kaṇa niri vāḻkki eṉṟu iyitturittalumām. mucci puraḷa toṭali olkuvayiṉ olki eṉak kūṭṭuka. olki - acintu naṭantu. nēr - camam. koḷḷīrō eṉa - nellit tantu uppik koḷvīr eṉṟu. mey vāḻ uppu - uṭampiṟ poruntum iṉpam; `ūṭip peṟukuvaṅ kollō nutalveyarppak - kūṭaliṟ ṟōṉṟiya uppu'1 eṉpuḻi, uppu ip poruṭṭātal kāṇka. ic ceyyuḷ tali makaṉ pāṅkaṟku uṟṟatu urittalākum; talimakaṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu eṉak koḷḷiṉ, neñcē! pētikku oḻintatu eṉ? eṉak kūṭṭiyurikka.
391. pāli
(piriviṭi vaṟpuṟuttun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		pārval verukiṉ kūreyiṟ ṟaṉṉa
		varimeṉ mukiya nuṇkoṭi yatiral
		2malkakal vaṭṭiyar koḷviṭam peṟāar
		viliña roḻitta talivēy kāṉmalar
	5.	tēmpāy mulliyoṭu ñāṅkarp pōkkit
		taṇṇaṟuṅ katuppiṟ puṇarntōr puṉintaveṉ
		3potimāṇ mucci kāṇṭoṟum paṇṭip
		paḻavaṇi yuḷḷap paṭumāl tōḻi
		iṉṟoṭu ciṉṉāḷ variṉuñ ceṉṟunaṉi
	10.	paṭāa vākumeṅ kaṇṇē kaṭāa
		vāṉmarup pacittal cellātu yāṉitaṉ
		vāyniṟi koṇṭa valitēmpu taṭakki
		kuṉṟupuku pāmpiṟ ṟōṉṟum
		eṉṟūḻ vippiṟ curaṉiṟan tōrō.
- 1kāvaṉ mullip pūtaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 8. tōḻi-, 1-6.  pārval verukiṉ kūr eyiṟṟu  
aṉṉa - taṉ iriyiṉip pārttirukkum kāṭṭup pūṉiyiṉ kūriya paṟkaḷi yotta, varimel mukiya nuṇ koṭi atiral - melliya varikaḷiyuṭiya arumpukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya nuṇṇiya koṭiyākiya kāṭṭu mallikiyiṉ pūkkaḷ, malku akal vaṭṭiyar viliñar - niṟinta akaṉṟa vaṭṭiyi yuṭiyōrākiya pū viṟpōr, koḷvu iṭam peṟāar oḻitta - koḷḷum iṭam peṟārāy viñciyavaṟṟi oḻittuk koṇarnta, tali vēy kāṉ malar - taliyiṟ cūṭiya maṇamuṭiya malarkaḷi, tēm pāy mulliyoṭu - tēṉpāyum mullip pūkkaḷoṭu, ñāṅkar pōkki - puṟattē akaṟṟi, taṇ naṟu katuppil puṇarntōr - eṉatu taṇṇiya naṟiya kūntaliṭattē tuyiṉṟa talivar,
10-14. kaṭāa yāṉi - matak kaḷiṟāṉatu, vāṉ maruppu acittal cellātu - maṇṇil ūṉṟiya veḷḷiya kompiṉi acittup peyarkka iyalātu, taṉ vāy niṟi koṇṭa vali tēmpu taṭa ki - taṉ vāyil niṟiyumāṟu pukuttiya vali kuṉṟiya periya ki, kuṉṟu puku pāmpil tōṉṟum - mali muḻiñciṉuḷ nuḻiyum pāmpu pōlat tōṉṟum, eṉṟūḻ vippiṉ curaṉ iṟantōr - veppam mikka iṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭik kaṭantu ceṉṟār,
6-8. puṉinta eṉ poti māṇ mucci kāṇtoṟum - avar (paṇṭu) puṉinta eṉatu niṟinta māṇpuḷḷa kūntal muṭiyik kāṇuntōṟum, paṇṭip paḻa aṇi uḷḷappaṭum - muṉpuḷḷa eṉatu paḻiya aḻaku niṉikkappaṭukiṉṟatu,
9-10. ceṉṟu iṉṟoṭu ciṉṉāḷ variṉum - avar ceṉṟa piṉ iṉṟoṭu cila nāṭkaḷē vantaṉa āyiṉum, em kaṇ naṉi paṭāa ākum - em kaṇkaḷ mikavum tuyilātaṉavākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! tali vēy kāṉ malar mulliyoṭu pōkkik katuppiṟ puṇarntōr curam iṟantōr. avar puṉinta eṉ mucci kāṇṭoṟum paṇṭip paḻa aṇi uḷḷappaṭum. ceṉṟu ciṉṉāḷ variṉum em kaṇ paṭāa ākum.
(vi-ri) vaṭṭi - malar peyyum ceppu. vaṭṭiyar oḻinta kāṉ malar - vaṭṭiyarāy mikuntavaṟṟi oḻittuk koṇarnta kāṉ malar eṉka. eṉavē vaṭṭiyiluḷḷa kāṉ malar eṉpatu peṟṟām. tali vēy eṉpataṉi mulliyoṭuṅ kūṭṭit taliyiṟ cūṭiya atiraṟ pūviyum mullip pūviyum akaṟṟi eṉak koḷka. puṉinta eṉpatu puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉarp putitu puṉinta eṉka. ciṉṉāḷ variṉum - cila nāḷē āyiṉum eṉṟapaṭi. kaṭāa yāṉi eṉak kūṭṭuka. maruppu acittal cellātu eṉṟatu, curattiṉ vemmi mikutiyāl vali tēmpik kōṭu nilattiṟ patiyumāṟu kaviḻnta yāṉi taṉ kompiṉi acikkalāṟṟātu eṉka. kuṉṟu - maliyiṉ kuki. āṟṟiyirukkavēṇṭu meṉa vaṟpuṟuttiya tōḻikku, talivar curam iṟantār ākaliṉ, avar puṉinta mucci kāṇṭoṟum paḻa aṇi uḷḷappaṭum, em kaṇ paṭāa ākum, yāṉ eṅṅaṉam āṟṟuvēṉ eṉat talivi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
392. kuṟiñci
(piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi talimakaṭkuk 
kuṟi nayappak kūṟiyatu.)
		tāḻperun taṭakki 1taliiya kāṉattu
		vīḻpiṭi keṭutta veṇkōṭṭu yāṉi
		uṇkuḷaku maṟutta uyakkat taṉṉa
		paṇpuṭi yākkic citivunaṉku aṟīip
	5.	piṉṉili muṉiyāṉ āki naṉṟun
		tātucey pāvi aṉṉa tiyal
		mātar melliyal maṭanal lōḷvayiṉ
		tītiṉ ṟāka nīpuṇi pukukeṉa
		eṉṉun taṇṭu māyiṉ maṟṟavaṉ
	10.	aḻitakap peyartal naṉiyiṉ ṉātē
		olliṉi vāḻi tōḻi kalleṉak
		kaṇamaḻi poḻinta kāṉmaṭi yiravil
		tiṉimēy yāṉi iṉaṉirin tōṭak
		kalluyar kaḻutiṟ cēṇōṉ eṟinta
	15.	valvāyk kavaṇiṉ kaṭuveṭi olleṉa
		maṟappuli uraṟa vāraṇam kataṟa
		naṉavuṟu kaṭciyiṉ naṉmayil āla
		maliyuṭaṉ verūum mākkal veṟpaṉ
		piriyunaṉ ākalō aritē atāaṉṟu
	20.	urital paṇpiṟ piriyunaṉ āyiṉ
		viṉitavap peyarnta veṉvēl vēntaṉ
		muṉikol tāṉiyoṭu muṉvan tiṟuppat
		taṉvaram pākiya maṉṉeyil irukki
		1āṟṟā miyiṟ piṭitta vēlvalit
	25.	tōṟṟam piḻiyāt tolpukaḻ peṟṟa
		viḻitaka ōṅkiya kaḻituñcu maruṅkiṟ
		kāṉamar naṉṉaṉ pōla
		yāṉā kuvalniṉ nalaṉtaru vēṉē.		       - mōcikīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, ol iṉi - ippoḻutu yāṉ  kūṟavataṉi maṉam poruntik kēṭpāyāka.
11-18. kal eṉa kaṇam maḻi poḻinta kāṉ maṭi iravil - kalleṉṉum oliyuṭaṉ kūṭṭamākiya mēkaṅkaḷ maḻiyic corinta kāṉamellām aṭaṅkiya iravil, tiṉi mēy yāṉi iṉaṉ irintu ōṭa - tiṉiyi mēynta yāṉiyiṉ kūṭṭam nili keṭṭu ōṭa, kal uyar kaḻutil cēṇōṉ eṟinta - maliyiṭattē uyarnta paraṇiliruntu kuṟavaṉ vīci yeṟinta, val vāy kavaṇiṉ kaṭu veṭi ol eṉa - valiya vici vāynta kavaṇ kalliṉ kaṭiya ōci olleṉ ṟolikka, maṟa puli uraṟa - (atu kēṭṭut) taṟu kaṇmi yuṭiya puli muḻaṅka, vāraṇam kataṟa - yāṉi kataṟa, naṉavu uṟu kaṭciyiṉ nal mayil āla - akaṟciyuṟṟa kāṭṭiluḷḷa aḻakiya mayil (iṭiyōci yeṉa) āṭa, mali uṭaṉ verūum mā kal veṟpaṉ - mali muḻuvatum añcum periya kaṟkaḷi yuṭiya mali poruntiya nāṭṭiyuṭiya talivaṉ,
1-5. kāṉattu - kāṭṭil, vīḻ piṭi keṭutta - virumpappaṭum peṇ yāṉiyi iḻanta, tāḻ peru taṭa ki taliiya - toṅkukiṉṟa nīṇṭa periya ki poruntiya, veṇ kōṭṭu yāṉi - veḷḷiya kompiṉiyuṭiya kaḷiṟu, uṇ kuḷaku maṟutta uyakkattu aṉṉa - uṇṇum taḻiyiṉi uṇṇātu nīkkiya vāṭṭatti otta, paṇpu uṭi yākkic citivu naṉku aṟīi - aḻakuṭiya taṉatu yākkiyiṉ meliviṉi naṉku aṟintuvittum, piṉṉili muṉiyāṉ āki - nampāl irantu piṉṉiṟṟali veṟāṉāki,
5-10. naṉṟum tātu cey pāvi aṉṉa tiyal - mikavum poṉṉāṟ ceyta pāvi pōṉṟa kaṭṭaḻakiṉiyum, mātar melliyal - virumpappaṭum meṉmit taṉmiyiṉiyum uṭiya, maṭa nallōḷvayiṉ - maṭappam vāynta taliviyiṉpāl, tītu iṉṟu āka nī puṇi pukuka eṉa - tīṅku illiyāmpaṭi nī puṇiyākap pukuvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi, eṉṉum taṇṭumāyiṉ - ciṟitēṉum nīṅkuvāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ aḻitakap peyartal naṉi iṉṉātē - avaṉ nām varuntumpaṭi peyarntu cellutal namakku mikavum tuṉpamāvatē yākum,
19. piriyunaṉ ākalō aritu - (āṉāl) avaṉ nammip pirintu celvāṉātal illi.
19-20. atāaṉṟu - aḵtaṉṟiyum, uritu al paṇpil piriyunaṉ āyiṉ - taṉakku urittallāta paṇpiṉāl, orōvaḻip pirivāṉ āyiṉ,
21-28. viṉi tavap peyarnta veṉvēl vēntaṉ - pōr oḻiya nīṅkiya veṉṟi vēliṉiyuṭiya paki yaracaṉ, muṉi kol tāṉiyoṭu muṉvantu iṟuppa - pōriṉkaṇ kollu taliyuṭiya cēṉiyuṭaṉ mīḷavum matiṟ puṟattē vantu taṅka, taṉ varampu ākiya maṉeyil irukki - taṉṉip paṟṟuk kōṭākavuṭiya nili
peṟṟa matiliyuṭiya araṇ, āṟṟāmi yiṉ - appaki vēntaṉi etirttu niṟṟal āṟṟāmiyāṉ, viḻitaka ōṅkiya maḻi tuñcu maruṅkiṉ - kaṇṭār virumpumāṟu uyarnta mūṅkilkaḷ ceṟinta malip pakkattatākiya, kāṉamar - kāṭṭil vatintirunta, piṭitta vēl valit tōṟṟam piḻiyāt tolpukaḻ peṟṟa - kik koṇṭa vēliṉiyuṭiya valiyaminta taṉatu tōṟṟamāṉatu piḻiyātavāṟu (av vēntaṉuṭaṉ porutu) taṉ paḻiya pukaḻi nili niṟuttiyavaṉākiya, naṉṉaṉ pōla - naṉṉaṉip pōla, yāṉ niṉ nalam taruvēṉ ākuval - (avaṉic cērttu) yāṉ niṉatu nalattiṉi mīṇṭum kūṭṭi vippēṉ āvēṉ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! mākkal veṟpaṉ, yākkic citivu naṉku aṟīi, piṉṉili muṉiyāṉ āki, `nallōḷvayiṉ tītiṉṟāka nī puṇi puku' keṉa eṉṉum taṇṭum āyiṉ, maṟṟu avaṉ aḻitakap peyartal naṉi iṉṉātu, iṉi, ol (avaṉ) piriyunaṉ ākalō aritē. atāaṉṟu piriyunaṉ āyiṉ, niṉ nalam taruvēṉ yāṉ ākuval.
yāṉi uyakkattaṉṉa yākki eṉavum, naṉṉaṉ pōla nalantaruvēṉ eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
(vi-ri) piṭiyik keṭuttamiyāl, kōṭṭilē kiyi vitta yāṉi yeṉka. maṟutta uyakkam - peyareccam, kāraṇap poruṭṭu. paṇpu - īṇṭu aḻakiṉ mēṟṟu; ilakkaṇattāl tiruntutal eṉṟumām. aṟīi - aṟintu vittum eṉka. tātu - poṉ. tītu iṉṟāka - yāṉ piritalākiya tīṅku nikaḻāmikku. pukukeṉa - pukukeṉak kūṟi eṉa viritturikka. eṉṉum - ciṟitum. taṇṭutal - nīṅkutal. ollutal - poruntutal. cēṇōṉ - uyarnta iṭattil iruppavaṉ - kuṟavaṉ. veṭi - ōci. yāṉi irintu ōṭa eṟiyum kavaṇ kalliṉ ōciyālum, puliyiṉ muḻakkattālum, yāṉiyiṉ kataṟutalālum mali muḻutum añcum eṉka. avaṟṟi mēkattiṉ muḻakka meṉakkoṇṭu mayil ālumeṉka. veṟpaṉ pukukeṉak kūṟi nīṅkumāyiṉ aṅṅaṉam nīṅkutal naṉi iṉṉātu, āyiṉ avaṉ avvāṟu pirital aritu. aḵtaṉṟi orōvaḻip piriyunaṉ āyiṉ, yāṉ avaṉi niṉpāṟ cērttu niṉatu nalatti mīṭṭut taruvēṉ eṉṟu tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kuṟi nayappak kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. viṉitavap peyarnta eṉṟatu, muṉ pōr ceytu āṟṟātu ōṭiya eṉṟapaṭi. viḻi taka ōṅkiya kaḻi tuñcu maruṅkiṟ kāṉamar naṉṉaṉ eṉṟataṉāl, kāṭci viruppattāl, naṉṉaṉ oru kāṭṭil vatintāṉātal vēṇṭum. tol pukaḻ peṟṟa naṉṉaṉ eṉavum kāṉamar naṉṉaṉ eṉavum kūṭṭuka. naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉukku uriya araṇiṉi māṟṟu vēntaṉ oruvaṉ muṟṟip porutu tōṟṟu ōṭiyavaṉ, piṉ periya tāṉiyuṭaṉ mīṭṭum vantu taṅka, araṇiluḷḷār avaṉuṭaṉ porutaṟku āṟṟātirukka, kāṭṭi
lirunta naṉṉaṉ mīḷa vantu taṉatu paḻiya pukaḻ nilipeṟumpaṭi ap pakivaṉi veṉṟu araṇil uḷḷārip pātukāttaṉaṉ. ākaliṉ an naṉṉaṉpōla niṉ nalam taruvēṉ eṉṟāḷ eṉka.
393. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		kōṭuyar piṟaṅkaṟ kuṉṟupala nīnti
		vēṟupulam paṭarnta viṉitaral uḷḷattu
		āṟucel vampalar 1kāypaci tīriya
		2iticcuvaṟ kalitta īrili neṭuntōṭṭuk
	5.	kavikkatir varakiṉ kāltoku poṅkaḻi
		kavaṭṭaṭip poruta 3palciṉi utirvi
		akaṉkaṭ 4pāṟic cevvayiṟ ṟeṟīi
		variyaṇi paṇittōḷ 5vārcevit taṉṉiyar
		paṇṇi veṇpaḻat tarici ēyppac
	10.	cuḻalmarañ colitta cuḷakali veṇkāḻ
		toṭimāṇ ulakki ūḻiṟ pōkki
		uralmukaṅ kāṭṭiya curiniṟi koḷḷi
		āṅkaṇ iruñcuṉi nīroṭu mukavāk
		kaḷipaṭu kuḻicik kallaṭup pēṟṟi
	15.	iṇartati kaṭukki īṇṭiya tātiṟ
		kuṭavar puḻukkiya poṅkaviḻp puṉkam
		matarvi nallāṉ pāloṭu pakukkum
		niripala kuḻīiya neṭumoḻip pulli
		tēṉṟūṅku uyarvari naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
	20.	vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉa rāyiṉum āṇṭavar
		nīṭalar vāḻi tōḻi tōṭukoḷ
		urukeḻu maññi olicīr ēyppat
		takaram maṇṇiya taṇṇaṟu muccip
		pukaril kuvaḷip pōtoṭu 1teriyitaḻ
	25.	vēṉil atiral vēyntaniṉ
		ēmuṟu puṇarcci iṉtuyil maṟantē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 21. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, (nam talivar,)
1-2. kōṭu uyar piṟaṅkal kuṉṟu pala nīnti - cikaram uyarnta pāṟikaḷiyuṭiya malikaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṭantu, vēṟu pulam paṭarnta viṉitaral uḷḷattu - vēṟṟu nāṭṭi aṭinta poruḷ tēṭum muyaṟciyiyuṭiya uḷḷattāl,
3-20. āṟu cel vampalar - vaḻic cellum putiyaratu, kāy paci tīriya - mikka paci oḻiya, iti cuval kalitta - putuk kolliyil taḻitta, īr ili neṭu tōṭṭu kavi katirvarakiṉ kāl toku poṅkaḻi - īriya iliyiṉ nīṇṭa itaḻiyuṭiya varakiṉ kavartta katiruṭaṉ kūṭiya taṭṭikaḷit tokutta poliyil, kavaṭṭu aṭi poruta palciṉi utirvi - māṭukaḷiṉ kavartta kuḷampāl tuvikkap paṭṭa pala kiḷikaḷiṉiṉṟu utirnta varakiṉi, akaṉ kaṇ pāṟi cevvayiṉ teṟīi - iṭam akaṉṟa pāṟiyil cevviya iṭattil kuvittu, vari aṇi paṇi tōḷ vār cevi taṉṉiyar - varikaḷ poruntiya parutta tōḷkaḷiyum nīṇṭa cevikaḷiyu muṭiya tāyar, paṇṇi veṇ paḻattu arici ēyppa - paṇṇik kīriyiṉ veḷḷiya paḻattiṉ ariciyi oppa, cuḻal maram colitta cuḷaku ali veṇ kāḻ - tirimaram tēytta cuḷakiṉāl koḻikkappeṟṟa veḷḷiya ariciyi, toṭi māṇ ulakki ūḻil pōkki - pūṇ māṭcimippaṭṭa ulakkiyāl muṟiyākac celutti, ural mukam kāṭṭiya - uraliṟ peytu tīṭṭiya, curi niṟi koḷḷi - uraliṉ kuḻi niṟinta avvariciyi, āṅkaṇ iru cuṉi nīroṭu mukavā - aṅkuḷḷa periya cuṉiyiṉ nīroṭu mukantu, kaḷi paṭu kuḻici kal aṭuppu ēṟṟi - maṇṇāṟ ceyta pāṉiyil kaṟkaḷi aṭukki yamitta aṭuppil ēṟṟi, iṇar tati kaṭukki īṇṭiya tātil - kottukkaḷ niṟinta koṉṟiyiṉ niṟinta pūn tātu pōla, kuṭavar puḻukkiya poṅku aviḻ puṉkam- iṭiyar ākkiya viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aviḻākiya cōṟṟi, matarvi nal āṉ pāloṭu pakukkum - matartta nalla pacuviṉ pāluṭaṉ kūṭṭi aḷikkum iṭamākiya, niri pala kuḻīiya - pacu nirikaḷi mikap peṟṟa, neṭu moḻi pulli - mikka pukaḻuṭiya pulli eṉpāṉatu, tēṉ tūṅku uyar vari nal nāṭṭu umpar - tēṉiṟāl toṅkum uyarnta malikaḷiyuṭiya nalla nāṭukaṭku appāṟ
paṭṭa, vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉar āyiṉum - vēṅkaṭa maliyik kaṭantu ceṉṟuḷḷā rāyiṉum,
21-26. tōṭukoḷ urukeḻu maññi oli cīr ēyppa - aḻaku poruntiya mayiliṉ tokuti koṇṭa taḻitta tōkiyiṉi oppa, takaram maṇṇiya taṇ naṟu mucci - mayirc cāntu pūcap peṟṟa taṇṇiya naṟiya kūntalil, pukar il kuvaḷip pōtoṭu teri itaḻ vēṉil atiral vēynta - kuṟṟamillāta kuvaḷip pūvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya vēṉiliṟ pūkkum kāṭṭu mallikip pūviṉiyum cūṭiya, niṉ ēm uṟu puṇarcci iṉ tuyil maṟantu - niṉatu iṉpam vāynta kūṭṭamākiya iṉiya tuyiliṉi maṟantu,
20-21. āṇṭu avar nīṭalar - āṅkaṇ avar tāḻttiruppār allar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! nam talivar, kuṉṟu pala nīnti, viṉi taral uḷḷattāl, vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉarāyiṉum, niṉ ēmuṟu puṇarcci iṉtuyil maṟantu, āṇṭu avar nīṭalar.
(vi-ri) paṭarnta viṉitaral uḷḷam - paṭarntu poruḷ tarutaliyuṭiya āḷviṉiyuṭiya uḷḷam eṉka. uḷḷattāl vēṅkaṭam iṟantaṉarāyiṉum eṉak kūṭṭuka. uḷḷattiyuṭiya āṟu cel vampalar eṉṟalumām. itic cuval - kāṭu tiruttiya mēṭṭu nilamākiya putuk kolli. varakiṉ katirkaḷit taṭṭikaḷoṭu koytu kuvittu vipparākaliṉ, kavik katir varakiṉ kāl toku poṅkaḻi eṉappaṭṭatu. kāl - taṭṭi. poṅkaḻi - tūṟṟāp poli. kavaṭṭu aṭi - māṭukaḷiṉ kālkaḷiluḷḷa kavartta kuḷampukaḷ. utirvi - utirnta tāṉiya maṇi. teṟīi - kuvittu vittuk koṇṭu, paṇṇi - oruvakik kīri; ataṉ paḻam veḷḷoḷi poruntiya katirāka irukkum; ataṉ ariciyi yoppac colitta veṇkāḻ eṉka. cuḻal maram - tiri maram, tiriki. colittal - tēyttal. ulakki ūḻiṟ pōkki ural mukam kāṭṭiya eṉṟatu, uraliṟ peytu ciṟitu tīṭṭiya eṉṟapaṭi. curi - uraliṉ kuḻi; kuḻiciyiṉ uḷḷiṭam eṉṟalumām. kuṭavar - āyar. vampalar paci tīriya taṉṉiyar puṉkam pāloṭu pakukkum eṉka. pāloṭu pakukkum naṉṉāṭu eṉavum, niri pala kuḻīiya naṉṉāṭu eṉavum, pulliyiṉ naṉṉāṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka. neṭumoḻi - vañciṉamumām. naṉṉāṭṭumpar pulliyiṉ vēṅkaṭam eṉak kūṭṭutalumām. oli cīr ēyppa - taḻitta tōkiyiṉ aḻakiṉi yoppa eṉka.
394. mulli
(iravukkuṟit talimakaḷi iṭattuyttu vantu tōḻi 
talimakaṉi varivu kaṭāayatu.)
		kaḷavum puḷittaṉa viḷavum paḻuniṉa
		ciṟutalit turuviṉ paḻuppuṟu viḷitayir
		itippuṉa varakiṉ avippumāṇ ariciyoṭu
		kārvāyt toḻinta īrvāyp puṟṟattu
	5.	īyalpey taṭṭa iṉpuḷi veñcōṟu
		cētāṉ veṇṇey vempuṟat turuka
		iḷiyar aruntap piṉṟi nīyum
		iṭumuḷ vēli muṭakkāṟ pantarp
		putukkalat taṉṉa cevvāyc ciṟṟil
	10.	puṉiyiruṅ katuppiṉiṉ maṉiyō ḷayarap
		pāluṭi aṭicil toṭīiya orunāḷ
		māvaṇ tōṉṟal vantaṉi ceṉmō
		kāṭuṟi iṭiyaṉ yāṭutalip peyarkkum
		maṭiviṭu vīḷi verīik kuṟumuyal
	15.	maṉṟa irumputal oḷikkum
		puṉpula vippiṉeñ ciṟunal lūrē.
- naṉpalūrc ciṟumētāviyār.
(co-ḷ) 12. māvaṇ tōṉṟal - mikka vaṇmiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
13-16. kāṭu uṟi iṭiyaṉ - kāṭṭil taṅkum iṭiyaṉ, yāṭutali peyarkkum - taṉ yāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷi oruvaḻik kūṭṭum, maṭi viṭu vīḷi - vāyi maṭittu eḻuppum cīḻkki yoliyi, verīi - kēṭṭu añci, kuṟumuyal - kuṟumuyalāṉatu, maṉṟa irumputal oḷikkum - maṉṟattiṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa periya putaril maṟintu koḷḷum, puṉpula vippiṉ - mulli nilattu ūrākiya, emciṟu nal ūr ematu ciṟiya nalla ūriṉkaṇ,
1. kaḷavum puḷittaṉa - kaḷāvum paḻuttup puḷippuc cuviyi eytiṉa, viḷavum paḻuniṉa - viḷāvum mutirntu paḻuttaṉa,
2-11. ciṟu tali turuviṉ paḻuppu uṟu viḷi tayir - ciṟiya taliyiṉiyuṭiya cemmaṟiyāṭṭiṉatu paḻuppu niṟam vāynta muṟṟiya tayirākiya uliyil, iti puṉa varakiṉ avippu māṇ ariciyoṭu - putuk kolliyil viḷinta varakiṉatu kuttutal māṭcimippaṭṭa ariciyuṭaṉ, kār vāyttu īr vāyp puṟṟattu oḻinta - kārmaḻi mikkamiyāl naṉinta vāyiṉiyuṭiya puṟṟiliruntu veḷippaṭ ṭoḻinta, īyal peytu aṭṭa - īyaliyum iṭṭuc camitta, iṉ puḷi vem cōṟu - iṉiya puḷippu vāynta viruppam tarum cōṟṟiṉi, cētāṉ veṇṇey vem puṟattu uruka - civanta pacuviṉ veṇṇey veppam poruntiya ac cōṟṟiṉ mēlē kiṭantu uruka ataṉ mēṟ peytu, iḷiyar arunta - niṉ ēvalar muṉpu uṇṇa, piṉṟi - ataṉ piṉpu, nīyum, iṭumuḷ vēli muṭa kāl pantar - muḷḷiṭṭuk kaṭṭiya vēliyiṉakattē vaḷinta kālkaḷiyuṭiya pantariyuṭiyatum, putukkalattu aṉṉa cevvāy ciṟṟil - putukkalam pōṉṟa cemmaṇ pūciyatumākiya  ciṟiya illil, puṉi irum katuppiṉ niṉ maṉiyōḷ ayara - puṉinta kariya kūntaliyuṭiya niṉ maṉivi viruntayara, pāl uṭi aṭicil toṭīiya - pāl peyta uṇaviṉi uṇṇutaṟku, oru nāḷ-,
12. vantaṉi ceṉmō - vantu celvāyāka,
(muṭipu) māvaṇ tōṉṟal! em ciṟu nallūr, kaḷavum puḷittaṉa, viḷavum paḻuniṉa. iṉpuḷi veñcōṟu veṇṇey puṟatturuka iḷiyar arunta, piṉpu nīyum cevvāyc ciṟṟil, maṉiyōḷ ayara pāluṭi aṭicil toṭīiya oru nāḷ vantaṉi ceṉmō.
(vi-ri) puḷittaṉa - puḷiccuvi eytap paḻuttaṉa eṉṟapaṭi. kaḷāp paḻaṅkaḷ paḻuttaṉa, viḷām paḻaṅkaḷ mutirntaṉa eṉṟataṉ karuttu, viruntayartaṟku vēṇṭuvaṉa yāvum uḷḷaṉa eṉpatākum. veṇṇey urukap peytu eṉa oru col varuvikka. cevvāyc ciṟṟil eṉṟataṉālum, puṉiyiruṅ katuppiṉ niṉ maṉiyōḷ ayarap pāluṭi aṭicil eṉṟataṉālum taliviyi nī varintu koṇṭu viruntuṇṭaṟku varuka eṉak kuṟittavāṟāyiṟṟu.
395. pāli
(piriviṭit tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		taṇkayam payanta vaṇkāṟ kuvaḷi
		māri māmalar peyaṟkēṟ ṟaṉṉa
		nīroṭu niṟinta pēramar maḻikkaṇ
		paṉivār evvam tīra iṉivariṉ
	5.	naṉṟumaṉ vāḻi tōḻi teṟukatir
		īram nitta nīraṟu naṉantali
		aḻaṉmēyn tuṇṭa niḻaṉmāy iyaviṉ
		vaṟaṉmarat taṉṉa kavimarup peḻiṟkali
		aṟalavirn taṉṉa tērnaci yōṭip
	10.	pulampuvaḻip paṭṭa ulamaral 1uḷḷamoṭu
		mēypiṇip payirum melintaḻi paṭarkural
		aruñcurañ cellunar āṭcet tōrkkun
		tiruntari ñemiya perumpuṉak kuṉṟattu
		āṭukaḻi iruvetir naraluṅ
	15.	kōṭukāy kaṭaṟṟa kāṭiṟan tōrē.
- eyiṉanti makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
