5-15. teṟu katir īram nitta nīr aṟu naṉantali - oṟukkum ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir īrattiṉit tolittamiyāṉ nīr aṟṟa akaṉṟa iṭattiluḷḷa, aḻal mēyntu uṇṭa niḻal māy iyaviṉ - tī kavarntu uṇṭamiyāl maraṅkaḷiṉ niḻalum oḻinta neṟiyil, vaṟal marattaṉṉa kavi maruppu eḻil kali - vaṟṟiya marak kompiṉi yotta kavartta kompiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya kalimāṉ, aṟal avirntaṉṉa tēr naci ōṭi -aṟal viḷaṅkiṉā lotta pēyt tēriṉi nīreṉa virumpi yōṭi, pulampu vaḻippaṭṭa ulamaral uḷḷamoṭu - taṉimiyuṟṟamiyālāṉa kalaṅkiya uḷḷattuṭaṉ, mēy piṇi payirum -mēyum taṉ peṇ māṉi aḻikkum, melintu aḻi paṭar kural - melivuṟṟu oḻiyum tuṉpiṉit tarum kurali, arum curam cellunar āḷ cettu ōrkkum - ariya kāṭṭiṉiṟ celluvōr āḷiṉ kuraleṉṟu karuti ārāyum iṭamākiya, tiruntu ari ñemiya perum puṉak kuṉṟattu - cevviya aṭi poruntiya ñemi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya periya kollikaḷiyuṭiya kuṉṟil, āṭu kaḻi iru vetir naralum - aciyum taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya periya mūṅkilkaḷ olikkum, kōṭu kāy kaṭaṟṟa kāṭu iṟantōr - cimiyam vempiya malip pakkattiluḷḷavākiya kāṭukaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam talivar,
1-5. taṇkayam payanta vaṇ kāl kuvaḷi - kuḷirnta kuḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟiya vaḷaviya kāmpiṉiyuṭiya kuvaḷiyiṉ, māri mā malar - maḻikkālattē malarnta kariya malar, peyaṟku ēṟṟaṉṉa - maḻikku etirppaṭṭā lotta, nīroṭu niṟinta pēr amar maḻi kaṇ - nīrāl niṟinta periya amartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟu, paṉi vār evvam tīra - nīr oḻukum tuṉpam oḻiya, iṉivariṉ naṉṟu - ippoḻutu varuvārāyiṉ naṉṟākum, maṉ - vantilarē, eṉ ceyvām.
(muṭipu) tōḻi!vāḻi! kāṭiṟantōr, maḻikkaṇ evvam tīra iṉivariṉ naṉṟu; maṉ.
(vi-ri) nitta - aḻitta `vēlē .... nāṭu nittaliṉ'1 eṉpuḻi nittal ip poruṭṭātal kāṇka. aḻal mēyntu uṇṭa - `uṇṭaṟ kuriya vallāp poruḷi, uṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalu marapē'2  eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. aṟal - aṟalpaṭa oḻukum ciṟiya nīr. nīraṟu naṉantali iyaviṉ kali ōṭi uḷḷamoṭu piṇip payir kural eṉka. kuralic curam cellunar ōrkkum kāṭu eṉavum, kuṉṟattu vetir naralum kaṭaṟṟa kāṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
396. marutam
(kātaṟ paratti talimakaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		toṭutteṉ makiḻna cellal koṭittērp
		polampūṇ naṉṉaṉ 3 puṉaṉāṭu kaṭinteṉa
		yāḻici maṟukiṟ pāḻi āṅkaṇ
		añcal eṉṟa āay eyiṉaṉ
	5.	ikalaṭu kaṟpiṉ miñiliyoṭu tākkit
		taṉṉuyir 4koṭuttaṉaṉ colliya tamiyātu
		teṟalaruṅ kaṭavuḷ muṉṉart 5tēṟṟi
		melliṟi muṉki paṟṟiya colliṟantu
		ārva neñcan talittali ciṟappaniṉ
	10.	mārputaru kallāy piṟaṉā yiṉiyē
		iṉiyāṉ viṭukkuveṉ allēṉ manti
		paṉivār kaṇṇaḷ palapulan tuṟiyak
		kaṭuntiṟal atti āṭaṇi nacii
		neṭunīrk kāviri koṇṭoḷit tāṅkuniṉ
	15.	maṉiyōḷ vavvalum añcuval ciṉii
		āriyar alaṟat tākkip pēricit
		toṉṟumutir vaṭavari vaṇaṅkuviṟ poṟittu
		veñciṉa vēntarip 1piṇittōṉ
		vañci aṉṉaveṉ nalantantu ceṉmē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1. makiḻna - talivaṉē! toṭutteṉ - niṉṉi yāṉ paṟṟik koṇṭēṉ, cellal - cellātē;
1-6. koṭi tēr polam pūṇ naṉṉaṉ - koṭiyaṇinta tēriṉiyum poṉṉālāya pūṇkaḷiyumuṭiya naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, puṉṉāṭu kaṭinteṉa - puṉṉāṭeṉṉum nāṭṭi ṉuḷḷāri vekuṇṭeḻuntāṉāka, yāḻ ici maṟukiṉ pāḻi āṅkaṇ - yāḻiṉ ici poruntiya terukkaḷiyuṭiya pāḻi eṉṉum nakariṭattē niṉṟu, añcal eṉṟa - añcātīr eṉṟu kūṟiya, āay eyiṉaṉ - āay eyiṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, colliyatu amiyātu - tāṉ colliya coṟ piḻiyātu, ikal aṭu kaṟpiṉ miñiliyoṭu tākki - pōr vellum payiṟciyiyuṭiya miñili eṉpāṉoṭu porutu, taṉ uyir koṭuttaṉaṉ - taṉ uyiriyum tantāṉ; (nīyō), 
7-11. teṟal aru kaṭavuḷ muṉṉar tēṟṟi - teṟutaṟku ariya teyvattiṉ muṉ teḷivittu, mel iṟi muṉ ki paṟṟiya col iṟantu - melliya cantiṉiyuṭiya eṉ muṉ kiyiṉip paṟṟic colliya colliṉik kaṭantu, ārva neñcam tali tali ciṟappa - aṉpu mikka uḷḷa mēṉmēlum makiḻntu ciṟakka, niṉ mārpu tarukallāy - niṉ mārpiṉit tārāyāy, piṟaṉ āyiṉiyē- ētilaṉ āyiṉiyē, iṉi yāṉ viṭukkuveṉ allēṉ - iṉi yāṉ niṉṉi viṭuppēṉ allēṉ;
11-15. manti paṉi vār kaṇṇaḷ pala pulantu uṟiya - ātimanti eṉpāḷ nīr oḻukum kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiyaḷāyp palavaṟṟiyum veṟuttirukka, kaṭu tiṟal atti āṭu aṇi nacii - kaṭiya tiṟam poruntiya āṭṭaṉ atti eṉṉum avaḷ kātalaṉ āṭum aḻakiṉi virumpi, neṭu nīrk kāviri koṇṭoḷittāṅku - nīrp perukkiṉiyuṭiya kāviriyāṟu koṇṭu maṟintāṟpōla, niṉ maṉiyōḷ vavvalum añcuval - niṉ maṉiyāḷ niṉṉip paṟṟikkōṭalum añcāniṟpēṉ;
15-19. ciṉii - vekuṇṭeḻuntu, āriyar alaṟat tākki - āriya maṉṉarkaḷ alaṟumāṟu avarkaḷit tākki, pēr ici toṉṟu mutir vaṭavari vaṇaṅku vil poṟittu - periya pukaḻiyuṭiya paḻiyatākiya mutirnta imiya maliyiṉmītu vaḷinta viṟpoṟiyip patittu, vem ciṉa vēntarip piṇittōṉ - koṭiya ciṉam poruntiya paki vēntarip piṇittu vantōṉākiya cēraṉatu, vañci yaṉṉa - vañcinakari yotta, eṉ nalam tantu ceṉmē - eṉ aḻakiṉit tantu celvāyāka.
(muṭipu) makiḻna! toṭutteṉ; cellal. āay eyiṉaṉ colliyatu amiyātu, taṉ uyir koṭuttaṉaṉ; (nīyō) kaṭavuḷ muṉṉart tēṟṟi muṉki paṟṟiya col iṟantu mārpu tarukallāy piṟaṉ āyiṉi. iṉi yāṉ viṭukkuveṉ alleṉ. manti pala pulantu uṟiya, atti āṭaṇi nacii, kāviri koṇ ṭoḻittāṅku, niṉ maṉiyōḷ vavvalum añcuval; vañci yaṉṉa eṉ nalam tantu ceṉmē.
(vi-ri) toṭutteṉ - vaḷittēṉ, paṟṟiṉēṉ. puṉṉāṭu -kuṭa kaṭaṟ pakkattatoru nāṭu, puḷḷunāṭu eṉpatum pāṭamākak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, puṇṇāṭeṉpatu puṉṉāṭeṉac citintatu eṉalumām. yāḻici maṟuku eṉṟamiyāl, pāḻiyiṉkaṇ paricil peṟum pāṇar mutalāyiṉār iṭiyaṟātu varuvar eṉpatu peṟṟām. naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉukku āay eyiṉaṉ pakivaṉum, miñili naṭpāḷaṉum āvar eṉpatum, āay eyiṉaṉ pāḻiyiṉkaṇ miñiliyoṭu porutu uyir tuṟantaṉaṉ eṉpatum, muṉṉar `yāma iraviṉ'1 eṉṉum ceyyuḷuṭ pōntami kāṇka. colliyatu amiyātu - tāṉ colliya coṟ piḻiyātu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. tēṟṟi - teḷivittu. tēṟṟi muṉ ki paṟṟiya col eṉṟataṉi, muṉ ki paṟṟit tēṟṟiya col eṉa māṟuka. kaṭavuḷ muṉṉarc cūḷurittut tēṟṟuvatu, `muṉṟēṟṟu' eṉappaṭum eṉpatu, `kaṭiyeṉ kiḷavi'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu muṉṟēṟṟu eṉa varuvataṉālum, ataṟku uriyāciriyar palarum `kaṭuñcūḷ taruku veṉ niṉakkē'3 eṉpataṉi eṭuttukkāṭṭi yiruttalālum peṟappaṭum. eṉavē akanāṉūṟṟup patippukkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum tōṟṟi eṉṉum pāṭattiṉum tēṟṟi* yeṉpatē ciṟappuṭit tātal kāṇka. āay eyiṉaṉ, oru kāl colliya coṟ piḻiyātu, ētilār poruṭṭut taṉ uyiriyum koṭuttaṉaṉ; nīyō niṉakku iṉiyaḷākak koṇṭu eṉatu nalattiyum nukarntu, piṉ kaṭavuḷ muṉṉarc cūḷuṭaṉ kūṟiya uriyiyum tappi eṉṉik ki viṭuttup piṟaṉ āyiṉi eṉat talivaṉ koṭumiyi avaṉ uṇarumāṟu kātaṟ paratti eṭutturittu, attakiya niṉṉi viṭukkuveṉ allēṉ eṉṟāḷ eṉka. manti - ātimanti, atti - āṭṭaṉatti. āriyar alaṟat tākki vaṭa variyil viṟ poṟittavaṉ, imaya varampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ eṉṉum cēra maṉṉaṉ; itaṉi, `imiyam viṟ poṟittu' eṉavum `pērici marapiṉ āriyar vaṇakki' eṉavum patiṟṟuppattiṉ iraṇṭām pattup patikattil neṭuñcēralātaṉ kūṟappaṭutalāṉ aṟika. in nikaḻccikaḷ avaṉ mintaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉālum nikaḻntami cilappatikārattāl aṟiyappaṭutaliṉ, piṇittōṉ eṉṟatu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉalum poruntum.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum pulavik kaṇṇum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itu kāmakkiḻatti eṉ nalam tā eṉṟatu eṉṟaṉar nac.
397. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		eṉmakaḷ perumaṭam yāṉpā rāṭṭat
		tāytaṉ cemmal kaṇṭukaṭaṉ iṟuppa
		muḻavumukam pularā viḻavuṭi viyaṉakar
		maṇaṉiṭi yākak koḷḷāṉ kalpakak
	5.	kaṇamaḻi tuṟanta kāṉmayaṅ kaḻuvam
		eḷiya vāka ēntukoṭi paranta
		poṟivari alkul māa yōṭkeṉat
		taṇinta paruvañ cellāṉ paṭartarat
		tuṇintōṉ maṉṟa tuṉiveṅ kāḷi
	10.	kaṭumpakaṭ ṭoruttal naṭuṅkak kuttip
		pōḻpuṇ paṭutta poriyari ōmip
		perumpoḷic cēyari nōkki ūṉcettuk
		karuṅkāl yāttup paruntuvan tiṟukkum
		cēṇuyarn tōṅkiya vāṉuyar neṭuṅkōṭṭuk
	15.	kōṭi vevvaḷik kulamarum
		pullili vetira nelviḷi kāṭē.
- kayamaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tuṉi vem kāḷi - viritaliyuṭiya vevviya kāḷi pōṉṟavaṉ,
1-4. muḻavu mukam pularā viḻavu uṭi viyaṉ nakar - muḻaviṉ oli māṟāta viḻāvuṭiya akaṉṟa maṉiyil, eṉ makaḷ peru maṭam yāṉ pārāṭṭa - eṉ makaḷatu periya maṭappattiṉi yāṉ pārāṭṭavum, tāy - naṟṟāy, taṉ cemmal kaṇṭu kaṭaṉ iṟuppa - taṉatu talimiyi aṟintu taṉ kaṭamikaḷic celuttavum, maṇaṉ iṭiyākak koḷḷāṉ - maṇattiṉ vāyilākak koḷḷāmalum,
4-8. kal paka kaṇa maḻi tuṟanta - pāṟikaḷ piḷavupaṭak kūṭṭamākiya mēkam peyyā toḻinta, ēntu koṭi paranta kāṉ mayaṅku aḻuvam - uyarnta koṭi paranta kāṭaṭarnta parappukkaḷ, poṟi vari alkul māyōṭku - puḷḷikaḷum varikaḷum poruntiya alkuliyuṭiya māmi niṟattiṉiyuṭiyāṭku, eḷiyavāka eṉa - naṭattaṟku eḷimiyuṭiyaṉavāka veṉṟu, taṇinta paruvam cellāṉ - veppam taṇinta paruvattē cellāmalum,
10-16. kaṭum pakaṭṭu oruttal - kaṭiya perumiyiyuṭiya āṇ yāṉi, naṭuṅkak kutti - maram aciyumpaṭi kutti, pōḻ puṇ paṭutta - piḷattalāl puṇ uṇṭākkiya, pori ari ōmi - porikaḷ poruntiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ōmi marattiṉ, perum poḷi cēy ari nōkki - periya paṭṭi yurinta civanta aṭi marattiṉi nōkki, ūṉ cettu - ataṉi ūṉ eṉak karuti, cēṇ uyarntu ōṅkiya - neṭitu uyarnta, karuṅ kāl yāttu - kariya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya yā marattiluḷḷa, paruntu vantu iṟukkum - paruntu vantu taṅkum, vāṉ uyar neṭuṅkōṭṭu - vāṉiṉkaṇ uyarnta neṭiya cimiyaṅkaḷiluḷḷa, kōṭi vevvaḷikku ulamarum - vevviya mēl kāṟṟiṉāl aliyum, pul ili vetira nel viḷi kāṭu - pulliya ilikaḷiyuṭiya mūṅkiliṉ nel viḷiyum kāṭṭiṉkaṇ,
8-9. paṭartarat tuṇintōṉ maṉṟa - uṭaṉkoṇṭu cella oru taliyākat tuṇintāṉ.
(muṭipu) tuṉi veṅ kāḷi, yāṉ pārāṭṭa, tāy kaṭaṉ iṟuppa viyaṉakar maṇaṉ iṭiyākak koḷḷāṉ, kāṉmayaṅku aḻuvam māyōṭku eḷiyavāka eṉat taṇinta paruvam cellāṉ, nel viḷi kāṭu paṭartarat tuṇintōṉ maṉṟa.
(vi-ri) taṉ cemmal - talivaṉatu māṇpu. muḻavu mukam pularāmiyāvatu - iṭiyaṟātu olittal. maṇaṉ iṭiyākak koḷḷāṉ - varintu koḷḷāṉāy eṉṟapaṭi. ēntu koṭi paranta eṉṟataṉi alkulukku aṭiyākki, uyarnta koṭipōṟ paranta varikaḷiyuṭiya eṉṟalumām. māyōṭku eḷiyavāka eṉa māṟik kūṭṭuka. tuṉital - virital, poṟumiyillāmi eṉṟapaṭi. yāṉi uṇṭaṟ poruṭṭuk kuttip puṇpaṭutta ōmiyiṉ ari eṉka. yāṉi ōmiyi urittut tiṉṉumeṉpatu `kayantali maṭappiṭi yuyaṅku paci kaḷiiyar; peruṅkaḷiṟu tolitta muṭattā ḷōmi'1 `kāṉa yāṉi tōṉayan tuṇṭa poritā ḷōmi'2 eṉpaṉavaṟṟālum aṟika. ōṅkiya yāttu eṉka. iṟukkum kāṭu eṉavum, neṭuṅkōṭṭu vetira kāṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
398. kuṟiñci
(kāmamikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷaviyāl (variviṭattukkaṇ) talimakaḷ talimakaṉ variyiṉiṉṟum pōnta āṟṟoṭu pulantu colliyatu.)
		3iḻinili nekiḻnta evvaṅ kūrap
		paṭarmali varuttamoṭu palapulan tacii
		meṉṟōḷ nekiḻac cāayk koṉṟi
		ūḻuṟu malariṟ pāḻpaṭa muṟṟiya
	5.	pacali mēṉi nōkki nutalpacantu
		iṉṉē mākiya emmivaṇ aruḷāṉ
		nummōṉ ceyta koṭumikku immeṉṟu
		alamaral maḻikkaṇ teṇpaṉi malka
		naṉṟu 4puṟa māṟiakaṟal yāḻaniṉ
	10.	kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭaṟ keṉṉeṉap paṭumō
		kariporu nīttam uriyeṉak kaḻaṟi
		niṉṉoṭu pulattal añci avarmalip
		paṉmalar pōrttu nāṇumika oṭuṅki
		maṟintaṉi kaḻiyum niṟṟantu celutti
	15.	nayaṉaṟat tuṟattal valli yōrē
		notuma lāḷar atuka ṇōṭātu
		aḻaṟciṉi vēṅki niḻaṟṟavirn tacii
		māri puṟantara nanti āriyar
		poṉpaṭu neṭuvari puriyum enti
	20.	palpūṅ kāṉat talki iṉṟivaṇ
		cērntaṉi celiṉē citikuva tuṇṭō
		kuyavari irumpōttup poruta puṇkūrntu
		uyaṅkupiṭi taḻīiya mataṉaḻi yāṉi
		vāṅkamik kaḻiyiṉ naralumavar
	25.	ōṅkumali nāṭṭiṉ varūu vōyē.
- immeṉkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. kari poru nīttam - kariyip porutu varum veḷḷam poruntiya yāṟē!
22-25. kuyavari irupōttu poruta puṇ kūrntu - periya āṇ puli tākkiyatālākiya puṇ mikuntu, uyaṅku piṭi taḻīiya mataṉ aḻi yāṉi - varuntum peṇ yāṉiyāl taḻuvappaṭum vali kuṉṟiya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi, vāṅku ami kaḻiyiṉ naralum - vaḷinta mūṅkilāṟ ceyta tūmpu pōla olikkum, avar ōṅku mali nāṭṭiṉ varūuvōy - em talivaratu uyarnta mali nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum varuvōy!
1-16. iḻi nili nekiḻnta evvam kūra - aṇikaḷ niliyiṉiṉṟum nekiḻtaṟku ētuvākiya tuṉpam mika, paṭar mali varuttamoṭu - niṉivu mikka varuttattuṭaṉ, pala pulantu acii - palavaṟṟiyum veṟuttuk kūṟittaṅki, mel tōḷ nekiḻa - melliya tōḷ meliya, cāay - varunti, koṉṟi ūḻ uṟu malaril - koṉṟiyiṉatu naṉku malarnta pūkkaḷippōla, pāḻpaṭa muṟṟiya pacali mēṉi nōkki - pāḻpaṭa niṟinta pacaliyuṟṟa mēṉiyip pārttu, nutal pacantu - neṟṟi pacaliyuṟappeṟṟu, iṉṉēm ākiya em ivaṇ aruḷāṉ - iṉṉa niliyiṉēm ākiya emmi iṅku aruḷutal ceyyāṉāy, nummōṉ ceyta koṭumikku - num talivaṉ ceyta vaṉkaṇmikku, immeṉṟu alamaral maḻi kaṇ teṇ paṉi malka - kalaṅkum  kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum virintu teḷinta nīr perukavum, naṉṟu puṟam māṟi akaṟal - aṟattiṉik kiviṭṭu nīṅkutal, niṉ kuṉṟu keḻu nāṭaṟku eṉ eṉappaṭumō - kuṉṟu poruntiya nāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya niṉ talivaṉukku yāteṉappaṭumō, uri eṉak kaḻaṟi - urip
pāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi, niṉṉoṭu pulattal añci - niṉṉoṭu yām veṟuttali añci, avar mali pala malar pōrttu - avar maliyiluḷḷa pala malarkaḷiyum pōrttuk koṇṭu, nāṇu mika oṭuṅki - nāṇattāl mikavum oṭuṅki, maṟintaṉi kaḻiyum - maṟintu nīṅkum, niṉ tantu celutti - niṉṉik koṇarntu pōkki, notumalāḷar - ētilāḷarākiya avar, nayaṉ aṟat tuṟattal valliyōr - nītiyiṉṟik kiviṭutaṟku vaṉmiyuṭiyōr āvar, atu kaṇ ōṭātu - ataṉi nōkkātu,
17-21. aḻal ciṉi vēṅki niḻal tavirntu acii - tīyiṉi yokkum pūkkaḷ niṟinta kiḷiyiyuṭiya vēṅkiyiṉ niḻalil niṉ celaviṉit tavirntu taṅki, māri puṟantara nanti - peyyum maḻi purattalāl perukkamuṟṟu, āriyar poṉpaṭu neṭu vari puriyum - āriyaratu poṉ poruntiya nīṇṭa imiya maliyi yokkum, enti palpūṅ kāṉattu alki - em tantiyatu pala pūkkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭil amarntu, iṉṟu ivaṇ cērntaṉi celiṉē - iṟṟip
poḻutu ivviṭattē cērntiruntu celliṉ, citikuvatu uṇṭō - niṉ kāriyam ētēṉum keṭuvatu uṇṭō.
(muṭipu) kari poru nīttam! avar ōṅku mali nāṭṭiṉ varūuvōy! evvam kūra, varuttamoṭu acii tōḷ nekiḻac cāay, pacali mēṉi nōkki nutal pacantu iṉṉēmākiya em ivaṇ aruḷāṉ nummōṉ ceyta koṭumikku, kaṇ paṉi malka, naṉṟu puṟamāṟi akaṟal, niṉ kuṉṟu keḻu nāṭaṟku eṉṉeṉap paṭumō uri eṉak kaḻaṟip pulattal añci, malarpōrttu oṭuṅki maṟintaṉi kaḻiyum niṟṟantu celutti notumalāḷar tuṟattal valliyōr, atu kaṇ ōṭātu vēṅki niḻal tavirntu acii, nanti, kāṉattu alki, ivaṇ cērntaṉi celiṉē citikuvatu uṇṭō.
(vi-ri) nekiḻnta - nekiḻtaṟku ētuvākiya. pāḻpaṭa - vaṟitē. nummōṉ - numaṉ, num talivaṉ. naṉṟu - aṟam. puṟam māṟal - ki viṭutal. kuṉṟu keḻu nāṭaṟku - cuṭṭu. eṉ eṉap paṭumō - yātākumō eṉṟapaṭi. nummōṉ eṉavum niṉ kuṉṟu keḻunāṭaṉ eṉavum kūṟiṉamiyāl, kaḻaṟutalum pulattalum yāṟṟiṉ mēla āyiṉa. maṟintu kaḻitalāvatu malar pōrttuk kaḻital. niṉṉoṭu pulattal añci nī malar pōrttu oṭuṅki maṟintu kaḻiyāniṉṟaṉi, aṅṅaṉamākavum notumalāḷar niṉṉit tantu celuttit tuṟattal valliyōr āvar eṉa virittu urittuk koḷka. kaṇ ōṭātu - nōkkātu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. nantutal - perukutal. iṉṟu ivaṇ cērntaṉi celiṉē citikuvatu uṇṭō eṉṟatu, ataṉāl niṉakku varakkaṭavatoru tīṅku illāmiyoṭu avaratu malināṭṭiṉiṉṟum varukiṉṟā yākaliṉ niṉṉuṭaṉ oru nāḷ taṅki yāmum āṟṟiyiruttalumākum eṉṟu kaḻipaṭar tōṉṟak kūṟiyavāṟu. kuyavari - kuyampōlum varikaḷiyuṭiya puli. kuyam - arivāḷ. vali yaḻinta yāṉi tūmpupōl uyirkkum eṉpatu. `ōy kaḷi ṟeṭutta nōyuṭi neṭuṅki, toku coṟ kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ uyirkkum'1 eṉa muṟ pōntataṉālum aṟika. kaḻi eṉṟatu īṇṭut tūmpu eṉṉum poruṭṭu.
399. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		cimiyak kurala cāntarunti iruḷi
		imiyak kāṉam nāṟuṅ kūntal
		naṉṉutal arivi iṉṉuṟal ākam
		paruku vaṉṉa kātal uḷḷamoṭu
	5.	tirukupu muyaṅkal iṉṟiyavaṇ nīṭār
		kaṭaṟṟaṭi maruṅkiṉ kaṇicciyiṟ kuḻitta
		uṭikkaṇ nīṭami ūṟal uṇṭa
		pāṭiṉ teṇmaṇip payaṅkeḻu peruniri
		vāṭupulam pukkeṉak kōṭutuvit takaṟṟi
	10.	olkunilik kaṭukki alkuniḻal aciip
		pallāṉ kōvalar kallātu ūtum
		ciṟuvetirn tīṅkuḻaṟ pulampukoḷ teḷviḷi
		miyil paḷiṅkiṉ aṉṉa tōṟṟap
		palkōḷ nellip piṅkāy arunti
	15.	melkiṭu maṭamari ōrkkum attam
		kāykatir kaṭukiya kaviṉaḻi piṟaṅkal
		vēykaṇ uṭinta cimiya
		vāypaṭu maruṅkiṉ maliyiṟan tōrē.
- eyiṉanti makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. cimiya kurala cāntu arunti iruḷi - ucciyiluḷḷa pūṅkottukkaḷi yuṭiyaṉavāy mayirccāntiṉik koṇṭu iruṇṭu, imiya kāṉam nāṟum - imiya maliyiluḷḷa kāṉam pōla nāṟum, kūntal - kūntaliyum, nalnutal - nalla neṟṟiyiṉiyu
muṭiya, arivi - taliviyē!
6-18. kaṭaṟu aṭi maruṅkiṉ - kaṟ kāṭṭiṉic cārnta pakkattil, kaṇicciyil kuḻitta - kuntāliyāl tōṇṭiṉa, uṭikkaṇ - kuḻiyiṭattē, nīṭu ami ūṟal uṇṭa - tāḻttal poruntiya ūṟṟu nīri yuṇṭa, pāṭu iṉ teḷ maṇi - iṉiya teḷinta ōciyiyuṭiya maṇikaḷip pūṇṭa, payam keḻu peru niri - payaṉ mikka periya āṉiri, vāṭu pulam pukkeṉa - vaṟaṇṭa pāliyiṟ pukuntatāka, kōṭu tuvittu akaṟṟi - ūtu kompiṉāl oliyi yuṇṭākki avaṟṟi aṅku niṉṟum vilakki, olku nili kaṭukki alku niḻal acii - taḷarnta niliyiṉiyuṭiya koṉṟiyiṉ kuṟinta niḻalil taṅkuvittu, pal āṉ kōvalar - pala āṉiṉaṅkaḷiyuṭiya āyar, kallātu ūtum - iciyik kallāmal ūtum, ciṟu vetir tīm kuḻal - ciṟiya mūṅkilāṟ ceyta iṉiya kuḻaliṉiṉṟeḻum, pulampu koḷ teḷ viḷi - taṉimi koṇṭa teḷinta ōciyiṉi, mi il paḷiṅkiṉ aṉṉa tōṟṟap palkōḷ nellip piṅkāy arunti - kuṟṟamaṟṟa paḷiṅkiṉip pōṉṟa tōṟṟattiyuṭiya palavākak kāytta nelliyiṉ paciya kāykaḷi uṇṭu, melkiṭu maṭa mari - aciyiṭukiṉṟa iḷiya marimāṉ, ōrkkum - kūrntu kēṭkum, attam - kāṭṭilē, kāy katir kaṭukiya kaviṉ aḻi piṟaṅkal - kāyum ñāyiṟu muṭukiya aḻaku oḻinta pāṟikaḷiyum, vēy kaṇ uṭinta cimiyam - mūṅkil kaṇukkaḷ uṭinta cikaraṅ kaḷiyum, vāy paṭu maruṅkiṉ - iṭamakaṉṟa pakka maliyiṉiyu muṭiya, mali iṟantōr - maliyiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam talivar,
3-5. iṉ uṟal ākam - niṉatu iṉimi poruntutali yuṭiya mārpiṉi, paruku aṉṉa kātal uḷḷamoṭu - parukutal pōlum kātal mikka uḷḷattuṭaṉ, tirukupu muyaṅkal iṉṟi- piṉṉit taḻuvutal iṉṟi, avaṇ nīṭār - aṅkut tāḻttirār.
(muṭipu) naṉṉutal arivi! maliyiṟantōr, niṉ iṉ uṟal ākam kātal uḷḷamoṭu muyaṅkal iṉṟi, avaṇ nīṭār.
(vi-ri) cimiyam - maram putarkaḷiṉ ucci. kural - pūṅkottu. kuralvāy nāṟuṅ kūntal eṉka. tirukutal - oruvar meyyuṭaṉ oruvar mey piṉṉutal. uṭi - uṭitteṭutta kiṇaṟṟiṉik kuṟippa tāyiṟṟu. payam - payaṉ, pāl. kallātu ūtum - āciriyaṉiṭattuk kaṟkāmalē iyalpāka ūtum eṉka. kōvalar ūtum tīṅkuḻaliṉ teḷviḷiyi mari ōrkkum attattilē piṟaṅkalum cimiyamum maruṅkum poruntiya mali yiṟantu ceṉṟōr eṉka.
400. neytal
(talimakaṉ varinteytiya piṉṟit tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.)
		nakinaṉ ṟamma tāṉē avaṉoṭu
		maṉiyiṟan talkiṉum alareṉa nayantu
		kāṉal alkiya naṅkaḷa vakalap
		palpurin tiyaṟal uṟṟa nalviṉi
	5.	1nūlami piṟappiṉ nīla uttik
		koymayir eruttam piṇarpaṭap peruki
		neymmiti muṉiiya koḻuñcōṟ ṟārki
		niraliyin toṉṟiya celaviṟ centiṉik
		kuralvārnta taṉṉa 2kulavuttali naṉṉāṉku
	10.	vīṅkucuval mociyat tāṅku 3nukam taḻīip
		pūmpoṟip palpaṭi olippap pūṭṭi
		matiyuṭi valavaṉ ēvaliṉ ikutuṟip
		puṉalpāyn taṉṉa vāmāṉ tiṇṭērk
		kaṇikaḻin taṉṉa nōṉkāl vaṇpari
	15.	pālkaṇ ṭaṉṉa ūti veṇmaṇal
		kālkaṇ ṭaṉṉa vaḻipaṭap pōki
		ayirccēṟ ṟaḷḷal aḻuvat tāṅkaṇ
		iruṇīr iṭṭuccuram nīntit tuṟikeḻu
		mellam pulampaṉ vanta ñāṉṟip
	20.	pūmali iruṅkaḻit tuyalvarum aṭiyoṭu
		nēmi tanta neṭunīr neytal
		viḷiyā iḷaṅkaḷ nāṟap palavuṭaṉ
		1potiyaviḻ taṇmalar kaṇṭu naṉṟum
		putuva tākiṉ ṟamma paḻaviṟaṟ
	25.	pāṭeḻun tiraṅkum munnīr
		nīṭirum peṇṇi nam aḻuṅkal ūrē.
- ulōccaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. (tōḻi,) maṉi iṟantu alkiṉum - maṉiyik kaṭantu taṅkiṉum, alar eṉa - alarākum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭavum, avaṉoṭu nayantu kāṉal alkiya nam kaḷavu akala - nam talivaṉuṭaṉ virumpic cōliyilē ceṉṟu taṅkiya nam kaḷavoḻukkam nīṅkumpaṭi, pal purintu iyaṟal uṟṟa nalviṉi - muṟ piṟappil pala naṉmikaḷum ceytu poruntutaluṟṟa nalviṉiyāl,
5-19. nūl ami piṟappiṉ - puravi nūl kūṟum ilakkaṇam aminta piṟappiṉiyum, nīla utti - nīla maṇiyālākiya neṟṟic cuṭṭiyiṉiyum, koy mayir eruttam piṇarpaṭap peruki - caruccari yuṇṭāka aṭarntu koyyappeṟṟa mayir poruntiya piṭariyiṉiyum uṭiya, ney miti muṉiiya koḻuñ cōṟṟu ārki - ney peytu mitittiyaṟṟiya kavaḷatti veṟutta koḻuviya cōṟṟi uṇṇutaliyuṭiya, niral iyintu oṉṟiya celavil - variciyākap porunti oru peṟṟiyē cellum celaviṉiyum, centiṉi kural vārntaṉṉa kulavu tali - cevviya tiṉiyiṉ katir nīṇṭu vaḷintāṟ pōṉṟa vaḷinta taliyiṉiyum uṭiya, nal nāṉku - naṉṟākiya nāṉku kutirikaḷi, tāṅku nukam taḻīi - tāṅkum nukattiṉip porunti, vīṅku cuval mociya - parutta kaḻuttil neruṅka (aṇinta), pūm poṟi pal paṭi olippa pūṭṭi,  aḻakiya puḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya pala vaṭaṅkaḷ olikkumpaṭi pūṭṭi, matiyuṭi valavaṉ ēvaliṉ - aṟivuṭiya pākaṉ celuttaliṉ, iku tuṟi puṉal pāyntaṉṉa - tāḻnta tuṟiyil nīr pāyntālotta, vām māṉtiṇ tēr - tāvic cellum kutiri pūṭṭiya tiṇṇiya tēriṉ, kaṇi kaḻintaṉṉa nōṉ kāl vaṇ pari - ampu pāyntāṟ pōṉṟa valiya kāliṉ virinta celavu, pāl kaṇṭaṉṉa ūti veṇmaṇal - pālik kaṇṭāṟ pōṉṟa kāṟṟāl tiraṭṭappeṟṟa veḷḷiya maṇaliṭattē, kāl kaṇṭaṉṉa vaḻipaṭa pōki - vāykkālik kaṇṭāṟpōṉṟa cuvaṭu uṇṭākap pōka, ayir cēṟṟu aḷḷal aḻuvattu āṅkaṇ - nuṇṇiya kuḻampākiya cēṟṟiṉ parappiṉiyuṭiya avviṭattē, iruḷ nīr iṭṭu curam nīnti - iruṇṭa nīriṉiyuṭiya kuṟukiya kaḻiyik kaṭantu, tuṟi keḻu mel am pulampaṉ - tuṟi - poruntiya kaṭaṟ kariyiyuṭiya nam talivaṉ, vanta ñāṉṟi - vanta aṉṟu,
20-23. pūmali iru kaḻi tuyalvarum aṭiyoṭu - pūkkaḷ niṟinta kariya kaḻiyiṭattē ilikaḷōṭu aciyum, nēmi tanta neṭu nīr neytal - āḻamāṉa nīriyuṭiya kaṭal tanta neytaliṉ, viḷiyā iḷaṅ kaḷ nāṟa - mutirāta iḷiya kaḷ nāṟumpaṭi, poti aviḻ taṇ malar - kaṭṭu aviḻnta taṇṇiya malarkaḷi, palavuṭaṉ kaṇṭu - ēṉip pūkkaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ (cūṭi varutalik) kaṇṭu,
23-26. paḻa viṟal pāṭu eḻuntu iraṅkum - paḻiya perumiyuṭiya āravāram mikku muḻaṅkum, munnīr - kaṭaṟkarik kaṇṇuḷḷa, nīṭu iru peṇṇi - nīṇṭa periya paṉimaratti yuṭiya, nam aḻuṅkal ūr - alar āravāram mikka namatu ūr, naṉṟum putuvatu ākiṉṟu- (anta alariṉi viṭuttu) mikavum putiya taṉmiyiṉi uṭiyatu ākiṉṟatu,
1. naki naṉṟu - atu, peritum nakiyiṉi viḷippatākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāṉal alkiya nam kaḷavu akala, nalviṉiyāl, mellam pulampaṉ iruḷnīr iṭṭuc curam nīnti vanta ñāṉṟi, avaṉ malar cūṭi varutalik kaṇṭu, nam aḻuṅ
kalūr naṉṟum putuvatākiṉṟu, amma! atu naki naṉṟu, amma!
(vi-ri) kāṉal alkiya - maṉiyiṉ aḷavilaṉṟik kāṭṭilum ceṉṟu taṅkiya eṉka. kaḷavu akala vanta ñāṉṟi eṉavum, nal viṉiyāl vanta ñāṉṟi eṉavum kūṭṭuka. neymmiti - koḷḷuṭaṉ ney peytu amitta kavaḷam. nāṉku - nāṉkākiya kutirikaḷ, ākupeyar. piṟappiṉiyum uttiyiṉiyum eruttattiṉiyum uṭiya nāṉku eṉavum, ārkiyuṭiya nāṉku eṉavum, celaviṉiyum taliyiṉiyum uṭiya nāṉku eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. mociya aṇinta palpaṭi eṉṟu viritturikka. paṭi eṉṟatu kaḻuttu aṇikaḷi. pōki - pōka eṉat tirikka. tiṇtēr nōṉ kāl veṇmaṇal vaḻipaṭap pōka eṉka. vanta ñāṉṟi - vantu varinta ñāṉṟi. aṭiyoṭu tuyalvarum eṉavum neṭunīr nēmi eṉavum māṟik kūṭṭuka. neytaliṉ taṇ malarip palavuṭaṉ cūṭi varutal kaṇṭu eṉka. nāṟa eṉṉum viṉiyeccam aviḻ eṉṉum viṉikoṇṭu muṭintatu. talivaṉ varin teytutal kaṇṭu, ūr alar kūṟutal aṭaṅkiviṭṭa teṉpāḷ, aḻuṅkal ūr putuvatākiṉṟu eṉṟāḷ. amma iraṇṭum uriyaci iṭiccol.
eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya neṭuntoki 
yeṉappaṭum akanāṉūṟṟukku nāvalar paṇṭita, na.mu. vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭārum, karantik kaviyaracu, ār. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷiyum iyaṟṟiya "vēṅkaṭa viḷakku" eṉṉum uri muṟṟiṟṟu.
*****


pāyiram
		niṉṟa nīti veṉṟa nēmip
		paḻutil koḷki vaḻutiya ravikkaṇ
		aṟivuvīṟ ṟirunta ceṟivuṭi maṉattu
		vāṉṟōy nallicic cāṉṟōr kuḻīi
	5.	aruntamiḻ mūṉṟun terinta kāli
		āynta koḷkit tīntamiḻp pāṭṭuḷ
		neṭiya vāki yaṭinimirn toḻukiya
		iṉpap pakuti yiṉporuṭ pāṭal
		nāṉū ṟeṭuttu nūṉavil pulavar
	10.	kaḷitta mummatak kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiniri
		maṇiyoṭu miṭinta aṇikiḷar pavaḷam
		mēviya nittilak kōvi yeṉṟāṅku
		attaku paṇpiṉ muttiṟa māka
		muṉṉiṉar tokutta naṉṉeṭun tokikkuk
	15.	karutteṉap paṇpiṉō rurittavi nāṭiṉ
		avvakik kavitām cevviya vaṉṟi
		ariyavi yākiya poruṇmi nōkkik
		kōṭṭa miṉṟip pāṭṭoṭu poruntat
		takavoṭu ciṟanta akaval naṭiyāṟ
	20.	karuttiṉi tiyaṟṟi yōṉē parittēr
		vaḷavar kākkum vaḷanāṭ ṭuḷḷum
		nāṭeṉac ciṟanta pīṭukeḻu ciṟappiṟ
		keṭalaruñ ciṟappiṉ iṭiyaḷa nāṭṭut
		tītil koḷki mūtū ruḷḷum
	25.	ūreṉac ciṟanta cīrkeḻu maṇakkuṭic
		cemmi cāṉṟa tēvaṉ
		toṉmi cāṉṟa naṉmi yōṉē.
"it tokikkuk karuttu akavalāṟ pāṭiṉāṉ, iṭiyaḷa nāṭṭu maṇakkuṭiyāṉ pālvaṇṇa tēvaṉāṉa villava tariyaṉ. neṭuntoki nāṉūṟum karuttiṉoṭu muṭintaṉa. ivi pāṭiṉa kavikaḷ nūṟṟu nāṟpattivar.
ittokip pāṭṭiṟ kaṭiyaḷavu ciṟumi patiṉmūṉṟu; perumi muppattoṉṟu. tokuppittāṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti. tokuttāṉ maturi uppūri kuṭikiḻār makaṉāvāṉ uruttiracaṉmaṉ eṉpāṉ.
`vaṇṭupaṭat tatinta kaṇṇi' eṉpatu mutalāka `neṭuvēṇ mārpiṉ' eṉpatu īṟākak kiṭanta pāṭṭu nūṟṟirupatum kaḷiṟṟi yāṉi niri yeṉappaṭum. ip peyar kāraṇattāṟ peṟṟatu; itu poruṭ kāraṇamākak koḷka. `nānakiyuṭiya neñcē; eṉpatu mutalāka `nāḷvali mukanta' eṉpatu īṟākak kiṭanta pāṭṭu nūṟṟeṇpatum maṇimiṭi pavaḷam eṉappaṭum. ituvuṅ kāraṇap peyar; eṉṉi, ceyyuḷum poruḷum ovvāmiyāl. `vaṟaṉuṟu ceyti' eṉpatu mutalāka `nakinaṉṟamma' eṉpatu īṟākak kiṭanta pāṭṭu nūṟum nittilak kōvi yeṉappaṭum; ceyyuḷum poruḷum okkumākalāṉ.'
akanāṉūṟṟiṉ tokuppu muṟi
		viyamellām veṇṭē riyakkam; kayamalarnta
		tāmariyā ṟākat takipeṟīik kāmar
		naṟumulli nāṉkāka nāṭṭi veṟimāṇṭa
		eṭṭum iraṇṭum kuṟiñciyāk kuṭṭattu
		ivartiri pattā iyaṟpaṭa yāttāṉ
		tokiyi ṉeṭiyataṉit tōlāc ceviyāṉ
		vakiyi ṉeṭiyataṉi vippu.
		oṉṟu mūṉṟin tēḻoṉ pāṉpāli; ōtātu
		niṉṟavaṟṟiṉ nāṉku neṟimulli; - aṉṟiyē
		āṟām marutam; aṇineytal aiyiraṇṭu;
		kūṟāta vikuṟiñcik kūṟṟu.
		pāli viyamellām; pattām paṉineytal;
		nālu naḷimulli nāṭuṅkāl; - mēliyōr
		tēṟum iraṇṭeṭ ṭivikuṟiñci; centamiḻiṉ
		āṟu marutam akam.
viyam - oṟṟiyeṇkaḷ. veṇṭē riyakkam - pāli. tāmari- marutam. kuṭṭattu ivar tiri - neytal. tōlāc ceviyāṉ - kēḷviyuṇarvu mikkavaṉ.
akanāṉūṟu - nittilak kōvi
